Across multiple retrosynthesis benchmarks, MORetro* produces diverse, high-quality Pareto fronts, uncovering solutions overlooked by single-objective approaches and better aligning CASP outputs with industrial decision-making.

</details>


### [60] [Multi-Environment POMDPs with Finite-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07537)
*Léonard Brice,Filip Cano,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Thomas A. Henzinger,Stefanie Muroya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了有限视界目标下多环境POMDP最优值与策略计算问题的PSPACE完全性，并提出了一个显著优于现有方法的实用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观察马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）在智能体仅能获得部分状态信息的情况下与环境交互。多环境POMDP（MEPOMDP）是一个更一般的设定，其初始状态未知且由对抗者选择。现有研究已知POMDP中有限视界目标的策略计算是PSPACE完全的，但MEPOMDP的计算复杂度未知，且缺乏高效的实用算法。

Method: 首先通过理论分析证明了MEPOMDP在有限视界目标下的最优值与策略计算问题同样是PSPACE完全的。随后提出了一种新的实用算法用于计算MEPOMDP的最优值和策略，并在经典基准测试上进行评估。

Result: 理论方面证明了MEPOMDP有限视界目标计算问题的PSPACE完全性；实验方面，所提出的算法在经典基准测试上显著优于此前唯一的已知算法。

Conclusion: MEPOMDP的有限视界最优策略计算问题在计算复杂度上与POMDP相同，均为PSPACE完全；同时本文提供的算法在实践中表现优异，显著提升了求解效率。

Abstract: Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) are systems in which one agent interacts with a stochastic environment, and receives only partial information about the current state. In a multi-environment POMDP (MEPOMDP), the initial state is unknown, and assumed to be adversarially chosen. In this work we focus on computing the optimal value and policy in MEPOMDPs with finite-horizon objectives. That problem is known to be PSPACE-complete in POMDPs. Our main results are as follows: (1) we establish that it is also PSPACE-complete in the more general setting of MEPOMDPs; (2) we present a practical algorithm and evaluate it on classical benchmarks, significantly outperforming the only previously known algorithm.

</details>


### [61] [From Pixels to Prompts: Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07544)
*Khang Hoang Nhat Vo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一本关于视觉-语言模型的指南书籍，旨在为读者提供该领域的清晰思维导图和直觉理解，而非详尽的目录清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型领域发展迅速，新模型层出不穷，学习者容易迷失方向；作者感受到“知道流行词汇”与“真正理解工作原理”之间存在显著差距，希望为读者提供清晰的结构框架。

Method: 采用非穷尽式目录的方法，专注于提供视觉-语言模型的清晰思维导图和核心概念，旨在让读者能够自信地阅读新论文并独立设计系统，而非盲目组装组件。

Result: 提供了一个持久的、清晰的心智模型，使读者能够自信地阅读新论文，并在设计自己的系统时具备足够的直觉和结构化知识。

Conclusion: 本书的目标是提供一份适度而持久的知识框架，帮助读者建立对视觉-语言模型的清晰理解和直觉，跨越从概念认知到实际应用的鸿沟。

Abstract: When you read a paper about a new Vision-Language Model today, it can be easy to forget how strange this idea would have sounded not so long ago. Teaching machines to see was already hard. Teaching them to read and generate language was already hard. Asking them to do both at once - and then to reason, answer questions, follow instructions, and sometimes even surprise us - still carries a quiet trace of science fiction, even as it becomes routine. This book was born from a simple feeling: \emph{it is too easy to get lost}. The field moves quickly, new model names appear constantly, and the gap between ``I know the buzzwords'' and ``I actually understand how this works'' can feel uncomfortably wide. I have felt that gap many times. If you are holding this book, you probably have too. My goal is not to provide an exhaustive catalog of every dataset, benchmark, and new model variant. Instead, I want to offer something more modest - and, I hope, more durable: a clear mental map of Vision-Language Models. Enough structure that you can read new papers with confidence; enough intuition that you can design your own systems without feeling as if you are assembling LEGO bricks blindly.

</details>


### [62] [Open-Ended Task Discovery via Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07572)
*Masaki Adachi,Yuta Suzuki,Juliusz Ziomek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GSR框架，通过交替任务生成与优化解决科学工作流中任务本身的不确定性问题，在多个应用场景中优于现有LLM优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学工作流中应用贝叶斯优化时，任务本身（优化目标和评估方式）会随证据积累而演化，这是一个常被忽视的不确定性来源。现有方法通常假设任务是固定的，无法适应任务定义的动态变化。

Method: 提出Generate-Select-Refine (GSR)开放型贝叶斯优化框架：1) 从用户提供的种子任务出发，以粗到细的方式生成新任务；2) 使用任务采集函数调度优化过程；3) 在任务生成与任务优化之间交替迭代。该框架能渐近地将评估集中在最优任务上。

Result: GSR框架相对于单任务贝叶斯优化仅产生对数级的遗憾开销。在四个实际应用场景（新产品开发、化学合成规模化、算法分析和专利再利用）中，GSR的性能优于现有的基于大语言模型的优化器。

Conclusion: GSR框架成功解决了科学工作流中任务演化的挑战，实现了任务生成与优化的有效平衡，为开放型贝叶斯优化提供了理论基础和实用方法，在多个科学应用领域展现出优越性能。

Abstract: When applying Bayesian optimization (BO) to scientific workflow, a major yet often overlooked source of uncertainty is the task itself -- namely, what to optimize and how to evaluate it -- which can evolve as evidence accumulates. We introduce Generate-Select-Refine (GSR), a open-ended BO framework that alternates between task generation and task optimization. Starting from a user-provided seed task, GSR generates new tasks in a coarse-to-fine manner while a task-acquisition function schedules optimization. Asymptotically, it concentrates evaluations on the best task, incurring only logarithmic regret overhead relative to single-task BO. We apply GSR to new product development, chemical synthesis scaling, algorithm analysis, and patent repurposing, where it outperforms existing LLM-based optimizers.

</details>


### [63] [Parallel Lifted Planning via Semi-Naive Datalog Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07584)
*Dominik Drexler,Oliver Joergensen,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有两层并行性（规则级并行和实例化并行）的Datalog执行模型，并设计了基于团枚举的专用求解器，在8核上实现了最高6倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升式经典规划器虽然避免了计算代价高昂的实例化步骤，但由于必须在搜索过程中重复实例化底层结构，导致其通常速度较慢。现有的许多核心组件（如后继生成、公理评估、任务实例化和删除松弛启发式）已通过Datalog求值的视角进行研究，本文旨在延续这一工作路线并进一步扩展。

Method: 开发并分析了一个具有两层并行性的执行模型：规则级并行性和实例化并行性。同时，针对规划特定的工作负载对求解器进行了专门化，设计了一个基于团枚举的实例化器，并将其扩展以支持半朴素Datalog求值。

Result: 在单核情况下，该实现已比基线解决了更多任务；随着核心数增加，差距进一步扩大。在实例化困难的任务中（平均97.6%的运行时用于Datalog执行），该执行模型平均并行比例达到92.4%，在8核上实现了最高6倍的实际加速。

Conclusion: 所提出的并行Datalog执行模型能够有效提升提升式经典规划的性能，特别是在实例化密集型任务中表现出极高的并行效率和显著的加速效果。

Abstract: Lifted classical planners operate directly on first-order planning tasks to avoid the computationally demanding grounding step. However, lifted planning is typically slower, as planners must repeatedly instantiate ground structures during search. Many core components of lifted classical planning, such as successor generation, axiom evaluation, task grounding, and delete-relaxed heuristics, have previously been studied through the lens of Datalog evaluation. We build upon this line of work and extend it by developing and analyzing an execution model with two levels of parallelism: rule-level parallelism and grounding parallelism. We further specialize this solver for planning-specific workloads with a grounder based on clique enumeration, which we extend to support semi-naive Datalog evaluation. Our experimental evaluation using greedy best-first search with the FF heuristic shows that our implementation already solves more tasks than the baselines on a single core, and the gap widens as additional cores are used. Moreover, on hard-to-ground tasks where on average 97.6% of the total runtime is spent in Datalog execution, the proposed execution model exhibits an average parallel fraction of 92.4%, while achieving up to a 6-fold speedup on 8 cores in practice.

</details>


### [64] [Inference Time Causal Probing in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07631)
*Sadegh Khorasani,Saber Salehkaleybar,Negar Kiyavash,Matthias Grossglauser*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了HDMI，一种无需探测器的因果干预方法，通过模型原生输出直接引导隐藏状态，在可靠性和有效性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果探测方法依赖辅助探测分类器定义干预，这导致方法与特定任务或模型绑定，且存在与模型预测几何不一致的风险。

Method: 提出无探测器的梯度技术HDMI，直接利用模型原生输出引导隐藏状态。该方法应用边际目标，在无需探测分类器的情况下增加目标续写的概率并降低源续写的概率。进一步提出了前瞻变体LA-HDMI，用于文本编辑，通过反向传播softmax嵌入来修改当前隐藏状态，以在保持流畅性的同时提高用户指定token的生成可能性。

Result: 在LGD一致性语料库和CausalGym基准测试中，HDMI在Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct和Pythia-70M模型上的一致性比现有方法更高。评估指标包括完整性（目标属性是否按预期变化）和选择性（无关属性是否得以保留），并报告二者的调和平均值作为整体可靠性度量。

Conclusion: HDMI作为一种无探测器的因果干预方法，能够更可靠地控制生成模型的内部表示对行为的影响，在多个基准和模型上表现优异。

Abstract: Causal probing methods aim to test and control how internal representations influence the behavior of generative models. In causal probing, an intervention modifies hidden states so that a property takes on a different value. Most existing approaches define such interventions by training an auxiliary probe classifier, which ties the method to a specific task or model and risks misalignment with the model's predictive geometry. We propose Hidden-state Driven Margin Intervention (HDMI), a probe-free, gradient-based technique that directly steers hidden states using the model's native output. HDMI applies a margin objective that increases the probability of a target continuation while decreasing that of the source, without relying on probe classifiers. We further introduce a lookahead variant (LA-HDMI) for text editing that backpropagates through the softmax embeddings, modifying the current hidden state so that the likelihood of user-specified tokens increases in next token generations while preserving fluency. To evaluate interventions, we measure completeness (whether the targeted property changes as intended) and selectivity (whether unrelated properties are preserved), and report their harmonic mean as an overall measure of reliability. HDMI consistently achieves higher reliability than prior methods on the LGD agreement corpus and the CausalGym benchmark, across Meta-Llama-3-8B-Instruct, and Pythia-70M.

</details>


### [65] [Tacit Knowledge Extraction via Logic Augmented Generation and Active Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07639)
*Lorenzo Lamazzi,Aldo Gangemi,Alessio Giberti,Andrea Giovanni Nuzzolese,Vittorio Andrea Rocca,Mattia Torta,Francesco Poggi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号框架，结合逻辑增强生成（LAG）和主动推理方法，用于构建本体支撑的知识图谱，有效解决了程序性领域中隐性知识难以形式化和机器复用的难题，并在制造装配场景中验证了其提升知识完整性和语义质量的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐性知识在人类专业知识中起核心作用，但在程序性领域（如制造、维修）中，这类包含隐式假设、上下文约束、具身技能和经验判断的知识难以被捕获、形式化并转化为机器可解释的表示，导致现有知识工程流程无法对其进行有效查询、验证、推理和复用。

Method: 提出一种神经符号框架，结合逻辑增强生成（Logic-Augmented Generation, LAG）与受主动推理（Active Inference）启发的方法，构建基于本体支撑的知识图谱。该框架旨在将隐性和以过程为中心的知识转化为形式化、机器可解释的表示。

Result: 在制造领域的知识转移案例研究中，以教学视频中的装配式维修程序作为可复现代理域进行评估。结果表明，所提方法在知识的完整性和语义质量方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了工业领域中神经符号知识工程的发展，为隐性知识的捕获、形式化与复用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Tacit knowledge plays a central role in human expertise, yet it remains difficult to capture, formalize, and reuse in machine-interpretable form. This challenge is especially relevant in procedural domains, where successful execution depends not only on explicit instructions, but also on implicit assumptions, contextual constraints, embodied skills, and experience-based judgments rarely documented. As a result, current knowledge engineering pipelines struggle to transform tacit and process-centric knowledge into formally specified, machine-interpretable representations that can be queried, validated, reasoned over, and reused. In this paper, we introduce a neuro-symbolic framework that combines Logic-Augmented Generation and an Active-Inference-inspired approach for ontology-grounded Knowledge Graph construction. We evaluate the approach in a knowledge transfer case study in manufacturing, using assembly-like repair procedures from instructional videos as a reproducible proxy domain. Results show that the proposed solution improves completeness and semantic quality, advancing neuro-symbolic knowledge engineering for industrial domains.

</details>


### [66] [FactoryBench: Evaluating Industrial Machine Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07675)
*Yanis Merzouki,Coral Izquierdo,Matei Ignuta-Ciuncanu,Marcos Gomez-Bracamonte,Riccardo Maggioni,Alessandro Lombardi,Camilla Mazzoleni,Federico Martelli,Balazs Gunther,Jonas Petersen,Philipp Petersen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FactoryBench，这是一个用于评估时间序列模型和大语言模型在工业机器人遥测数据上机器理解能力的基准测试，包含超过70,000个问答对。对六个前沿大语言模型的零样本评估显示，没有任何模型在结构化层次上超过50%准确率，在决策制定上不超过18%，揭示了当前模型与实际机器理解之间存在巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏专门用于评估机器理解能力的基准测试，特别是在工业机器人遥测数据领域。现有模型在真实工业环境中的因果推理和决策制定能力尚未得到充分验证，需要构建一个系统性的评估框架来量化当前模型与实际操作机器理解之间的差距。

Method: 构建了FactoryBench基准测试，基于Pearl因果阶梯的四个因果层次（状态、干预、反事实、决策）组织问答对，涵盖五种答案格式（四种结构化格式通过确定性评分，自由格式通过LLM-as-judge投票协议评分）。提出了可扩展的问答生成框架，围绕结构化问题模板构建。收集了FactoryWave数据集（来自UR3协作机器人和KUKA KR10工业机械臂的密集、多任务、多变量传感器数据集），并结合AURSAD和voraus-AD数据集构建了包含70,000多个问答项的大规模基准测试。

Result: 对六个前沿大语言模型进行零样本评估，结果显示：没有任何模型在结构化层次上超过50%准确率，在决策制定任务上不超过18%准确率。这一结果揭示了当前最先进模型与实际操作机器理解能力之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在工业机器人遥测数据的机器理解任务上表现不足，特别是在高层因果推理和决策制定方面存在明显短板。FactoryBench为未来研究提供了重要的评估工具和基准，突显了需要开发更专门化的模型来弥合当前能力与实际工业应用需求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We introduce FactoryBench, a benchmark for evaluating time-series models and LLMs on machine understanding over industrial robotic telemetry. Q&A pairs are organized along four causal levels (state, intervention, counterfactual, decision) instantiating Pearl's ladder of causation, and span five answer formats: four structured formats are scored deterministically and free-form answers are scored by an LLM-as-judge voting protocol. We propose a scalable Q&A generation framework built around structured question templates, present FactoryWave (a dense, multitask, multivariate sensor dataset collected from a UR3 cobot and a KUKA KR10 industrial arm), and construct FactoryBench as a large-scale benchmark of over 70k Q&A items grounded in roughly 15k normalized episodes from FactoryWave, AURSAD, and voraus-AD. Zero-shot evaluation of six frontier LLMs shows that no model exceeds 50% on structured levels or 18% on decision-making, revealing a wide gap between current models and operational machine understanding.

</details>


### [67] [GASim: A Graph-Accelerated Hybrid Framework for Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07692)
*Xuan Zhou,Yanhui Sun,Hantao Yao,Allen He,Yongdong Zhang,Wu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GASim图加速混合多智能体框架，通过图优化内存和图消息传递技术，实现了9.94倍加速并节省80%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模社会模拟的混合方法将LLM智能体与数值ABM结合，但存在高昂延迟问题，主要源于昂贵的内存检索和顺序ABM执行。

Method: 提出GASim框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) Graph-Optimized Memory (GOM)用稀疏记忆图上的轻量级传播替代LLM检索管道；(2) Graph Message Passing (GMP)用图注意力网络的细粒度特征聚合替代顺序ABM执行；(3) Entropy-Driven Grouping (EDG)利用信息熵动态识别信息多样化邻域中的涌现核心智能体。

Result: 相比传统混合框架实现了9.94倍端到端加速，token消耗低于基线的20%，同时保持与现实世界舆论趋势的强一致性。

Conclusion: GASim成功解决了大规模社会模拟的效率瓶颈问题，在大幅降低成本和延迟的同时保持了模拟质量，为复杂社会模式研究提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale social simulators are essential for studying complex social patterns. Prior work explores hybrid methods to scale up simulations, combining large language models (LLM)-based agents with numerical agent-based models (ABM). However, this incurs high latency due to expensive memory retrieval and sequential ABM execution. To address this challenge, we propose GASim, a graph-accelerated hybrid multi-agent framework for large-scale social simulations. For core agents driven by LLM, GASim introduces Graph-Optimized Memory (GOM) to replace intensive LLM-based retrieval pipelines with lightweight propagation over a sparse memory graph. For the majority of ordinary agents, GASim employs Graph Message Passing (GMP), substituting sequential ABM execution with parallel updates by fine-grained feature aggregation and Graph Attention Network. We further introduce Entropy-Driven Grouping (EDG) that coordinates this hybrid partitioning, leveraging information entropy to dynamically identify emergent core agents situated in information-diverse neighborhoods. Extensive experiments show that GASim not only delivers a substantial 9.94-fold end-to-end speedup over the traditional hybrid framework but also consumes less than 20% of baseline tokens, significantly reducing costs while preserving strong alignment with real-world public opinion trends. Our code is available at https://github.com/Jasmine0201/GASim.

</details>


### [68] [Finite-Time Analysis of MCTS in Continuous POMDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07703)
*Da Kong,Vadim Indelman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）提供了首个有限时间理论分析，在离散和连续观测空间中均建立了概率集中界限，并提出了具有理论保证的Voro-POMCPOW算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管POMCP等MCTS类求解器在实际应用中表现出色，但由于启发式动作选择（如UCB）引起的非平稳性和相互依赖性，严格的有限时间保证仍然是一个未解决的开放性问题。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 针对离散观测空间，将多项式探索奖励扩展到POMDP场景的UCB中；针对连续观测空间，引入抽象划分框架并提出划分损失的有限时间界限，开发了Voro-POMCPOW算法，利用Voronoi单元自适应划分连续观测空间，保持有限分支因子。

Result: 在温和条件下，证明了离散和连续观测空间POMDP中价值估计的高概率界限；Voro-POMCPOW在保持竞争性能的同时提供了有限时间理论保证。所开发的技术同样适用于连续MDP。

Conclusion: 本文首次为POMDP中的MCTS提供了有限时间分析框架，建立了严格的理论保证，提出的新算法在实证中表现优异，且分析方法可推广至连续MDP领域。

Abstract: This paper presents a finite-time analysis for Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) in Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs), with probabilistic concentration bounds in both discrete and continuous observation spaces. While MCTS-style solvers such as POMCP achieve empirical success in many applications, rigorous finite-time guarantees remain an open problem due to the nonstationarity and the interdependencies induced by heuristic action selection (e.g., UCB). In the discrete setting, we address these challenges by extending the polynomial exploration bonus to UCB in POMDP setting, yielding polynomial concentration bounds for the empirical value estimation at the root node. For continuous observation spaces, we introduce an abstract partitioning framework and propose a finite-time bound on partitioning loss. Under mild conditions, we prove highprobability bound on value estimates in POMDPs with continuous observation space. Specifically, we propose Voro-POMCPOW, a variant of POMCPOW with f inite-time guarantees that adaptively partitions the continuous observation space using Voronoi cells. This approach maintains a finite branching factor while preserving the original observation generator. Empirical validation demonstrates that the proposed Voro-POMCPOW shows competitive performance while providing theoretical guarantees. Although our analysis focuses on continuous POMDPs, the techniques developed herein are also applicable to continuous MDPs, closing another gap on the MDP side.

</details>


### [69] [Hierarchical Task Network Planning with LLM-Generated Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07707)
*Felipe Meneguzzi,Alexandre Buchweitz,Augusto B. Corrêa,Victor Scherer Putrich,André Grahl Pereira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大语言模型（LLM）为分层任务网络（HTN）规划生成搜索启发式函数。实验结果表明，LLM生成的启发式函数在覆盖率上接近现有最佳HTN规划器，并在83%的共同问题上显著降低了搜索工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTN规划虽然能通过方法库引入领域知识加速搜索，但其启发式函数的信息量仍不及经典规划算法。鉴于LLM在经典规划中生成启发式函数的成功应用，本文旨在探索将该方法扩展至分层规划领域，以生成更有效的搜索启发式。

Method: 基于Corrêa等人（2025）在经典规划中的方法，将其扩展至分层规划。研究使用了九个大语言模型，在六个标准全序HTN基准领域上，通过领域特定的提示词生成启发式函数。评估基于Pytrich规划器，并将结果与领域无关的基线方法（TDG和LMCount）以及PANDA规划器进行对比。

Result: LLM生成的启发式函数在覆盖率上几乎匹配了目前可用的最佳HTN规划器。在所有共同解决的问题中，有83%的问题显著降低了搜索工作量（如搜索节点数等指标）。

Conclusion: 大语言模型能够有效地为HTN规划生成高质量的搜索启发式函数，证明了将LLM应用于分层规划启发式生成的可行性与优越性，为未来HTN规划器的性能提升提供了新方向。

Abstract: HTN planning is a variation of classical planning where, instead of searching for a linear sequence of actions, an algorithm decomposes higher-level tasks using a method library until only executable actions remain. On one hand, this allows one to introduce domain knowledge that can speed up the search for a solution through the method library. On the other hand, it creates challenges that go beyond those of classical state-space search. While recent research produced a number of heuristics and novel algorithms that speed up HTN planning, these heuristics are not yet as informative as those available in classical planning algorithms. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate effective search heuristics for HTN planning, extending the methodology of Corrêa, Pereira, and Seipp (2025) from classical to hierarchical planning. Using the Pytrich planner on six standard total-order HTN benchmark domains, we evaluate heuristics generated by nine LLMs under domain-specific prompting and compare them against the TDG and LMCount domain-independent baselines and the PANDA planner. Our results show that LLM-generated heuristics nearly match the coverage of the best available HTN planner, while substantially reducing search effort on 83% of shared problems.

</details>


### [70] [Online Goal Recognition using Path Signature and Dynamic Time Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07736)
*Douglas Tesch,Nathan Gavenski,Leonardo Amado,Odinaldo Rodrigues,Felipe Meneguzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径签名的新型在线目标识别方法，通过利用粗糙路径理论的紧凑表示，在连续域中显著提升了预测准确性和在线规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线目标识别在连续域中面临两个核心挑战：如何高效编码大规模轨迹以及如何有效比较这些轨迹。现有方法通常使用定制的状态空间表示和度量标准，但忽略了其他领域中已成熟的编码技术，而这些技术可能带来显著优势。

Method: 引入路径签名（path signatures）作为轨迹表示方法，这是粗糙路径理论中一种紧凑且富有表达力的表示方式。该方法能够高效捕捉轨迹的关键语义特征，从而实现轨迹之间更有意义的比较。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在预测准确性和在线规划效率方面始终优于现有最先进方法，同时离线性能保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 路径签名为在线目标识别提供了一种有效的轨迹编码方案，通过借鉴其他领域的成熟技术，成功解决了连续域目标识别中的核心挑战，实现了性能的全面提升。

Abstract: Online goal recognition in continuous domains poses two central challenges: efficiently encoding large trajectories and effectively comparing them. Recent work addresses these challenges by using custom state-space representations and metrics to compare observations against hypotheses. However, these approaches often overlook well-established encoding techniques used in other domains that offer substantial advantages. This paper introduces a novel method for online goal recognition that leverages path signatures, a compact, expressive representation of rough path theory that efficiently captures key semantic features of trajectories, enabling more meaningful comparisons between them. Experiments show that our method consistently outperforms the state of the art in predictive accuracy and online planning efficiency, while remaining competitive offline.

</details>


### [71] [Alternating Target-Path Planning for Scalable Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07744)
*Yu Kumagai,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代优化框架，通过解耦目标分配与路径规划来解决TAPF问题，利用MAPF反馈循环重新分配目标，在保证解质量的同时显著提升了计算规模和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TAPF（并发目标分配与路径规划）问题求解方法完全依赖基于冲突搜索（CBS）的算法，这种算法将目标分配与路径规划紧密耦合，导致计算开销大、可扩展性差，难以应用于大规模实际场景。

Method: 提出一种迭代优化框架，将目标分配与路径规划解耦。该框架基于现代快速次优MAPF求解器（如LaCAM），在给定时间预算内重复执行以下过程：对当前目标分配求解MAPF，通过MAPF反馈识别瓶颈智能体，并据此优化目标分配方案。

Result: 实验结果表明，该反馈驱动的重分配循环非常有效。该方法能够很好地扩展到远超当前最先进CBS求解器的计算规模，同时保持良好的解质量。

Conclusion: 本研究通过解耦目标分配与路径规划并引入反馈机制，成功提升了TAPF问题的求解规模与效率，向适用于现实世界大规模场景的实用化TAPF迈出了坚实的一步。

Abstract: The concurrent target assignment and pathfinding (TAPF) problem extends multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF) by asking planners to allocate distinct targets and collision-free paths to agents. Prior work on TAPF has relied exclusively on Conflict-Based Search (CBS), which tightly couples target assignment and pathfinding, resulting in compute-intensive, non-scalable solutions. In contrast, we propose an iterative refinement framework that decouples target assignment from pathfinding. Our framework builds on modern, fast, suboptimal MAPF solvers, such as LaCAM. Specifically, within a given time budget, it repeatedly solves MAPF for the current target assignment, identifies bottleneck agents via MAPF feedback, and refines the assignment. Empirical results show that feedback-driven reassignment loop is effective, enabling our framework to scale well beyond the reach of the state-of-the-art CBS-based solver while maintaining decent solution quality. This represents a solid step toward practical, large scale TAPF suitable for real-world setups.

</details>


### [72] [RuleSafe-VL: Evaluating Rule-Conditioned Decision Reasoning in Vision-Language Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07760)
*Zhifeng Lu,Dianyuan Wang,Yuhu Shang,Zhenbo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RuleSafe-VL基准，用于评估视觉语言内容审核中基于规则的决策推理能力，发现现有VLMs在规则关系恢复和决策状态预测方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态安全基准主要将内容审核简化为最终标签匹配，忽略了底层规则结构，导致高分数无法证明模型是否正确应用策略或仅依赖表面线索。

Method: 基于公开平台审核政策构建RuleSafe-VL基准，形式化93条原子规则和92种类型化规则关系，生成2,166个上下文敏感的图文案例，设计四个诊断任务分解审核决策链：识别激活规则、恢复规则交互、判断决策充分性、补充缺失上下文后解决结果。

Result: 在10个前沿、开源和安全导向VLM上的实验表明：规则关系恢复是主要瓶颈，最佳模型仅达64.8 Macro-F1，部分安全导向模型低于7 Macro-F1；决策状态预测最高仅64.5 Macro-F1。

Conclusion: RuleSafe-VL将内容审核评估从最终标签评分转向对规则条件决策推理的诊断性评估，揭示了现有模型在理解规则交互和决策推理方面的不足。

Abstract: Platform content moderation applies explicit policy rules and context-dependent conditions to decide whether user content is allowed, restricted, or removed. A correct moderation outcome must therefore depend on which rules a case activates, how those rules interact, and whether the available evidence is sufficient. Current multimodal safety benchmarks largely reduce moderation to matching predefined final labels, leaving this underlying rule structure untested. As a result, a high benchmark score reveals little about whether a model applies the policy correctly or arrives at the correct label through superficial cues. To evaluate this rule-governed process, we introduce RuleSafe-VL, a benchmark for rule-conditioned decision reasoning in vision-language content moderation. Derived from publicly available platform moderation policies, RuleSafe-VL formalizes 93 atomic rules and 92 typed rule relations, yielding 2,166 context-sensitive image-text cases across three high-risk policy families. Its four diagnostic tasks decompose moderation into a rule-conditioned decision chain. They identify activated rules, recover rule interactions, judge decision sufficiency, and resolve outcomes once missing context is supplied. Experiments on 10 frontier, open-source, and safety-oriented VLMs reveal rule-relation recovery as the dominant bottleneck, where the best model reaches only 64.8 Macro-F1 and some safety-oriented models fall below 7 Macro-F1. Decision-state prediction also remains unreliable, peaking at 64.5 Macro-F1. RuleSafe-VL shifts moderation evaluation from final-label scoring toward diagnostic assessment of rule-conditioned decision reasoning.

</details>


### [73] [Exact Regular-Constrained Variable-Order Markov Generation via Sparse Context-State Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07839)
*François Pachet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将现有基于信念传播（BP）的正则约束方法扩展到变阶马尔可夫模型的方案，通过构建观测上下文状态与正则约束自动机的乘积空间，实现了在正则约束下精确的变阶序列生成与推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精确方法仅能处理一阶马尔可夫链的正则约束问题。当生成器为变阶/回退模型时，一阶约束层会合并本应被变阶生成器区分的历史，导致分布不匹配，因此需要一种适用于变阶模型的正则约束处理方法。

Method: 通过用“观测上下文状态”替代一阶马尔可夫状态，然后与正则约束自动机构建标准乘积，得到了稀疏的状态空间构造。该方法将精确BP推断与生成时的回退策略（如单例回避）分离，并明确了后者的随机语义。

Result: 对于固定的已训练上下文图和自动机，推断复杂度在序列视界上呈线性；在一般情况下，复杂度在可达乘积边数上呈多项式级。该方法无需展开所有K元组即可获得正确的条件分布，并支持通过逆计数查找实现可逆数据增强。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将正则约束的精确推断扩展到了变阶马尔可夫模型，填补了现有方法在变阶模型上的空白，同时提供了统一的理论框架来处理生成时的回退策略。

Abstract: Variable-order Markov models generate sequences over a finite alphabet by conditioning each symbol on the longest available suffix of the generated history. Regular constraints, by contrast, describe finite-horizon control requirements by an automaton: fixed positions, forced endings, metrical patterns, and forbidden copied fragments are all special cases. Existing exact methods already handle regular constraints with belief propagation for first-order Markov chains. The contribution here is the variable-order extension: identifying the state space on which the existing BP-regular machinery must be run when the generator is a variable-order/backoff model. A first-order constraint layer can enforce useful support conditions, but it computes future mass after merging histories that a variable-order generator deliberately keeps distinct. We formalize this mismatch and give the sparse construction obtained by replacing the first-order Markov state with the observed context state, then taking the standard product with the regular constraint automaton. For a fixed trained context graph and automaton, inference is linear in the sequence horizon; in general it is polynomial in the number of reachable product edges. This gives the correct variable-order distribution conditioned on regular constraints without expanding to all K-tuples. The same finite-source interface supports reversible data augmentation by inverse count lookup, matching materialized transposition augmentation without storing transformed corpora. We also separate exact BP inference from generation-time backoff policies, such as singleton avoidance, whose stochastic semantics must be made explicit if exactness is claimed.

</details>


### [74] [AgentEscapeBench: Evaluating Out-of-Domain Tool-Grounded Reasoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07926)
*Zhengkang Guo,Yiyang Li,Lin Qiu,Xiaohua Wang,Jingwen Xv,Dongyu Ru,Xiaoyu Li,Xiaoqing Zheng,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AgentEscapeBench基准，用于评估LLM智能体在显式长程依赖约束下推理、执行和修正新颖工具使用程序的能力。实验表明，随着依赖深度增加，模型性能急剧下降，主要归因于长程状态跟踪、线索遵循和中间结果传播的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体评估多局限于熟悉的工作流程和短程交互，缺乏对智能体在面对新颖、长程依赖约束下的工具使用能力的系统性测试。因此，作者希望构建一个能够诊断智能体在深层上下文依赖推理方面缺陷的基准。

Method: 提出AgentEscapeBench，一个密室逃脱风格的基准。每个任务定义了工具与物品之间的有向无环依赖图，要求智能体调用真实外部函数、跟踪逐步揭示的隐藏状态、传播中间结果并提交可验证的最终答案。基准包含5个难度等级共270个实例，并支持全自动化评估。

Result: 对16个LLM智能体和人类参与者进行测试。结果显示性能随依赖深度增加而急剧下降：人类成功率从难度-5的98.3%降至难度-25的80.0%，最佳模型则从90.0%降至60.0%。轨迹分析表明，模型失败主要源于长程状态跟踪、线索遵循和中间结果传播的崩溃。

Conclusion: 当前智能体能够处理局部工具使用，但在处理深层上下文依赖方面仍存在显著困难。AgentEscapeBench可作为诊断当前智能体能力的测试平台，并为未来训练更鲁棒的通用推理、行动和适应能力提供参考。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents increasingly rely on external tools, it is important to evaluate their ability to sustain tool-grounded reasoning beyond familiar workflows and short-range interactions. We introduce AgentEscapeBench, an escape-room-style benchmark that tests whether agents can infer, execute, and revise novel tool-use procedures under explicit long-range dependency constraints. Each task defines a directed acyclic dependency graph over tools and items, requiring agents to invoke real external functions, track hidden state revealed incrementally, propagate intermediate results, and submit a deterministically verifiable final answer. AgentEscapeBench includes 270 instances across five difficulty tiers and supports fully automated evaluation. Experiments with sixteen LLM agents and human participants show that performance drops sharply as dependency depth increases: humans decline from 98.3% success at difficulty-5 to 80.0% at difficulty-25, while the best model drops from 90.0% to 60.0%. Trajectory analysis attributes model failures mainly to breakdowns in long-range state tracking, clue adherence, and intermediate-result propagation. These findings suggest that current agents can often handle local tool use but still struggle with deep contextual dependencies. We hope AgentEscapeBench can serve as a diagnostic testbed for measuring current agent capabilities and informing future training efforts toward more robust general-purpose reasoning, action, and adaptation.

</details>


### [75] [The Limits of AI-Driven Allocation: Optimal Screening under Aleatoric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07979)
*Santiago Cortes-Gomez,Mateo Dulce Rubio,Carlos Patino,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在两阶段资源分配框架中最优结合筛查与算法靶向。最优策略是在算法分配的边际进行筛查，同时直接靶向最高风险单位。筛查的效率收益随群体中不可约不确定性的增加而增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习推动了基于预测风险评分的算法靶向资源分配，这比传统筛查更便宜快捷。然而，即使获得真实的条件脆弱性概率也无法消除错配：个体脆弱性状态的认知不确定性是不可约的，概率靶向不可避免地会错配部分资源。因此需要研究如何最优地结合筛查与算法靶向。

Method: 构建两阶段分配框架：筛查阶段对部分单位观测真实结果，最终分配阶段在固定覆盖预算下分配资源。理论推导最优策略，并在实际应用中进行实证分析，包括基于收入的社会保护项目和哥伦比亚人道主义排雷。

Result: 最优策略是在算法分配的边际筛查单位，同时直接靶向最高风险单位。实证表明筛查与算法靶向的关系：当群体中认知不确定性增加时，筛查带来的效率收益增长，两者呈互补关系；反之则呈替代关系。

Conclusion: 筛查成本与分配效率之间存在张力，这在哥伦比亚的社会保护和排雷应用中具有实际操作意义。算法靶向与筛查应根据群体中的不确定性水平进行最优组合，以最大化资源分配效率。

Abstract: The rise of machine learning has shifted targeted resource allocation in policy and humanitarian settings toward algorithmic targeting based on predicted risk scores. This approach is typically cheaper and faster than traditional screening procedures that directly observe the latent vulnerability status through physical verification. Yet, even access to the true conditional vulnerability probability cannot eliminate misallocation: aleatoric uncertainty over individual vulnerability status is irreducible, and probabilistic targeting inevitably misallocates some resources. In this work we study how screening and algorithmic targeting should be optimally combined in a two-stage allocation framework where a screening stage observes true outcomes for a subset of units before a final allocation stage assigns the resource under a fixed coverage budget. We show that the optimal strategy screens units at the margin of algorithmic allocation, while directly targeting the highest-risk units. Furthermore, we empirically characterize when screening and algorithmic targeting act as complements or substitutes: efficiency gains from screening grow as the aleatoric uncertainty in the population increases. We illustrate our framework with applications in income-based social protection programs and humanitarian demining in Colombia, where the tension between screening costs and allocation efficiency is operationally consequential.

</details>


### [76] [Abductive Reasoning with Probabilistic Commonsense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08011)
*Joseph Cotnareanu,Chiara Roverato,Han Zhou,Didier Chetelat,Yingxue Zhang,Mark Coates*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PACS算法，通过概率框架显式建模常识信念的个体差异，结合LLM与形式求解器进行采样与聚合，显著提升了常识推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号方法利用LLM补充缺失的常识假设，但隐含假设了常识事实具有普遍一致性。实际上，个体间的常识信念存在差异，现有方法无法处理这种变异性。

Method: 提出一个概率性溯因常识推理框架，并引入PACS算法。该算法利用LLM和形式求解器生成证明样本，以此模拟不同个体的常识信念，并通过聚合这些样本的结论来进行推理判断。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，PACS的表现优于思维链推理、先前的神经符号方法以及基于搜索的方法。

Conclusion: 显式地对常识信念的个体差异进行概率建模，能够有效提升大模型在常识推理任务中的表现。

Abstract: Recent efforts to improve the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) have focused on integrating formal logic solvers within neurosymbolic frameworks. A key challenge is that formal solvers lack commonsense world knowledge, preventing them from making reasoning steps that humans find obvious. Prior methods address this by using LLMs to supply missing commonsense assumptions, but these approaches implicitly assume universal agreement on such commonsense facts. In reality, commonsense beliefs vary across individuals. We propose a probabilistic framework for abductive commonsense reasoning that explicitly models this variation, aiming to determine whether most people would judge a statement as true or false. We introduce Probabilistic Abductive CommonSense (PACS), a novel algorithm that uses an LLM and a formal solver to sample proofs as observations of individuals' distinct commonsense beliefs, and aggregates conclusions across these samples. Empirically, PACS outperforms chain-of-thought reasoning, prior neurosymbolic methods, and search-based approaches across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [77] [Learning CLI Agents with Structured Action Credit under Selective Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08013)
*Haoyang Su,Ying Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对命令行界面(CLI)智能体学习中的两大瓶颈——部分观测下的选择性观察和稀疏奖励下的信用分配问题，提出了σ-Reveal推理时上下文选择机制和A³原生智能体强化学习方法，并构建了ShellOps数据集进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CLI智能体强化学习方法未能充分利用CLI动作的原生结构属性作为学习信号，同时面临两个核心瓶颈：(1)智能体需要从部分观测中识别大型代码库中的任务相关证据；(2)稀疏的终端奖励需要分配给塑造长多轮轨迹的动作。

Method: 提出了两个关键技术：1) σ-Reveal：一种推理时机制，为CLI选择令牌预算约束的上下文，实现选择性观察；2) Action Advantage Assignment (A³)：一种原生智能体RL方法，通过构建回合级优势值来保持标准智能体RL的算法复杂度，该优势值来源于回合级相对反馈、基于抽象语法树(AST)的动作子链残差以及树级轨迹边界。此外构建了ShellOps数据集套件用于评估。

Result: 在ShellOps数据集上验证了所提方法在shell驱动的信息提取和文件编辑任务中的有效性。A³方法成功将回合级反馈转化为轮次级优势值，实现了更精细的信用分配。

Conclusion: 通过利用CLI动作的原生结构属性和改进信用分配机制，可以有效缓解CLI智能体学习中的观察选择和奖励稀疏问题，为智能体-计算机交互提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Command line interface (CLI) agents are emerging as a practical paradigm for agent-computer interaction over evolving filesystems, executable command line programs, and online execution feedback. Recent work has used reinforcement learning (RL) to learn these interaction abilities from verifiable task feedback, yet few methods exploit the native structured attributes of CLI actions as learning signals. Beyond this underused action structure, CLI learning also couples two bottlenecks for coding agents. First, the agent must identify task-relevant evidence in a large codebase from partial observations. Second, sparse terminal rewards must be assigned to the actions that shape a long multi-turn trajectory. We study these bottlenecks through shell-driven information extraction and file editing tasks. For selective observation, we introduce $σ$-Reveal, an inference-time mechanism that selects token-budgeted context for the same CLI. For credit assignment, we propose Action Advantage Assignment ($\mathrm{A}^3$), a native agentic RL method that preserves the algorithmic complexity of standard agentic RL. $\mathrm{A}^3$ constructs turn-level advantages from episode-level relative feedback, abstract syntax tree (AST) based action sub-chain residuals, and tree-level trajectory margins. To further evaluate this problem setting, we construct ShellOps, a verifiable dataset suite covering CLI tasks in repository environments.

</details>


### [78] [Reason to Play: Behavioral and Brain Alignment Between Frontier LRMs and Human Game Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08019)
*Botos Csaba,Sreejan Kumar,Austin Tudor David Andrews,Laurence Hunt,Chris Summerfield,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Rui Ponte Costa,Marcelo G. Mattar,Momchil Tomov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过人类游玩新视频游戏的fMRI数据，发现前沿大型推理模型（LRMs）在行为匹配和脑活动预测上显著优于深度强化学习和贝叶斯模型，表明LRMs是复杂环境中人类学习与决策的有力计算模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类在面对新环境时能快速学习抽象知识并灵活规划行动，研究旨在探讨现代AI系统是否能以类似方式学习和规划，从而寻找能够解释人类在复杂自然环境中学习与决策的计算模型。

Method: 使用包含fMRI记录的复杂人类游戏数据集，参与者需要发现规则、修正假设和多步规划。通过联合评估模型的游戏表现、对人类学习行为的匹配程度以及对脑活动的预测能力，比较前沿大型推理模型（LRMs）、无模型和基于模型的深度强化学习智能体以及贝叶斯理论智能体。

Result: 前沿LRMs在游戏发现过程中最接近人类行为模式，对皮层和皮下区域脑活动的预测能力比强化学习替代方案高出一个数量级，且经过排列检验证实结果稳健。通过针对性操纵发现，脑对齐反映的是模型对游戏状态的上下文表征，而非其下游规划或推理过程。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了LRMs作为人类在复杂自然环境中学习和决策的可靠计算模型，为理解人类认知过程提供了新的计算视角，并展示了LRMs在认知建模领域的潜力。

Abstract: Humans rapidly learn abstract knowledge when encountering novel environments and flexibly deploy this knowledge to guide efficient and intelligent action. Can modern AI systems learn and plan in a similar way? We study this question using a dataset of complex human gameplay with concurrent fMRI recordings, in which participants learn novel video games that require rule discovery, hypothesis revision, and multi-step planning. We jointly evaluate models by their ability to play the games, match human learning behavior, and predict brain activity during the same task, comparing a suite of frontier Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) against model-free and model-based deep reinforcement learning agents and a Bayesian theory-based agent. We find that frontier LRMs most closely match human behavioral patterns during game discovery and predict brain activity an order of magnitude better than both reinforcement learning alternatives across cortical and subcortical regions, with effects robust to permutation controls. Through targeted manipulations, we further show that brain alignment reflects the model's in-context representation of the game state rather than its downstream planning or reasoning. Our results establish LRMs as compelling computational accounts of human learning and decision making in complex, naturalistic environments. Project page with interactive replays: https://botcs.github.io/reason-to-play/

</details>


### [79] [MPD$^2$-Router: Mask-aware Multi-expert Prior-regularized Dual-head Deferral Router in Glaucoma Screening and Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08024)
*Wenxin Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MPD²-Router，一种面向青光眼筛查的掩码感知多专家延迟框架，通过受限的人机路由策略，有效降低了临床成本并提升了诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习延迟（L2D）方法在青光眼筛查中忽视了专家可用性、读片者行为异质性、工作负载失衡、非对称诊断风险、形态学难度以及部署偏移等关键实际因素，限制了其临床应用价值。

Method: 提出MPD²-Router框架，将眼科分诊重构为受限的人机路由问题。该方法结合双头延迟/分配策略与掩码感知的Gumbel-sigmoid门控机制，严格约束每样本的专家可用性，并融合不确定性、形态学、图像质量及分布外（OOD）信号。训练过程采用非对称成本敏感目标函数，结合增广拉格朗日延迟预算、组特定分布先验和秩主要化JS正则化器，在不强制均匀分配的情况下有效防止专家坍塌。

Result: 在三个跨国青光眼队列（REFUGE、CHAKSU、ORIGA）上，基于REFUGE训练的固定骨干网络，MPD²-Router在中等延迟率下显著降低了临床成本，并在MCC指标上优于纯AI方案。该方法在F1-MCC-成本维度上达到帕累托最优，在跨域偏移下表现稳健，并实现了均衡的专家利用率。

Conclusion: MPD²-Router通过综合考虑专家可用性、异质性和成本敏感性等因素，成功构建了更安全、高效的青光眼筛查人机协作系统，为临床部署提供了实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning-to-defer (L2D) can make glaucoma screening safer by routing difficult/uncertain cases to humans, yet standard formulations overlook expert availability, heterogeneous readers behavior, workload imbalance, asymmetric diagnostic harm, case difficulty from morphology and deployment shift. We introduce MPD$^2$-Router, a mask-aware multi-expert deferral framework that recasts ophthalmic triage as constrained human--AI routing: whether to defer and to which available expert. It couples a dual-head deferral/allocation policy with mask-aware Gumbel--sigmoid gating that strictly enforces per-sample availability, and fuses uncertainty, morphology, image-quality, and OOD signals. Training uses an asymmetric cost-sensitive objective with an augmented-Lagrangian deferral budget, a group-specific distribution prior, and a rank-majorization JS regularizer that jointly prevent expert collapse without forcing uniform allocation. Across three cross-national glaucoma cohorts (REFUGE, CHAKSU, ORIGA) with a frozen REFUGE-trained backbone, MPD$^2$-Router substantially lowers clinical cost and improves MCC over AI-only at a moderate deferral rate. It is Pareto-optimal in F1--MCC--cost, robust under cross-domain shift, and yields balanced expert utilization.

</details>


### [80] [Rubric-Grounded RL: Structured Judge Rewards for Generalizable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08061)
*Manish Bhattarai,Ismael Boureima,Nishath Rajiv Ranasinghe,Scott Pakin,Dan O'Malley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于评分准则的强化学习框架，利用冻结的LLM评判器对多个可验证标准进行加权评分，提供细粒度的部分信用优化信号。在OSTI语料库上训练后，模型在保留集评分准则评估中达到71.7%的归一化奖励，并在四个未参与训练的推理基准（GSM8K、MATH、GPQA Main、GPQA Diamond）上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习依赖二元结果或单一整体评分，无法提供细粒度的优化信号。作者旨在通过将奖励分解为多个可验证的加权标准，利用LLM评判器对每个标准进行评分，从而为策略优化提供更丰富的部分信用信号。

Method: 提出基于评分准则的强化学习框架：从约10万篇科学技术文档（OSTI语料库）中提取评分准则，使用冻结的LLM评判器依据辅助文档生成多标准结构化奖励。采用群组相对策略优化（GRPO）算法对Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型进行训练，策略优化时无法访问辅助文档。

Result: GRPO训练的模型在保留集评分准则评估中达到71.7%归一化奖励。在四个未参与训练的推理基准测试（GSM8K、MATH、GPQA Main、GPQA Diamond）上均优于基础模型，证明结构化文档grounded奖励能够泛化并诱导可迁移的推理能力。

Conclusion: 结构化、文档grounded的多标准奖励机制不仅能提升目标任务的评分准则表现，还能促进模型习得可迁移的推理行为，验证了该方法在提升模型泛化能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: We argue that decomposing reward into weighted, verifiable criteria and using an LLM judge to score them provides a partial-credit optimization signal: instead of a binary outcome or a single holistic score, each response is graded along multiple task-specific criteria. We formalize \emph{rubric-grounded reinforcement learning (RL)}: a framework in which the policy is optimized against a structured, multi-criterion reward produced by a frozen LLM judge that conditions on auxiliary grounding the policy never sees. We instantiate the framework by deriving rubrics from an Office of Scientific and Technical Information (OSTI)-derived corpus of roughly 100,000 scientific and technical documents and training Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). With GRPO-based training, the model achieves $71.7\%$ normalized reward on held-out rubric evaluation. The GRPO-tuned policy also improves over the base model on four reasoning benchmarks not derived from the training corpus -- GSM8K, MATH, GPQA Main, and GPQA Diamond. These results provide evidence that structured, document-grounded rewards can improve held-out rubric performance and induce transferable reasoning behaviors beyond the corpus used to construct the training environment.

</details>


### [81] [VecCISC: Improving Confidence-Informed Self-Consistency with Reasoning Trace Clustering and Candidate Answer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08070)
*James Petullo,Sonny George,Dylan Cashman,Nianwen Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VecCISC框架，通过语义相似度过滤冗余推理链，将加权多数投票的token消耗降低47%，同时保持或超越基线准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权多数投票（如CISC）虽然比标准自一致性更准确，但需要对每个候选答案调用评论家LLM计算置信度，导致高昂的计算开销和成本，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出VecCISC轻量级自适应框架，利用语义相似度度量来过滤推理链，剔除语义等价、退化或幻觉的候选答案，从而减少需要被评论家评估的候选答案数量。

Result: 在数学、化学、生物、常识推理和人文学科五个数据集上的实验表明，VecCISC在保持或超越CISC准确率的同时，将总token使用量减少了47%。

Conclusion: VecCISC成功平衡了推理性能与计算成本，通过智能过滤机制显著提升了加权多数投票方法的实用性和效率。

Abstract: A standard technique for scaling inference-time reasoning is Self-Consistency, whereby multiple candidate answers are sampled from an LLM and the most common answer is selected. More recently, it has been shown that weighted majority voting (e.g. Confidence-Informed Self Consistency (CISC)), which assigns a confidence value to each candidate answer and chooses the answer with the largest accumulated score, tends to be more accurate on a wide range of popular benchmarks. In practice, weighted majority voting necessitates calling a critic LLM on each candidate's reasoning trace to produce the answer's confidence score. This secondary series of LLM calls greatly increases the overhead and cost of weighted majority voting, despite its potential performance benefits. To reduce this expense, we propose VecCISC, a lightweight, adaptive framework that uses a measure of semantic similarity to filter reasoning traces that are semantically equivalent to others, degenerate, or hallucinated, thus decreasing the number of candidate answers that must be evaluated by the critic. To ensure adequate experimental thoroughness, we evaluate VecCISC on five challenging, widely-adopted datasets spanning the domains of mathematics, chemistry, biology, commonsense reasoning, and the humanities. Our results demonstrate that VecCISC reduces the total token usage by 47%, while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of CISC.

</details>


### [82] [XDecomposer: Learning Prior-Free Set Decomposition for Multiphase X-ray Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05866)
*Hanyu Gao,Bin Cao,Yunyue Su,Tong-Yi Zhang,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了XDecomposer，一个无先验框架，可直接从多相粉末X射线衍射（PXRD）图谱中联合实现分解与物相鉴定，解决了传统方法需依赖候选相列表或结构模板的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的合成过程常产生复杂的混合物，现有PXRD分析方法在多相环境下难以可靠解离各组分相。近期基于表征的晶体检索与生成方法虽有进展，但大多假设单相输入，在多相设置下失效，因此亟需一种无需先验知识的多相XRD分析方法。

Method: 该研究将多相衍射分析建模为一个集合预测问题，提出了XDecomposer框架。该模型在统一架构中推断无序的相分辨组分集合、混合比例及相应的结构表征，无需候选相列表、结构模板或相数量的先验知识。核心机制包括相位查询驱动的分解机制和衍射一致的物理重建，以在保持晶体学保真度的同时实现精确的源分离。

Result: 在模拟和实验数据集上的广泛实验表明，XDecomposer显著提高了多种化学体系的重建精度和物相鉴定能力，并对未见过的混合物保持了强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究为数据驱动、源解析的多相XRD分析提供了一条实用路径，减少了对长期以来依赖先验引导的迭代相匹配的依赖，推动了多相粉末衍射分析的发展。

Abstract: Multiphase powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) analysis remains a fundamental bottleneck in structure identification, as real-world synthesis often produces complex mixtures whose constituent phases (components) cannot be reliably disentangled. While recent advances in representation-based crystal retrieval and generation suggest the possibility of inferring structures directly from PXRD, existing approaches largely assume single-phase inputs and break down in multiphase settings. Here, we present XDecomposer, a prior-free framework for joint decomposition and identification of multiphase XRD patterns without requiring candidate phase lists, structural templates, or prior knowledge of phase number. We formulate multiphase diffraction analysis as a set prediction problem, where the model infers an unordered set of phase-resolved components, their mixture proportions, and corresponding structural representations within a unified architecture. A phase-query-driven decomposition mechanism, together with diffraction-consistent physical reconstruction, enables accurate source separation while preserving crystallographic fidelity. Extensive experiments on both simulated and experimental datasets show that XDecomposer substantially improves reconstruction accuracy and phase identification across diverse chemical systems, while maintaining strong generalization to unseen mixtures. These results provide a practical route toward data-driven, source-resolved multiphase XRD analysis and reduce long-standing dependence on prior-guided iteratively phase matching. The code is openly available at https://github.com/Licht0812/XDecomposer

</details>

### [83] [Generalising Travel Time Prediction To Varying Route Choices In Urban Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06918)
*Łukasz Gorczyca,Kacper Drozd,Michał Bujak,Rafał Kucharski*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了GenTTP框架，解决了现有基于图神经网络的系统级行程时间预测方法无法处理变化路径选择的问题，能够区分不同路径选择并提供准确的流量和行程时间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图神经网络的系统级行程时间预测方法局限于典型和重复的需求模式，虽然能预测日常通勤后的拥堵，但本质上只近似单一需求实现，无法捕捉变化的路径选择。同一需求因出行者在可用路径上的分布不同会产生显著不同的全网结果，这是当前预测模型的关键缺口。

Method: 提出广义行程时间预测器(GenTTP)框架，学习揭示复杂时空交通模式以及路径选择与结果行程时间之间的微观关系，从而在变化路径分配下实现泛化。

Result: GenTTP能够成功区分路径选择，提供准确的流量和行程时间预测，在变化路径分配场景下实现了泛化能力。

Conclusion: GenTTP填补了在变化路径分配下缺乏可泛化的行程时间预测模型这一关键空白，为处理同一需求产生不同网络结果的实际场景提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Previous methods that predict system-wide travel time, predominantly grounded in graph neural networks, remain limited to typical and recurring demand patterns. While they successfully predict future congestion following daily commute, they inherently approximate a single demand realisation and fail to capture varying route choices. In this work, we propose a Generalised Travel Time Predictor (GenTTP) that successfully differentiates route choices and offers accurate flow and travel time predictions. Our framework learns to uncover complex spatiotemporal traffic patterns and microscopic relationships between route choices and the resulting travel times. This addresses a critical gap: the lack of travel time prediction models that generalise across varying route assignments, where the same demand can produce substantially different network-wide outcomes depending on how travellers are distributed over available paths.

</details>


### [84] [The Cost of Consensus: Malignant Epistemic Herding and Adaptive Gating in Distributed Multi-Agent Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06988)
*David Farr,Iain Cruickshank,Kate Starbird,Jevin West*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出“认知对齐”概念，揭示在带宽受限的多智能体协作中，不当通信设计可能导致智能体自信地收敛到错误假设，且无法通过现有协调指标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实分布式智能体需在不确定性和部分观测下协作，但通信会消耗带宽、引入延迟，不当通信甚至会降低集体推理质量。现有研究缺乏对通信频率与内容如何共同影响集体信念状态的系统性分析。

Method: 形式化定义“认知对齐”（epistemic alignment）这一概念，用于表征智能体内部环境信念的一致性程度，区分有效信念共享导致的正确收敛与通信设计不当导致的错误收敛。

Result: 证明认知对齐的缺失无法仅通过Jensen-Shannon散度或达成共识速率等现有协调指标检测，揭示了当前评估方法的盲区。

Conclusion: 在通信受限的部署场景中，优化通信策略不仅要关注协调指标，更需关注信念收敛的质量，避免智能体自信地形成错误共识。

Abstract: Distributed agents in real-world settings frequently must coordinate under uncertainty with only partial observations. Coordination is necessary to share beliefs to aid in task completion, but communication costs bandwidth, introduces latency, and if done poorly, can degrade collective reasoning. This tension is especially acute in bandwidth-constrained deployments such as distributed sensing networks, autonomous reconnaissance, and collaborative cyber defense, where excessive transmission carries direct operational costs. Existing work has focused on multi-agent exploration and communication strategies, but not on how communication frequency and content jointly shape the collective belief state. Central to this challenge is the degree to which agents maintain compatible internal beliefs about the environment, a property we term \textit{epistemic alignment}. When agents share beliefs effectively, they converge on correct hypotheses; when communication is poorly designed, agents may converge confidently on wrong ones. We formalize this distinction and show it is not detectable from coordination metrics alone such as Jensen-Shannon Divergence or rate to consensus.

</details>


### [85] [Social Theory Should Be a Structural Prior for Agentic AI: A Formal Framework for Multi-Agent Social Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07069)
*Lynnette Hui Xian Ng,Iain J. Cruickshank,Adrian Xuan Wei Lim,Kathleen M. Carley*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体社会系统(MASS)框架，主张用社会学理论作为结构先验来建模智能体AI系统，以理解和预测多智能体交互产生的系统级行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统越来越多地部署在包含其他智能体和人类的社会环境中（如社交媒体平台、多智能体LLM管道、自主机器人集群）。在这些环境中，系统行为不仅源于单个智能体，更源于随时间推移的多智能体交互。社会科学家长期以来一直研究人类社会中群体的涌现动力学，这为智能体AI系统的研究提供了重要启示。

Method: 将MASS表示为一类动力学系统，包括信息生成、局部影响和交互结构，并由四个基于社会理论的结构先验来形式化：战略性异质性、网络约束依赖性、协同演化以及分布不稳定性。通过形式化命题论证每个结构先验的重要性，并提出建模、评估和治理MASS的研究议程。

Result: 成功形式化了多智能体社会系统(MASS)框架，明确了四个关键结构先验，并通过形式化命题论证了每个先验的重要性。该框架为理解智能体AI系统中多智能体交互如何产生系统级结果提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 智能体AI系统必须以社会理论作为结构先验进行建模，MASS框架为理解和研究多智能体交互产生的涌现行为提供了系统性的方法论基础，并为未来的建模、评估和治理研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly deployed not in isolation, but inside social environments populated by other agents and humans, such as in social media platforms, multi-agent LLM pipelines or autonomous robotics fleets. In these settings, system behavior emerges not from individual agents alone, but from the multi-agent interactions over time. Emergent dynamics of individuals in a social group have been long studied by social scientists in human contexts. \textbf{This position paper argues that agentic AI systems must be modeled with social theory as a structural prior, and formalizes a Multi-Agent Social Systems (MASS) framework for how agents interact and influence to generate system-level outcomes.} We represent MASS as a class of dynamical system of information generation, local influence and interaction structure, formulated by four structural priors anchored in social theory: strategic heterogeneity, networked-constrained dependence, co-evolution and distributional instability. We demonstrate the importance of each structural prior through formal propositions, and articulate a research agenda for how MASS should be modeled, evaluated and governed.

</details>


### [86] [Decentralized Diffusion Policy Learning for Enhanced Exploration in Cooperative Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07101)
*Yuyang Zhang,Haldun Balim,Na Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出DDPL方法，利用扩散模型参数化多智能体强化学习中的策略，解决了高斯策略在探索能力上的局限性，并在多个基准测试上取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化软策略梯度（DecSPG）算法中，基于能量的策略通常投影到高斯策略类中进行维护，但高斯策略的表达能力有限，严重限制了探索效果，且该问题随智能体数量增加而恶化。

Method: 提出去中心化扩散策略学习（DDPL），使用去噪扩散概率模型参数化每个智能体的策略，以捕获多模态动作分布来增强探索；同时设计重要性采样分数匹配（ISSM）方法，实现扩散策略的高效在线训练，并提供理论保证。

Result: 在多个连续动作多智能体强化学习基准（多智能体粒子环境、多智能体MuJoCo、IsaacLab和JAX重实现的星际争霸多智能体挑战）上，DDPL均展现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过扩散模型的强大表达能力，DDPL有效解决了高斯策略在DecSPG框架下探索不足的问题，为多智能体强化学习提供了一种更具表达力和理论保证的策略学习方法。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) involves complex agent interactions and requires effective exploration strategies. A prominent class of MARL algorithms, decentralized softmax policy gradient (DecSPG), addresses this through energy-based policy updates. In practice, however, such energy-based policies are intractable to maintain and are commonly projected onto the Gaussian policy class. In this work, we show that the limited expressiveness of Gaussian policies severely hinders exploration in DecSPG, and this limitation worsens as the number of agents grows. To address this issue, we propose decentralized diffusion policy learning (DDPL), which parameterizes each agent's policy with a denoising diffusion probabilistic model, an expressive generative model that captures multi-modal action distributions for enhanced exploration. DDPL enables efficient online training of diffusion policies via importance sampling score matching (ISSM), a novel training method with theoretical guarantee. We evaluate DDPL on representative continuous-action MARL benchmarks, including multi-agent particle environment, multi-agent MuJoCo, IsaacLab, and JAX-reimplemented StarCraft multi-agent challenge, and observe consistently improved performance.

</details>


### [87] [Rethinking Priority Scheduling for Sequential Multi-Agent Decision Making in Stackelberg Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07240)
*Xiangyu Liu,Liang Zhang,Bo Jin,Ziqi Wei*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出HPA方法，通过动态调整多智能体系统中的决策顺序来优化N层Stackelberg博弈的均衡点，在MuJoCo等控制任务上优于基准算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有N层Stackelberg博弈研究默认使用环境提供的智能体决策顺序，但决策顺序的改变会导致过定系统，从而使均衡点发生偏移。因此需要研究并优化智能体的决策顺序对博弈均衡的影响。

Method: 提出分层优先级调整（HPA）方法：上层策略根据当前博弈状态动态选择最优决策顺序；下层智能体在时空序贯马尔可夫博弈（STMG）中按选定顺序执行策略；采用快慢更新机制和共享内在奖励来协调不同时间尺度的学习。

Result: 在多智能体MuJoCo等高精度控制任务上的实验表明，HPA方法性能优于基准算法，并能鲁棒地适应环境变化。

Conclusion: 在N层Stackelberg博弈中优化智能体决策顺序对提升系统性能具有重要作用，所提方法有效解决了决策顺序影响均衡点的问题。

Abstract: Current research applying N-level Stackelberg Game to multi-agent systems often uses the default decision order of agents provided by the environment. However, this raises the question: does the order of agents necessarily affect the final equilibrium point of the game? To address this, we formally analyze the N-level Stackelberg Game, where changing the order in which agents make decisions typically leads to an overdetermined system. As a result, the equilibrium point shifts unless special structural conditions are satisfied. Based on this analysis, we propose the Hierarchical Priority Adjustment (HPA) method, which adjusts and selects the agents' decision order. At the upper level, an upper policy dynamically selects the optimal decision order of agents based on the current game state. At the lower level, agents execute strategies in the Spatio-Temporal Sequential Markov Game (STMG) according to the selected order. To coordinate learning across time scales, we employ a slow-fast update scheme with shared intrinsic rewards derived from the advantage function of the upper policy. Experimental results on high-precision control tasks, including multi-agent MuJoCo, show that HPA outperforms benchmark algorithms and robustly adapts to changing environments. These results highlight the crucial role of optimizing the agents' decision order in N-level Stackelberg Game.

</details>


### [88] [OrchJail: Jailbreaking Tool-Calling Text-to-Image Agents by Orchestration-Guided Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07414)
*Jianming Chen,Yawen Wang,Junjie Wang,Zhe Liu,Qing Wang,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了OrchJail框架，针对工具调用的文本到图像（T2I）智能体，通过利用高风险工具编排模式引导模糊测试，成功揭示了多步工具链组合带来的新安全攻击面，显著提升了越狱攻击的效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具调用文本到图像（T2I）智能体能够规划和执行多步工具链，但这也引入了新的安全漏洞：单一的良性步骤可能通过工具编排组合产生有害输出。传统的基于提示词的越狱技术仅关注表面文本扰动，无法有效应对这种由多步编排引发的复杂安全风险。

Method: 提出OrchJail框架，这是一种编排引导的模糊测试方法。其核心是学习成功的越狱工具调用轨迹及其与提示词措辞的因果关系，从而识别高风险工具编排模式。该框架直接引导模糊测试搜索过程，使其聚焦于更可能触发不安全多步工具行为的提示词，而非依赖表面的文本扰动。

Result: 实验表明，OrchJail在代表性工具调用T2I智能体上显著提升了越狱攻击的成功率和效率，同时保持了较高的图像保真度，并降低了查询成本。此外，该方法在面对常见防御措施时表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了工具编排是T2I智能体中一个关键且此前未被探索的攻击面。OrchJail框架为发现此类智能体的安全风险提供了新思路，强调了在多模态智能体安全研究中关注工具链组合风险的重要性。

Abstract: Tool-calling text-to-image (T2I) agents can plan and execute multi-step tool chains to accomplish complex generation and editing queries. However, this capability introduces a new safety attack surface: harmful outputs may arise from tool orchestration, where individually benign steps combine into unsafe results, making prompt-only jailbreak techniques insufficient. We present OrchJail, an orchestration-guided fuzzing framework for jailbreaking tool-calling T2I agents. Its core idea is to exploit high-risk tool-orchestration patterns: by learning from successful jailbreak tool-calling traces and their causal relationships to prompt wording, OrchJail directly guides the fuzzing search toward prompts that are more likely to trigger unsafe multi-step tool behaviors, rather than relying on surface-level textual perturbations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OrchJail improves jailbreak effectiveness and efficiency across representative toolcalling T2I agents, achieving higher attack success rates, better image fidelity, and lower query costs, while remaining robust against common jailbreak defenses. Our work highlights tool orchestration as a critical, previously unexplored attack surface and provides a novel framework for uncovering safety risks in T2I agents.

</details>


### [89] [Synchronizing Minds through Collective Predictive Coding: A Computational Model of Parent-Infant Homeostatic Co-Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07524)
*Yushi Tsubamoto,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出整合主动内感受推断与群体预测编码假设的计算模型，成功解释了亲子互动中大脑间同步现象如何从局部感知和不对称知识中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实互动（如亲子交流）中观测到的大脑间同步现象表明个体间存在潜在表征对齐，但目前缺乏计算模型来解释仅有局部感知和不对称内部知识的个体如何实现这种对齐。

Method: 构建亲子稳态共调节模型，结合POMDP框架下的主动内感受推断和源自群体预测编码假设的Metropolis-Hastings命名博弈。父母通过外感受信号观察婴儿，婴儿直接感知自身内感受状态；双方具有不对称的生成模型知识，通过局部可计算的MH概率接受共享通信变量以达成调节行动共识。

Result: 在6×6内脏状态网格世界中，MHNG介导的互动比单向控制条件更适应性地调节婴儿内脏状态，两个后验分布快速对齐。潜在状态对齐的出现远早于学习生成矩阵的收敛，表明表征同步不预设完全共享的世界模型。

Conclusion: 该模型为大脑间同步研究中报告的潜在状态对齐提供了最小构造性解释，支持群体预测编码作为大脑间对齐的计算基础。

Abstract: Inter-brain synchrony (IBS) observed in real-time dyadic interactions, including parent--infant exchanges, suggests that two agents come to share aligned latent representations through interaction. Yet computational accounts of how such alignment can arise between agents that have only local sensory access and asymmetric internal knowledge remain underdeveloped. We propose a constructive model of parent--infant homeostatic co-regulation that integrates a POMDP formulation of active interoceptive inference with the Metropolis--Hastings Naming Game (MHNG) derived from the Collective Predictive Coding (CPC) hypothesis. In our model, the parent observes the infant only through an exteroceptive signal while the infant directly senses its own interoceptive state; the two agents agree on regulatory actions through a shared communicative variable whose acceptance is determined by a locally computable Metropolis--Hastings probability. The agents are further endowed with asymmetric generative-model knowledge: the parent knows how actions transform visceral states but must learn what the infant's body is communicating, whereas the infant perceives its visceral state directly but must learn how actions affect it. In a $6 \times 6$ visceral-state grid world, MHNG-mediated interaction regulated the infant's visceral state more adaptively than one-sided control conditions, and the two posteriors became rapidly aligned. Notably, this latent-state alignment emerged far earlier than the convergence of the learned generative matrices, indicating that representational synchrony does not presuppose fully shared world models. These results offer a minimal constructive account of latent-state alignment compatible with IBS reported in hyperscanning studies and support CPC as a candidate computational basis for inter-brain alignment.

</details>


### [90] [Emergence of Social Reality of Emotion through a Social Allostasis Model with Dynamic Interpretants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07761)
*Kentaro Nomura,Yushi Tsubamoto,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究通过计算模型模拟了社会现实的涌现过程，发现两个智能体通过交换符号和相互适应，其内感受先验偏好和符号概率分布能够收敛，从而证实了基于社会共识的社会现实的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于情绪建构理论，该理论将社会现实定义为群体层面对情绪概念的共识，这些概念被赋予源于身体稳态和社会互动的内感受。本研究旨在通过计算模型模拟这一涌现过程，探索社会现实如何在智能体互动中产生。

Method: 采用一种集成符号涌现、符号解释自由度和主动推断的计算模型。两个智能体接收内感受信号，交换推断的符号，并同时使其身体控制目标和符号解释相互适应。

Result: 实验结果显示，两个智能体的内感受先验偏好和符号概率分布均发生了收敛，验证了基于社会共识的社会现实的涌现。

Conclusion: 研究成功证实了社会现实可以通过智能体间的互动和适应过程而涌现，为情绪建构理论提供了计算层面的支持，表明社会共识能够使符号意义在群体层面得以确立。

Abstract: The theory of constructed emotion defines social reality as the community-level consensus on emotion concepts assigned to interoceptive sensations arising from bodily allostasis and social interaction. In this study, we simulate this emergence process using a computational model that integrates symbol emergence with degrees of freedom in symbol interpretation and active inference. Two agents receive interoceptive signals, exchange inferred symbols, and simultaneously adapt their bodily control goals and symbol interpretations to each other. Experimental results show that the interoceptive prior preferences and symbol probability distributions of the two agents converge, confirming the emergence of social reality grounded in social consensus.

</details>

### [91] [RateQuant: Optimal Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization via Rate-Distortion Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06675)
*Fei Zuo,Zikang Zhou,Hao Cong,Xiaoyan Xi,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RateQuant方法，通过为每个量化器拟合失真模型并利用率失真理论进行位宽分配，解决了KV缓存混合精度量化中的失真模型不匹配问题，在低比特场景下显著降低了困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，成为服务的主要内存瓶颈。现有量化方法对所有注意力头分配相同位宽，忽略了头重要性的差异。直观的混合精度分配方案存在隐藏缺陷：不同量化器具有不同的失真曲线衰减率，直接应用会导致分配顺序颠倒，性能反而不如均匀量化。

Method: 提出RateQuant方法：首先在小规模校准集上为每个量化器拟合失真模型D(b)=αβ^{-b}，然后利用率失真理论中的逆注水算法，通过闭式求解最优位宽分配问题。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B模型上以2.5平均比特进行实验，RateQuant将KIVI的困惑度从49.3降至14.9（降低70%），相比QuaRot提升6.6 PPL。校准过程在单GPU上仅需1.6秒，推理时零额外开销。

Conclusion: RateQuant成功识别并解决了混合精度KV缓存量化中的失真模型不匹配问题，通过率失真理论实现了高效的位宽分配，在极低比特量化下取得了显著的性能提升，为LLM推理优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models cache all previously computed key-value (KV) pairs during generation, and this KV cache grows linearly with sequence length, making it a primary memory bottleneck for serving. Quantizing the KV cache to fewer bits reduces this cost, yet all current quantizers assign the same bit-width to every attention head, ignoring the large variation in head importance. A natural idea is to allocate more bits to important heads and fewer to the rest. We show, however, that such mixed-precision allocation has a hidden pitfall: each quantizer follows a different distortion curve D(b)=alpha*beta^{-b}, and the decay rate beta varies from 3.6 to 5.3 across quantizer designs. Applying one quantizer's distortion model to another inverts the allocation order and makes performance worse than uniform quantization. We call this failure mode distortion model mismatch and propose RateQuant to resolve it. RateQuant fits a per-quantizer distortion model from a small calibration set, then solves the resulting bit-allocation problem in closed form via reverse waterfilling from rate-distortion theory. On Qwen3-8B at 2.5 average bits, calibrated RateQuant reduces KIVI's perplexity from 49.3 to 14.9 (70% reduction) and improves QuaRot by 6.6 PPL. The entire calibration takes 1.6 s on a single GPU and adds zero overhead at inference time.

</details>


### [92] [LKV: End-to-End Learning of Head-wise Budgets and Token Selection for LLM KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06676)
*Enshuai Zhou,Yifan Hao,Chao Wang,Rui Zhang,Di Huang,Jiaming Guo,Xing Hu,Zidong Du,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LKV框架，将KV缓存压缩建模为端到端可微分优化问题，通过学习全局预算和内在重要性评估，在保持近无损性能的同时实现了显著压缩（仅保留15%缓存）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的长上下文推理受限于KV缓存内存的线性增长；现有启发式压缩方法存在根本局限：启发式预算分配依赖统计先验而非任务目标导致资源错配，启发式选择依赖耦合的查询-键交互或静态归纳偏置，限制了压缩效果。

Method: 提出LKV（Learned KV Eviction）框架，将KV压缩建模为端到端可微分优化问题。包含两个核心组件：LKV-H用于学习任务优化的全局预算，LKV-T用于推导内在KV重要性（无需实体化注意力矩阵），从而绕过启发式代理，严格对齐压缩与任务目标。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER基准测试中达到最先进性能；在LongBench上仅保留15%的KV缓存即可实现近无损性能；分析表明学习式预算分配是保真度的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的资源分配对于克服手工启发式方法的局限性至关重要，将KV压缩从启发式方法转变为任务导向的可学习优化是提升长上下文推理效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Long-context inference in Large Language Models (LLMs) is bottlenecked by the linear growth of Key-Value (KV) cache memory. Existing KV cache compression paradigms are fundamentally limited by heuristics: heuristic budgeting relies on statistical priors rather than task objectives, causing resource misallocation, while heuristic selection relies on coupled query-key interactions or static inductive biases (e.g., attention sinks). To address this limitation, we introduce LKV (Learned KV Eviction), which formulates KV compression as an end-to-end differentiable optimization problem. LKV integrates LKV-H to learn task-optimized global budgets, and LKV-T to derive intrinsic KV importance without materializing attention matrices. This design bypasses heuristic proxies, strictly aligning compression with task objectives. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that LKV achieves state-of-the-art performance on both LongBench and RULER benchmarks at high compression rates. In particular, on LongBench, LKV achieves near-lossless performance with only 15\% KV cache retention. Crucially, our analysis identifies learned budgeting as the dominant driver of fidelity, demonstrating that data-driven allocation is essential to overcome the limitations of hand-crafted heuristics.

</details>


### [93] [A Wasserstein GAN-based climate scenario generator for risk management and insurance: the case of soil subsidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06678)
*Antoine Heranval,Olivier Lopez,Didier Ngatcha,Daniel Nkameni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SwiGAN模型，一种基于条件生成对抗网络的AI框架，用于生成土壤湿度指数（SWI）的时空轨迹，模拟法国易旱地区至2050年的干旱传播模式，以支持保险业应对气候变化风险的长期策略设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害年均成本从1970-2000年的700-800亿美元激增至2001-2020年的1800-2000亿美元。现有保险监管框架（如Solvency II）仅关注一年期视野，无法满足气候变化背景下的中长期风险管理需求。干旱占法国自然灾害保险赔偿的约30%，亟需预测工具支持适应性策略制定。

Method: 开发了一种基于条件生成对抗网络（Conditional GANs）的人工智能框架SwiGAN。该模型以土壤湿度指数（SWI）为核心指标，通过生成对抗机制学习干旱时空演变规律，模拟生成至2050年法国特定高风险区域的SWI地图序列。该方法具有泛化性，可应用于其他气候相关灾害。

Result: SwiGAN成功生成了法国易旱地区至2050年的SWI时空轨迹序列，能够模拟气候变暖情景下干旱传播模式的合理演变。生成的SWI地图序列具有真实性，可用于分析气候变化下的干旱动态特征。

Conclusion: SwiGAN框架为保险行业提供了中长期气候风险预测工具，填补了当前监管框架短期视野的空白。该模型支持适应性风险管理和保险策略设计，具有广泛的精算应用前景，可推广至经济情景生成等其他气候灾害领域。

Abstract: According to the United Nations Office for Disaster Risk Reduction (2025), the average annual cost of natural catastrophes increased from 70--80 billion USD between 1970 and 2000 to 180--200 billion USD between 2001 and 2020. Reports from organizations such as the IFOA and the WWF highlight the need for the insurance sector to adapt to this rapidly evolving context by developing medium- to long-term strategies that go beyond the one-year horizon of prudential regulations such as Solvency II. This paper introduces an artificial intelligence framework based on Conditional Generative Adversarial Networks (Conditional GANs) to generate future spatio-temporal trajectories of climatic indices. The approach focuses on the Soil Wetness Index (SWI), a key indicator used in France to assess drought severity. Drought accounts for approximately 30% of the indemnities paid under the French natural catastrophe insurance scheme. The proposed model, SwiGAN, simulates plausible drought propagation patterns up to 2050 for a region of France particularly exposed to this hazard. By generating realistic sequences of SWI maps, SwiGAN provides insights into drought dynamics under climate change scenarios and supports the design of adaptive risk management and insurance strategies. The methodology is also generalizable to other climate-related perils and actuarial applications such as economic scenario generation.

</details>


### [94] [Breaking the Illusion: When Positive Meets Negative in Multimodal Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06679)
*Yubo Jiang,Yitong An,Xin Yang,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Xuxin Cheng,Fengying Xie,Zhiguo Jiang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的推理框架PND，通过双路径对比解码解决视觉语言模型中的对象幻觉问题，在多个基准测试上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型因过度依赖语言先验知识，常产生与视觉现实相矛盾的对象幻觉内容。作者发现VLMs存在注意力失衡问题，即视觉特征的权重被低估，导致生成内容偏离视觉输入。

Method: 提出Positive-and-Negative Decoding (PND)框架，这是一种无需训练的推理干预方法。该框架包含双路径对比机制：正路径用于放大视觉证据，负路径构建反事实样本以惩罚语言先验主导的生成过程。通过在解码阶段对比两条路径的输出，引导模型生成更具视觉依据的结果。

Result: 在POPE、MME和CHAIR三个基准数据集上进行了实验验证，所提方法无需重新训练模型即可达到最先进的性能表现，有效缓解了对象幻觉问题。

Conclusion: PND框架通过直接干预解码过程成功解决了VLMs中的注意力失衡问题，无需额外训练即可提升视觉忠实度，为缓解对象幻觉提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are frequently undermined by object hallucination, generating content that contradicts visual reality, due to an over-reliance on linguistic priors. We introduce Positive-and-Negative Decoding (PND), a training-free inference framework that intervenes directly in the decoding process to enforce visual fidelity. PND is motivated by our finding of an attention imbalance in VLMs, where visual features are under-weighted. Our framework introduces a dual-path contrast: a positive path that amplifies visual evidence and a negative path that constructs counterfactuals to penalize prior-dominant generation. By contrasting outputs from both paths during decoding, PND steers generation toward visually grounded results. Experiments on POPE, MME, and CHAIR demonstrate state-of-the-art performance without retraining.

</details>


### [95] [On the Role of Strain and Vorticity in Numerical Integration Error for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06680)
*Chenxi Tao,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分解速度场雅可比矩阵研究了流匹配中的积分误差机制，发现应变控制指数误差放大而涡度仅线性影响误差，提出加权雅可比正则化方法可将积分误差降低2.7倍，在CIFAR-10上将FID提升14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型的推理成本由积分步数（NFE）决定，而现有方法对速度场性质如何影响积分误差缺乏深入理解。作者旨在分析速度场的哪些特性主导积分误差，以指导更高效推理方法的设计。

Method: 1. 将速度场雅可比矩阵分解为对称部分S（应变率）和反对称部分Ω（涡度），理论分析两者对积分误差的不同影响机制；2. 证明最优传输速度场具有无旋性和零物质导数，可实现二阶欧拉精度；3. 提出加权雅可比正则化方法，分别对应变和涡度设置权重α和β。

Result: 1. 理论证明应变通过对数范数控制指数误差放大，涡度仅线性贡献局部截断误差；2. 2D合成数据实验验证理论预测，NFE=5时积分误差降低达2.7倍；3. CIFAR-10实验中，轻量微调在NFE=10时FID改善14%，同时保持高NFE下的生成质量。

Conclusion: 速度场的应变和涡度对积分误差具有不同作用机制，最优传输路径具有理想积分性质。通过加权雅可比正则化可有效降低推理成本，为流匹配模型的高效部署提供了理论和实践指导。

Abstract: Flow matching generates data by integrating a learned velocity field, where the number of integration steps (NFE) directly determines inference cost. We analyze which properties of the velocity field govern integration error by decomposing the velocity Jacobian into its symmetric part S (strain rate) and antisymmetric part Omega (vorticity). We prove that strain and vorticity play different roles: strain controls exponential error amplification through the logarithmic norm, while vorticity contributes only linearly to the local truncation error. We further show that the optimal transport velocity field is irrotational and has zero material derivative, implying second-order Euler accuracy; for exact displacement interpolation, the associated Lagrangian particle dynamics are integrated exactly by Euler. Motivated by this analysis, we study weighted Jacobian regularization with strain weight alpha and vorticity weight beta. Experiments on 2D synthetic data confirm the main theoretical predictions, showing up to 2.7x lower integration error at NFE=5. Preliminary CIFAR-10 experiments show consistent trends, with a lightweight fine-tuning procedure improving FID by 14 percent at NFE=10 while preserving high-NFE quality.

</details>


### [96] [A Hierarchical Ensemble Pipeline for Anomaly Detection in ESA Satellite Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06681)
*Lorenzo Riccardo Allegrini,Geremia Pompei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个分层集成管道，用于欧空局（ESA）多变量遥测数据中的异常检测。该方法结合了基于形状和统计的特征提取、通道建模及聚合策略，在ESA-ADB挑战赛中表现出优异的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决欧洲航天局（ESA）提供的多变量遥测数据中的异常检测问题，特别是在现实卫星遥测场景中检测细微异常的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种分层集成管道（hierarchical ensemble pipeline）。该方法整合了基于形状的特征提取和统计特征提取；采用单通道建模与通道内堆叠；最终进行跨通道聚合。训练和验证过程使用了时间序列交叉验证和双层掩码策略，以防止信息泄露。

Result: 在欧洲航天局异常检测基准（ESA-ADB）挑战赛上的结果表明，该方法具有很强的泛化能力，证明了分层建模在现实卫星遥测中检测细微异常的有效性。

Conclusion: 分层建模方法在现实卫星遥测数据的异常检测中是有效的，能够实现强泛化性能，为处理多变量遥测数据提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: A hierarchical ensemble pipeline is introduced to address anomaly detection in multivariate telemetry data provided by European Space Agency (ESA). The method integrates shapelet-based and statistical feature extraction, per-channel modeling, intra-channel stacking, and a final cross-channel aggregation. The pipeline is trained and validated using time-series cross-validation and two-level masking strategies to prevent information leakage. Results on the European Space Agency Anomaly Detection Benchmark (ESA-ADB) challenge demonstrate strong generalization, highlighting the effectiveness of hierarchical modeling in detecting subtle anomalies in realistic satellite telemetry.

</details>


### [97] [Graph Representation Learning Augmented Model Manipulation on Federated Fine-Tuning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07961)
*Hanlin Cai,Kai Li,Houtianfu Wang,Haofan Dong,Yichen Li,Falko Dressler,Ozgur B. Akan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AugMP攻击策略，通过图表示学习和增强拉格朗日对偶方法生成恶意模型更新，能够有效破坏联邦微调大语言模型的性能，同时规避现有防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调(FFT)作为一种保护隐私的分布式大语言模型协作训练范式，虽然避免了原始数据共享，但仍面临模型操纵威胁。恶意参与者可以上传被操纵的模型更新来破坏聚合过程，降低全局模型性能。现有研究缺乏对这种攻击的有效方法，因此需要设计更强大的攻击策略来揭示FFT系统的脆弱性。

Method: 提出增强模型操纵(AugMP)策略：1) 设计图表示学习框架，捕捉良性模型更新之间的特征相关性来指导恶意更新生成；2) 基于增强拉格朗日对偶公式开发迭代操纵算法，在优化恶意更新的同时保持与良性更新相似的参数特征，从而增强攻击效果和隐蔽性。

Result: 在多个LLM骨干网络上的实验表明：1) AugMP在所有竞争基线中实现最强的操纵性能；2) 全局LLM准确率降低高达26%；3) 本地LLM代理的平均准确率下降高达22%；4) 与良性更新保持高度统计和几何一致性，能够成功规避基于距离和相似性的传统防御方法。

Conclusion: AugMP策略揭示了联邦微调大语言模型面临的严重安全威胁。该方法通过巧妙的优化框架实现了高效且隐蔽的模型操纵攻击，表明现有防御机制不足以应对此类高级攻击，需要开发更强大的防御策略来保护FFT系统的安全性。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning (FFT) has emerged as a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaboratively adapting large language models (LLMs). Built upon federated learning, FFT enables distributed agents to jointly refine a shared pretrained LLM by aggregating local LLM updates without sharing local raw data. However, FFT-based LLMs remain vulnerable to model manipulation threats, in which adversarial participants upload manipulated LLM updates that corrupt the aggregation process and degrade the performance of the global LLM. In this paper, we propose an Augmented Model maniPulation (AugMP) strategy against FFT-based LLMs. Specifically, we design a novel graph representation learning framework that captures feature correlations among benign LLM updates to guide the generation of malicious updates. To enhance manipulation effectiveness and stealthiness, we develop an iterative manipulation algorithm based on an augmented Lagrangian dual formulation. Through this formulation, malicious updates are optimized to embed adversarial objectives while preserving benign-like parameter characteristics. Experimental results across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that the AugMP strategy achieves the strongest manipulation performance among all competing baselines, reducing the global LLM accuracy by up to 26% and degrading the average accuracy of local LLM agents by up to 22%. Meanwhile, AugMP maintains high statistical and geometric consistency with benign updates, enabling it to evade conventional distance- and similarity-based defense methods.

</details>


### [98] [Toeplitz MLP Mixers are Low Complexity, Information-Rich Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06683)
*Benjamin L. Badger,Ethan Roland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM)，一种用三角掩码Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代注意力机制的类Transformer架构，实现了训练时O(dn log n)时间和O(dn)空间复杂度，推理时O(dn)复杂度，在计算效率和设备内存使用方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的大语言模型受限于注意力机制二次方的时间和空间计算复杂度，需要开发更高效的亚二次复杂度架构。

Method: 提出TMM架构，用序列维度上的三角掩码Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代注意力机制，避免复杂输入调制或状态维护，保持架构简洁。

Result: TMM在单位计算量和设备内存下实现更低的训练损失，保留更多输入信息提升复制能力，在信息检索和上下文学习基准测试中优于可比架构。

Conclusion: 从算子指标理论角度分析发现，训练后的因果不可逆模型的Toeplitz层比实际输入可逆的模型更可能可逆或接近可逆。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models are in some respects limited by the quadratic time and space computational complexity of attention. We introduce the Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM), a transformer-like architecture that swaps attention for triangular-masked Toeplitz matrix multiplication over the sequence dimension resulting in $\mathcal{O} (dn \log n)$ time and $\mathcal O(dn)$ space complexity during training and $\mathcal O(dn)$ time and space at inference prefill. Despite the lack of sophisticated input modulation or state maintenance present in other sub-quadratic architectures, TMMs yield greater training efficiency in terms of loss achieved per compute and device memory. We demonstrate that TMMs are capable of retaining more input information resulting in improved copying ability, which we argue results from a lack of architectural biases.
