Consistent with higher input information retention, TMMs exhibit superior information retrieval and in-context learning benchmark accuracy compared to comparable architectures. We conclude with an analysis from the perspective of operator index theory and show that, counterintuitively, trained Toeplitz layers of causal non-invertible models are more likely to be invertible or nearly so than models that are actually invertible over their inputs.

</details>


### [99] [From Canopy to Collision: A Hybrid Predictive Framework for Identifying Risk Factors in Tree-Involved Traffic Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06684)
*Abdul Azim,Ahmed Hossain,Soumyadip Maitra,Panick Kalambay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于2020-2023年CRSS数据，利用CatBoost、SHAP和逻辑回归构建分析框架，识别出未使用安全带是树木碰撞事故严重程度的最关键预测因子，车辆年龄、超速和驾驶人受损状态也有显著影响，并揭示了关键因素间的交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树木相关碰撞是路外（ROR）碰撞的关键子集，常因高能撞击导致致命或重伤。研究旨在开发一个综合分析框架，以识别和量化导致此类事故严重程度的风险因素。

Method: 提出多步骤建模框架：首先利用机器学习分类模型（CatBoost）识别关键因素；其次应用SHAP工具量化并可视化影响因素的边际效应；再次通过二元逻辑回归模型估算因子效应并验证SHAP结果；最后利用SHAP交互图考察关键因素的联合效应。

Result: 未使用安全带是最具影响力的预测因子，未受约束乘员发生严重后果的概率近三倍；车辆年龄、超速违规和驾驶人受损状态影响显著；揭示了照明条件与车辆年龄、超速与照明条件、安全带使用与车辆年龄、路面状况与超速之间的关键交互作用，表现出特定的叠加风险效应。

Conclusion: 研究结果为基于安全系统的针对性干预措施提供了关键见解，建议加强安全带执法、在能见度降低条件下进行速度管理以及推动车辆车队现代化。

Abstract: Tree-involved crashes represent a critical subset of run-off-road (ROR) collisions, often resulting in fatal or severe injuries due to high-energy impacts. This study develops a comprehensive analytical framework to identify and quantify risk factors contributing to crash severity in tree-involved collisions using the Crash Report Sampling System (CRSS) database spanning 2020-2023. The modeling framework follows a multi-step process. First, a machine learning based classification model (CatBoost) identifies key factors associated with binary crash injury severity (KA: fatal or incapacitating injury versus BC: non-incapacitating or possible injury). Second, SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) tool is used to quantify and visualize the marginal effects of top influential factors on crash severity. Third, a binary logistic regression model estimates factor effects and validates SHAP-derived importance measures. Finally, SHAP interaction plots examine the combined effects of key contributing factors. Results reveal restraint non-use as the most influential predictor, with unrestrained occupants nearly three times more likely to experience severe outcomes due to ejection risk. Vehicle age, speeding violations, and driver impairment demonstrate substantial effects, reflecting reduced crashworthiness, increased impact forces, and reduced control capabilities. Critical interactions emerge between lighting conditions and vehicle age, speeding and lighting conditions, restraint use and vehicle age, and road surface and speeding, demonstrating additive risk effects with specific interactions. These findings provide critical insights for targeted safe system-based interventions, including enhanced seat belt enforcement, speed management in reduced visibility conditions, and vehicle fleet modernization.

</details>


### [100] [Conformal Agent Error Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06788)
*Naihe Feng,Yi Sui,Shiyi Hou,Ga Wu,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于共形预测（CP）的多智能体系统错误归因框架，通过设计适用于序列数据的新型过滤算法，生成连续的错误预测集合，从而实现对错误位置的精确定位与系统回滚恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）发生故障时，识别关键错误发生的位置是实现自动恢复的首要步骤。然而，由于MAS产生漫长的交互轨迹，错误归因仍然是一个根本性挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于共形预测（CP）的错误归因框架。作者引入了适用于智能体轨迹等序列数据的新型过滤CP算法，与现有CP算法不同，该方法预测的是连续序列集合，以支持高效的系统恢复和调试。该方法具有模型无关性。

Result: 在多种智能体和数据集上验证了理论保证，证明了该方法能够精确隔离错误位置，并利用预测集成功将MAS回滚至正确状态以修正自身错误。

Conclusion: 该框架为多智能体系统的错误归因提供了一个具有有限样本、无分布覆盖保证的原则性不确定性层，实现了模型无关的精确错误定位与自动恢复。

Abstract: When multi-agent systems (MAS) fail, identifying where the decisive error occurred is the first step for automated recovery to an earlier state. Error attribution remains a fundamental challenge due to the long interaction traces that large language model-based MAS generate. This paper presents a framework for error attribution based on conformal prediction (CP) which provides finite-sample, distribution-free coverage guarantees. We introduce new algorithms for filtration-based CP designed for sequential data such as agent trajectories. Unlike existing CP algorithms, our approach predicts sets that are contiguous sequences to enable efficient recovery and debugging. We verify our theoretical guarantees on a variety of agents and datasets, show that errors can be precisely isolated, then use prediction sets to rollback MAS to correct their own errors. Our overall approach is model-agnostic, and offers a principled uncertainty layer for MAS error attribution. We release code at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/conformal-agent-error-attribution.

</details>


### [101] [Robustness of Refugee-Matching Gains to Off-Policy Evaluation Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06686)
*Kirk Bansak,Elisabeth Paulson,Dominik Rothenhäusler,Jeremy Ferwerda,Jens Hainmueller,Michael Hotard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多种离策略评估方法验证了美国难民匹配政策反事实影响评估结果的稳健性，发现结果在统计显著性和幅度上均保持一致，并与Bansak et al. (2018)的原始研究结论相符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Bansak et al. (2018)首次提出的难民匹配提升难民安置效果的研究，本文旨在检验反事实影响评估结果的稳定性，验证初始研究结论是否稳健可靠。

Method: 采用多种离策略评估方法进行反事实影响估计，包括逆概率加权（IPW）、多种增强逆概率加权（AIPW）变体；同时考虑不同建模架构和分配程序等修改方案进行稳健性测试。

Result: 在所有评估场景中，影响估计的幅度保持一致，且在大多数情况下具有统计显著性；评估结果与Bansak et al. (2018)最初呈现的结果一致。

Conclusion: 美国难民匹配背景下的反事实影响评估结果具有稳健性，不同评估方法均得出一致的结论，验证了难民匹配政策效果评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Previous research has investigated the potential of refugee matching for boosting refugee outcomes, first considered by Bansak et al. (2018). This paper demonstrates the stability of counterfactual impact evaluation results in the context of refugee matching in the United States using a range of off-policy evaluation methods. In order to estimate counterfactual impact and test the robustness of our results, we employ several evaluation methods, including inverse probability weighting (IPW) and multiple variants of augmented inverse probability weighting (AIPW). We also consider various modifications, including alternative modeling architectures and different assignment procedures. The impact estimates remain consistent in magnitude in all scenarios as well as statistically significant in most cases. Furthermore, the estimates are also consistent with the results originally presented in Bansak et al. (2018).

</details>


### [102] [Conditional generation of antibody sequences with classifier-guided germline-absorbing discrete diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06720)
*Justin Sanders,Luca Giancardo,Lan Guo,Yue Zhao,Kemal Sonmez,Nina Cheng,Melih Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散扩散模型的抗体序列设计方法，通过引入“种系吸收扩散”机制，有效缓解了现有蛋白语言模型的种系偏差问题，并在条件生成任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管抗体治疗药物非常成功，但计算设计具有理想结合性和可开发性的抗体仍然具有挑战性。现有基于蛋白语言模型（pLMs）的方法存在两大局限：1）主要记忆种系序列而非建模有生物学意义的体细胞变异；2）对灵活的分类器引导条件生成支持有限。

Method: 1）采用离散扩散微调方法，在抗体序列上实现强语言建模性能，并支持基于任意现成分类器的条件生成；2）提出“种系吸收扩散”方法，修改离散扩散噪声过程，将种系序列（而非掩码序列）作为吸收状态。这种生物学驱动的归纳偏置限制模型学习从种系到观测序列的轨迹，有效排除了遗传变异和V(D)J重组统计，显著缓解种系偏差。

Result: 1）种系扩散将非种系残基预测准确率从26%提升至46%，接近真实生物变异设定的理论上限；2）在条件生成任务（采样具有改善疏水性和预测结合亲和力的抗体）中，模型在类别一致性和样本质量之间展现出更好的权衡，显著优于EvoProtGrad方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的种系扩散模型成功解决了抗体序列设计中的种系偏差问题，实现了更准确的体细胞变异建模，并在条件生成任务中表现出优越性能，为抗体计算设计提供了有效的新工具。

Abstract: Antibody therapeutics are among the most successful modern medicines, yet computationally designing antibodies with desirable binding and developability properties remains challenging. While protein language models (pLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for antibody sequence design, existing approaches largely suffer from two key limitations: they predominantly memorize germline sequences rather than modeling biologically meaningful somatic variation, and they offer limited support for flexible classifier-guided conditional generation. We address these challenges through two primary contributions. First, we demonstrate that discrete diffusion fine-tuning achieves strong language modeling performance on antibody sequences while allowing for generation conditioned on any off-the-shelf classifier. Second, we introduce germline absorbing diffusion, a novel modification of the discrete diffusion noise process in which the germline sequence - rather than a masked sequence - serves as the absorbing state. This biologically motivated inductive bias restricts the model to learning the trajectory from germline to observed sequence, effectively excluding genetic variation and V(D)J recombination statistics from the learned distribution and dramatically mitigating germline bias. We show that germline diffusion improves non-germline residue prediction accuracy from 26 percent to 46 percent, approaching the theoretical upper bound set by true biological variability. We then demonstrate the utility of our germline diffusion model on the conditional generation tasks of sampling antibodies with improved hydrophobicity and predicted binding affinity. On both tasks our model shows an improved tradeoff between class adherence and sample quality, significantly outperforming EvoProtGrad, a popular strategy to sample from pLMs with gradient-based discrete Markov Chain Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [103] [MAGIQ: A Post-Quantum Multi-Agentic AI Governance System with Provable Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06933)
*Sepideh Avizeh,Tushin Mallick,Alina Oprea,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Reihaneh Safavi-Naini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAGIQ框架，这是首个针对多智能体AI系统的后量子安全策略定义与执行解决方案，在保证可证明安全性的同时实现了高效的密码学协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算生态系统面临两大变革：智能体AI系统的广泛部署和量子计算的进步。对于智能体AI系统，亟需构建安全的治理架构以确保智能体遵循所有者的通信和交互策略，并能对其与其他智能体交换的消息负责。对于量子计算，现有系统必须进行改造并设计新的密码机制以确保长期安全性和抗量子性。NIST建议从2030年开始弃用RSA、Diffie-Hellman和椭圆曲线等标准公钥密码算法，2035年后完全禁用。

Method: 提出MAGIQ框架，采用具有可证明安全保证的新型高效抗量子密码协议：1）允许用户为智能体间会话和任务定义丰富的通信与访问控制策略预算，包括一对多智能体会话的全局预算；2）使用后量子密码原语执行策略；3）支持智能体间和一对多智能体会话的基于会话的策略执行；4）通过消息归属提供智能体对用户的责任追溯。使用通用可组合性(UC)框架对系统的正确性和安全性进行形式化建模和证明。

Result: 评估了框架的计算和通信开销，并与最先进的智能体AI框架SAGA进行对比分析，证明了MAGIQ的有效性和效率优势。

Conclusion: MAGIQ是面向智能体AI系统的后量子安全解决方案的第一步，为解决智能体AI系统在量子计算时代的策略执行和安全性问题提供了重要的技术基础和实践参考。

Abstract: Our computing ecosystem is being transformed by two emerging paradigms: the increased deployment of agentic AI systems and advancements in quantum computing. With respect to agentic AI systems, one of the most critical problems is creating secure governing architectures that ensure agents follow their owners' communication and interaction policies and can be held accountable for the messages they exchange with other agents. With respect to quantum computing, existing systems must be retrofitted and new cryptographic mechanisms must be designed to ensure long-term security and quantum resistance. In fact, NIST recommends that standard public-key cryptographic algorithms, including RSA, Diffie-Hellman (DH), and elliptic-curve constructions (ECC), be deprecated starting in 2030 and disallowed after 2035.
  In this paper, we present MAGIQ, a framework for policy definition and enforcement in multi-agent AI systems using novel, highly efficient, quantum-resistant cryptographic protocols with proven security guarantees. MAGIQ (i) allows users to define rich communication and access-control policy budgets for agent-to-agent sessions and tasks, including global budgets for one-to-many agent sessions; (ii) enforces such policies using post-quantum cryptographic primitives; (iii) supports session-based enforcement of policies for agent-to-agent and one-to-many agent sessions; and (iv) provides accountability of agents to their users through message attribution. We formally model and prove the correctness and security of the system using the Universal Composability (UC) framework. We evaluate the computation and communication overhead of our framework and compare it with the state-of-the-art agentic AI framework SAGA. MAGIQ is a first step toward post-quantum-secure solutions for agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [104] [Enabling Unsupervised Training of Deep EEG Denoisers With Intelligent Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06724)
*Qiyu Rao,Haozhe Tian,Homayoun Hamedmoghadam,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出iPSD，一种用于可穿戴EEG的自监督去噪框架。该方法无需干净参考信号，通过智能分区实现自监督学习，在极低信噪比（-10 dB）下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴EEG信号微弱且与频谱重叠的噪声伪影难以分离。传统信号处理方法依赖固定规则无法处理时变伪影，而深度学习方法虽然强大，但训练需要无法获取的无噪EEG作为参考。

Method: 提出智能分区自监督去噪（iPSD）。核心思想是将输入EEG段分区为具有相同潜在信号的独立噪声实现，从而在零样本场景下实现深度去噪网络的自监督训练，无需干净EEG参考信号。

Result: 在耳内传感器可穿戴EEG上的广泛实验表明，iPSD在极低信噪比（低至-10 dB）和EMG等挑战性伪影下达到最先进性能，其频谱保真度比竞争基线高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: iPSD成功解决了可穿戴EEG去噪中缺乏干净参考信号的难题，在极端噪声条件下展现出卓越性能，为实际应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Denoising wearable electroencephalogram (EEG) is inherently challenging since neural activity is not only subtle but also inseparable from spectrally overlapping noise artifacts. Classical signal processing methods, relying on fixed or heuristic rules, cannot handle the time-varying pervasive artifacts in wearable EEGs. Deep learning methods, on the other hand, show promise in decomposition-free EEG denoising using highly expressive neural networks, but the training requires artifact-free EEG, which is inherently unobtainable. To address this, we propose Intelligent Partitioning for Self-supervised Denoising (iPSD). Our method eliminates the need for clean references by learning to partition an input EEG segment into independent noisy realizations with the same underlying signal. This enables self-supervision of deep learning denoisers, even in zero-shot settings where only a single EEG segment to be denoised is available. We validate iPSD through extensive experiments, including validations on wearable EEG from in-ear sensors. The results show that iPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance, most notably under extremely low signal-to-noise ratios (down to -10 dB) and challenging artifacts (e.g., EMG), with spectral fidelity orders of magnitude higher than competitive baselines.

</details>


### [105] [Transformer-Based Wildlife Species Classification from Daily Movement Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06726)
*Obed Irakoze,Prasenjit Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究使用Transformer模型分析野生动物GPS轨迹数据以识别物种，相比LSTM、CNN等基线模型准确率提升8-22个百分点，并通过特征增强和统一时间分辨率优化了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从日常运动数据推断野生动物物种身份是一项具有挑战性的任务，现有方法在数据稀疏、物种代表性不足等条件下性能有限，需要更有效的序列模型和特征表示方法。

Method: 基于Movebank平台大规模7物种GPS轨迹数据训练序列模型，采用整个遥测研究或区域留出的评估协议，比较Transformer与LSTM、CNN、时序卷积网络的性能，分析基于位移编码与包含速度、方向、转向行为的扩展运动描述符的差异，对比1小时和30分钟时间分辨率的影响。

Result: Transformer在所有实验设置中均优于基线模型，在大象二分类任务中实现0.83平衡准确率和0.92 AUC；特征增强对代表性不足的物种（如大型食肉动物、狮子、斑马）带来明显性能提升；统一1小时分辨率比30分钟分辨率表现更好，能减少缺失数据并确保一致的时间覆盖。

Conclusion: Transformer模型在野生动物物种识别任务中具有显著优势，特征增强和适当的时间分辨率选择对提升模型性能至关重要，尤其是对稀疏数据的物种识别问题。

Abstract: Inferring the identity of wildlife species from daily movement data alone is a challenging task. We train sequence models on large-scale, 7-species GPS trajectories from the Movebank platform. Trajectories models are evaluated using a protocol in which entire telemetry studies or regions are heldout during testing. We compare Transformer-based sequence models to LSTM, CNN, and Temporal Convolutional Networks, and find that Transformers consistently achieve higher balanced accuracy with gains of approximately 8 to 22 percentage points, depending on the species and experimental setting. In an elephant binary classification task with 1-hour resolution, the Transformer achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.83 and an AUC of 0.92, substantially outperforming all baseline models. We examine, under data-limited conditions, feature representations by analyzing the differences between a basic displacement-based encoding and an expanded range of movement descriptors that include speed, direction, and turning behavior. With feature augmentation, we see clear performance gains, especially for underrepresented and sparsely represented species, such as large carnivores, lions, and Zebras. Finally, experiments comparing 1-hour and 30-minutetemporal resolutions show that while finer sampling can capture short-term movement patterns for some species, a unified 1-hour resolution yields more promising performance across studies by reducing missing data and ensuring consistent temporal coverage.

</details>


### [106] [SparseRL-Sync: Lossless Weight Synchronization with ~100x Less Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07330)
*Lucas Hu,Ranchi Zhao,Isaac Zhu,Zach Zhang,Hscos Zhang,Hugh Yin,Jason Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SparseRL-Sync，一种利用参数更新稀疏性（通常>99%）的无损压缩同步方法，将通信量降低约100倍，有效解决了大规模强化学习系统中策略权重同步的带宽瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模强化学习系统中，随着模型规模增长，Trainer与Rollout之间的策略权重同步通信需求急剧上升。在带宽受限或网络多变的部署环境中（如跨数据中心、跨集群、异构资源池和在线RL场景），权重同步已成为吞吐量和尾延迟的主要瓶颈。

Method: 观察到主流大模型RL训练中参数变化的元素级稀疏度极高（通常>99%），提出SparseRL-Sync方法。该方法用稀疏更新负载（索引和值）替代完整权重传输，在推理侧进行无损精确重构，保证100%保真度。结合适当的分桶策略，进一步降低启动和控制平面开销。

Result: 在简化的成本模型下，稀疏同步将每次更新的通信量从S降至约S/X。在99%稀疏度（X≈100）情况下，传输数据量减少约100倍。该方法显著提升了带宽受限和高异步RL场景下的可扩展性和端到端效率。

Conclusion: SparseRL-Sync通过利用参数更新的高度稀疏性，实现了无损的通信量大幅压缩，为带宽受限环境下的大规模强化学习系统提供了有效的同步优化方案，显著改善了系统性能。

Abstract: In large-scale reinforcement learning (RL) systems with decoupled Trainer-Rollout execution, the Trainer must regularly synchronize policy weights to the Rollout side to limit policy staleness. When inter-node bandwidth is abundant, such synchronization is usually only a small fraction of end-to-end cost. As model size grows, however, the communication demand rises rapidly. In bandwidth-constrained or network-variable deployments -- for example, cross-datacenter or cross-cluster settings, heterogeneous resource pools, and online RL -- weight synchronization can become a dominant bottleneck for throughput and tail latency. We observe that, in mainstream large-model RL training, the locations where parameters actually change are highly sparse at the element level (often 99%+ sparsity). Building on this observation, we propose and implement SparseRL-Sync, which replaces full-weight transfers with a lossless sparse update payload (indices and values) that can be exactly reconstructed on the inference side, thereby preserving 100% fidelity. Under a simplified cost model, sparse synchronization reduces the per-update communication volume from S to approximately S/X; with 99% sparsity (X ~ 100), this yields about a 100x reduction in transmitted data. Combined with appropriate bucketing, SparseRL-Sync also reduces launch and control-plane overhead, significantly improving scalability and end-to-end efficiency in bandwidth-limited and highly asynchronous RL settings.

</details>


### [107] [Medical Imaging Classification with Cold-Atom Reservoir Computing using Auto-Encoders and Surrogate-Driven Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06727)
*Nuno Batista,Ana Morgado,Oscar Ferraz,Sagar Silva Pratapsi,Jorge Lobo,Gabriel Falcao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于中性原子储备池计算的混合量子-经典流程，用于医学图像（息肉）二分类。通过引入可微分代理模型克服了量子测量的梯度障碍，实现了端到端训练，性能优于传统PCA等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类任务面临高维数据处理挑战。尽管量子储备池计算具有潜力，但量子测量的不可微分特性导致了“梯度障碍”，使得标准端到端反向传播训练难以实现，限制了其在高维医学影像等实际应用中的性能。

Method: 提出一种混合量子-经典流程：首先集成引导式自编码器处理高维图像数据，学习紧凑且具有判别性的潜在表征；引入可微分代理模型模拟量子层，以克服梯度障碍，实现整个系统的端到端反向传播；训练过程联合优化分类精度与图像重建保真度；将学习到的潜在表征编码为里德伯哈密顿量中的脉冲失谐参数，通过期望值获得量子嵌入，最后输入线性分类器。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法在息肉检测任务上优于使用PCA或非引导自编码器的传统方法；消融研究验证了各种量子和训练参数的影响，证明了该流程在当前NISQ时代的鲁棒性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 该混合量子-经典流程成功克服了量子储备池计算中的梯度障碍，实现了高效的医学图像分类，证明了量子计算在当前NISQ时代下解决现实世界医学影像问题的可行性与潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a hybrid quantum-classical pipeline, based on neutral-atom reservoir computing, for medical image classification, focusing on the binary classification task of polyp detection. To deal effectively with the high dimensionality, we integrate a guided auto-encoder. This pipeline learns compact and discriminative representations of image data that are also well-suited for quantum reservoir computing. A key challenge in such systems is the non-differentiable nature of quantum measurements, which creates a 'gradient barrier' for standard training. We overcome this barrier by incorporating a differentiable surrogate model that emulates the quantum layer, enabling end-to-end backpropagation through the entire system. This guided training process is jointly optimized for classification accuracy and for faithful image recovery from the auto-encoder. The learned latent representations are encoded as pulse detuning parameters within a Rydberg Hamiltonian, and quantum embeddings are subsequently obtained through expectation values. These embeddings are then passed to a linear classifier. Our simulations show that this method outperforms some traditional approaches that use PCA or unguided autoencoders. We also conduct ablation studies to assess the impact of various quantum and training parameters, demonstrating the robustness and flexibility of our proposed pipeline for real-world medical imaging applications, even in the current NISQ era.

</details>


### [108] [Learning Material-Aware Hamiltonian Risk Fields for Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07038)
*Aditya Sai Ellendula,Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有选择性的风险感知导航策略，通过在端口哈密顿导航策略中添加上下文能量项，实现了在可行低风险路径存在时激活规避力、在无安全替代方案时抑制横向力的特性，显著提升了导航成功率和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险感知导航策略应具有选择性：当局部场景允许更低风险的可行机动时应暴露规避自由度，当不存在更安全的替代方案时应抑制这些自由度。现有方法往往在无安全出口时也会产生不安全的机动行为，缺乏这种选择性。

Method: 在端口哈密顿导航策略中添加一个上下文能量项，构建具有可验证签名的学习力通道。当局部风险场包含可行的低风险方向时，诱导的上下文力向其激活；当逃生路径被阻塞或尚未可用时，路径感知门控抑制横向力。采用CVaR尾部风险目标函数，将梯度更新集中在稀有但后果严重的风险转换上。

Result: 在延迟必需逃生基准测试中，路径感知CVaR将过早力激活从0.950降至0.180（对比DWA），成功率从0.480提升至0.810，零次重新规划；在RELLIS-3D真实越野地形上，正确激活率达0.837，误激活率0.114；在DFC2018静态语义地图上，灾难性故障从0.60降至0.10，振荡减少90.7%；在高速公路交通中，可行时碰撞从100%降至0%，无逃生路径时成功抑制横向机动。

Conclusion: 选择性特性源于上下文能量的梯度结构而非训练时的调参。所提出的方法成功实现了风险感知导航的选择性行为，在多种场景下显著提升了导航性能和安全性。

Abstract: Risk-aware navigation should be selective: a policy should expose evasive degrees of freedom only when the local scene admits a lower-risk feasible maneuver, and suppress them when no safer alternative exists. We show that adding one context-energy term to a port-Hamiltonian navigation policy produces a learned force channel with exactly this falsifiable signature. When the local risk field contains a feasible lower-risk direction, the induced context force activates toward it; when the apparent escape is blocked or not yet available, a route-aware gate suppresses lateral force rather than hallucinating an unsafe maneuver. A CVaR tail-risk objective focuses gradient updates on rare but consequential risk transitions. We validate the selectivity signature across four settings. In the primary delayed-required-escape benchmark, route-aware CVaR reduces premature force activation from 0.950 to 0.180 versus DWA while raising success from 0.480 to 0.810 with zero replans. On real off-road terrain (RELLIS-3D), route-aware enrichment achieves correct activation rate 0.837 and false activation rate 0.114, compared to 0.378/0.752 for scalar risk gradients. On static semantic maps (DFC2018), enrichment reduces catastrophic failure from 0.60 to 0.10 and oscillation by 90.7% while preserving path efficiency. In highway traffic, collisions drop from 100% to 0% when a lane escape is feasible; when no escape exists, the policy suppresses the lateral maneuver. The selectivity property follows from the gradient structure of the context energy rather than from training-time tuning.

</details>


### [109] [\mathsf{VISTA}: Decentralized Machine Learning in Adversary Dominated Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07841)
*Hanzaleh Akbari Nodehi,Parsa Moradi,Soheil Mohajer,Mohammad Ali Maddah-Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对去中心化机器学习中多数节点被敌手控制的场景，提出了一种基于激励的一致性验证框架VISTA，通过动态调整接受阈值，在不依赖诚实多数假设的情况下实现了与标准SGD相当的渐近收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的鲁棒聚合方法通常假设诚实节点占多数，但在敌手控制大部分工作节点的对抗主导环境中会失效。如何在这种敌手主导的去中心化学习场景中保证学习过程的收敛性和准确性，是一个尚未解决的关键问题。

Method: 提出了一种激励导向的框架：仅当报告相互一致至某阈值时才接受并奖励报告，将敌手从纯粹的破坏者转化为理性代理。针对迭代优化场景，设计了VISTA自适应算法，根据优化历史动态调整接受阈值，平衡早期收敛速度和敌手腐败风险。

Result: 数值实验表明VISTA相比静态阈值方法具有更好的收敛性能。理论分析证明，通过合适的激励感知自适应机制，敌手主导的去中心化学习可以保持标准SGD的渐近收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该研究突破了诚实多数假设的限制，证明了即使在工作节点多数被敌手控制的情况下，通过激励相容的自适应机制仍可实现有效的去中心化机器学习，为对抗性更强的分布式学习场景提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Decentralized machine learning often relies on outsourcing computations, such as gradient evaluations, to untrusted worker nodes. Existing robust aggregation methods can mitigate malicious behavior under honest-majority assumptions, but may fail when adversaries control a majority of the workers. We study this adversary-dominated setting through an incentive-oriented framework in which reports are accepted and rewarded only when they are mutually consistent up to a threshold. This turns the adversary from a pure saboteur into a rational agent that trades off increasing estimation error against the risk of rejection and loss of reward.
  We consider iterative optimization under this model. Unlike one-shot computation, iterative learning requires long-horizon decisions: permissive acceptance rules enable faster early progress but admit more adversarial corruption, while strict rules improve estimation accuracy but cause frequent rejections. We propose \mathsf{VISTA}, an adaptive algorithm that tunes the acceptance threshold using the optimization history. Numerical results show that \mathsf{VISTA} improves convergence over static thresholds. We also provide a rigorous convergence analysis showing that, with suitable incentive-aware adaptation, adversary-dominated decentralized learning can retain the asymptotic convergence behavior of standard SGD without relying on an honest majority.

</details>


### [110] [FLAM: Evaluating Model Performance with Aggregatable Measures in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07962)
*Fabian Stricker,Jose A. Peregrina,David Bermbach,Christian Zirpins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAM方法，通过可聚合指标实现联邦学习中的性能评估与集中式评估结果一致，无需全局测试数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据分布在各参与者之间，协调者依赖本地评估指标的聚合来评估全局模型。现有聚合策略（如基于本地样本数的加权平均）与集中式评估结果不一致，现有定义主要针对准确率且难以推广到其他指标，导致评估偏差。

Method: 分析参与者评估与集中式评估差异的根本原因，提出FLAM方法，基于可聚合指标（aggregatable measures）设计性能评估方案，无需全局测试数据集即可获得与集中式评估相同的结果。

Result: FLAM方法能够在联邦学习环境下实现与集中式评估一致的性能评估结果，解决了现有聚合策略的偏差问题，并适用于多种评估指标。

Conclusion: FLAM为联邦学习提供了可靠、一致的性能评估框架，消除了参与者评估与集中式评估之间的差异，提升了联邦学习模型部署决策的可信度。

Abstract: Performance evaluation is essential for assessing the quality of machine learning (ML) models and guiding deployment decisions. In federated learning (FL), assessing the performance is challenging because data are distributed across participants. Consequently, the coordinator must rely on locally computed evaluation metrics and aggregate them to assess the global model. A key challenge is that common aggregation strategies, such as weighted averaging based on the local samples per participant, do not always produce the same results as centralized evaluation. Existing definitions of performance evaluation are largely tailored to accuracy and do not generalize to other metrics, leading to inconsistencies between participant-based and centralized evaluation. However, such discrepancies are inconsistent with the FL objective and lead to a wrong calculation of the metric.
  To address this issue, we examine the underlying reasons for these discrepancies and propose FLAM, a performance evaluation method based on aggregatable measures that yields the same results as centralized evaluation without the need for a global test dataset.

</details>


### [111] [On Training in Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06732)
*Nadav Timor,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Micah Goldblum,Yann LeCun,David Harel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于模型的强化学习中“在想象轨迹上训练策略”的范式进行了理论分析：推导了在动力学和奖励模型存在误差时的最优样本分配比例，并揭示了REINFORCE算法对零均值奖励噪声的鲁棒性，将“使用廉价噪声奖励增加轨迹数”与“使用昂贵精确奖励减少轨迹数”的权衡简化为一维优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最先进的基于模型的强化学习方法在想象的轨迹（由学习的动力学模型生成并由学习的奖励模型评分）上训练策略，而无需在策略更新时查询真实环境。本文旨在量化学习的动力学和奖励模型中的误差如何影响回报和策略优化，为该训练范式提供理论基础与实践指导。

Method: 1. 将 Asadi et al. (2018) 的分析扩展到包含学习奖励模型的MDP，在幂律缩放假设下推导最优样本分配比例（动力学样本与奖励样本之比），以最小化回报误差上界，并指出动力学、奖励和策略的下Lipschitz常数作为表示要求可收紧该界限，将其与时间拉直目标联系起来。
2. 分析REINFORCE策略优化对奖励噪声的容忍度，证明零均值奖励噪声保持梯度估计无偏且仅增加方差项（随轨迹数增加而减小）。基于此，将固定预算下“更多噪声奖励轨迹”与“更少精确奖励轨迹”的权衡简化为一维优化问题并刻画最优解。

Result: 1. 推导了最小化回报误差上界的最优样本分配比例，揭示了动力学、奖励和策略的下Lipschitz常数对收紧界限的重要性。
2. 证明了使用REINFORCE时，零均值奖励噪声不引入梯度偏差，仅增加方差。
3. 将噪声奖励下的预算分配问题形式化为一维优化问题并给出了最优解的刻画。

Conclusion: 本文为基于模型的强化学习中动力学与奖励模型的样本分配提供了理论依据，并展示了策略优化对奖励噪声的鲁棒性，为在有限预算下权衡样本数量与奖励质量提供了可操作的优化框架。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods train policies on imagined rollouts. These rollouts are trajectories generated by a learned dynamics model and are scored by a learned reward model, but without querying the true environment during policy updates. We study this training paradigm by quantifying how errors in learned dynamics and reward models affect returns and policy optimization. First, we extend the analysis of Asadi et al. (2018) to MDPs with learned reward models, and derive the optimal sample allocation--the ratio of dynamics samples to reward samples that minimizes a bound on return error under power-law scaling assumptions. We identify lower Lipschitz constants of the learned dynamics, reward, and policy as a representation desideratum that tightens this bound, and we connect this perspective to the temporal-straightening objective of Wang et al. (2026). Second, we examine how policy optimization with REINFORCE tolerates noisy rewards, which are often cheaper to obtain. We show that zero-mean reward noise leaves the gradient estimator unbiased and adds at most a variance term that decreases with the number of rollouts. This introduces a practical tradeoff: given a fixed budget, should one buy more rollouts with cheaper but noisier rewards, or fewer rollouts with more expensive but less noisy rewards? We reduce this choice to a one-dimensional optimization problem and characterize the optimum.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Factor Aggregation: Gauge-Aware Low-Rank Server Representations for Federated LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06733)
*Jinqian Chen,Chang Liu,Jihua Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GLoRA，一种针对联邦LoRA的规范感知服务器表示方法，通过在共享参考坐标系中聚合客户端更新解决了现有方法中因子级聚合的语义不匹配问题，在GLUE和SuperNI数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA方法直接平均LoRA因子存在表示依赖性问题：相同的内在更新允许无限多个规范等价分解，导致因子级聚合在任意坐标选择下会发生变化，而底层更新保持不变，这揭示了现有联邦LoRA聚合规则中的语义不匹配问题。

Method: 提出GLoRA（Gauge-aware LoRA），不直接聚合原始因子，而是从客户端投影器估计共识更新子空间，在共享参考坐标系中聚合客户端更新，以低秩形式完全表示语义更新聚合。为支持异构客户端能力，GLoRA提供了一种秩兼容的读出机制，可从相同服务器状态实例化不同秩的适配器，无需密集更新重构。

Result: 在GLUE和SuperNI数据集上的实验表明，GLoRA在数据、资源和任务异构性条件下（包括异构客户端秩、稀疏参与、更大骨干网络和未见任务评估）始终优于联邦LoRA基线方法。GLoRA还实现了良好的效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 有效的联邦LoRA不仅需要平均低秩因子，更需要定义语义上有意义的服务器端表示进行聚合。GLoRA通过规范感知的服务器表示成功解决了语义不匹配问题，为联邦学习中的参数高效适应提供了新思路。

Abstract: Federated LoRA enables parameter-efficient adaptation of large language models under decentralized data and limited client resources.However, directly averaging LoRA factors is representation-dependent: the same intrinsic update admits infinitely many gauge-equivalent factorizations, so factor-level aggregation can change under arbitrary coordinate choices while the underlying update remains unchanged. This reveals a semantic mismatch in existing federated LoRA aggregation rules. We propose \textbf{GLoRA}, a gauge-aware server representation for federated LoRA.Instead of aggregating raw factors, GLoRA estimates a consensus update subspace from client projectors and aggregates client updates in shared reference coordinates, thereby representing semantic update aggregation entirely in low-rank form. To support heterogeneous client capacities, GLoRA further provides a rank-compatible readout that instantiates adapters of different ranks from the same server state without dense update reconstruction. Experiments on GLUE and SuperNI show that GLoRA consistently outperforms federated LoRA baselines under data, resource, and task heterogeneity, including heterogeneous client ranks, sparse participation, larger backbones, and unseen-task evaluation. GLoRA also achieves a favorable efficiency--performance trade-off, suggesting that effective federated LoRA requires not merely averaging low-rank factors, but defining a semantically meaningful server-side representation for aggregation.

</details>


### [113] [Gated QKAN-FWP: Scalable Quantum-inspired Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06734)
*Kuo-Chung Peng,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chen-Yu Liu,En-Jui Kuo,Yun-Yuan Wang,Prayag Tiwari,Andrea Ceschini,Chi-Sheng Chen,Yu-Chao Hsu,Chun-Hua Lin,Tai-Yue Li,Antonello Rosato,Massimo Panella,Simon See,Saif Al-Kuwari,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Nan-Yow Chen,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了门控QKAN-FWP框架，一种结合快速权重编程器与量子启发式KAN的单量子比特方法。在太阳周期预测任务中，仅用12.5k参数便击败了参数量多达13倍的LSTM等经典循环网络基线，并在真实量子硬件上实现了与无噪声模拟器仅相差0.1%相对MSE的性能，证明了其可扩展性、参数高效性和NISQ兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子快速权重编程器（QFWPs）依赖多量子比特架构，在含噪中等规模量子（NISQ）设备上难以扩展，且经典模拟成本高昂。研究旨在解决QFWPs的可扩展性和参数效率问题，同时保持对时序依赖关系的强大建模能力。

Method: 提出门控QKAN-FWP框架，将FWP与量子启发式科尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（QKAN）集成。核心包括：(1) 使用单量子比特数据重上传电路（DARUAN）作为可学习的非线性激活函数，避免多量子比特架构的扩展难题；(2) 引入标量门控快速权重更新规则，以稳定参数演化，并提供了关于自适应记忆核、几何有界性和可并行化梯度路径的理论分析。

Result: 在时间序列基准测试、MiniGrid强化学习和太阳周期预测任务上验证了框架有效性。主要成果：(1) 在长视界太阳周期预测（输入窗口528个月，预测视界132个月）中，12.5k参数模型在缩放MSE、峰值幅度误差和峰值时间误差上均优于LSTM（25.9k-89.1k参数）、WaveNet-LSTM（167k）等参数量高达13倍的基线模型；(2) 在IonQ和IBM Quantum处理器上部署模型，在1024次采样下，预测准确度与无噪声模拟器相比仅相差0.1%的相对MSE，验证了NISQ兼容性。

Conclusion: 门控QKAN-FWP成功解决了现有QFWPs的扩展难题，通过单量子比特架构实现了参数效率和预测精度的双重提升。该框架在经典模拟和真实量子硬件上均表现出色，为量子启发式序列建模提供了一种可扩展、参数高效且NISQ兼容的新途径。

Abstract: Fast Weight Programmers (FWPs) encode temporal dependencies through dynamically updated parameters rather than recurrent hidden states. Quantum FWPs (QFWPs) extend this idea with variational quantum circuits (VQCs), but existing implementations rely on multi-qubit architectures that are difficult to scale on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices and expensive to simulate classically. We propose gated QKAN-FWP, a fast-weight framework that integrates FWP with Quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (QKAN) using single-qubit data re-uploading circuits as learnable nonlinear activation, known as DatA Re-Uploading ActivatioN (DARUAN). We further introduce a scalar-gated fast-weight update rule that stabilizes parameter evolution, supported by a theoretical analysis of its adaptive memory kernel, geometric boundedness, and parallelizable gradient paths. We evaluate the framework across time-series benchmarks, MiniGrid reinforcement learning, and highlight real-world solar cycle forecasting as our main practical result. In the long-horizon setting with 528-month input window and 132-month forecast horizon, our 12.5k-parameter model achieves lower scaled Mean Square Error (MSE), peak amplitude error, and peak timing error than a suite of classical recurrent baselines with up to 13x more parameters, including Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks (25.9k-89.1k parameters), WaveNet-LSTM (167k), Vanilla recurrent neural network (11.5k), and a Modified Echo State Network (132k). To validate NISQ compatibility, we further deploy the trained fast programmer on IonQ and IBM Quantum processors, recovering forecasting accuracy within 0.1% relative MSE of the noiseless simulator at 1024 shots. These results position gated QKAN-FWP as a scalable, parameter-efficient, and NISQ-compatible approach to quantum-inspired sequence modeling.

</details>


### [114] [STDA-Net: Spectrogram-Based Domain Adaptation for cross-dataset Sleep Stage Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06736)
*Unaza Tallal,Shruti Kshirsagar,Ankita Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STDA-Net框架，首次将二维时频谱表征与对抗性域自适应结合用于跨数据集睡眠分期，平均准确率达89.03%，优于传统一维方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨数据集睡眠分期方法主要依赖一维EEG信号，忽略了二维时频谱表征在无监督域自适应框架下的潜力，且面临不同数据集间通道配置、采样率、环境等差异导致的域偏移挑战。

Method: 提出STDA-Net框架：1) 利用CNN提取二维时频谱特征；2) 使用双向LSTM建模睡眠阶段的时间动态特性；3) 引入域对抗神经网络(DANN)实现源域到目标域的特征对齐，无需目标域标签。

Result: 在Sleep-EDF、SHHS-1和SHHS-2三个公开数据集的六种跨数据集迁移设置下，平均准确率达89.03%，宏平均F1分数达87.64%，性能稳定且方差显著低于一维基线方法。

Conclusion: 二维时频谱表征结合时序建模与对抗域自适应，为跨数据集睡眠分期提供了比传统一维EEG输入更鲁棒、更具竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate sleep stage classification across datasets remains challenging due to variability in EEG channel montages, sampling rates, recording environments, and subject populations. Although deep learning has shown considerable promise for automated sleep staging, most existing cross-dataset methods rely on one-dimensional EEG signal representations, whereas the use of two-dimensional spectrogram-based inputs within an unsupervised domain adaptation framework has remained largely unexplored. Here, we propose STDA-Net (Spectrogram-based Temporal Domain Adaptation Network), a framework that combines a convolutional neural network (CNN) for spectrogram-based feature extraction, a bidirectional long short-term memory (BiLSTM) module for temporal modeling of sleep dynamics, and a domain-adversarial neural network (DANN) for source-to-target feature alignment without requiring any labeled target-domain data during training. Experiments are conducted on three publicly available datasets Sleep-EDF, SHHS-1, and SHHS-2 under six cross-dataset transfer settings. Results show that the proposed framework achieves an average accuracy of 89.03% and an average macro F1-score of 87.64%, consistently outperforming existing 1D baseline methods in terms of balanced classification performance, with substantially lower variance across five independent runs, indicating improved stability and reproducibility. Overall, these findings demonstrate that 2D spectrogram-based representations, combined with temporal modeling and adversarial domain adaptation, provide a robust and competitive alternative to conventional 1D EEG inputs for cross-dataset sleep staging.

</details>


### [115] [Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Network (GeoKAN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06740)
*Abhijit Sen,Bikram Keshari Parida,Giridas Maiti,Mahima Arya,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了几何感知的 Kolmogorov--Arnold 网络（GeoKANs），通过学习对角黎曼度量来扭曲输入坐标，从而在几何适应的坐标中进行逼近，实现了在变化剧烈区域拉伸、平滑区域压缩的自适应表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 KAN 模型在固定的欧几里得输入坐标中进行逼近，缺乏对输入空间几何结构的适应能力。在科学机器学习和微分方程问题中，常面临尖锐、刚性强、局部化和高度非均匀的机制，固定坐标系难以高效分配表示容量。因此，作者希望引入几何归纳偏置，使模型能够根据任务需求自适应地重新分配表示分辨率。

Method: GeoKAN 通过学习一个对角黎曼度量来扭曲输入空间，在基展开和特征混合之前对输入进行几何变换。该度量通过局部长度缩放和体积扭曲提供几何归纳偏置，在物理信息设置中还会影响模型所感知的微分结构。作者开发了三个主要变体：GeoKAN-NNMetric、GeoKAN-γ 和 LM-KAN，其中 LM-KAN 进一步分为三个基函数特定版本（RBF、小波、傅里叶），用于研究几何感知 KAN 模型作为通用函数逼近器和物理信息学习代理的性能。

Result: GeoKAN 能够通过拉伸变化剧烈的区域、压缩平滑区域，以任务相关的方式重新分配表示分辨率，将模型容量投放到最需要的地方。这使得模型特别适合科学机器学习和微分方程问题中出现的尖锐、刚性强、局部化和强非均匀情况。

Conclusion: GeoKAN 成功地将几何感知引入 KAN 架构，通过学习的黎曼度量实现了自适应的表示分辨率分配，为科学机器学习和微分方程问题提供了一种有效的几何感知深度学习框架。

Abstract: We introduce Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (GeoKANs), a family of geometry-aware KAN-type models in which approximation is carried out in learned, geometry-adapted coordinates rather than in fixed Euclidean input coordinates. GeoKAN achieves this by learning a diagonal Riemannian metric that warps the input before basis expansion and feature mixing. The learned metric provides a geometric inductive bias through local length scaling and volume distortion, and in physics-informed settings it also affects the differential structure seen by the model. Within this framework, we develop three main variants, namely GeoKAN-NNMetric, GeoKAN-$γ$, and LM-KAN. For LM-KAN, we further consider three basis-specific versions, LM-KAN-RBF, LM-KAN-Wav, and LM-KAN-Fourier. These variants allow us to study geometry-aware KAN models both as general function approximators and as surrogates in physics-informed learning. By stretching regions with rapid variation and compressing smoother regions, GeoKAN reallocates representational resolution in a task-dependent manner, allowing the model to place capacity where it is most needed. As a result, GeoKAN is well suited to sharp, stiff, localized, and strongly non-uniform regimes arising in scientific machine learning and differential-equation problems.

</details>


### [116] [A Closed-Form Upper Bound for Admissible Learning-Rate Steps in Belief-Space Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06741)
*Zixi Li,Youzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将学习率步长从超参数转化为可计算的局部信念空间公式，通过KL/Bregman几何证明了可接受步长的上界具有解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将学习率步长视为需要调优的超参数，缺乏理论指导。本文旨在建立理论基础，通过局部信念空间计算为步长选择提供可计算的准则。

Method: 将参数更新建模为概率单纯形上的投影前向步，利用KL散度/Bregman几何的自然度量，推导可接受步长的解析上界公式。

Result: 证明了可接受步长的上界可以通过公式直接计算获得，而非依赖经验调优，该公式基于收缩性条件在自然几何度量下的要求。

Conclusion: 学习率步长选择可以转化为可计算的理论问题，为深度学习中的超参数调优提供了新的理论框架和实用指导。

Abstract: Learning-rate steps are usually treated as hyperparameters. This paper isolates a local beliefspace calculation: when an update is modeled as a projected forward step on the probability simplex, admissibility means contractivity in the natural KL/Bregman geometry. Under this model, the upper bound of an admissible step is not a tuning slogan but a formula.

</details>


### [117] [Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06755)
*Ismam Nur Swapnil,Aranya Saha,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Mohammad Ariful Haque,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GXPO方法，通过三次反向传播近似多步前瞻优化，在数学推理任务上显著提升GRPO性能，实现最高4倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO仅使用当前步更新模型，完整多步前瞻虽能提供更优更新方向但计算成本过高（需要大量反向传播）。因此需要在有限计算预算下近似多步前瞻，以改善策略更新方向。

Method: 提出梯度外推策略优化（GXPO）：一种GRPO风格的策略更新规则。在活跃阶段仅使用三次反向传播近似更长的局部前瞻：复用同一批rollouts、奖励和优势，执行两次快速优化器步，测量梯度变化预测虚拟K步前瞻点，将策略部分移向该点后应用校正更新。当前瞻信号不稳定时自动切回标准单遍GRPO。还提供plain-gradient-descent代理分析解释外推精确性和局部误差来源。

Result: 在Qwen2.5和Llama数学推理实验中，相比GRPO平均采样pass@1提升+1.65至+5.00分，相比最强SFPO设置提升+0.14至+1.28分。在保持活跃阶段三次反向传播成本不变下，达到GRPO峰值准确率时实现最高4.00倍步加速、2.33倍时钟加速和1.33倍反向传播加速。

Conclusion: GXPO成功在有限计算预算下实现了多步前瞻的近似，显著提升了强化学习推理模型的性能和训练效率，为大语言模型的推理能力提升提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is widely used to improve the reasoning ability of large language models, especially when answers can be automatically checked. Standard GRPO-style training updates the model using only the current step, while full multi-step lookahead can give a better update direction but is too expensive because it needs many backward passes. We propose Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization (GXPO), a plug-compatible policy-update rule for GRPO-style reasoning RL. GXPO approximates a longer local lookahead using only three backward passes during an active phase. It reuses the same batch of rollouts, rewards, advantages, and GRPO loss, so it does not require new rollouts or reward computation at the lookahead points. GXPO takes two fast optimizer steps, measures how the gradients change, predicts a virtual K-step lookahead point, moves the policy partway toward that point, and then applies a corrective update using the true gradient at the new position. When the lookahead signal becomes unstable, GXPO automatically switches back to standard single-pass GRPO. We also give a plain-gradient-descent surrogate analysis that explains when the extrapolation is exact and where its local errors come from. Across Qwen2.5 and Llama math-reasoning experiments, GXPO improves the average sampled pass@1 by +1.65 to +5.00 points over GRPO and by +0.14 to +1.28 points over the strongest SFPO setting, while keeping the active-phase cost fixed at three backward passes. It also achieves up to 4.00x step speedup, 2.33x wall-clock speedup, and 1.33x backward-pass speedup in reaching GRPO's peak accuracy.

</details>


### [118] [Physics-based Digital Twins for Integrated Thermal Energy Systems Using Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06756)
*Umme Mahbuba Nabila,Paul Seurin,Linyu Lin,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种主动学习框架，通过结合Modelica仿真与四种代理模型（SINDyC、MvG-SINDyC、FNN、GRU），在热能分配系统的数字孪生建模中仅需随机采样五分之一的仿真轨迹即可达到相当的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热能分配系统的实时监控需要既准确、可解释、具备不确定性感知能力，又保持数据和计算效率的数字孪生模型。高保真仿真成本昂贵，而纯数据驱动的代理模型又缺乏鲁棒性，因此需要一种兼顾效率与准确性的解决方案。

Method: 提出一个主动学习框架，将系统级Modelica仿真与四种代理模型相结合：确定性SINDyC、概率性MvG-SINDyC、前馈神经网络（FNN）和门控循环单元（GRU）。针对每种代理模型设计了特定的主动学习查询策略，包括MvG-SINDyC的系数空间马氏距离采样，以及SINDyC、FNN和GRU的预测空间误差采样，使学习过程优先处理动态信息丰富的轨迹。

Result: 在爱达荷国家实验室热能分配系统的乙二醇换热器子系统中进行验证。关键输出（旁路质量流量和传热速率）的预测结果显示，主动学习框架仅需随机采样五分之一的仿真轨迹即可达到相当的预测精度。GRU网络具有最高的预测保真度，SINDyC在计算效率和可解释性方面表现最佳，概率性MvG-SINDyC代理模型能够实现不确定性量化，并在主动学习下展现出最大的计算增益。

Conclusion: 该主动学习框架成功实现了热能分配系统数字孪生的高效准确建模，平衡了不同代理模型的优势，在保证预测精度的同时显著降低了计算成本，为实时监控提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time supervisory control of thermal energy distribution systems requires digital twins that are accurate, interpretable, and uncertainty-aware, yet remain data and computationally efficient. High-fidelity simulations alone are costly, while purely data-driven surrogates often lack robustness. To address these challenges, this work proposes an active learning (AL) framework that couples system-level Modelica simulations with four simpler physics-informed and data-driven surrogate modeling approaches: deterministic Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics with Control (SINDyC), its probabilistic multivariate-Gaussian extension (MvG-SINDyC), feedforward neural network (FNN), and gated recurrent unit (GRU) network. Tailored to each surrogate, model-specific AL query strategies are employed, including Mahalanobis-distance sampling in coefficient space for MvG-SINDyC and error-based sampling in prediction space for SINDyC, FNN, and GRU, allowing the learning process to prioritize dynamically informative trajectories.
  The proposed approach is demonstrated on the glycol heat exchanger (GHX) subsystem of the Thermal Energy Distribution System (TEDS) at Idaho National Laboratory. Across key GHX outputs--the bypass mass flow rate $\dot{m}_{\mathrm{GHX}}$ and heat transfer rate $Q_{\mathrm{GHX}}$-the AL framework achieves comparable predictive accuracy using as few as one-fifth of the simulation trajectories required by random sampling. Among the evaluated surrogates, the GRU achieves the highest predictive fidelity, while SINDyC remains the most computationally efficient and interpretable. The probabilistic MvG-SINDyC surrogate further enables uncertainty quantification and exhibits the largest computational gains under AL.

</details>


### [119] [Sparse Attention as a Range Searching Problem: Towards an Inference-Efficient Index for KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06763)
*Mohsen Dehghankar,Abolfazl Asudeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Louver，一种新型的索引结构，将稀疏注意力重构为半空间范围搜索问题，在保证零假阴性（理论召回率保证）的前提下，实现了比FlashAttention更快的推理速度和更高的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法通过选择部分KV条目来提高效率，但可能遗漏关键KV条目导致准确率显著下降。作者观察到在长推理任务中，遗漏单个关键键值就会导致错误激增，而重要token集合在解码过程中随查询和步骤动态变化，现有方法无法保证零假阴性。

Method: 将稀疏注意力问题重构为半空间范围搜索问题，提出Louver索引结构。该结构针对现代LLM推理优化，具有三个特点：（i）理论上和实践中均保证相对于指定阈值的零假阴性；（ii）轻量级，易于集成到现有LLM流程；（iii）针对CPU和GPU执行进行了硬件感知优化。

Result: 实验表明Louver在准确性和运行时间上均优于现有稀疏注意力方法，且比高度优化的密集注意力方法（如FlashAttention）更快。验证了召回率保证是稀疏注意力中关键但被忽视的维度。

Conclusion: 召回率保证是稀疏注意力设计中关键但被忽视的维度，为构建理论上可靠、高效的KV缓存索引开辟了新方向。Louver证明了在保证理论召回率的前提下，仍能实现超越密集注意力的推理效率。

Abstract: Sparse attention improves LLM inference efficiency by selecting a subset of key-value entries, but at the cost of potential accuracy degradation. In particular, omitting critical KV entries can induce substantial errors in model outputs. Existing methods typically operate under fixed or adaptive token budgets and provide empirical robustness or partial theoretical guarantees, yet they do not ensure zero false negatives in decoding steps, particularly since the set of relevant tokens is both query- and step-dependent. Our empirical observations confirm that missing even one critical key can lead to sharp error spikes, especially in long reasoning tasks where the set of important tokens varies throughout decoding. This observation motivates the need for indexing methods that dynamically adapt to these variations across decoding steps while guaranteeing a full recall of the relevant keys above a certain threshold. We address this challenge by reformulating sparse attention as the halfspace range searching problem. However, existing range searching indices are not suitable for modern LLM inference due to their computational and implementation overheads. To overcome this, we introduce Louver, a novel index structure tailored for efficient KV cache retrieval. Louver (i) guarantees zero false negatives with respect to a specified threshold in both theory and practice, (ii) is lightweight to integrate into existing LLM pipelines, and (iii) incorporates hardware-aware optimizations for both CPU and GPU executions. Our experiments demonstrate that Louver outperforms prior sparse attention methods in both accuracy and runtime, and is faster than highly optimized dense attentions such as FlashAttention. These results highlight that recall guarantees are a critical and overlooked dimension of sparse attention, and open a new direction for building theoretically grounded, efficient KV cache indices.

</details>


### [120] [Revisiting Adam for Streaming Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06764)
*Florin Gogianu,Adrian Catalin Lutu,Razvan Pascanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在线强化学习中稳健性能的两个关键特性（目标导数有界和权重更新方差调整），证明C51在在线设置下表现优异，并提出了新算法Adaptive Q(λ)在Atari游戏中达到人类基线的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习依赖经验回放缓冲区或并行采样来维持稳定性，但这增加了算法复杂度。Elsayed等人(2024)提出的StreamQ算法重新探索了在线学习方向，本文旨在深入研究已确立的更新方法（如DQN和C51）在在线设置中的有效性，理解其成功背后的机制。

Method: 系统分析优化算法（特别是Adam）与在线更新的交互机制，识别出稳健性能的两个关键特性：目标函数导数有界和权重更新经过方差调整。基于这些洞察，推导出一个基于资格迹的方差调整算法Adaptive Q(λ)，在55个Atari游戏子集上进行严格全面的实验验证。

Result: C51算法天然具备两个关键特性，在在线设置下与StreamQ性能相当；新提出的Adaptive Q(λ)算法在同一Atari游戏子集上达到人类基线性能的两倍，在所有性能指标上超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 目标导数有界和方差调整是在线强化学习实现稳健性能的关键要素，C51的成功并非偶然而是源于其算法设计满足这些特性；Adaptive Q(λ)的优越性能验证了理论分析的正确性，为简化、高效的在线强化学习算法设计提供了明确指导。

Abstract: Learning from a sequence of interactions, as soon as observations are perceived and acted upon, without explicitly storing them, holds the promise of simpler, more efficient and adaptive algorithms. For over a decade, however, deep reinforcement learning walked the contrary path, augmenting agents with replay buffers or parallel sampling routines, in an effort to tame learning instability. Recently, this topic has been revisited by Elsayed et al. (2024), focusing on update computation through eligibility traces and modifications to the optimisation routine, resulting in the StreamQ algorithm. In this work we take a step back, investigating the efficacy of established updates, such as those implemented by DQN and C51 within this online setting. Not only do we find that they perform well, but through analysing how the optimisation algorithm generally, and Adam in particular, interacts with these updates, we contend that two properties are essential for robust performance: i) the derivative of the objective is to be bounded and ii) weight updates are variance-adjusted. Rigorous and exhaustive experimentation demonstrates that C51, which exhibits both characteristics, is competitive with StreamQ across a subset of 55 Atari games. Using these insights, we derive a variance-adjusted algorithm based on eligibility traces, termed Adaptive Q$(λ)$, which approaches double the human baseline on the same subset, surpassing existing methods by all performance metrics.

</details>


### [121] [Distributional Process Reward Models: Calibrated Prediction of Future Rewards via Conditional Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06785)
*Rachel Ma,Dylan Hadfield-Menell,Kristjan Greenewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出利用条件最优传输（Conditional OT）校准过程奖励模型（PRM），通过学习单调条件分位数函数改善概率估计的校准性能，并在数学推理基准测试中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展方法依赖过程奖励模型（PRMs），但这些模型通常校准不佳，会高估成功概率，导致不确定性估计不可靠。

Method: 提出一种基于条件最优传输的校准方法，改进CondOT映射学习以估计基于PRM隐藏状态的单调条件分位数函数，从而获得结构有效的分位数估计，并支持任意置信水平的置信区间提取，将其集成到实例自适应扩展（IAS）框架中。

Result: 在中等难度的MATH-500和更具挑战性的分布外AIME数学推理基准上评估，对于具有可靠排序信号的PRM，该方法在校准性能上显著优于未校准PRM和分位数回归；在下游Best-of-N IAS任务中，性能普遍优于未校准PRM。

Conclusion: 条件最优传输是一种原理性强且实用的PRM校准方法，能够提供结构性保证和灵活的不确定性估计，为PRM校准领域提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling methods rely on Process Reward Models (PRMs), which are often poorly calibrated and overestimate success probabilities. We propose, to our knowledge, the first use of conditional optimal transport for calibrating PRMs, modifying conditional OT (CondOT) map learning \cite{bunne2022supervised} to estimate a monotonic conditional quantile function over success probabilities estimated by the PRM, conditioned on PRM hidden states. This yields structurally valid quantile estimates and enables efficient extraction of confidence bounds at arbitrary levels, which we integrate into the instance-adaptive scaling (IAS) framework of \cite{park2025know}. We evaluate on mathematical reasoning benchmarks spanning moderate-difficulty problems (MATH-500) and harder out-of-distribution problems (AIME). For PRMs with reliable ranking signals, our method substantially improves calibration over both uncalibrated PRMs and quantile regression. On downstream Best-of-N IAS performance, our method generally improves over uncalibrated PRMs. These results establish conditional optimal transport as another principled and practical approach to PRM calibration, offering structural guarantees and flexible uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [122] [MIND: Monge Inception Distance for Generative Models Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06797)
*Quentin Berthet,Yu-Han Wu,Clement Crepy,Romuald Elie,Klaus Greff,Michael Eli Sander*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MIND指标替代FID，通过切片Wasserstein距离实现更高的样本效率（提升一个数量级）、更快的计算速度（提升两个数量级）及更强的对抗鲁棒性，仅需5k样本即可匹敌FID 50k样本的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广泛采用的FID指标存在明显的局限性，包括由于需要估计高维均值和协方差矩阵而导致的样本复杂性差，以及对对抗性攻击（如矩匹配攻击）的脆弱性。

Method: 提出Monge Inception Distance (MIND)，利用切片Wasserstein距离比较分布。该方法通过平均一维最优传输距离来工作，计算过程仅需排序操作，从而规避了对高维均值和协方差矩阵的估计需求。

Result: 实验表明MIND具有三个主要优势：（i）样本效率提高一个数量级；（ii）计算速度快两个数量级；（iii）对对抗性攻击更具鲁棒性。MIND仅需5k样本即可替代FID 50k样本的评估性能，且与标准基准具有高相关性，判别性能更优。即便是1k或2k的小样本规模，仍能提供高信息量以支持快速模型迭代。

Conclusion: MIND作为一种新的生成模型评估指标，成功解决了FID的关键缺陷，在效率、速度和鲁棒性上均有显著提升，为生成模型的评估和快速迭代提供了更优的工具。

Abstract: We propose the Monge Inception Distance (MIND), a metric for evaluating generative models that addresses key limitations of the widely adopted Fréchet Inception Distance (FID). The MIND metric leverages the sliced Wasserstein distance to compare distributions by averaging one-dimensional optimal transport distances, efficiently computed via sorting. This approach circumvents the estimation of high-dimensional means and covariance matrices, which underlie FID's poor sample complexity and vulnerability to adversarial attacks. We empirically demonstrate three primary advantages: (i) it is more sample-efficient by one order of magnitude, (ii) it is faster to compute by two orders of magnitude, (iii) it is more robust to adversarial attacks such as moment-matching. We show that MIND with 5k samples can replace the evaluation performance of FID with 50k samples, providing high correlation with this standard benchmark and superior discriminative performance. We further demonstrate that even smaller sample sizes (e.g., 1k or 2k) remain highly informative for rapid model iteration.

</details>


### [123] [From Model to Data (M2D): Shifting Complexity from GNNs to Graphs for Transparent Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06814)
*Debolina Halder Lina,Arlei Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模型到数据(M2D)蒸馏框架，通过将模型复杂度转化为数据空间（增强图特征与结构），使简单学生模型达到复杂教师模型的性能，从而提供GNN架构的透明度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络(GNN)可解释性方法仅关注对单个预测进行归因，无法提供架构层面的透明度，也难以解释简单模型与复杂模型之间的性能差距。

Method: 提出Model-to-Data (M2D)蒸馏框架，将教师模型的复杂度转移到数据空间中，具体通过将模型蒸馏为具有丰富特征和结构的增强图，使简单学生模型能够在增强数据上匹配教师模型的性能，从而将模型行为具象化到数据中以便直接检验。

Result: M2D能够以可解释的方式揭示GNN的底层机制，如公平性目标和基于注意力的聚合机制；在提升GNN透明度的同时保持了模型性能。

Conclusion: M2D框架成功实现了架构透明度与性能的平衡，通过将模型行为物化到数据空间，使人类能够直接检验架构优势，为理解与比较GNN架构提供了新途径。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve high performance but can be opaque to humans, making it difficult to understand and compare the many proposed architectures. While existing explainability methods attribute individual predictions to nodes, edges, or features, they do not provide architectural transparency or explain the fundamental performance gap between simple and more complex models. To address this limitation, we introduce Model-to-Data (M2D) distillation, a new framework that increases transparency by transferring model complexity into the data space. M2D distills the teacher model into an augmented graph with enriched features and structure, enabling a simple student to match the teacher's performance. By materializing model behavior in the data, our approach allows humans to inspect architectural advantages directly. We show that M2D reveals underlying mechanisms such as fairness objectives and attention-based aggregation in an interpretable way, enhancing GNN transparency while preserving performance.

</details>


### [124] [A Theory of Online Learning with Autoregressive Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06819)
*Ilan Doron-Arad,Idan Mehalel,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自回归生成的在线学习框架，区分了端到端模型和思维链模型两种反馈形式，发现前者错误界限随生成长度M呈对数增长，后者可完全消除对M的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的自回归生成机制可以通过重复应用下一token生成器来理解。[Joshi et al., 2025]提出了一个PAC模型来研究由此过程产生的输入-输出映射的可学习性。本文旨在开发这一框架的在线类比版本，重点关注学习由未知下一token生成器诱导的最终输出时的错误界限。

Method: 区分两种反馈形式：端到端模型（每轮后学习者只能观察到M步自回归后的最终token）和思维链模型（学习者额外能看到整个M步轨迹）。目标是理解最优错误界限如何依赖于生成长度M，以及观察中间token能在多大程度上减少这种依赖。同时分析自回归线性阈值类，证明最优错误界限。

Result: 在端到端模型中，证明了关于生成长度M的错误界限增长率的分类：基本上常数到对数之间的任何速率都可能出现，且这个对数上限是不可避免的。在思维链模型中，访问完整生成轨迹可以完全消除对M的依赖。对于自回归线性阈值类，证明了最优错误界限以及统计设置的新下界。

Conclusion: 自回归学习的在线理论呈现出与[Hanneke et al., 2026]发现的统计理论类似的定性图景，但对生成长度的依赖尺度不同。思维链反馈能显著改善学习性能。研究结果解决了[Joshi et al., 2025]留下的若干开放问题。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation lies at the heart of the mechanism of large language models. It can be viewed as the repeated application of a next-token generator: starting from an input string (prompt), the generator is applied for $M$ steps, and the last generated token is taken as the final output. [Joshi et al., 2025] proposed a PAC model for studying the learnability of the input-output maps arising from this process. We develop an online analogue of this framework, focusing on the mistake bound of learning the final output induced by an unknown next-token generator.
  We distinguish between two forms of feedback. In the End-to-End model, after each round the learner observes only the final token produced after $M$ autoregressive steps. In the Chain-of-Thought model, the learner is additionally shown the entire $M$-step trajectory. Our goal is to understand how the optimal mistake bound depends on the generation horizon $M$, and to what extent observing intermediate tokens can reduce this dependence.
  Our main results show that the online theory of autoregressive learning exhibits a qualitative picture analogous to the statistical one found by [Hanneke et al., 2026], but with a different scale of dependence on the generation horizon. In the End-to-End model, we prove a taxonomy of possible mistake-bound growth rates in the generation horizon $M$: essentially any rate between constant and logarithmic can arise. We further show that this logarithmic ceiling is unavoidable. In the Chain-of-Thought model, we show that access to the full generated trajectory eliminates the dependence on $M$ altogether.
  We also analyze autoregressive linear threshold classes, and prove optimal mistake bounds, as well as a new lower bound for the statistical setting. Along the way, our results resolve several questions left open by [Joshi et al., 2025].

</details>


### [125] [A Rod Flow Model for Adam at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06821)
*Eric Regis,Sinho Chewi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Rod Flow方法从梯度下降扩展到了八种动量和自适应优化器（包括Adam），在边缘稳定性区域更准确地建模离散迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cohen等人观察到Adam等自适应梯度方法在“边缘稳定性”状态下运行，虽然已有针对梯度下降的连续时间建模方法，但针对动量方法的建模尚不完善。Regis等人提出了针对梯度下降的Rod Flow方法，本文旨在将其扩展到动量和自适应优化器。

Method: 将Rod Flow扩展到Adam，方法是在参数和一阶矩的联合相空间$(w, m)$中建模，并将二阶矩$ν$视为平滑辅助变量。此外，还为Heavy Ball动量、Nesterov动量以及RMSProp、Adam和NAdam的标量和分量化版本开发了Rod Flow。

Result: 在代表性的机器学习架构上对八种优化器进行了实证评估，结果显示Rod Flow在边缘稳定性区域跟踪离散迭代的准确性显著高于对应的Stable Flow方法。

Conclusion: Rod Flow方法成功扩展至多种动量和自适应优化器，能够更准确地描述优化器在边缘稳定性区域的行为，为理解此类优化算法的动力学机制提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Cohen et al. (arXiv:2207.14484) observed that adaptive gradient methods such as Adam operate at the edge of stability. While there has been significant work on continuous-time modeling of gradient descent at the edge of stability, extending these models to momentum methods remains underdeveloped. In the gradient descent setting, Regis et al. (arXiv:2602.01480) introduced rod flow, which models consecutive iterates as an extended one-dimensional object -- a "rod." Here we extend rod flow to Adam by working in the joint phase space of parameters and first moment $(w, m)$ and treating the second moment $ν$ as a smooth auxiliary variable. We also develop rod flows for heavy ball momentum, Nesterov momentum, and scalar and per-component versions of RMSProp, Adam, and NAdam. For all eight optimizers, we empirically evaluate rod flow on representative machine learning architectures, where it tracks the discrete iterates through the edge-of-stability regime significantly more accurately than the corresponding stable flow.

</details>


### [126] [SHARP: A Self-Evolving Human-Auditable Rubric Policy for Financial Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06822)
*Xiwen Chen,Wenhui Zhu,Songzhu Zheng,Kashif Rasul,Yueyue Deng,Huayu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SHARP框架，一种神经符号框架，通过结构化策略优化来解决大语言模型在金融交易中面临的问题，显著提升了模型性能和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主金融交易LLM智能体采用无界自由形式的提示优化方法，在低信噪比和延迟标量奖励（盈亏）环境下，无法可靠区分系统性逻辑缺陷与随机市场波动，加剧了信用分配问题，导致策略漂移。

Method: SHARP是一种神经符号框架，将无约束文本变异替换为结构化策略优化。它将智能体的推理限制在有界、人类可读的明确条件-行动规则评分表中。当次优交易发生时，归因智能体通过跨样本推理来隔离特定规则失败，实现针对性的原子策略编辑，并通过严格的前向验证进行正则化。

Result: 在三个不同的股票板块和四个LLM骨干网络上评估，SHARP将通用初始启发式策略一致地转换为高度鲁棒的策略，使紧凑模型的实证性能平均提升10-20个百分点（如GPT-4o-mini）。

Conclusion: SHARP证明了大语言模型可以在实现动态高效适应的同时，显著增强机构金融所要求的结构透明度和可审计性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for autonomous financial trading, a domain requiring continuous adaptation to noisy, non-stationary markets. Existing self-improving agents typically address this through unbounded free-form prompt optimization. However, in low signal-to-noise environments with delayed scalar rewards (P\&L), this unstructured approach exacerbates the fundamental credit assignment problem: optimizers cannot reliably distinguish systematic logic flaws from stochastic market variance, inevitably leading to policy drift. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce the Self-Evolving Human-Auditable Rubric Policy (SHARP), a neuro-symbolic framework that replaces unconstrained text mutation with structured, symbolic policy optimization. SHARP confines the agent's reasoning to a bounded, human-readable rubric of explicit condition-action rules. When sub-optimal trades occur, an attribution agent employs cross-sample reasoning across multiple samples to isolate specific rule failures. This enables targeted, atomic policy edits that are subsequently regularized through strict walk-forward validation. Evaluated across three diverse equity sectors and four LLM backbones, SHARP consistently transforms generic initial heuristics into highly robust strategies, lifting the empirical performance of compact models by 10 to 20 percentage points on average (e.g., GPT-4o-mini). Ultimately, SHARP demonstrates that LLMs can achieve dynamic and efficient adaptation while significantly enhancing the structural transparency and auditability demanded by institutional finance.

</details>


### [127] [A Unified Measure-Theoretic View of Diffusion, Score-Based, and Flow Matching Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06829)
*Aditya Ranganath,Mukesh Singhal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于连续时间动态的生成模型，在统一框架下整合了扩散模型、基于分数的生成模型和流匹配方法，阐明了它们共享的结构和实际权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型、基于分数的生成模型和流匹配等方法在方法论上正在趋同，但碎片化的符号表示和相互竞争的推导过程掩盖了它们的共同结构和影响采样、稳定性及计算的实际权衡，因此需要建立一个统一的理论框架。

Method: 提出了一个统一框架，将这些方法视为学习时变向量场，该向量场诱导一组由连续性方程和Fokker-Planck方程控制的边际分布族$(ρ_t)_{t \in [0,1]}$。在此框架下：(i) 将扩散和基于分数模型的逆时间采样推导为受控随机动态；(ii) 证明概率流ODE产生相同的边际分布，并将扩散与基于似然的归一化流联系起来；(iii) 将流匹配解释为在选定插值下速度场的直接回归。

Result: 在统一符号下比较了目标函数、采样方案和离散化误差，明确了流匹配何时与基于分数的训练一致或不同，建立了扩散与归一化流之间的联系，并讨论了与薛定谔桥和熵最优传输的关联。

Conclusion: 本文总结了相关理论保证，以及在逼近、稳定性和可扩展性方面的开放问题，为理解和使用连续时间生成模型提供了清晰的理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: We survey continuous-time generative modeling methods based on transporting a simple reference distribution to a data distribution via stochastic or deterministic dynamics. We present a unified framework in which diffusion models, score-based generative models, and flow matching are instances of learning a time-dependent vector field that induces a family of marginals $(ρ_t)_{t \in [0,1]}$ governed by continuity and Fokker-Planck equations. Such a unified theory is timely because these methods are converging methodologically, yet fragmented notation and competing derivations continue to obscure their shared structure and the practical tradeoffs governing sampling, stability, and computation. Within this framework, we (i) derive reverse-time sampling for diffusion and score-based models as controlled stochastic dynamics, (ii) show that the probability flow ODE yields identical marginals and connects diffusion to likelihood-based normalizing flows, and (iii) interpret flow matching as direct regression of the velocity field under a chosen interpolation, clarifying when it coincides with or differs from score-based training. We compare objectives, sampling schemes, and discretization errors under unified notation, discuss connections to Schrodinger bridges and entropic optimal transport, and summarize theoretical guarantees and open problems on approximation, stability, and scalability.

</details>


### [128] [ProtSent: Protein Sentence Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06830)
*Dan Ofer,Oriel Perets,Michal Linial,Nadav Rappoport*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Protein Sentence Transformers (ProtSent)，一个通过对比学习微调蛋白质语言模型的框架，使其序列嵌入能更好反映蛋白质的功能和结构相似性，在远程同源性检测、变异效应预测等任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的蛋白质语言模型(pLMs)虽然能生成捕捉进化和结构信息的残基级表示，但其平均池化的序列嵌入并未被显式训练来反映蛋白质间的功能、进化或结构相似性，限制了其在蛋白质相似性检索等任务中的效果。

Method: 提出ProtSent框架，使用MultipleNegativesRankingLoss对比学习损失函数，在五个蛋白质配对数据集上进行微调：Pfam家族、结构衍生的困难负样本、AlphaFold DB结构配对、StringDB蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用以及深度突变扫描数据。通过对比微调重构嵌入空间以更好地捕捉蛋白质功能与结构。

Result: 在ESM-2 150M模型上，ProtSent在23个下游任务中提升了15个任务性能，其中远程同源性检测提升+105%，变异效应预测提升+17%，SCOPe-40结构检索Recall@1提升+19.9%；35M参数变体在16/23任务上有提升，远程同源性检测提升+40.5%，SCOPe-40 Recall@1提升+15.5%。

Conclusion: 对比微调能够有效重构嵌入空间以更好地捕捉蛋白质功能和结构信息，无需任何特定任务的监督信号，证明了该方法的通用性和有效性。作者发布了模型、公开数据、训练方案和代码以促进社区发展。

Abstract: Protein language models (pLMs) produce per-residue representations that capture evolutionary and structural information, yet their mean-pooled sequence embeddings are not explicitly trained to reflect functional, evolutionary or structural similarity between proteins. We present Protein Sentence Transformers (ProtSent), a contrastive fine-tuning framework for adapting PLMs into general-purpose embedding models. ProtSent trains with MultipleNegativesRankingLoss across five protein-pair datasets: Pfam families, structurally derived hard negatives, AlphaFold DB structural pairs, and StringDB protein--protein interactions, and Deep Mutational Scanning data. We evaluate on 23~downstream tasks using frozen embeddings with a k-nearest-neighbor probe to measure embedding neighborhood quality. On ESM-2 150M, ProtSent improves 15 of 23 tasks, with gains of +105% on remote homology detection, +17% on variant effect prediction, and +19.9% Recall@1 on SCOPe-40 structural retrieval. The 35M variant improves 16 of 23 tasks with +40.5% on remote homology and +15.5% Recall@1 on SCOPe-40. Contrastive fine-tuning restructures the embedding space to better capture protein function and structure, without any task-specific supervision. We release the models, public data, and training recipe and code.

</details>


### [129] [Why DDIM Hallucinates More than DDPM: A Theoretical Analysis of Reverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06831)
*Muhammad H. Ashiq,Samanyu Arora,Abhinav N. Harish,Ishaan Kharbanda,Hung Yun Tseng,Grigorios G. Chrysos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了确定性采样器(DDIM)在临界时间后容易陷入模式间的连接区域产生幻觉，而随机性采样器(DDPM)能借助随机性逃离该区域，从而降低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中的幻觉现象是一个关键问题，但缺乏理论理解。本文旨在研究两种代表性扩散采样器——随机性的DDPM和确定性的DDIM——在幻觉现象上的差异及其内在机制。

Method: 针对高斯混合目标分布，理论分析DDPM对应的逆向SDE和DDIM对应的逆向ODE。证明在临界时间τ后，DDIM会被困在连接两个最近模式的线段上，而DDPM的随机性有助于逃离该区域。通过实验验证DDPM相比DDIM具有更低的幻觉率，并探索增加随机步骤对DDIM的影响。

Result: 理论证明：(1)DDIM在临界时间后会卡在连接两个最近模式的线段上；(2)DDPM的随机性帮助其逃离该区域，避免幻觉。实验验证：当进入该区域时，DDPM的幻觉率显著低于DDIM。额外随机步骤可以帮助DDIM避免幻觉。

Conclusion: 随机性是避免扩散模型幻觉现象的关键因素，这为设计改进的采样器提供了新见解——在确定性采样过程中引入适当的随机性可以有效降低幻觉率。

Abstract: We theoretically study the hallucination phenomena in two canonical diffusion samplers: the stochastic Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) and the deterministic Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM). We analyze the reverse ODE (DDIM) and SDE (DDPM) for a Gaussian mixture target, proving that after a critical time $τ$, (a) DDIM can become stuck on the segment connecting the two nearest modes and (b) DDPM *stochasticity* helps it become unstuck from this region, thus avoiding hallucination. Our empirical validation verifies that DDPM has a significantly lower hallucination rate than DDIM when this region is entered. Building on our observations, we exhibit how using additional stochastic steps can help DDIM avoid hallucinations and offer new insights on how to design improved samplers.

</details>


### [130] [Attribution-Based Neuron Utility for Plasticity Restoration in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06834)
*Patrick Elisii,Lucas Beauchemin,Dawer Jamshed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GXD（梯度差异参考法），一种基于参考梯度归因的效用度量方法，用于改进持续学习中的自适应重置策略，解决现有代理信号与干预目标不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习研究关注新知识获取与旧知识保留的平衡，现有研究多集中于灾难性遗忘问题，但深度网络还面临可塑性丧失（loss of plasticity）的挑战——在持续训练过程中网络逐渐难以更新。现有的自适应重置干预方法依赖于激活幅度、贡献效用或梯度活动等代理信号，这些信号可能与它们所要引导的干预措施产生错位。

Method: 提出了梯度差异参考法（GXD），这是一种基于参考梯度归因的理论驱动的效用度量方法。GXD通过估算替换单元的一阶功能成本，将自适应重置重构为干预成本估计问题，使效用度量与重置的功能成本保持一致。

Result: 实验结果表明，与重置功能成本一致的效用度量（GXD）在现有重置标准退化的场景中能够使干预措施更加可靠。GXD在代理信号失效的情况下表现更加稳健。

Conclusion: GXD成功地将自适应重置重新定义为一个干预成本估计问题，为构建更加稳健的持续学习系统提供了实用的技术路径。该方法解决了现有代理信号与干预目标不一致的根本问题。

Abstract: Continual learning research attempts to conserve two fundamental capabilities: new knowledge acquisition and the preservation of previously acquired knowledge. While knowledge in this case can be measured through performance over an implicit or explicit task space, model plasticity generally concerns adaptability as data distributions evolve. Though much of the literature has focused on catastrophic forgetting, deep networks can also suffer from loss of plasticity, becoming progressively harder to update under continued training. Recent research has identified multiple mechanisms underlying this phenomenon, including neuron saturation, parameter norm growth, and loss of useful curvature directions. Adaptive reset-based interventions, which selectively reinitialize low-utility network parameters, have emerged as practical solutions to restore trainability. Existing utility measures used to guide resets, such as activation magnitude, contribution utility, or gradient-based activity, rely on proxy signals that can become misaligned with the intervention they are meant to guide. In this paper, we introduce gradient times difference from reference (GXD), a theoretically motivated utility measure based on reference-based gradient attribution that estimates the first-order functional cost of replacing a unit. Our results show that utility measures aligned with the functional cost of the reset can make interventions more reliable in settings where existing reset criteria degrade. GXD reframes adaptive resetting as an intervention cost estimation problem, providing a practical path toward more robust continual learning systems.

</details>


### [131] [On Privacy Leakage in Tabular Diffusion Models: Influential Factors, Attacker Knowledge, and Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06835)
*Masoumeh Shafieinejad,D. B. Emerson,Behnoosh Zamanlooy,Elaheh Bassak,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Sara Kodeiri,Marcelo Lotif,Xi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对表格扩散模型（TDM），利用成员推理攻击量化了训练设置、合成选择及攻击者知识对隐私泄露的影响，揭示了攻击者无需完美知识或大量计算资源即可成功攻击，并指出了启发式隐私指标的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据在许多隐私敏感领域至关重要，合成数据代理虽能降低隐私风险，但领先的表格扩散模型（TDMs）带来的隐私风险尚不明确，亟需对其隐私泄露进行系统测量与理解。

Method: 利用黑盒和白盒设置下最先进的成员推理攻击，系统量化训练配置、合成选择以及攻击者知识对TDMs隐私泄露的影响，并分析了启发式隐私指标（如到最近记录的距离）的有效性。

Result: 研究发现攻击者无需对训练设置有完美知识、无需相同数据分布或大量计算资源即可构造成功的攻击；同时揭示了应用启发式隐私指标存在陷阱与误导。

Conclusion: TDMs存在显著隐私泄露风险，简单启发式隐私度量不可靠，需采用更严谨的隐私评估方法来指导安全合成数据发布。

Abstract: Tabular data plays an important role in many fields and industries, including those with elevated privacy considerations and risks. As such, there is a rising interest in generating high-quality synthetic proxies for real tabular data as a means of reducing privacy risk and proprietary data exposure. With tabular diffusion models (TDMs) demonstrating leading performance in synthesizing such data, understanding and measuring the privacy risks associated with these models is imperative. Leveraging state-of-the-art membership inference attacks for TDMs in both black- and white-box settings, this work quantifies the impact of training setup, synthesis choices, and attacker knowledge on privacy leakage. Moreover, the results demonstrate that adversaries need not have perfect knowledge of the training setup, identical data distributions, or massive compute resources to construct successful attacks. Finally, the pitfalls associated with applying heuristic privacy metrics, such as distance-to-closest record, are revealed.

</details>


### [132] [How to Compress KV Cache in RL Post-Training? Shadow Mask Distillation for Memory-Efficient Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06850)
*Rui Zhu,Weiheng Bai,Qiushi Wu,Yang Ren,Haixu Tang,Yuchu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在长上下文推理任务中，使用强化学习训练大语言模型时面临的核心挑战：在线RL的探索阶段会产生巨大的KV缓存开销，而压缩缓存虽然缓解了内存问题，却会引入严重的离策略偏差，现有的统计方法难以有效修正这一偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为解锁大语言模型高级推理能力的关键范式（如RLHF、RLAIF），但在处理长上下文推理任务时，在线RL的探索轨迹生成阶段会产生巨大的KV缓存占用，形成"内存墙"瓶颈，迫切需要解决内存开销与策略偏差之间的矛盾。

Method: 分析了在rollout阶段应用KV缓存压缩所带来的问题，探讨了KV压缩虽然是近似无损的，但在RL优化的内在不稳定性下，微小的近似误差会被显著放大，导致采样器在稀疏上下文下生成响应，而学习器使用完整的密集上下文更新参数。

Result: 发现即使现代KV压缩在标准推理中几乎是无损的，但在RL优化过程中，极小的近似误差也会被内在的不稳定性大幅放大，导致严重的离策略偏差；现有的统计解决方案（如重要性重加权）在修正这种放大偏差时面临高梯度方差和严重的样本低效问题。

Conclusion: 在长上下文推理任务的强化学习训练中，简单地应用KV缓存压缩会引入难以纠正的离策略偏差，这一发现为设计新的训练策略提供了重要的理论依据，提示需要开发更有效的偏差修正方法或替代的训练框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for unlocking the advanced reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), encompassing frameworks like RLHF and RLAIF. Regardless of the specific optimization algorithm (e.g., PPO, GRPO, or Online DPO), online RL inherently requires an exploratory trajectory generation (rollout) phase. However, for long-context reasoning tasks, this rollout phase imposes a severe ``memory wall'' due to the exorbitant Key-Value (KV) cache footprint. While applying KV cache compression during rollouts mitigates this memory overhead, it induces a critical off-policy bias. Although modern KV compression is often nearly lossless during standard inference, even minuscule approximation errors are drastically amplified by the inherent instability of RL optimization. Specifically, the sampler generates responses under a sparse context, whereas the learner updates parameters using the full, dense context. Existing statistical solutions, such as importance reweighting, struggle to correct this magnified bias, suffering from high gradient variance and severe sample inefficiency.

</details>


### [133] [Benchmarked Yet Not Measured -- Generative AI Should be Evaluated Against Real-World Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06856)
*Ishani Mondal,Shweta Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出生成式AI在标准基准测试与实际应用效用之间存在差距，提出从静态基准评估转向以人为中心的效用评估范式，并设计了SCU-GenEval四阶段评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者在28个部署案例（涵盖教育、医疗、软件工程和法律领域）中发现生成式AI系统在基准测试中表现优异却无法提供实际效用。这种基准-效用差距源于评估实践中的三个常见失败：代理置换、时间坍缩和分布隐藏。

Method: 提出SCU-GenEval四阶段评估框架：(1)利益相关者-目标映射；(2)构念-指标规范；(3)机制建模；(4)纵向效用测量。同时引入三个支持工具：结构化部署协议、情境条件化用户模拟器、以及角色和目标条件化代理指标。

Result: 识别出当前评估的核心缺失构念——效用，即在部署情境中通过持续AI交互所引发的利益相关者能力变化。建立了从模型输出属性评估转向人类结果轨迹评估的理论框架和实操工具。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的进步应通过人类结果的可测量改进来评估，而非仅依赖基准测试性能，并针对不同领域提出具体行动倡议。

Abstract: Generative AI systems achieve impressive performance on standard benchmarks yet fail to deliver real-world utility, a disconnect we identify across 28 deployment cases spanning education, healthcare, software engineering, and law. We argue that this benchmark utility gap arises from three recurring failures in evaluation practice: proxy displacement, temporal collapse, and distributional concealment. Motivated by these observations, we argue that generative AI evaluation requires a paradigm shift from static benchmark-centered transparency toward stakeholder, goal, and context-conditioned utility transparency grounded in human outcome trajectories. Existing evaluations primarily characterize properties of model outputs, while deployment success depends on whether interaction with AI improves stakeholders' ability to achieve their goals over time. The missing construct is therefore utility: the change in a stakeholder's capability induced through sustained interaction with an AI system within a deployment context. To operationalize this perspective, we propose SCU-GenEval, a four-stage evaluation framework consisting of stakeholder-goal mapping, construct-indicator specification, mechanism modeling, and longitudinal utility measurement. To make these stages practically deployable, we introduce three supporting instruments: structured deployment protocols, context-conditioned user simulators, and persona- and goal-conditioned proxy metrics. We conclude with domain-specific calls to action, arguing that progress in generative AI must be evaluated through measurable improvements in human outcomes rather than benchmark performance alone.

</details>


### [134] [Christoffel-DPS: Optimal sensor placement in diffusion posterior sampling for arbitrary distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06861)
*James Rowbottom,Nick Huang,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Ben Adcock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Christoffel函数的分布无关最优传感器放置框架（Christoffel-DPS），该框架无需高斯假设，在稀疏测量场景下结合生成式模型实现了优于传统方法的信号重构性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传感器放置（OSP）方法依赖高斯假设，无法处理实际系统中常见的复杂分布；而现有的基于生成式模型的传感器选择方法要么需要不切实际的大量传感器，要么沿用了与先进重构模型不匹配的经典OSP工具，因此亟需一种与现代化重构模型相适应的新型OSP方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于Christoffel函数的分布无关传感器放置框架，该框架为任意传感器和信号分布的后验采样提供了最优采样与重构保证的数学表述，并据此推导出具有非渐近界的OSP策略；具体实现了离线和在线两种Christoffel-DPS变体，将其与无条件扩散后验采样（DPS）和流匹配模型相结合。

Result: Christoffel-DPS在低传感器预算环境下，于结构非高斯基准测试中表现优于高斯OSP基线和现有的生成式模型传感器放置方法，验证了分布无关感知策略的理论有效性和实践优越性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个模型无关的、理论上有严格保证的分布无关传感器放置框架，成功弥合了现代生成式重构模型与传感器放置策略之间的差距，为稀疏测量场景下的状态估计提供了新思路。

Abstract: State estimation is a critical task in scientific, engineering and control applications.
