Since the reliability of reconstructions depends on the number and position of sensors, optimal sensor placement (OSP) is essential in scenarios where measurements are sparse and expensive. Classical OSP approaches rely on Gaussian assumptions and are consequently unable to account for the complex distributions encountered in many real-world systems. Generative-model-based reconstruction using sensor guided diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) has emerged as a promising technique for reconstructing states from highly complex distributions. However, existing sensor-selection methods either require unrealistically many sensors or emulate classical OSP, creating a mismatch between modern recovery models with classical OSP tools motivating the need for fundamentally new ideas towards OSP that match the recent advances made in powerful recovery models. We introduce a distribution-free sensor placement framework based on the Christoffel function: a mathematical formulation of optimal sampling and recovery guarantees for posterior sampling with arbitrary sensors and signal distributions, from which we derive a new OSP strategy with non-asymptotic bounds on the number of sensors needed for recovery. We develop Christoffel-DPS, with offline and online variants, instantiating Christoffel sampling for generative models. Christoffel-DPS outperforms Gaussian OSP baselines and existing generative-model placement methods, validating that distribution-free sensing is both theoretically principled and practically superior. The framework is model-agnostic; we demonstrate its application to a range of unconditional DPS and flow-matching models on structurally non-Gaussian benchmarks, showing the efficacy of Christoffel-DPS in low sensor budget regimes.

</details>


### [135] [Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Bandits: From Learning Efficiency to Fairness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06864)
*John Wang,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机奖励下的多目标多智能体多臂赌博机（MO-MA-MAB）问题，提出了Pareto UCB1 Gossip算法以优化学习效率（帕累托遗憾），并提出Simulated NSW UCB Gossip算法以实现公平学习（纳什社会福利），证明了公平约束会降低信息聚合速度和收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对多目标多智能体多臂赌博机问题中同时考虑学习效率和公平性的框架。本文旨在填补这一空白，在智能体通过时变图通信且观察到异构奖励向量的场景下，同时实现高效学习（以帕累托遗憾度量）和公平学习（以社会福利度量）。

Method: 针对效率目标，提出Pareto UCB1 Gossip算法，设计了新颖的探索半径，明确分离帕累托推断中的统计不确定性和共识误差。针对公平性约束，提出Simulated NSW UCB Gossip算法，整合了基于偏好的奖励模拟、基于Gossip的效用估计和UCB式探索策略。

Result: Pareto UCB1 Gossip算法实现了O(log T)的遗憾上界和O(√T)的实例无关收敛率；Simulated NSW UCB Gossip算法实现了O(T^{3/4})的实例无关遗憾上界。实验表明，所提方法在效率和公平性设置下分别比基线方法提升约100%和50%的性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在多目标多智能体学习问题中，公平约束会限制信息聚合并降低收敛速度，揭示了效率与公平性之间的内在权衡。所提出的算法框架能够有效平衡这两个目标，为分布式决策系统中的公平高效学习提供了理论基础和实践方法。

Abstract: We study multi-objective multi-agent multi-armed bandits (MO-MA-MAB) under stochastic rewards, where agents observe heterogeneous reward vectors and communicate over time-varying graphs. We formulate this emerging problem setting to address \emph{efficient learning}, measured by Pareto regret, and incorporate \emph{fair learning} as an additional goal, captured via social welfare. To measure efficiency, we formulate Pareto regret and develop \textsc{Pareto UCB1 Gossip}, whose novel exploration radius explicitly separates statistical uncertainty in Pareto-based inference from consensus error. To express the fairness constraint, we formulate a Nash Social Welfare objective over preference-scalarized rewards and propose \textsc{Simulated NSW UCB Gossip}, which integrates preference-based reward simulation, gossip-based utility estimation, and UCB-style exploration. We prove that \textsc{Pareto UCB1 Gossip} achieves \(\mathcal{O}(\log T)\) regret and an instance-independent rate of \(\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})\), while \textsc{Simulated NSW UCB Gossip} achieves an instance-independent regret bound of \(\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})\). This separation reveals the cost of imposing the fairness constraint to our efficiency objective: fairness limits information aggregation and slows convergence. Experiments show that our methods consistently outperform baselines, improving performance by approximately \(100\%\) and \(50\%\) in the efficiency and fairness settings, respectively.

</details>


### [136] [Dataset Watermarking for Closed LLMs with Provable Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06865)
*Pengrun Huang,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对闭源大语言模型、具有可证明检测能力的数据集水印方法，通过增加随机词对共现频率嵌入水印，并能有效检测（p<0.01），即使在数据混合比例低至约1%时仍有效，且保持了数据集的实用性和语义完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在海量数据上进行预训练和后训练，这些数据可能包含专有数据集或评估所用的基准测试数据，引发了对数据集水印的需求，即设计一种机制，使在特定数据集上训练的模型留下可检测的签名。

Method: 通过重述数据增加随机选择的词对的共现频率，在数据集级别嵌入水印信号；在检测阶段，使用统计测试方法分析模型生成输出中的词对共现模式。

Result: 在多个基础模型和基准数据集上的评估表明，该方法在微调阶段能可靠检测水印（p<0.01）；在数据混合设置中，即使水印数据集仅占总微调词元的约1%，方法依然有效。

Conclusion: 该方法是首个针对闭源LLM具有可证明检测能力的数据集水印方案，不仅检测效果显著，而且在低比例混合场景下依然有效，同时保持了基准数据集的实用性和语义完整性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are pre-trained and post-trained on vast amounts of loosely curated data, raising the possibility that these models may have been trained on proprietary datasets or the same benchmarks used for evaluation. This motivates the need for dataset watermarking: designing datasets such that training on them leaves detectable signatures in the resulting model. Prior work has explored this problem for open models. We introduce the first dataset watermarking method for closed LLMs with provable detection. In particular, we embed a dataset-level watermark signal by increasing the co-occurrence frequency of randomly selected word pairs through rephrasing, and detect it using a statistical test on co-occurrence patterns in model-generated outputs. We evaluate our method with multiple base models and benchmark datasets and show that it reliably detects the watermark ($p <0.01$) in the fine-tuning stage. Notably, our method remains effective in a data mixture setting where the watermarked dataset constitutes only approximately $1\%$ of the total fine-tuning tokens. Furthermore, we show that our method preserves the utility and semantic integrity of the benchmark.

</details>


### [137] [A Finite-Iteration Theory for Asynchronous Categorical Distributional Temporal-Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06866)
*Ege C. Kaya,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文填补了现有有限迭代理论与实际分布时序差分实现之间的空白，为标量分类TD学习和多变量符号分类TD学习提供了在i.i.d.和马尔可夫采样下的有限迭代保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非渐近分析主要关注同步全状态更新，但标准分类时序差分学习采用异步单状态更新并由马尔可夫轨迹驱动，这导致现有理论与实际实现之间存在重要差距。

Method: 通过合适的等距嵌入，将标量分类TD学习（Cramér几何）和多变量符号分类TD学习（MMD几何）转化为在状态逐点 supremum 范数下收缩的异步单状态随机逼近递归。

Result: 在折扣问题中，针对i.i.d.和马尔可夫状态采样建立了有限迭代保证；在无折扣固定时域问题中，针对i.i.d.回合采样建立了有限迭代保证。

Conclusion: 成功弥合了有限迭代理论与实用分布时序差分实现之间的差距，为两种分类策略评估方法提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Recent non-asymptotic analyses have substantially advanced the theory of distributional policy evaluation, but they largely concern synchronous full-state updates under a generative model, model-based estimators, accelerated variants, or different approximation architectures. Standard categorical temporal-difference learning is typically used in a different regime. It asynchronously performs a single-state update at each iteration and, in online settings, is driven by a Markovian trajectory. This leaves an important gap between existing finite-iteration theory and the categorical recursions most closely aligned with practical distributional temporal-difference implementations. We bridge this gap for two categorical policy-evaluation methods: scalar categorical temporal-difference learning in the Cramér geometry and multivariate signed-categorical temporal-difference learning in the maximum mean discrepancy geometry. After suitable isometric embeddings, both algorithms take the form of asynchronous single-state stochastic-approximation recursions that contract in a statewise supremum norm. This permits finite-iteration guarantees in discounted problems under both i.i.d. and Markovian state sampling, and in undiscounted fixed-horizon problems under i.i.d. episodic sampling.

</details>


### [138] [When Descent Is Too Stable: Event-Triggered Hamiltonian Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06868)
*Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了SHAPE算法，一种基于端口哈密顿动力学的自适应优化器，通过能量整形机制解决固定预算非凸优化中因过早收敛到无信息局部极小值而导致的性能停滞问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算非凸优化的核心挑战并非局部下降的不稳定性，而是其过度稳定性：优化器在到达附近驻点后，可能会将剩余评估预算浪费在精炼无信息的局部极小值上。作者将这一失败模式建模为优化器动力学的控制问题，学习者需要在下降、利用有利盆地以及在停滞时转移探索之间做出决策。

Method: 提出SHAPE（Structured Adaptive Port-Hamiltonian task-family Optimizer），从梯度下降动力学出发，将优化提升到增广相空间(q, p)，其中q为候选解，p携带方向敏感性，控制器u提供来自当前梯度预言机的处理后信息。在每个阶段内，学习得到的哈密顿向量场诱导结构化局部下降；在阶段间，事件触发的固定时钟在检测到局部平衡时更新端口和记忆。该设计保持了无源兼容结构，允许同一训练策略使用干净、随机或估计梯度输入。

Result: 在固定预算非凸优化任务上的实验表明，与固定策略优化器相比，SHAPE在最佳性能（best-so-far）指标上有显著提升，证明了自适应哈密顿能量整形机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应哈密顿能量整形为在复杂优化景观中平衡下降、探索和预算分配提供了一个有原则的机制，为固定预算非凸优化问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Fixed-budget nonconvex optimization can fail not because local descent is unstable, but because it is too stable: after reaching a nearby stationary point, an optimizer may spend the remaining evaluations refining an uninformative local minimum. We formulate this failure mode as a control problem over optimizer dynamics, where the learner must decide when to descend, when to exploit a promising basin, and when stagnation should trigger movement elsewhere. We introduce SHAPE, a structured adaptive port-Hamiltonian task-family optimizer for event-triggered minima hunting under local information. Starting from gradient-descent dynamics, SHAPE lifts optimization to an augmented phase space $(q, p)$, where the primal state $q$ represents the candidate solution, the cotangent variable $p$ carries directional sensitivity, and a controller $u$ provides processed information from current gradient oracle. Within each stage, a learned Hamiltonian vector field induces structured local descent; across stages, a fixed event clock in the implementation updates ports and memory when local equilibria are detected, with stage-dependent horizons treated in the analysis as a direct generalization. This design preserves a passivity-compatible structure while allowing the same trained policy to use clean, stochastic, or estimated gradient inputs. Experiments on fixed-budget nonconvex optimization tasks show that SHAPE improves best-so-far performance compared with fixed-policy optimizers. These results suggest that adaptive Hamiltonian energy shaping provides a principled mechanism for balancing descent, exploration, and budget allocation in difficult optimization landscapes.

</details>


### [139] [Continuous First, Discrete Later: VQ-VAEs Without Dimensional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06870)
*Xinyu Zhao,Nikita Karagodin,Hamed Hassani,Sinan Hersek,Paul Pu Liang,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了VQ-VAE中的维度坍缩问题（表示仅存在于极低维子空间中），提出了通过自编码器预热阶段来恢复表示维度的解决方案，在图像和音频任务上显著提升了重建质量和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有改进VQ-VAE性能的方法主要关注码本大小和利用率，但维度坍缩问题（训练后的VQ-VAE表示仅存在于极低维子空间，仅占满秩的1-2%）尚未得到解决。作者从理论和实证角度发现，维度坍缩会导致损失的下界，且各种码本改进技术无法突破这一限制。

Method: 作者扩展了Saxe等人[2014]的序贯学习效应分析框架，结合率失真理论，揭示了VQ通过抑制低方差方向导致潜在表示坍缩的机制。基于此理论分析，提出了一个简单的解决方案：在引入VQ之前，先用（未量化的）自编码器进行预热训练（AE Warm-Up）。通过控制预热时长来调节最终可达到的损失值。

Result: 在合成实验和大规模图像（VQGAN）及音频（WavTokenizer）VQ-VAE上验证了方法的有效性。VQGAN的码本有效维度从3-5提升至17-19，rFID降低17-35%；WavTokenizer的码本维度从4提升至17-19，PESQ提升11-14%。理论分析能够根据预热时长预测下游任务性能，为从AE预热切换到VQ-VAE训练提供了自适应准则。

Conclusion: 维度坍缩是限制VQ-VAE性能的关键因素，AE Warm-Up是一种简单有效的解决方案。该方法能够在相同训练预算下显著改善重建和感知损失，理论框架为实践中预热时长的选择提供了指导。

Abstract: While many approaches to improve VQ-VAE performance focus on codebook size and utilization, the effect of dimensional collapse, where trained VQ-VAE representations live in an extremely low-dimensional subspace (1-2% of full rank), remains unaddressed. We show theoretically and empirically that dimension collapse causes a hard loss lower bound that various codebook improvement techniques fail to surpass. Our analytic framework extends the sequential learning effect of Saxe et al. [2014] by introducing ideas from rate-distortion theory and explains how the latent collapse is caused by the VQ suppressing lower-variance directions. Our theory justifies a simple solution: a "warm-up phase" that trains the model as an (unquantized) autoencoder before introducing VQ. On both synthetic experiments and large-scale image (VQGAN) and audio (WavTokenizer) VQ-VAEs, we show that AE Warm-Up successfully restores representation dimension, leading to lower reconstruction and perceptual loss at the same training budget. Across codebook sizes $K \in$ {$2^{10}, 2^{14}, 2^{16}$}, AE warm-up raises VQGAN codebook effective dimension from 3-5 to 17-19 and reduces rFID by 17-35%; on WavTokenizer at $K \in$ {$2^{13}, 2^{14}$}, it raises codebook dimension from 4 to 17-19 and improves PESQ by 11-14%. We empirically characterize how warm-up duration governs the achievable final loss. In agreement with experiment, our theoretical analysis predicts downstream performance as a function of warm-up length, enabling an adaptive criterion for switching from AE Warm-up to VQ-VAE training.

</details>


### [140] [On the Divergence of Differential Temporal Difference Learning without Local Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06874)
*David Antrobius,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了平均奖励强化学习中全局时钟学习率与局部时钟学习率收敛性的根本差异，构建反例证明了局部时钟收敛的算法在全局时钟下可能发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献表明，在折扣强化学习中，全局时钟学习率与局部时钟学习率在收敛性上等价，但平均奖励强化学习中的情况尚不明确，存在开放问题需要解决。

Method: 区分两种学习率形式：仅依赖时间步的全局时钟学习率α_t和依赖状态访问次数的局部时钟学习率α_ν(S_t, t)；针对平均奖励强化学习中的差分时序差分学习算法，构建特定反例进行理论分析。

Result: 成功构造反例证明：差分时序差分学习算法在使用局部时钟学习率时收敛，但在使用全局时钟学习率时可能发散，打破了折扣强化学习中的良好对应关系。

Conclusion: 平均奖励强化学习中全局时钟与局部时钟学习率的收敛性不等价，该发现解决了Wan等人[2021]和Blaser等人[2026]提出的开放问题，为学习率设计提供了重要理论指导。

Abstract: Learning rate is a critical component of reinforcement learning (RL). This work uses global and local clocks to distinguish two types of learning rates. The former is of the standard form $α_t$ that depends only on the time step $t$ (i.e., a global clock). The latter is of the form $α_{ν(S_t, t)}$, where $ν(s, t)$ counts the number of visits to state $s$ until time $t$ (i.e., a local clock). In discounted RL, an RL algorithm that is convergent with a local clock is always also convergent with a global clock, and vice versa. We are not aware of any counterexample. The key contribution of this work is to show that this nice correspondence breaks down in average-reward RL. Specifically, we construct a counterexample showing that although differential temporal difference learning is convergent with a local clock, it can diverge with a global clock. This counterexample closes the open problem in Wan et al. [2021], Blaser et al. [2026].

</details>


### [141] [Temporal Attention for Adaptive Control of Euler-Lagrange Systems with Unobservable Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06877)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione,Adriano Fagiolini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种元控制架构，利用自注意力机制处理运动历史窗口来生成计算力矩控制器的增益，并通过自协方差分析预先确定注意力头数量，在短记忆和匹配记忆场景下显著降低了跟踪误差，但在长记忆场景下表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有有限时域内部摩擦状态的欧拉-拉格朗日系统，由于该内部状态无法从关节测量中直接观测，导致测量闭环状态不再具有马尔可夫性，传统的确定性等价自适应律因此失去收敛保证，这使得自适应控制面临挑战。

Method: 提出一种元控制架构，通过自注意力模块处理近期运动历史的短窗口来生成计算力矩控制器的增益。注意力头数量在策略训练前通过基于记忆状态梯度自协方差的代理分析来选择，该代理基于作者之前开发的增量秩跟踪框架的时间自适应版本。选定的头数量作为架构超参数固定，并在强化学习阶段于屏蔽许可约束下训练策略。

Result: 在带有非线性摩擦和可变负载的2自由度机械臂上进行测试。在短记忆和匹配记忆场景下，单层纯注意力元控制器优于更深的Transformer基线，跟踪误差分别降低12和19个百分点（效应量大，d分别约为-1.1和-2.1，Mann-Whitney p < 0.05）。但在长记忆场景下优势消失，十次训练运行中有四次出现发散或负载不变策略崩溃，揭示了静态头数量预定的缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明所提方法在短记忆场景有效，但在长记忆场景存在局限性。静态的注意力头数量预定方法在长记忆情况下表现不佳，这促使将秩跟踪移入强化学习循环内部，允许注意力头在运行时进行修剪或增长，而非在训练前固定。

Abstract: Adaptive control of Euler-Lagrange systems is challenging when friction is governed by a finite-horizon internal state that is not directly observable from joint measurements. In this setting, the measured closed-loop state is no longer Markovian, and standard certainty-equivalence adaptive laws may lose their convergence guarantees.
  The paper proposes a meta-control architecture in which the gains of a computed-torque controller are generated by a self-attention block processing a short window of recent motion history. The number of attention heads is selected before policy training through a surrogate analysis of the autocovariance of the memory-state gradient along the temporal window. This surrogate is based on a temporal adaptation of an incremental rank-tracking framework previously developed by the authors. The selected head count is then fixed and used as an architectural hyperparameter in a reinforcement-learning stage, where the policy is trained under a shielded admissibility constraint.
  The approach is tested on a 2-DOF manipulator with nonlinear friction and variable payload. In the short and matched memory regimes, the single-layer attention-only meta-controller outperforms a deeper Transformer baseline, with tracking-error reductions of 12 and 19 percentage points, respectively. The reported effect sizes are large, with d approximately -1.1 and -2.1, and Mann-Whitney p < 0.05 in both cases.
  In the long memory regime, however, the advantage disappears. Four out of ten training runs show either divergence or payload-invariant policy collapse, revealing a weakness in the static Phase-1 head-count prescription. This motivates moving rank-tracking inside the reinforcement-learning loop, allowing attention heads to be pruned or grown at runtime instead of fixed before training.

</details>


### [142] [Better Protein Function Prediction by Modeling Survivorship Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06879)
*Zhongmou Chao,Poompol Buathong,Ekaterina Selivanovitch,Susan Daniel,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Evo-PU框架，利用核苷酸突变知识建模幸存者偏差，在蛋白质功能预测任务上优于现有PU学习、单类分类和蛋白质语言模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质序列数据存在幸存者偏差：只观察到存活生物的功能性蛋白质，非功能性突变被自然选择淘汰，因此常需仅从正例学习。现有正未标记（PU）学习方法忽略了塑造序列可观察性的进化过程，未区分"因不具功能性而未被观察"与"因突变未发生而缺失"两种不同情况。

Method: 提出Evo-PU框架，利用核苷酸突变的科学理解对幸存者偏差进行建模。该方法针对监测充分的单生物体序列数据，区分对待两种缺失序列：（1）距离常见变异仅一个突变且若具功能性本应被观察到的序列，提示非功能性；（2）通过突变不太可能产生的序列，仅因未发生而缺失。据此在训练时对这两种情况差异化处理。

Result: 在三个单生物体均匀覆盖监测数据的预测任务上——预测流感病毒和呼吸道合胞病毒（RSV）诱变研究的留存结果，以及预测未来SARS-CoV-2变异株——Evo-PU均优于标准PU学习、单类分类（OCC）和蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）。在监测覆盖更异质的多生物体ProteinGym数据集预测任务上，识别了方法泛化的机会。

Conclusion: 通过将进化过程中的突变机制纳入PU学习框架，Evo-PU有效解决了蛋白质序列数据中的幸存者偏差问题，显著提升了功能预测性能，为未来扩展到多生物体异质监测覆盖场景指明了方向。

Abstract: Protein sequence data from nature exhibits survivorship bias: we only observe data from those organisms that survive and reproduce, while non-functional protein mutations are eliminated by natural selection. Thus, predicting whether a protein sequence is functional often requires learning from positive examples alone. While positive-unlabeled (PU) learning frameworks offer a generic solution to this problem, existing PU methods ignore the evolutionary processes that shape sequence observability and cause survivorship bias. Consider a sequence that is one mutation away from a commonly-observed protein variant in a well-surveilled organism. If the sequence were functional, it would likely be observed. If it is not observed, this suggests non-functionality. In contrast, sequences that are unlikely to arise through mutation may be missing simply because they never arose. Thus, these two kinds of missing sequences should be treated differently when training models. In this work, we propose Evo-PU, a PU learning framework that uses a scientific understanding of nucleotide mutation to model survivorship bias for well-surveilled single-organism sequence data. On three prediction tasks using single-organism uniform-coverage surveillance data -- predicting results from held-out influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) mutagenesis studies, and predicting future SARS-CoV-2 variants -- Evo-PU outperforms standard PU learning, one-class classification (OCC), and protein language models (PLMs). On prediction tasks from multi-organism ProteinGym datasets with more heterogeneous surveillance coverage, we identify opportunities to generalize our approach.

</details>


### [143] [Don't Retrain, Align: Adapting Autoregressive LMs to Diffusion LMs via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06885)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Alexis Fox,Anru R. Zhang,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REPR-ALIGN方法，通过对齐隐藏状态表征将预训练自回归（AR）模型转换为扩散语言模型（DLM），实现高达4倍训练加速，证明了语言表征可以跨生成顺序迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AR到DLM转换方法主要通过去噪训练转移参数，但忽略了AR预训练学到的内部表征几何结构是否可以显式保留。作者假设AR预训练学到的语义结构可以跨生成顺序迁移，DLM训练应被视为重新学习解码路径而非重新学习语言表征。

Method: 提出REPR-ALIGN表征对齐目标：在保持预训练AR模型冻结的情况下，使用余弦相似度在每一层对齐DLM与AR模型的隐藏状态，同时优化标准的掩码去噪目标。该方法无需适配器，除注意力掩码外无需架构修改。

Result: 该方法实现了高达4倍的训练加速，在低数据场景下尤为有效。实验结果表明语言表征可以跨生成顺序迁移，表征对齐为训练扩散语言模型提供了简单有效的技术。

Conclusion: 语言表征可以在不同生成顺序间迁移，表征对齐是一种简单有效的训练扩散语言模型的技术，为AR到DLM的转换提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently demonstrated capabilities that complement standard autoregressive (AR) models, particularly in non-sequential generation and bidirectional editing. Although recent work has shown that pretrained autoregressive checkpoints can be converted into diffusion language models, existing recipes primarily transfer parameters through continued denoising training with objective- and attention-level modifications. We instead ask whether the internal representation geometry learned by next-token prediction can be explicitly preserved during AR-to-DLM conversion. We hypothesize that much of the semantic structure learned by AR pretraining can transfer across generation orders, and thus DLM training should be viewed as relearning the decoding path rather than relearning language representations. To investigate this, we introduce REPR-ALIGN, a representation alignment objective that adapts a bidirectional masked diffusion model to reuse representations from a pretrained AR model of identical architecture. Concretely, we align the hidden states of the DLM to the frozen AR model at every layer using cosine similarity, while optimizing the standard masked denoising objective. This simple alignment, with no adapters and no architectural changes beyond the attention mask, yields up to 4x training acceleration in our setting and is particularly effective in low-data regimes. Our results suggest that linguistic representations can transfer across generation order, and that representation alignment provides a simple and effective technique for training diffusion language models. Code is available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/Open-dLLM.

</details>


### [144] [Streaming Adversarial Robustness in Fuzzy ARTMAP: Mechanism-Aligned Evaluation, Progressive Training, and Interpretable Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06902)
*Shane Cairns,Leonardo Enzo Brito da Silva,Sasha Petrenko,Donald C. Wunsch,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究流式原型学习器Fuzzy ARTMAP的对抗鲁棒性，提出WB-Softmax白盒攻击方法，发现防御排名在不同评估协议下会反转，并揭示了ART显式类别几何在可解释诊断中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗鲁棒性研究主要集中于离线深度网络，而对于严格的单次通过流式神经学习器的鲁棒性知之甚少。Fuzzy ARTMAP作为一种基于类别竞争、补码编码、匹配追踪和无回放原型更新的自适应谐振理论架构，需要专门的鲁棒性研究。

Method: 1. 提出WB-Softmax：一种与ARTMAP类别竞争和映射场预测机制对齐的可微分白盒攻击替代方法；2. 形式化流式评估原则：要求在最终部署模型上评估鲁棒性；3. 设计渐进式两阶段选择性训练作为防御策略；4. 利用ART的显式类别几何特性进行可解释诊断。

Result: 在四个图像基准测试中，WB-Softmax对原始Fuzzy ARTMAP模型实现89-100%的攻击成功率。发现防御排名在不同协议下会反转：离线对抗训练在迁移攻击下看似强大，但在自适应白盒评估下崩溃；而渐进式两阶段选择性训练提供最强的无回放鲁棒性。ART的显式类别几何能够诊断分离崩溃和匹配分数反转现象。

Conclusion: 为流式原型学习器的对抗鲁棒性提供了机制对齐、协议感知的分析框架，证明了不同评估协议会导致防御排名反转，并展示了ART架构在可解释诊断方面的独特优势。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness has been studied extensively for offline deep networks, but less is known about strict single-pass streaming neural learners. This paper studies adversarial robustness in Fuzzy ARTMAP, an Adaptive Resonance Theory architecture based on category competition, complement coding, match tracking, and replay-free prototype updates. We introduce WB-Softmax, a differentiable white-box attack surrogate aligned with ARTMAP's category-competition and map-field prediction mechanism, and formalize a streaming evaluation principle requiring robustness to be assessed on the final deployed model. Across four image benchmarks, WB-Softmax achieves 89-100% attack success on vanilla Fuzzy ARTMAP models. We show that defense rankings can reverse across protocols: offline adversarial training may appear strong under transfer attacks yet collapse under adaptive white-box evaluation, whereas progressive two-stage selective training provides the strongest overall replay-free robustness. We further show that ART's explicit category geometry enables interpretable diagnosis of separation collapse and match-score inversion. These results provide a mechanism-aligned, protocol-aware framework for adversarial robustness in streaming prototype-based learners.

</details>


### [145] [Conservative Flows: A New Paradigm of Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06905)
*Eshed Gal,Md Shahriar Rahim Siddiqui,Moshe Eliasof,Eldad Haber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的生成范式：利用保持数据分布不变的离散随机动力学进行生成，无需从噪声先验传输，而是直接利用预训练流模型，通过校正Langevin动力学和预测-校正流两种概率保持采样机制，显著提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型主要依赖从噪声先验到数据的传输方法，本文旨在探索一种替代范式，即通过离散随机动力学直接在数据支持的初始状态下进行生成，以避免噪声先验的局限性并充分利用现有预训练模型。

Method: 提出了一种基于离散随机动力学的生成框架，该框架保持数据分布不变，并可直接应用于任何预训练的流模型。具体开发了两种概率保持的采样机制：带有Metropolis调整的校正Langevin动力学，以及预测-校正流方法，这两种方法均直接操作于现有的模型检查点。

Result: 在合成Swiss-roll目标分布、ImageNet-256和Oxford Flowers-102数据集上的实验验证表明，所提出的采样器在生成质量上始终优于原始的生成过程，有效提升了预训练模型的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的替代生成范式成功绕过了噪声先验传输的需求，通过概率保持的采样机制显著改进了生成效果，为利用预训练模型提供了一种高效、通用的方法，具有重要的理论和实践价值。

Abstract: Modern generative modeling is dominated by transport from a noise prior to data. We propose an alternative paradigm in which generation is performed by a discrete stochastic dynamics that leaves the data distribution invariant, initialized from data-supported states rather than from noise. The framework can utilize any pretrained flow model. We develop two probability-preserving sampling mechanisms, a corrected Langevin dynamics with a Metropolis adjustment and a predictor-corrector flow, that operate directly on existing checkpoints. We validate the framework on a synthetic Swiss-roll target, ImageNet-256 and Oxford Flowers-102, where our samplers consistently improve over the original generation procedures.

</details>


### [146] [TraXion: Rethinking Pre-training Frameworks for Mobility and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06906)
*Shang-Ling Hsu,Mark Tenzer,Cyrus Shahabi,Khurram Shafique*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TraXion框架，针对多实体时空事件流（MESES）设计预训练方法，在移动性、企业认证日志和ICU死亡率预测等多个领域均达到或超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人类移动性预训练方法直接借鉴语言建模目标，将轨迹视为句子、访问视为词元，但忽略了移动数据特有的三个结构特性：访问是元组值事件、用户轨迹携带持久特征、用户间存在共置依赖关系，这些特性定义了更广泛的多实体时空事件流（MESES）类别。

Method: 将MESES特性形式化为三个公理，提出TraXion框架，其目标函数和架构联合设计以满足这些公理要求，实现对移动性、企业认证日志、电子健康记录等不同领域事件流的统一建模。

Result: 在六个公共移动数据集上，单个TraXion检查点在异常检测、下一兴趣点推荐、下一访问预测和社交链接预测等所有任务中均超越任务特定基线；在企业认证日志和ICU死亡率预测任务上匹配或超越现有工作。

Conclusion: TraXion证明了移动性、安全和医疗保健等不同领域的事件流可以在单一框架下建模，为MESES类数据提供了有效的预训练范式。

Abstract: Human mobility differs from text and from generic time series in three structural ways: visits are tuple-valued events whose meaning depends on the joint distribution over location, time, and activity; users carry persistent signatures across trajectories; and visits are not independent across users, since co-location at shared places is a primary signal. Existing pre-training recipes for mobility import objectives from language modeling, treating trajectories as sentences and visits as tokens, an analogy that fails against each of the three properties above. These properties define a broader class, multi-entity spatiotemporal event streams (MESES), spanning enterprise authentication logs, electronic health records, and other event-stream domains where entities share infrastructure, schedules, or contexts. We make the properties precise as three axioms that any pre-training framework for MESES should satisfy, and introduce TraXion, whose objectives and architecture are jointly designed to meet them. A single TraXion checkpoint per dataset beats task-specific baselines on every task across six public mobility datasets covering anomaly detection, next-POI recommendation, next-visit prediction, and social-link prediction. The same recipe, applied unchanged to enterprise authentication logs and ICU mortality prediction, matches or exceeds prior work on both, showing that event streams from domains as different as mobility, security, and healthcare can be modeled under a single framework.

</details>


### [147] [Same Signal, Opposite Meaning: Direction-Informed Adaptive Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06908)
*Ziming Li,Jiatan Huang,Xiaoguang Guo,Guilin Wang,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有自适应测试时计算方法中“门控信号方向不稳定”的问题，提出DIAL方法通过反事实探索学习状态特征的效用方向，实现了更好的成功-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应测试时计算方法通常基于置信度、不确定性或难度进行门控，假设存在固定的方向：门控信号→计算需求→计算价值。作者发现这种对齐是不稳定的，相同的信号在不同设置下可能预测截然相反的结果，导致错误方向的门控反而会选择有害状态，降低性能。这反映了“计算需求”与“计算适用性”之间的根本区别。

Method: 提出DIAL（Direction-Informed Adaptive Learning）方法：一种稀疏门控机制，通过信号无关的反事实探索来学习每个（环境, 骨干网络）组合下状态特征的效用方向，而非依赖固定方向的门控信号假设。

Result: 在6个环境和3个骨干网络上的实验表明，DIAL相比固定方向基线方法能够实现更强的总体成功-成本权衡。证明了根据具体环境-骨干网络组合学习效用方向的重要性。

Conclusion: 固定方向的门控方法在异构设置中不可靠，因为门控信号与计算效用之间的对齐是不稳定的。通过区分计算需求和计算适用性，并学习状态特征的效用方向，DIAL有效解决了这一问题，为LLM代理的自适应测试时计算提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Adaptive test-time compute for LLM agents aims to invoke extra computation only when it improves performance. Existing methods typically use confidence-, uncertainty-, or difficulty-based gates, assuming a fixed direction from the gating signal through compute need to the value of computation. This makes gating a utility-calibration problem: gating signals should align with whether extra computation improves the final outcome over the base policy. We show that this alignment is unstable: the same signal predicts rollout benefit in one setting and rollout harm in another, with reversals across environments and backbones even when the task is fixed. Wrong-direction gates can therefore worsen performance by precisely selecting harmful states. This reversal reflects a deeper distinction between compute need and compute suitability: a high uncertainty signal may indicate decision-difficult states where rollouts help compare alternatives, or intervention-unsuitable states where the current context does not support useful rollout-based improvement. Under this two-source model, fixed-direction gates are unreliable across heterogeneous settings. To address this, we propose DIAL (Direction-Informed Adaptive Learning), a sparse gate trained from signal-agnostic counterfactual exploration to learn the utility direction of state features per (environment, backbone). Across six environments and three backbones, DIAL yields a stronger overall success-cost trade-off than fixed-direction baselines.

</details>


### [148] [Dual-Scale Temporal Fusion Reveals Structured Predictability in Subseasonal-to-Seasonal Temperature Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06911)
*Elnaz Bashir,Jiali Wang,Lin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双尺度学习框架，通过融合历史气候背景和近期天气演变，揭示了S2S预测能力是由季节和地理调节的多尺度结构决定的，而非单纯随预报时效衰减，从而提升了30-90天温度预报的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 次季节至季节（S2S）温度预报对农业、能源规划和极端天气风险管理至关重要，但其可靠性在不同季节和地区差异显著。传统观点将预报技巧主要归因于预报时效（lead time），但这无法完全解释预测能力的时空分布特征，因此需要深入理解并利用S2S预测性的内在结构。

Method: 开发了一种双尺度学习框架，将日历对齐的历史气候背景与时效匹配的近期天气演变分离，并通过空间自适应融合机制将两者结合。此外，引入了拓扑感知的结构约束以增强预报场的空间一致性。

Result: 融合权重的学习结果表明，两种时间尺度的贡献比重随季节和地理位置系统性变化：冬季时，年际背景在预报难度最大的高纬度和复杂地形区域占主导；夏季时，全域内的时间贡献更为均衡。这种空间显式的预测性重组是影响S2S预报技巧的主要因素，而非简单的时效衰减。拓扑约束进一步提升了复杂地形上的空间相干性。

Conclusion: S2S预测性应被视为一种结构化的多尺度现象，而非单纯的时效衰减过程。该框架不仅提供了更具可解释性的预报基础，有效提升了30-90天窗口内的预报稳定性，也为改进预报系统及其实际应用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) temperature forecasts, spanning several weeks to a few months, are critically needed in agriculture practice, energy planning, and extreme-weather induced risk management, yet their reliability varies substantially across seasons and regions. Forecast skill is often attributed primarily to lead time, but this perspective does not fully explain the spatiotemporal patterns of predictability. Here we show that S2S predictability is organized across interacting temporal components, spatial heterogeneity, and large-scale pattern coherence, and that this structure can be explicitly characterized and exploited. We develop a dual-scale learning framework that separates calendar-aligned historical climate context from lead-time matched recent weather evolution, combining them through spatially adaptive fusion to enable stable temperature forecasts across the 30 to 90-day window. The learned fusion weights reveal that the balance between these two temporal scales shifts systematically with season and geography: during winter, interannual context dominates over high latitudes and complex terrain where forecast is the most difficult, while summer predictions reflect a more balanced temporal contribution across the domain. This spatially explicit reorganization of predictability, rather than simple lead-time decay, emerges as the primary determinant of forecast skill within the subseasonal window. Topology-aware structural constraints further improve spatial coherence of predicted temperature fields, stabilizing large-scale pattern organization particularly over complex terrain. These results reframe S2S predictability as a structured, multi-scale phenomenon, providing a more interpretable foundation for improving forecast systems and informing their use in practice.

</details>


### [149] [LLMs are not (consistently) Bayesian: Quantifying internal (in)consistencies of LLMs' probabilistic beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06915)
*Chacha Chen,Matthew Jörke,Adam Goliński,Masha Fedzechkina,Guillermo Sapiro,Sinead Williamson,Nicholas Foti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将大语言模型（LLM）视为信息处理规则进行研究，发现其概率信念更新方式存在内部不一致性。部分更新方式接近贝叶斯更新，另一些则采用启发式方法。令人惊讶的是，非贝叶斯启发式更新在下游任务表现上往往优于精确贝叶斯计算，揭示了LLM世界模型的错误设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统正被部署于医学、科学和法律等复杂领域。在这些领域中，系统不仅需要给出正确答案，更关键的是能够在新证据出现时正确地表示并更新对世界的不确定性信念。目前尚不清楚LLM如何根据新证据更新其概率信念，这促使研究者对其进行系统性研究。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的研究技术，将LLM视为信息处理规则，并利用“信息处理差距”这一指标来研究LLM在根据证据更新概率信念时的内部一致性问题。实验评估了LLM将证据纳入信念的多种方式。

Result: 实验发现LLM的信念更新方式呈现两种模式：部分方法产生（近乎）贝叶斯更新，另一部分则使用学习到的启发式方法。出人意料的是，非贝叶斯启发式更新在下游任务性能上往往优于精确贝叶斯计算，这一发现表明LLM对世界的概率模型存在设定错误。研究还展示了该指标如何为识别LLM推理系统的问题提供诊断工具。

Conclusion: LLM的概率信念更新机制存在内部不一致性。尽管某些更新方式符合贝叶斯原则，但表现更好的却是非贝叶斯启发式方法，这揭示了LLM内在世界模型的错误设定。本研究提出的测量方法可作为诊断工具，帮助识别基于LLM的推理系统所存在的问题。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are being deployed in complex domains such as medicine, science, and law, where it is important that they not only produce correct answers, but also represent and update uncertain beliefs about the world as new evidence arrives. We introduce the novel technique of studying LLMs as information processing rules and utilize the information processing gap to study the internal (in)consistencies of how LLMs update their probabilistic beliefs from evidence. Our extensive experiments evaluate multiple approaches in which LLMs can incorporate evidence into their beliefs. Some of these approaches produce (nearly) Bayesian updates; others seem to use a learned heuristic. Surprisingly, the non-Bayesian heuristic updates often outperform exact Bayesian computation in terms of downstream task performance -- indicating the LLMs' probabilistic models of the world are misspecified. Lastly, we show how our measure can provide diagnostics to identify issues with LLM-powered inferential systems.

</details>


### [150] [Tyche: One Step Flow for Efficient Probabilistic Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06916)
*Fan Xu,Yuan Gao,Kun Wang,Rui Su,Fenghua Ling,Hao Wu,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Tyche，一种用于高效概率天气预报的单步条件流模型，仅需一次函数评估即可达到或超越现有最先进的多步生成基线和ECMWF IFS集合预报的技能和校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率天气预报需要校准的分布，现有的数据驱动系统虽然确定性技能显著，基于扩散的集合预报也改善了样本真实性和不确定性量化，但其推理成本随预报时效、集合大小和去噪步数增加而显著上升，导致大型业务集合预报成本高昂。

Method: 提出Tyche，一种单步条件流模型：1) 使用目标感知平均速度流直接将高斯噪声映射到未来天气状态（1-NFE）；2) 推导JVP正则化矫正目标，在不显式形成雅可比矩阵的情况下强制源和目标流时间步之间的时间自一致性；3) 使用各向同性Swin风格变换器参数化传输场，保持精细空间结构；4) 引入基于滚动的微调阶段和课程CRPS校准监督以提高自回归预报下的集合可靠性。

Result: 在ERA5数据集（1.5°分辨率，6小时时间步）上的实验表明，Tyche仅使用单次NFE，在预报技能和校准方面匹配或超越了最先进的多步生成基线和业务ECMWF IFS集合预报。

Conclusion: Tyche成功实现了高效的概率天气预报，通过单步传输大幅降低了推理成本，同时保持了高预报技能和良好的集合校准，为业务化大规模集合预报提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic weather forecasting requires not only accurate trajectories, but calibrated distributions over plausible atmospheric futures. Recent data-driven systems have achieved remarkable deterministic skill, and diffusion-based ensemble forecasters have substantially improved sample realism and uncertainty quantification. However, their inference cost scales with forecast horizon, ensemble size, and the number of denoising steps required for each transition, making large operational ensembles expensive. To address this, we present Tyche, a one-step conditional flow model for efficient probabilistic weather forecasting. Tyche models the conditional forecast distribution with a destination-aware average-velocity flow that maps Gaussian noise directly to future weather states in a single function evaluation (1-NFE). To make this one-step transport learnable in high-dimensional geophysical fields, we derive a JVP-regularized rectification objective that enforces temporal self-consistency across source and destination flow timesteps without explicitly forming Jacobians. The transport field is parameterized by an isotropic Swin-style transformer that preserves fine-scale spatial structure while remaining scalable on global grids. To improve ensemble reliability under autoregressive forecasting, we further introduce a rollout-based finetuning stage with curriculum CRPS calibration supervision. Experiments on ERA5 at 1.5$^\circ$ and 6-hour resolution show that our Tyche, using merely a single NFE, matches or exceeds the forecast skill and calibration of state-of-the-art multi-step generative baselines and the operational ECMWF IFS ensemble.

</details>


### [151] [Target-Aware Data Augmentation for SAT Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06931)
*Eshed Gal,Uri Ascher,Eldad Haber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对NP难问题中求解器标注数据成本高昂的瓶颈，提出了一种无需求解器、目标感知的SAT实例数据生成框架，并设计了融合约束违反残差的线性规划感知图神经网络（LPGNN）。该方法实现了数据生成速度的数量级提升，且生成的合成数据能有效增强基于求解器标注的数据集，用于GNN的SAT预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的方法在解决NP难问题（如布尔可满足性问题SAT）方面展现出潜力，但其进展受限于标注训练数据生成成本过高。现有流水线依赖“求解器在环”标注，随问题规模增大扩展性差，限制了监督数据的规模，阻碍了机器学习捕获复杂组合问题结构的 broader 目标。

Method: 1. 提出一种目标感知、无需求解器的数据生成框架，通过构造方式直接生成正确标注的SAT和UNSAT实例，避免了昂贵的求解器调用，并使生成实例的结构属性与目标基准对齐。\n2. 开发一种线性规划感知图神经网络（LPGNN）架构，将约束违反残差融入消息传递过程，使模型能够利用底层的优化结构。

Result: 该方法在数据生成速度上实现了数量级的提升，并证明与基准对齐的合成数据可以有效增强基于求解器标注的数据集，用于基于GNN的SAT预测任务。

Conclusion: 本研究支持了一种面向NP难问题的以数据为中心的范式，即可扩展、任务对齐的数据生成与模型设计同样关键。通过消除求解器依赖并生成结构对齐的合成数据，有效缓解了数据稀缺瓶颈。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to NP-hard problems have shown increasing promise, but their progress is fundamentally constrained by the high cost of generating labeled training data. In domains such as Boolean satisfiability (SAT), standard pipelines rely on solver-in-the-loop labeling, which scales poorly with problem size and limits the amount of usable supervision. This bottleneck hinders the broader goal of leveraging machine learning to capture structure in hard combinatorial problems. In this work, we propose a target-aware, solver-free data generation framework for SAT that produces correctly labeled SAT and UNSAT instances by construction, eliminating the need for expensive solver calls. Our method aligns generated instances with the structural properties of a target benchmark, making synthetic data effective for downstream learning. We further develop a linear-programming-aware graph neural network (LPGNN) architecture that incorporates constraint-violation residuals into message passing, enabling the model to exploit underlying optimization structure. Together, these contributions support a data-centric paradigm for learning on NP-hard problems, where scalable, task-aligned data generation is as critical as model design. Our approach yields orders-of-magnitude speedups in data generation, demonstrating that benchmark-aligned synthetic data can effectively augment solver-labeled datasets for GNN-based SAT prediction.

</details>


### [152] [Learned Lyapunov Shielding for Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06934)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione,Adriano Fagiolini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Slotine-Li自适应控制器与三个学习组件相结合，通过闭式安全滤波器实现了数据驱动增强与理论保证的融合，在机械臂实验中显著降低了跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应控制器在面对复杂未建模动态（如非线性摩擦、变载荷）时性能受限。本文旨在通过引入学习组件增强控制性能，同时保持稳定性保证和安全性约束，解决纯数据驱动方法缺乏理论保证的问题。

Method: 提出三种学习组件：1) 基于Cholesky参数化的结构化二次Lyapunov函数V_ψ；2) 添加有界扭矩修正的残差Soft Actor-Critic策略；3) 估计未建模动态的物理信息神经网络。设计了基于单仿射约束的闭式安全滤波器，无需在线QP求解器即可将策略输出投影到安全集。采用三时间尺度策略-证书-乘子更新算法。

Result: 证明了安全滤波器在控制退化集漂移衰减条件下的全局可行性；建立了精确屏蔽下的指数稳定性和鲁棒扩展；证明了三时间尺度更新几乎必然收敛到KKT点；给出了证书在紧集上的PAC泛化界。在2自由度机械臂实验中，跟踪误差在标称摩擦下降低41%，在激进摩擦下降低24%。在7自由度Franka Panda机械臂上验证了工业规模的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 学习的Lyapunov证书是经验增益的主要贡献者。研究明确了在何种条件下可期望超越精确模型基线的增益，并记录了学习证书的热启动病态问题，这对实际部署具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: We augment the Slotine--Li adaptive controller for Euler--Lagrange systems with three learned components: a structured-quadratic Lyapunov function \(V_ψ\) whose positive-definiteness follows from a Cholesky parameterization, a residual Soft Actor--Critic policy that adds bounded torque corrections to the analytic baseline, and a physics-informed neural network that estimates unmodeled dynamics. A closed-form safety filter, derived from the single affine constraint \(\dot V_ψ+ αV_ψ\le 0\), projects every policy output onto the safe set without requiring an online QP solver. We prove: global feasibility of the filter under a drift-decay condition on the control-degeneracy set; exponential stability under exact shielding, with a robust extension whose margin depends on the PINN approximation error; almost-sure convergence of the three-timescale policy--certificate--multiplier updates to a KKT point; and a PAC generalization bound for the certificate over compacts. On a 2-DOF manipulator with nonlinear friction and variable payload, the learned certificate accounts for most of the empirical gain: tracking error drops by 41\% on nominal friction and 24\% on aggressive friction at the centroid of the training distribution. A 7-DOF scalability study on a Franka Emika Panda confirms clean convergence of the full pipeline at industrial scale, identifies the conditions under which gains over exact model-based baselines should and should not be expected, and documents a warm-start pathology of the learned certificate that has practical implications for deployment.

</details>


### [153] [A Reproducible Optimisation Protocol for Calibrating Prompt-Based Large Language Model Workflows in Evidence Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06937)
*Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对提示词驱动的大语言模型（LLM）在结构化证据综合任务中的可复现校准工作流，通过分离任务规则与提示词框架、引入优化机制与可溯源制品，实现了更稳健的科学任务自动化执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的提示词工程多依赖人工试错，缺乏可复现性与可追溯性。在结构化证据综合（如系统综述筛选）这类对准确性要求极高的科学任务中，如何建立一种标准化、可验证且能沉淀为可复用制品的校准流程，是亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: 该方法将科学任务的定义规则与可变的提示词框架（prompt harness）解耦。利用DSPy和GEPA等工具，基于标注数据与显式任务指标，通过大型反思模型（reflection LLM）引导对小型学生模型（student LLM）的提示词框架进行优化。最终将校准后的工作流保存为包含规范、指标、设置和评估痕迹的可检视制品。

Result: 以文献标题与摘要筛选作为验证案例，展示了该工作流的完整流程，包括编译、制品往返（artefact round-tripping）以及优化预算对小型模型性能的影响，证明了方法的有效性与可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为科学证据综合任务提供了一套结构化、可复现且可审计的LLM校准范式，其底层逻辑具有良好的通用性，可迁移至支持结构化任务定义、指标引导搜索与制品复用的其他提示词优化框架。

Abstract: This methods article presents a reproducible calibration workflow for prompt-based large language models (LLMs) in structured evidence-synthesis tasks. The method separates the rules that define the scientific task from the mutable prompt harness that frames and applies them. It optimises that harness against labelled or reference examples and an explicit task metric, then preserves the calibrated workflow as an inspectable artefact with its specification, metric, settings, and evaluation traces. The example code instantiates the protocol with DSPy and GEPA tools, but the underlying logic can transfer to other prompt-optimisation frameworks that support structured task definitions, metric-guided search, and artefact reuse. Title and abstract screening is the worked validation case because it provides labelled benchmark data and clear evaluation metrics. The demonstrated workflow uses a smaller student LLM for performing the scientific task execution and a larger reflection LLM to steer the prompt optimisation process during calibration. This work shows compilation, artefact round-tripping, and how optimisation budget affects a smaller student model.

</details>


### [154] [A Generalized Singular Value Theory for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06938)
*Brian Charles Brown,Robert Bridges,David Grimsman,Mauricio Munoz,Sean Warnick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于抽象GSVD理论，证明了现代神经网络架构可表示为左可逆的非线性部分加最终线性层，且该非线性部分可保持范数，从而建立了特征空间与输入空间距离的直接校准关系，并展示了其在对抗样本检测中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络缺乏输入空间与特征空间之间的明确距离映射关系，这使得理解模型对输入扰动的敏感度变得困难。作者旨在构建一种保持范数的左可逆表示，以实现特征空间距离与输入空间距离的直接校准，从而为对抗性扰动检测、模型偏差分析等应用提供理论基础。

Method: 1. 基于Brown等人[2025]的抽象广义奇异值分解(GSVD)理论进行理论推导；2. 证明现代神经网络架构可分解为左可逆的非线性部分和最终线性层，且保持输入输出行为不变；3. 提出数据驱动的算法从训练好的模型中估计该表示；4. 设计一种便于该分解的模型架构；5. 开发基于学习到的表示识别对抗性扰动的方法。

Result: 证明了大多数现代神经架构存在广义SVD表示，其非线性部分左可逆且可保持范数；提供了从训练模型估计该表示的数据驱动算法；成功展示了该表示可用于识别模型输入的对抗性扰动。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了神经网络输入空间与特征空间距离的校准理论框架，为对抗样本检测、模型偏差分析和可逆性研究等方向的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Building on the abstract Generalized Singular Value Decomposition (GSVD) theory of Brown et al. [2025], we prove that most modern neural architectures admit a generalized SVD representation in which they are left-invertible before a final linear layer, with no change in input-output behavior. Furthermore, the left-invertible nonlinear portion of the input-output behavior can be made to be \emph{norm preserving}, meaning that perturbations in the left-invertible ``embedding'' (the activations prior to the final linear layer in this representation) correspond proportionally to changes in the input, i.e., distance in feature space can be calibrated directly to distance in input space. We provide a data-driven algorithm for estimating this representation from trained models and propose a model architecture that naturally facilitates the decomposition. We then provide a proof-of-concept that the learned representation can be used to identify adversarial perturbations to model inputs, and develop the theory necessary for future applications to areas such as model bias and invertibility.

</details>


### [155] [Bias and Uncertainty in LLM-as-a-Judge Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06939)
*James Fiedler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM-as-a-Judge评估存在系统性偏差，现有纠偏方法的可靠性高度依赖裁判质量和校准稳定性，共享校准可能导致严重的比较偏差甚至方向反转。作者提出裁判质量(J)和跨模型校准不稳定性(ΔJ)作为诊断指标，用于判断纠偏估计的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge评估已成为评估基础模型性能的标准工具，但原始裁判输出存在系统性偏差。虽有研究提出纠偏估计器，但其可靠性严重依赖裁判质量和校准稳定性，尤其是共享校准的比较方法可能产生严重偏差，甚至出现高置信度的错误方向估计。

Method: 通过理论分析、仿真实验（针对裁判质量J和跨模型校准不稳定性ΔJ）以及MMLU-Pro真实数据案例研究（包含符号反转现象），系统性地研究了纠偏方法的失效模式。

Result: 发现共享校准在跨模型比较中可能引入严重偏差，包括估计方向错误但置信度极高的情况；提出了J和ΔJ作为诊断指标，用于判断何时纠偏估计（特别是共享校准比较）可能不可靠。

Conclusion: 为LaaJ评估提供了基于J和ΔJ诊断指标的报告指南，帮助研究者识别不可靠的纠偏估计，避免因方法局限性导致的错误结论。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation has become a standard tool for assessing base model performance. However, characterizing performance via the naive estimator, i.e., raw judge outputs, is systematically biased. Recent work has proposed estimators to correct this bias, but their reliability depends critically on judge quality and, for model comparisons, on calibration stability. Sharing calibration across compared models is practically attractive but can introduce severe bias, including cases where the comparison estimate points in the wrong direction with high apparent confidence. We study these failure modes through analytical results, simulations over judge quality ($J$) and cross-model calibration instability ($ΔJ$), and a real-data MMLU-Pro case study with sign reversal. We propose $J$ and $ΔJ$ as diagnostics for when corrected estimates, especially shared-calibration comparisons, are likely unreliable, and provide reporting guidance for LaaJ evaluation.

</details>


### [156] [Causal-Aware Foundation-Model for Bilevel Optimization in Discrete Choice Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06941)
*Shivaram Subramanian,Zhengliang Xue,Markus Ettl,Yingdong Lu,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了C3PO网络，一种基于因果感知的基础模型框架，用于在离散选择环境中进行实时最优定价决策，并通过仿真和实际应用验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决服务提供商在异质用户个性化偏好下进行最优品种选择与定价的双层决策问题，同时满足商业约束并提升定价效果。

Method: 提出约束三头定价优化（C3PO）网络，集成价格模仿学习、收入响应多任务学习和价格弹性上下文学习；推理时利用前沿模型提示从行为经济学文献中检索新产品弹性先验；模型在多个经典离散选择模型生成的仿真数据上训练。

Result: 在仿真、合成及真实数据集上展现出强上下文学习能力；随着客户价格敏感度增加，定价KPI提升更显著；在医疗、招标定价、航空辅助产品定价等真实场景中实现跨产品、市场和部门的实质性收益。

Conclusion: C3PO作为因果感知基础模型，能有效解决个性化离散选择环境下的实时最优定价问题，兼具理论创新与实际应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a causal aware foundation-model framework for real time optimal decision making in discrete choice environments. We propose a constrained triple-head price optimization (C3PO) network to solve a bilevel decision problem in which a service provider selects an optimal assortment while heterogeneous users make personalized acceptance or rejection choices optimizing their own personalized preferences. C3PO integrates imitation learning of prices, multi-task learning of revenue responses, and in context learning of price elasticity to generate pricing recommendations while adhering to business constraints. During inference, frontier model prompting retrieves an enhanced elasticity prior for new products from behavioral economics literature, improving pricing effectiveness. We demonstrate strong in context learning performance using simulated, synthetic, and real-world datasets. C3PO is trained on simulated data generated from multiple classical discrete choice models in economics. The model is trained on data comprising simulated customer segments and counterfactual action and outcome pairs and evaluated on randomly generated choice environments with no access to the underlying preference structure. The trained model consistently improves the pricing KPIs, with gains increasing as customer price sensitivity increases. We also deploy the tuned foundation model for optimal pricing in real-world applications such as healthcare, tender pricing, airline ancillary pricing, and other domains, achieving substantial gains across multiple products, markets, and divisions.

</details>


### [157] [ProtoSSL: Interpretable Prototype Learning from Unlabeled Time-Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06943)
*Steven Song,Sahil Sethi,Brett Beaulieu-Jones,Robert L. Grossman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ProtoSSL框架，通过自监督学习从无标签时间序列数据中学习可解释的原型，在低数据量场景下优于监督基线，并能有效泛化到新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的投影式原型网络依赖标签监督，将原型与特定任务绑定，限制了其可重用性并需要大量标注数据。在实际时间序列应用中，获取标注数据成本高昂，且下游任务的标签空间往往事先未知。

Method: ProtoSSL的核心思想是将基序发现与标签对齐解耦。首先，利用直接应用于原型激活的自监督目标函数从无标签数据中学习可重用的原型库；然后，通过高效的分配过程将这些原型对齐到下游任务。该框架分两个阶段：无监督原型学习阶段和有监督任务适配阶段。

Result: 在六个心电图（ECG）数据集上的实验表明，ProtoSSL在低数据量（仅256个标签样本）条件下优于监督原型基线；在全量数据微调后仍保持优势。人类评估研究显示，ProtoSSL生成的原型和解释比直接标签监督学习的结果更受认可。此外，该框架成功扩展到了音频分类任务。

Conclusion: ProtoSSL成功实现了从无标签数据中学习可泛化原型，并能将其有效分配给新的时间序列任务，为在标签稀缺且标签空间未知的场景下构建可解释的时间序列模型提供了新途径。

Abstract: In time-series domains where both predictive performance and interpretability are essential, deep neural networks achieve strong results but provide limited insight into how their predictions are made. Projection-based prototype networks address this limitation by grounding predictions in similarity to representative training examples, enabling case-based explanations and global prototype inspection. However, existing approaches rely on label supervision, tying prototypes to a specific task and requiring large labeled datasets. We introduce ProtoSSL, a novel framework for learning interpretable, projection-based prototypes from unlabeled time-series data and adapting them to downstream tasks. Our key idea is to separate motif discovery from label alignment. ProtoSSL first learns a reusable prototype bank using a self-supervised objective applied directly to prototype activations, and then aligns these prototypes to downstream tasks through an efficient assignment procedure. Across six electrocardiography (ECG) datasets, ProtoSSL improves label efficiency, outperforming supervised prototype baselines in low-data regimes with as few as 256 labeled examples; with fine-tuning, ProtoSSL outperforms supervised prototype baselines at full dataset scale. In a human evaluation study, ProtoSSL produces prototypes and prototype-based explanations that are judged more favorably than those learned with direct label supervision. We further show that the framework extends to audio classification. Thus, ProtoSSL enables both learning generalizable prototypes from unlabeled data before the downstream label space is known, and subsequent assignment of interpretable, projection-grounded prototypes to new time-series tasks.

</details>


### [158] [Adaptive Memory Decay for Log-Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06946)
*Yaxita Amin,Helen Zichen Li,Mengfan Zhang,Samet Ayhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了自适应对数线性注意力机制，通过从输入学习衰减参数λ来改进现有的对数线性注意力模型，在长程记忆任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对数线性注意力通过Fenwick树层次结构在对数线性计算复杂度下平衡记忆容量与效率，但其衰减参数λ是固定且独立于输入的，对所有层次赋予统一权重，导致不必要的刚性，限制了模型的内容自适应能力。

Method: 提出通过轻量级两层MLP直接从输入学习衰减参数λ，生成逐token、逐层次的自适应衰减值。使用softplus激活函数使每个Fenwick树层次独立缩放，避免softmax引入的层次间竞争，同时保持精确的对数线性复杂度且仅增加可忽略的参数开销。

Result: 在关联回忆、选择性复制和语言建模任务上进行评估，输入依赖的衰减机制一致优于基准方法。在长程记忆设置中获得最大提升，而基准方法在这些场景下会出现性能下降或完全崩溃。

Conclusion: 通过使衰减参数适应输入内容而非仅依赖位置，自适应对数线性注意力有效解决了原方法的刚性问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了模型的长程记忆能力。

Abstract: Sequence models face a fundamental tradeoff between memory capacity and computational efficiency. Transformers achieve expressive context modeling at quadratic cost, while linear attention and state-space models run in linear time by compressing context into a fixed-size hidden state, inherently limiting recall. Log-linear attention navigates this tradeoff by organizing memory across a Fenwick tree hierarchy, growing its hidden state logarithmically with sequence length at log-linear compute cost. However, its memory decay parameter λ is fixed and independent of the input, assigning uniform weights across all hierarchy levels regardless of the content, which introduces unnecessary rigidity. We propose learning λ directly from the input via a lightweight two-layer MLP, producing per-token, per-level decay that adapts to content rather than position. A softplus activation lets each Fenwick tree level scale independently, avoiding the inter-level competition that softmax introduces. This modification preserves log-linear complexity exactly and adds negligible parameter overhead. We evaluate on associative recall, selective copying, and language modeling, finding that input-dependent decay consistently outperforms the baseline, with the largest gains in long-range memory settings where baseline λ degrades or collapses entirely.

</details>


### [159] [Rollback-Free Stable Brick Structures Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06947)
*Chenhui Xu,Ziyue Bai,Fuxun Yu,Heng Huang,Jinjun Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出强化学习范式，将物理有效性约束从测试时修正移至训练时优化，实现首个无需回滚的稳定砖块结构生成方法，在保证SOTA生成质量的同时将推理速度提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归3D生成方法在创建物理稳定的砖块结构时，因重力和互连性的严格要求，需在推理时依赖外部物理模拟器进行拒绝采样和逐砖回滚，严重制约了效率。

Method: 提出强化学习范式，利用组装级别的奖励函数（包括碰撞避免、全局连通性、结构互锁和形状一致性），通过策略优化使模型内化物理先验，从而消除推理时的物理校验步骤。

Result: 实验表明该方法在生成质量上达到SOTA水平，同时推理速度相比现有方法提升了数个数量级，实现了首个无需回滚的稳定砖块结构生成。

Conclusion: 通过将物理约束内化到模型训练中，成功解决了砖块结构生成的效率瓶颈，为3D生成领域提供了新的解决思路，并开源了代码、数据集和模型。

Abstract: While autoregressive models have advanced 3D generation, creating physically stable brick structures remains a challenge due to the strict requirements of gravity and interconnectivity. Existing approaches rely on external physical simulators during inference to perform rejection sampling and brick-by-brick rollbacks, which severely bottlenecks efficiency. To address this, we propose a reinforcement learning paradigm that shifts physical validity enforcement from test-time correction to training-time policy optimization. By utilizing assembly-level rewards, the model optimizes for collision avoidance, global connectivity, structural interlocking, and shape conformity. This paradigm allows the model to internalize physical priors, enabling the first rollback-free generation of stable brick structures. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art generation quality while accelerating inference speed by orders of magnitude. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/miniHuiHui/STABLE. Our models are available at https://huggingface.co/miniHui/STABLE.

</details>


### [160] [Kurtosis-Guided Denoising Score Matching for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06955)
*Victor Livernoche,Jie Zan,Reihaneh Rabbany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出K-DSM方法，通过峰度自适应设置噪声尺度，使单尺度DSM模型成为强异常检测器，在半监督和完全无监督设置下均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去噪分数匹配(DSM)中噪声尺度选择困难：噪声过小导致稀疏区域分数估计不稳定，噪声过大破坏局部结构并削弱异常敏感性；且异常检测场景下无法进行超参数调优。

Method: 提出基于峰度的噪声缩放(K-DSM)，根据边缘分布形状为每个特征设置噪声水平；结合轻量级EMA教师过滤规则，在每次梯度更新前移除低密度训练点。

Result: 在标准表格异常检测基准测试中，K-DSM在半监督设置下达到最优性能；配合EMA-teacher过滤规则，在完全无监督（污染）设置下也表现优异。

Conclusion: 精心训练的单尺度模型即可成为强异常检测器，无需多尺度或噪声条件训练；简单、数据自适应的噪声缩放能实现鲁棒异常检测，减少对超参数调优的依赖。

Abstract: Denoising score matching (DSM) provides a way to learn data distributions by training a neural network to recover the score function, defined as the gradient of the log density, from noise-corrupted samples. Once trained, the score magnitude at a test point reflects how consistent that point is with the learned distribution, making it a natural anomaly signal. The key practical challenge is selecting the perturbation scale: too little noise yields unstable score estimates in sparse regions, while too much erases local structure and weakens anomaly sensitivity. This is compounded by the difficulty of hyperparameter tuning when anomalies are unknown and no validation set is available. We introduce kurtosis-based noise scaling (K-DSM), a per-feature scheme that sets noise levels from the shape of each marginal distribution, improving coverage of low-density regions and precision in high-density regions without extra model complexity. Contrary to prior claims that multi-scale or noise-conditioned training is necessary, we find that a carefully trained single-scale model is already a strong anomaly detector. On standard tabular anomaly detection benchmarks, K-DSM achieves state-of-the-art performance in the semi-supervised setting. When combined with a lightweight EMA-teacher filtering rule that removes low-density training points before each gradient step, it also achieves strong performance in the fully unsupervised (contaminated) setting, suggesting that simple, data-adaptive noise scaling enables robust anomaly detection while reducing reliance on hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [161] [$f$-Divergence Regularized RLHF: Two Tales of Sampling and Unified Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06977)
*Di Wu,Chengshuai Shi,Jing Yang,Cong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了在线RLHF中一般$f$-散度正则化的理论框架，提出了两种算法并证明了其具有$O(\log T)$的遗憾界和$O(1/T)$的次优性差距，填补了该领域理论分析的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLHF方法主要依赖反向KL正则化，尽管已有研究开始探索其他散度（如前向KL、卡方散度）作为正则化项，但对于一般$f$-散度正则化的统一理论理解仍然缺失。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为在线RLHF中的一般$f$-散度正则化目标建立全面的理论框架。

Method: 从整体视角研究整个$f$-散度函数类，提出了两种基于不同采样原理的算法。第一种算法扩展了经典的乐观主义原则，通过精心设计的探索奖励实现；第二种算法引入了新方法，利用在$f$-散度正则化下最优策略对奖励扰动的敏感性。

Result: 理论分析表明两种算法均可实现$O(\log T)$的遗憾界和$O(1/T)$的次优性差距，建立了算法的可证明效率，这是在线RLHF在一般$f$-散度正则化下的首个性能界限。

Conclusion: 本文成功为在线RLHF建立了一般$f$-散度正则化的理论框架，所提出的两种算法均具有可证明的理论保证，为理解和改进RLHF中的散度正则化选择提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a cornerstone technique for post-training large language models. While most existing approaches rely on the reverse KL-regularization, recent empirical studies have begun exploring alternative divergences (e.g., forward KL, chi-squared) as regularizers in RLHF. However, a unified theoretical understanding of general $f$-divergence regularization remains under-explored. To fill this gap, this work develops a comprehensive theoretical framework for online RLHF with a general $f$-divergence regularized objective. Rather than treating each possible divergence function individually, we adopt a holistic perspective across the entire function class and propose two algorithms based on distinct sampling principles. The first extends the classical optimism principle with a carefully designed exploration bonus, while the second introduces a new method that exploits the sensitivity of the optimal policy to reward perturbations under $f$-divergence regularization. Theoretical analysis shows that $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(1/T)$ sub-optimality gap are achievable, establishing provable efficiency of both algorithms and, to the best of our knowledge, the first performance bounds for online RLHF under general $f$-divergence regularization.

</details>


### [162] [PLOT: Progressive Localization via Optimal Transport in Neural Causal Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06979)
*Jonathn Chang,Arya Datla,Ziv Goldfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PLOT框架，利用最优传输理论从输出效应几何中定位因果变量，大幅降低了因果抽象中寻找干预句柄的计算成本，在保持准确率的同时显著提升了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果抽象方法（如DAS）虽然能学习表达性强的子空间干预，但相关神经位点是未知的，寻找干预句柄需要对候选位点进行计算负担极重的搜索，这限制了因果抽象研究在大规模模型上的应用。

Method: 提出PLOT（基于最优传输的渐进式定位）框架。该方法拟合抽象变量与候选神经位点之间的最优传输耦合，产生全局软对应关系，并校准为干预句柄。对于简单场景，单次耦合即可；对于大型模型，采用渐进式方法，从token、时间步、层等粗粒度位点逐步细化到坐标组或PCA跨度等细粒度支持，并可选择性指导DAS进行进一步搜索。

Result: 在多种复杂度的实验中，仅使用传输的PLOT句柄速度极快且准确率具有竞争力；PLOT引导的DAS以完整DAS运行时间的一小部分达到了DAS水平的准确率。

Conclusion: PLOT为大规模因果抽象研究提供了一个高效的定位引擎，成功解决了传统方法计算成本高昂的问题，实现了效率与准确性的良好平衡。

Abstract: Causal abstraction offers a principled framework for mechanistic interpretability, aligning a high-level causal model with the low-level computation realized by a neural network through counterfactual intervention analysis. Existing methods such as distributed alignment search (DAS) learn expressive subspace interventions, but the relevant neural site is unknown a priori, so finding a handle requires a computationally burdensome search over candidate sites. We introduce PLOT (Progressive Localization via Optimal Transport), a transport-based framework that localizes causal variables from the output effect geometry of abstract and neural interventions. PLOT fits an optimal transport coupling between abstract variables and candidate neural sites, yielding a global soft correspondence that can be calibrated into intervention handles. In simple settings, a single coupling over individual neurons suffices. In larger models, PLOT is applied progressively, moving from coarse sites such as tokens, timesteps, or layers to finer supports such as coordinate groups or PCA spans, and optionally guiding DAS based on the localized signal. Across experiments of increasing complexity, transport-only PLOT handles are exceedingly fast and competitive on accuracy, while PLOT-guided DAS reaches DAS-level accuracy at a fraction of full DAS runtime, providing an efficient localization engine for causal abstraction research at scale.

</details>


### [163] [FastOmniTMAE: Parallel Clause Learning for Scalable and Hardware-Efficient Tsetlin Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06982)
*Ahmed K. Kadhim,Lei Jiao,Rishad Shafik,Ole-Christoffer Granmo,Mayur Kishor Shende*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FastOmniTMAE，一种改进的基于Tsetlin Machine的静态词嵌入模型，通过两阶段并行训练过程实现了比原Omni TM-AE高达5倍的训练加速，并在SoC-FPGA平台上实现了高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度嵌入模型（如BERT）架构复杂，而简单的模型（如Word2Vec）可解释性有限。Tsetlin Machine提供了基于逻辑的学习范式，但其代表模型Omni TM-AE训练过程缓慢，且在传统GPU上效率受限。

Method: 将Omni TM-AE的顺序训练依赖重新表述为两阶段并行过程（评估和更新），消除了训练依赖瓶颈；并在SoC-FPGA平台上实现可重用的加速器设计。

Result: 在Single-Run Multi-Environment基准测试中，FastOmniTMAE在分类任务上训练速度提升高达5倍，同时保持相当的嵌入质量（Spearman和Kendall相似度指标）；在资源受限的FPGA上达到0.669的相似度分数，在UltraScale+ SoC上达到0.696。

Conclusion: FastOmniTMAE成功实现了高效且可解释的基于逻辑的嵌入训练，在小硬件资源占用下达到了良好的性能表现，证明了Tsetlin Machine在嵌入学习任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Embedding models in natural language processing (NLP) increasingly rely on deep architectures such as BERT, while simpler models such as Word2Vec provide efficient representations but limited interpretability. The Tsetlin Machine (TM) offers an alternative logic-based learning paradigm. Omni TM Autoencoder (Omni TM-AE) applies this paradigm to static embedding by exploiting automaton state distributions within a single clause layer, but its training process remains slow. In this work, we propose FastOmniTMAE, a reformulation of Omni TM-AE that replaces sequential training dependencies with a two-stage parallel process: evaluation and update. Using a Single-Run Multi-Environment Benchmark covering classification, similarity, and clustering, FastOmniTMAE achieves up to 5$\times$ faster training in classification while maintaining comparable embedding quality under both Spearman and Kendall similarity measures. To address the limited efficiency of TM training on conventional GPUs, we further implement FastOmniTMAE as a reusable accelerator on SoC-FPGA platforms. The Multi-Hardware Benchmark shows that FastOmniTMAE achieves similarity scores of 0.669 on a resource-constrained FPGA and 0.696 on an UltraScale+ SoC, demonstrating efficient logic-based embedding training with a small hardware footprint.

</details>


### [164] [Response Time Enhances Alignment with Heterogeneous Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06987)
*Federico Echenique,Alireza Fallah,Baihe Huang,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用用户响应时间这一辅助信号，结合漂移扩散模型（DDM），解决了在匿名、异质标注者场景下大语言模型偏好对齐中的不可识别性问题，实现了对真实群体平均偏好的一致估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM偏好对齐方法通常将反馈聚合为单一奖励模型，隐含假设所有标注者共享相同偏好。然而现实中的标注者高度异质且通常匿名，仅依赖二元选择数据会导致学习策略产生扭曲，使得真实的群体平均偏好无法被识别。

Method: 将每个决策建模为漂移扩散模型（DDM），通过在偏好数据集中增加用户的响应时间这一辅助信号，提出了一种新颖的一致估计器来估计异质偏好，从而校正仅使用选择标签带来的偏差。

Result: 理论上证明了该估计器即使在极端情况下（每个匿名标注者仅贡献单一选择）也能渐近收敛到真实平均偏好。在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法持续优于会陷入偏差底线的标准基线方法。

Conclusion: 由于响应时间本质上可以免费记录且无需用户追踪或身份识别，该方法为未来的数据收集流程提供了新机遇，能够在不需要用户级标识符或重复 elicitation 的情况下改善社会效益。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) to human preferences typically relies on aggregating pooled feedback into a single reward model. However, this standard approach assumes that all labelers share the same underlying preferences, ignoring the fact that real-world labelers are highly heterogeneous and usually anonymous. Consequently, relying solely on binary choice data fundamentally distorts the learned policy, making the true population-average preference unidentifiable. To overcome this critical limitation, we demonstrate that augmenting preference datasets with a simple, secondary signal -- the user's response time -- can restore the identifiability of the population's average preference. By modeling each decision as a Drift-Diffusion Model (DDM), we introduce a novel, consistent estimator of heterogeneous preferences that successfully corrects the distortions of standard choice-only labels. We prove that our estimator asymptotically converges to the true average preference even in extreme cases where each anonymous labeler contributes only a single choice. Empirically, across both synthetic and real-world datasets, our method consistently outperforms standard baselines that otherwise fail and plateau at a bias floor. Because response times are essentially free to record and require zero user tracking or identification, our results bring promises and open up new opportunities for future data-collection pipelines to improve the social benefit without requiring user-level identifiers or repeated elicitations.

</details>


### [165] [Why Does Agentic Safety Fail to Generalize Across Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06992)
*Yonatan Slutzky,Yotam Alexander,Tomer Slor,Yoav Nagel,Nadav Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实验两方面证明，智能体安全性的泛化难度高于任务执行本身的泛化难度，这是安全问题的固有属性，而非仅仅是训练方法的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多任务设置中，AI智能体需要根据测试时的指令执行未见过的任务。经验证据表明，虽然智能体执行任务的能力可以泛化到新任务，但安全执行的能力往往无法随之泛化。本文旨在探究这种安全性泛化失败是否仅源于训练方法的限制，还是反映了安全本身的某种固有属性。

Method: 1. 理论分析：在线性二次控制（Linear-Quadratic Control）框架下引入$H_{\infty}$-鲁棒性约束，从数学上证明从任务规范到最优控制器的映射在有安全要求时比无安全要求时具有更高的Lipschitz常数。\n2. 实验验证：在四旋翼无人机导航模拟环境中使用神经网络智能体，以及在CRM（Contextual Relation Management）框架中使用大语言模型（LLM）智能体进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 理论结果：证明了从任务规范到最优安全控制器的映射具有更高的Lipschitz常数，这意味着该映射对任务规范的变化更敏感，泛化难度更大。该Lipschitz界具有独立的理论价值。\n2. 实验结果：在模拟四旋翼导航和LLM智能体CRM实验中均验证了理论结论，表明安全性泛化失败是一个普遍存在的现象。

Conclusion: 安全性无法随任务泛化并非单纯的训练方法问题，而是安全本身的固有属性——任务与其安全执行之间的关系比任务与其执行本身之间的关系更为复杂。这表明当前增强智能体安全性的努力可能不足，未来需要从根本上不同的方法来解决这一问题。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed in multi-task settings, where the task to perform is specified at test time, and the agent must generalize to unseen tasks. A major concern in such settings is safety: often, an agent must not only execute unseen tasks, but do so while avoiding risks and handling ones that materialize. Empirical evidence suggests that even when the ability to execute generalizes to unseen tasks, the ability to do so safely frequently does not. This paper provides theory and experiments indicating that failures of agentic safety to generalize across tasks are not merely due to limitations of training methods, but reflect an inherent property of safety itself: the relationship between a task and its safe execution is more complex than the relationship between a task and its execution alone. Theoretically, we analyze linear-quadratic control with $H_{\infty}$-robustness, and prove that the mapping from task specification to an optimal controller has higher Lipschitz constant with safety requirements than without, yielding a Lipschitz bound of independent interest. Empirically, we demonstrate our conclusions in simulated quadcopter navigation with a neural network agent and in CRM with an LLM agent. Our findings suggest that current efforts to enhance agentic safety may be insufficient, and point to a need for fundamentally different approaches.

</details>


### [166] [Echo: KV-Cache-Free Associative Recall with Spectral Koopman Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06997)
*Anupama Sridhar,Alexander Johansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Echo架构，通过频谱Koopman注意力（SKA）替代传统注意力层，在常量内存下实现100%的关联检索准确率，解决了SSM的记忆悬崖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链推理和智能体工具调用产生数万token的轨迹，Transformer的KV缓存随序列长度线性增长导致内存瓶颈；状态空间模型（SSM）虽提供常量内存递归，但存在记忆悬崖问题——当存储事实与查询之间的间隔超过递归状态的有效视界时，检索准确率急剧下降。

Method: 引入Echo架构，核心为频谱Koopman注意力（SKA），作为注意力层的直接替代。SKA通过核岭回归将频谱线性系统拟合到键值历史，并通过学习的幂迭代滤波器进行检索，所有操作在O(r²)流状态中完成（r为小投影秩），无需KV缓存。该频谱动态算子以常量内存累积充分统计量。

Result: 在Multi-Query关联检索基准上，纯Mamba-2 SSM在所有间隔长度和KV对数量下准确率均接近随机（~3%），而50M参数的SKA增强模型在所有测试配置中达到100%检索准确率，包括4096 token干扰间隔和32个KV对的配置。在包括针在干草堆、工具轨迹和多跳检索等五个额外迁移基准上，SKA持续优于纯SSM和SSM+注意力混合模型。

Conclusion: 频谱算子（而非前缀掩码策略）驱动了检索增益。Echo成功实现了常量推理内存下的高效关联检索，为长序列推理提供了一种无需KV缓存的可行方案。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning and agentic tool-calling produce traces spanning tens of thousands of tokens, yet Transformer KV caches grow linearly with sequence length, creating a memory bottleneck on commodity hardware. State-space models offer constant-memory recurrence but suffer a memory cliff: retrieval accuracy collapses once the gap between a stored fact and its query exceeds the effective horizon of the recurrent state. We introduce Echo, a KV-cache-free associative recall architecture built around Spectral Koopman Attention (SKA); a drop-in replacement for attention layers that augments SSM blocks with a closed-form dynamical operator whose sufficient statistics are accumulated in constant memory with no KV cache. Echo fits a spectral linear system to the key and value history via kernel ridge regression and retrieves through a learned power-iterated filter, all from $O(r^{2})$ streaming state where $r$ is a small projection rank. On the Multi-Query Associative Recall benchmark, a pure Mamba-2 SSM fails to exceed chance accuracy (${\sim}3\%$) across all gap lengths and KV-pair counts, while at the 50M parameter scale SKA-augmented models achieve $100\%$ retrieval accuracy on every configuration tested, including distractor gaps of $4{,}096$ tokens with $32$ KV pairs. Across five additional transfer benchmarks including needle-in-a-haystack, tool-trace, and multi-hop retrieval, SKA consistently outperforms both pure SSM and SSM+Attention hybrids while maintaining constant inference memory. Ablations confirm that the spectral operator, not the prefix masking strategy, drives the retrieval gain.

</details>


### [167] [Inductive Power Grid Cascading Failure Analysis with GRU-Gated Graph Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07010)
*Tianxin Zhou,Xiang Li,Haibing Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于GRU门控图注意力网络的跨电网级联故障预测方法，能够在未见过的电网上进行零样本迁移，无需重新训练即可识别脆弱输电线路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的级联故障脆弱线路识别方法仅在单一电网上训练和评估，难以将学习到的知识迁移到未见过的电网，跨电网知识迁移仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 在有限训练电网的组合级联故障数据上训练单个GRU门控图注意力网络（GRU-gated Graph Attention Network），GRU门控制在每次级联迭代时控制每个节点保留或丢弃的信息，训练后的模型可直接应用于任何未见过的电网而无需重新训练。

Result: 模型在跨时间段和跨领域的多个新电网上实现了零样本迁移，利用训练模型提取的信息，相比现有的结构和电气基线方法，能够持续识别出更多的脆弱线路。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了级联故障知识在不同电网间的迁移，为零样本跨电网脆弱线路识别提供了有效解决方案，在多个实验设置下优于传统基线方法。

Abstract: Identifying vulnerable transmission lines in power grids before a cascading failure occurs is challenging: existing methods can learn inter-line failure correlations from cascade data, but they are trained and evaluated on a single grid, and transferring the learned knowledge to an unseen grid remains an open problem. We address this by training a single Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU)-gated Graph Attention Network on combined cascading failure data from limited training grids and applying it directly to any unseen grid without retraining. A GRU gate controls what information each node retains or discards at each cascade iteration. Empirical evaluation shows that the model transfers zero-shot to multiple new grids spanning inter-time and inter-domain settings. Using information extracted from the trained model, we consistently identify more vulnerable lines than established structural and electrical baselines.

</details>


### [168] [Dual-Agent Co-Training for Health Coaching via Implicit Adversarial Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07011)
*Da Long,Lingyi Fu,Diya Michelle Rao,Jasmine Ruales Carrera,Yang Bai,Shandian Zhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双智能体协同训练框架，通过多维度LLM评判识别帕累托优势响应对优化健康教练，并对抗性训练客户端模拟器，有效提升AI健康教练质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机访谈式健康辅导对改善心理健康和促进行为改变有效，但训练有素的人类教练稀缺且服务成本高昂，限制了受益人群范围，因此需要开发可规模化且经济实惠的AI健康教练。

Method: 提出双智能体框架协同训练健康教练代理和客户端模拟器：教练端利用多维度LLM评判识别帕累托优势响应对进行DPO优化；客户端通过反转偏好进行对抗性训练，形成隐式对抗训练动态，该协同训练过程可自然解释为随机博弈。

Result: 大量实验证明，该方法在多个重要维度上有效提升了健康辅导质量。

Conclusion: 所提出的双智能体协同训练框架突破了现有单边优化方法的局限性，通过教练与客户端的交互式协同训练，更好地探索交互空间并推动目标代理性能边界，为AI健康教练的发展提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Motivational-interviewing-based health coaching is an effective approach for improving mental health and promoting healthy behavior change. However, the scarcity of trained human coaches and the high cost of coaching services make such support inaccessible to many people who could benefit from it. This motivates the development of AI health coaches that can provide scalable and affordable support. Existing methods typically optimize only one side of the interaction: they either train a dialogue agent against a fixed client environment or train a client simulator against a fixed assistant. This one-sided setup can limit exploration of the interaction space and may be inefficient at developing the capabilities required by the target agent and pushing its performance boundaries. In this paper, we propose a dual-agent framework that interactively co-trains both the health coach agent and the client simulator. The coach is optimized with DPO using Pareto-dominant response pairs identified by a multi-dimensional LLM judge. In turn, the client is trained adversarially by reversing these preferences, inducing an implicit adversarial training dynamic. We further show that this co-training process admits a natural stochastic-game interpretation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively improves coaching quality across several important dimensions.

</details>


### [169] [FlashMol: High-Quality Molecule Generation in as Few as Four Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07020)
*Xinyuan Wei,Zian Li,Shaoheng Yan,Cai Zhou,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashMol是一种超快速的分子构象生成模型，仅需4步即可生成高质量分子构象，比传统方法快250倍，同时保持甚至超越了原有1000步教师模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于扩散的分子生成模型（如GeoLDM）需要数百步才能生成分子构象，使得大规模计算药物发现变得不切实际。现有的少步生成方法虽然将步骤减少到12-50步，但往往严重牺牲了样本稳定性。因此需要开发一种既快速又能保持高质量的新型分子生成方法。

Method: 该方法将分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）适应到分子领域，这是一个反向KL散度最小化目标。针对DMD的局部最小化行为，研究者重新规划了分子生成的时间步长，为生成器提供更好的初始化以实现有效蒸馏。同时，为了缓解DMD的模式寻求行为并提高多样性，引入了Jensen-Shannon散度项进行正则化，融合了正向KL散度的均值寻求行为。

Result: 在QM9和GEOM-DRUG数据集上的大量实验表明，FlashMol能够匹配甚至超越原始1000步教师模型的性能，实现了高达250倍的采样加速，同时保持高质量的分子生成质量。

Conclusion: FlashMol成功解决了分子生成中速度与质量的权衡问题，通过创新的蒸馏技术和正则化策略，在极少步数（4步）内实现了高质量的分子构象生成，为大规模计算药物筛选提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating chemically valid 3D molecular conformations is critical for computational drug discovery. Classical diffusion-based models like GeoLDM perform well but require hundreds of steps, making large-scale in silico screening impractical. Recent efforts on few-step molecular generation have accelerated this process to 12-50 steps, but they often largely sacrifice sample stability. In this work, we present FlashMol, an ultra-fast molecule generative model producing high-quality molecular conformations in as few as 4 steps. To achieve this, we adapt distribution matching distillation (DMD) - a reverse KL-divergence minimization objective - to the molecular domain for effective distillation. Considering the local minimization behavior of DMD, we respace the molecule generation timesteps, providing the generator with much better initialization and enables effective distillation. Additionally, to mitigate the mode-seeking behavior of DMD and improve diversity, we further regularize it with a Jensen-Shannon divergence term, which incorporates the mean-seeking behavior of the forward KL divergence. Extensive experiments on QM9 and GEOM-DRUG datasets demonstrate that FlashMol matches and even surpasses the original 1000-step teacher, achieving up to 250$\times$ acceleration in sampling speed while maintaining high molecular quality.

</details>


### [170] [Self Driving Datasets: From 20 Million Papers to Nuanced Biomedical Knowledge at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07022)
*Haydn Jones,Yimeng Zeng,Alden Rose,Li S. Yifei,Yining Huang,Kaiwen Wu,Jiaming Liang,Maggie Ziyu Huan,Yoseph Barash,Cesar de la Fuente-Nunez,Osbert Bastani,Zachary Ives,Mark Yatskar,Jacob R. Gardner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Starling系统，利用LLM将PubMed自动转化为结构化数据集，在规模、准确性和细节保留方面均超越传统人工策展数据库，为AI驱动的治疗设计奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工策展的生物医学数据库（涵盖生物活性、基因组学和化学领域）维护成本高昂、更新滞后于原始文献，且丢弃实验背景信息，导致难以评估数据的正确性和覆盖范围。作者旨在解决这些局限性，将PubMed本身转化为更大、更细致、更准确的结构化数据集。

Method: 研究提出三项核心技术贡献：（1）基于LLM的实体标注流水线，基于9个生物医学本体，在2250万篇论文、2.5万亿token的PubMed语料库中标注了19个类别的45亿个实体；（2）混合稀疏-密集检索系统，支持在标注语料库上进行实体过滤的语义查询；（3）Starling多智能体深度研究系统，仅凭自然语言任务描述即可设计针对精准率和召回率的检索过滤器，归纳提取模式，并输出包含丰富细节字段和支持性段落的结构化记录。
