Result: 在血脑屏障渗透性、口服生物利用度、急性毒性（LD50）、基因-疾病关联、蛋白质亚细胞定位和化学反应六个任务中，Starling生成了约630万条记录（每个任务9.1万至300万条），其中多个数据集据知是该领域最大的公开数据集。前沿模型对提取结果的拒绝率仅为0.6-7.7%，远低于广泛使用的策展数据库的测得错误率（如BBB_Martins为16.5%，Bioavailability_Ma为7.3%）。此外，支持性段落保留了表格数据库丢弃的细节信息，如口服生物利用度可能取决于进食与空腹状态。

Conclusion: 研究表明PubMed可以自主、经济高效地转化为结构化数据集，在规模、准确性和细节保留方面均超越其所替代的策展数据库。该语料库、检索系统和智能体的结合为AI驱动的治疗设计建立了基础，代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Manually curated biomedical repositories -- spanning bioactivity, genomics, and chemistry -- are expensive to maintain, lag behind primary literature, and discard experimental context, obscuring nuances needed to assess data correctness and coverage. We show that PubMed itself can be autonomously and cost-effectively turned into structured datasets that are larger, more nuanced, and more accurate than the curated databases they replace. We present three coupled contributions: (1) an LLM-based entity-tagging pipeline, grounded in nine biomedical ontologies, that tags 4.5B entities across 19 categories in a 22.5M-paper, 2.5T-token PubMed corpus; (2) hybrid sparse-dense retrieval supporting entity-filtered semantic queries over the tagged corpus; and (3) Starling, a multi-agent deep research system that, given only a natural-language task description, designs precision- and recall-targeted retrieval filters, induces an extraction schema, and emits structured records with nuance-rich fields and supporting passages. Across six tasks -- blood-brain barrier permeability, oral bioavailability, acute toxicity (LD50), gene-disease associations, protein subcellular localization, and chemical reactions -- Starling produces ~6.3M records (91K-3M per task); several are, to our knowledge, the largest public datasets for their property. Frontier-model rejection of our extractions is 0.6-7.7% across tasks, far below error rates we measure on widely used curated counterparts (e.g., 16.5% on BBB_Martins, 7.3% on Bioavailability_Ma). Beyond scale and accuracy, the supporting passages carry nuance tabular databases discard -- e.g., oral bioavailability may depend on fed vs. fasted state. Together, the corpus, retrieval, and agent establish a foundation for AI-driven therapeutic design. Code and datasets: https://github.com/starling-labs/starling.

</details>


### [171] [Delulu: A Verified Multi-Lingual Benchmark for Code Hallucination Detection in Fill-in-the-Middle Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07024)
*Mahdi Erfanian,Nelson Daniel Troncoso,Aashna Garg,Amabel Gale,Xiaoyu Liu,Pareesa Ameneh Golnari,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Delulu，一个包含1,951个样本的多语言Fill-in-the-Middle（FIM）幻觉基准测试，涵盖7种编程语言和4种幻觉类型。评估显示最强模型仅达到84.5% pass@1，揭示了代码生成模型在避免幻觉方面的显著困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码补全任务中频繁产生幻觉问题——生成看似合理但实际错误的代码，如虚构的API方法、无效参数、未定义变量或不存在的导入。这些错误能通过表面审查但会导致运行时错误，而现有研究缺乏对此类问题的系统性评估基准。

Method: 构建了一个对抗性流水线：1）使用前沿LLM生成合理的幻觉样本；2）四个不同的评判模型评估样本；3）基于嵌入的聚类挖掘渐进式更难的样本；4）自包含的Docker容器验证正确补全可编译而幻觉变体产生预期的运行时错误；5）最终人工专家审查移除有偏见或易判断的样本。最终形成包含1,951个样本的验证基准，覆盖7种语言和4种幻觉类型。

Result: 评估了来自五个模型家族的11个开源FIM模型（参数规模0.5B-32B），包括Qwen2.5-Coder的六个规模变体和跨家族变体（CodeLlama、DeepSeek-Coder-V2、StarCoder2）。最强模型仅达84.5% pass@1，所有家族的编辑相似度不超过0.77，且每个家族都在相当比例的样本上产生幻觉对齐的补全，证实Delulu揭示的困难是任务内在的而非家族特定的。

Conclusion: 当前代码生成模型在Fill-in-the-Middle任务中存在显著的幻觉问题，这属于任务本身的内在困难而非特定模型家族的问题。研究者发布了基准测试、容器和评估框架以促进未来研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models for code generation frequently produce hallucinations in Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) tasks -- plausible but incorrect completions such as invented API methods, invalid parameters, undefined variables, or non-existent imports. These failures pass superficial review yet introduce runtime errors. We introduce Delulu, a verified multi-lingual benchmark of 1,951 FIM samples across 7 languages and 4 hallucination types. Samples are curated through an adversarial pipeline: a frontier LLM generates plausible hallucinations, four diverse judge models evaluate them, embedding-based clustering mines progressively harder examples, self-contained Docker containers verify that golden completions compile while hallucinated variants produce the expected runtime error, and a final human-expert review removes any remaining biased or trivially decidable samples. We evaluate 11 open-weight FIM models from five families spanning 0.5B-32B parameters: a six-point Qwen2.5-Coder scaling slate, plus a cross-family slate (CodeLlama, DeepSeek-Coder-V2, StarCoder2). The strongest model reaches only 84.5% pass@1, no family exceeds 0.77 Edit Similarity, and every family produces hallucination-aligned completions on a non-trivial share of samples, confirming that the difficulty exposed by Delulu is task-intrinsic rather than family-specific. We release the benchmark, containers, and evaluation framework at https://github.com/microsoft/delulu.

</details>


### [172] [A Systematic Investigation of The RL-Jailbreaker in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07032)
*Montaser Mohammedalamen,Kevin Roice,Reginald McLean,Alyssa Lefaivre Škopac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地解构了基于强化学习（RL）的大模型越狱攻击框架，发现环境形式化（特别是密集奖励和长回合设置）是攻击成功的主要驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式模型的发展，对抗性越狱攻击已成为安全部署的主要威胁。虽然强化学习框架被用于构建多步越狱攻击，但关于该框架为何成功的机理理解尚不完整，缺乏系统性的分析。

Method: 提出首个RL越狱攻击的系统性分解方法。将框架解构为问题形式化（奖励函数、动作空间、回合长度）和算法措施（RL算法、训练数据、奖励整形），以识别对抗性成功的结构决定因素。

Result: RL越狱攻击者成功攻破了所有目标模型和安全防护措施。分析表明，环境形式化，特别是密集奖励和延长的回合长度，是越狱成功的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 这项工作为提高RL越狱攻击效率提供了工具，最终旨在帮助加固生成式模型，使其能够抵御基于RL的攻击，从而提升模型安全性。

Abstract: The evolution of generative models from next-token predictors to autonomous engines of complex systems necessitates rigorous safety hardening. Adversarial jailbreaking, the strategic manipulation of models to elicit harmful output, remains a primary threat to safe deployment. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) frames jailbreaking as a multi-step attack through sequential optimization, a mechanistic understanding of why the framework succeeds remains incomplete. To fill this gap, we present the first systematic decomposition of RL jailbreaking. We deconstruct the framework into problem formalization (reward function, action space, episode length), and algorithmic measures (RL algorithm, training data, reward-shaping) to identify the structural determinants of adversarial success. Our results reveal that the RL-jailbreaker successfully compromised all targeted models and safeguards. Through this first-of-its-kind analysis, we demonstrate that environment formalization, specifically dense rewards and extended episode lengths, is the primary driver of jailbreaking success. This work provides a tool for improving RL-jailbreaker efficiency and, ultimately, harden generative models resistant to RL-based attacks.

</details>


### [173] [CommFuse: Hiding Tail Latency via Communication Decomposition and Fusion for Distributed LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24013)
*Rezaul Karim,Austin Wen,Wang Zongzuo,Weiwei Zhang,Yang Liu,Walid Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CommFuse方法，通过分解P2P通信和精细调度，消除分布式大模型训练中的通信尾延迟，提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型的分布式训练面临严重的数据通信开销问题。虽然通信-计算重叠技术可以缓解该问题，但现有基于数据分片的方案存在尾延迟缺陷，限制了计算效率的进一步提升。

Method: 提出CommFuse方法，将传统的reduce-scatter和all-gather集合通信操作分解为点对点（P2P）通信，并通过调度分区计算实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠，从而精确消除尾延迟。该方法兼容数据并行训练及TPSP、UP等多种张量级并行策略。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方法在各类场景下均实现了更低的延迟、更高的模型FLOPS利用率（MFU）和吞吐量，有效缓解了张量并行和数据并行的通信瓶颈。

Conclusion: CommFuse为分布式LLM训练和推理提供了一种通用且高效的通信优化方案，成功消除了尾延迟问题，显著提升了计算资源利用率。

Abstract: The rapid growth in the size of large language models has necessitated the partitioning of computational workloads across accelerators such as GPUs, TPUs, and NPUs. However, these parallelization strategies incur substantial data communication overhead significantly hindering computational efficiency. While communication-computation overlap presents a promising direction, existing data slicing based solutions suffer from tail latency. To overcome this limitation, this research introduces a novel communication-computation overlap technique to eliminate this tail latency in state of the art overlap methods for distributed LLM training. The aim of this technique is to effectively mitigate communication bottleneck of tensor parallelism and data parallelism for distributed training and inference. In particular, we propose a novel method termed CommFuse that replaces conventional collective operations of reduce-scatter and all-gather with decomposed peer-to-peer (P2P) communication and schedules partitioned computations to enable fine-grained overlap. Our method provides an exact algorithm for reducing communication overhead that eliminates tail latency. Moreover, it presents a versatile solution compatible with data-parallel training and various tensor-level parallelism strategies, including TPSP and UP. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that our technique consistently achieves lower latency, superior Model FLOPS Utilization (MFU), and high throughput.

</details>


### [174] [PACEvolve++: Improving Test-time Learning for Evolutionary Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07039)
*Minghao Yan,Bo Peng,Benjamin Coleman,Ziqi Chen,Zhouhang Xie,Shuo Chen,Zhankui He,Noveen Sachdeva,Weili Wang,Ed H. Chi,Shivaram Venkataraman,Wang-Cheng Kang,Derek Zhiyuan Cheng,Beidou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACEvolve++提出了一种基于顾问模型强化学习的进化搜索框架，通过可训练的顾问模型实现测试时策略自适应，在多个任务上超越了现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型驱动的进化搜索系统大多依赖固定的提示策略来采样候选解，这在评估成本高昂的实际工程和研究任务中限制了系统的适应能力，无法学习任务特定的搜索动态。

Method: 提出PACEvolve++框架，将策略搜索决策与实现解耦：一个可训练的顾问模型负责生成、评估和选择假设，而更强的前沿模型将选中的假设转化为可执行候选解。采用阶段自适应方法训练顾问模型：进化早期使用组相对反馈学习广泛的搜索偏好；后期随着奖励差距压缩，强调最佳k前沿贡献以支持稳定精炼。

Result: 在专家并行负载均衡、序列推荐和蛋白质适应度外推三个任务上，PACEvolve++均超越了使用前沿模型的现有最先进进化搜索框架，实现了更快的收敛速度，并在进化搜索过程中稳定了测试时训练。

Conclusion: PACEvolve++通过解耦策略决策与实现，并采用阶段自适应训练方法，成功实现了进化搜索中的测试时策略自适应，为大语言模型驱动的进化搜索提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models have become drivers of evolutionary search, but most systems rely on a fixed, prompt-elicited policy to sample next candidates. This limits adaptation in practical engineering and research tasks, where evaluations are expensive, and progress depends on learning task-specific search dynamics. We introduce PACEvolve++, an advisor-model reinforcement learning framework for test-time policy adaptation in evolutionary search agents. PACEvolve++ decouples strategic search decisions from implementation: a trainable advisor generates, assesses, and selects hypotheses, while a stronger frontier model translates selected hypotheses into executable candidates. To train the advisor under non-stationary feedback, we propose a phase-adaptive approach that adapts its optimization strategy to different phases of the evolutionary process. Early in evolution, it uses group-relative feedback to learn broad search preferences; later, as reward gaps compress, it emphasizes best-of-$k$ frontier contribution to support stable refinement. Across expert-parallel load balancing, sequential recommendation, and protein fitness extrapolation, PACEvolve++ outperforms the state-of-the-art evolutionary search framework with frontier models, achieving faster convergence and stabilizing test-time training during evolutionary search.

</details>


### [175] [Unlocking High-Fidelity Molecular Generation from Mass Spectra via Dual-Stream Line Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07048)
*Xujun Che,Xiuxia Du,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualLGD双流线图扩散模型，通过分离原子级和键级推理解决分子生成的循环依赖问题，在基准测试中准确率提升约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从头分子生成面临原子级与键级推理之间的循环依赖难题：确定键的类型需要知道端点原子的化学环境，而原子的环境又由其键定义。现有图扩散方法采用单流架构处理原子和键，原子-键信息同步仅能跨层隐式发生，这种单流范式构成了关键架构瓶颈。

Method: 提出双流线图扩散模型(DualLGD)，将分子图去噪重构为两个耦合子问题的交替求解：原子级推理和键级推理，各自在独立表示空间中运行。利用线图为键空间提供自然数学构造，通过关联约束的双向交叉注意力在每层同步两个流，确保每个原子仅关注其关联键，反之亦然，遵循化学基本原则。

Result: 在NPLIB1和MassSpecGym基准测试上，DualLGD分别达到34.37%和23.89%的top-1准确率，约为先前最优结果的3倍。消融实验证实架构是提升的主要来源：无预训练的DualLGD已超越先前最优的全预训练模型。

Conclusion: 双流架构范式而非聚合核选择是分子生成的关键瓶颈，DualLGD通过分离原子级和键级推理并保持关联约束同步，有效解决了循环依赖问题，实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: De novo molecular generation from tandem mass spectra is a challenging inverse problem whose core difficulty lies in the circular dependency between atom-level and bond-level reasoning: determining a bond's type requires knowing its endpoint atoms' chemical environment, yet an atom's environment is in turn defined by its incident bonds. Existing graph diffusion methods process atoms and bonds within a single computation stream, where atom-bond information synchronization can only occur implicitly across layers. We argue that this single-stream paradigm, rather than the choice of any particular aggregation kernel, is a key architectural bottleneck. We propose DualLGD (Dual-stream Line Graph Diffusion), which reformulates molecular graph denoising as the alternating solution of two coupled subproblems: atom-level reasoning and bond-level reasoning, each operating in its own dedicated representation space. The line graph provides a natural mathematical construction for the bond space, in which bond angles, dihedrals, conjugation chains, and rings correspond to local topological motifs between bonds. Incidence-constrained bidirectional cross-attention synchronizes the two streams at every layer, ensuring that each atom attends only to its incident bonds and vice versa, respecting the fundamental chemical principle that an atom's environment is determined by its bonding context. On the NPLIB1 and MassSpecGym benchmarks, DualLGD achieves top-1 accuracy of 34.37\% and 23.89\%, approximately $3\times$ the previous state of the art. Ablation studies confirm the architecture as the primary source of improvement: DualLGD without any pre-training already surpasses the previous best fully pretrained model.

</details>


### [176] [Towards Differentially Private Reinforcement Learning with General Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07049)
*Yi He,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为一般函数逼近下的差分隐私在线强化学习提供了理论保证，其遗憾界达到了线性情形下的最优水平$\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$，并填补了批量更新设定下的相关理论空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私在线强化学习理论工作仅局限于表格型和线性情形，缺乏对一般函数逼近（即更广泛的函数类）的理论保证，这限制了理论在实际复杂场景中的适用性。

Method: 结合了批量策略更新方案与指数机制，并提出了一种新颖的遗憾分析方法，以处理一般函数逼近带来的复杂性。

Result: 在无模型设定下，即使使用一般函数逼近，其遗憾界也能匹配线性情形下的最先进结果，量级为$\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$；同时建立了首个依赖于覆盖性这一标准复杂度指标的批量更新遗憾界，并揭示了现有线性差分隐私强化学习结果中的根本性差距。

Conclusion: 该研究显著扩展了差分隐私强化学习的理论边界，证明了在一般函数逼近下仍能保持良好的理论性能，澄清了现有理论的局限性并补充了新的理论结果。

Abstract: We present the first theoretical guarantees for differentially private online reinforcement learning (RL) with general function approximation, extending beyond prior work restricted to tabular and linear settings. Our approach combines a batched policy update scheme with the exponential mechanism, together with a novel regret analysis. We show that, even under general function approximation, the regret in the model-free setting under differential privacy matches the state of the art for the linear case, scaling as $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$, where $K$ denotes the number of episodes. As an important by-product, we also establish the first regret bound for online RL with batch update that depends on the standard complexity measure of coverability, complementing existing results based on a newly introduced Eluder-Condition class. In addition, we uncover fundamental gaps in recent results for private RL with linear function approximation, thereby clarifying its landscape.

</details>


### [177] [Integrating Causal DAGs in Deep RL: Activating Minimal Markovian States with Multi-Order Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07057)
*Jiamin Xu,Jacqueline Maasch,Kyra Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSE方法，通过引入受控冗余的多阶历史状态表示，解决了从纵向因果图构建满足马尔可夫性的最小状态表示在深度强化学习中失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习依赖马尔可夫性质保证性能，但现实应用中往往缺乏定义良好的状态。现有因果强化学习工作假设马尔可夫状态已知，忽略了核心问题：给定观测变量的纵向因果图，如何构建可证明满足马尔可夫性质的状态？

Method: 首先提出构建可证明最小状态表示的过程。针对深度神经网络无法直接利用马尔可夫最小性的问题，提出MOSE（Multi-Order State Exposure）方法，将多阶历史状态构造输入到同一个Q函数中，实现受控冗余。

Result: MOSE在常见基准和合成数据集上始终优于最小状态构造和单窗口策略。将最小表示与MOSE结合使用可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 确立了因果深度强化学习的核心原则：仅满足最小充分性是不够的，需要引入受控冗余才能发挥因果状态信息的优势。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning (RL) relies on the Markov property for guaranteed performance, but real-world applications often lack well-defined states given raw observed variables. While causal RL has attracted growing interest, existing work typically assumes Markovian states are provided and focuses on using causality to accelerate learning, leaving a fundamental gap: \emph{given a longitudinal causal graph over observed variables, how does one construct MDP states that provably satisfy the Markov property?} We address this by providing a procedure that constructs a provably minimal state representation. In deep RL, we observe that the minimal representation alone empirically fails to improve performance, indicating that neural networks cannot directly exploit Markovian minimality. To address this, we propose \textbf{MOSE} (Multi-Order State Exposure), which feeds multi-order historical state constructions into the same $Q$-function. MOSE consistently outperforms both the minimal state construction and single-window policies on common benchmarks and synthetic datasets. Including the minimal representation alongside MOSE can further improve performance. Our results establish a core principle for causal deep RL: minimal sufficiency is not enough, and \emph{controlled redundancy} is necessary to unlock the benefit of causal state information.

</details>


### [178] [The E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Transformer: Adaptive Geodesic Operations with Guaranteed Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06729)
*Arash Shahmansoori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EΔ-MHC-Geo Transformer，通过混合Cayley旋转与Householder反射实现无条件正交残差连接，在长程稳定性上超越JPmHC 1.9倍、GPT 3.8倍，参数效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DDL方法的Householder算子仅在β∈{0,2}时正交，Cayley变换虽可保持全β正交但无法表示特征值-1（即反射操作），导致无法完整覆盖O(n)群的两个连通分量。JPmHC虽表现优异，但其有限Cayley残差混合器排除了精确的λ=-1算子且无反射分支。

Method: 提出数据依赖的Cayley旋转Q(x)=(I+(β/2)A(x))^{-1}(I-(β/2)A(x))，在所有β和输入下保持正交性；引入EΔ-MHC-Geo混合架构，通过学习门控γ(X)自适应选择Cayley旋转或Householder反射：X'=γ(X)Q(X)X+(1-γ(X))H₂(X)X；采用中点坍塌正则化器4γ(1-γ)鼓励边界门控决策，使各选定分量为正交。

Result: 在约1.79M参数、3个随机种子的匹配参数对比中：长程稳定性最佳，较JPmHC提升1.9倍、较GPT提升3.8倍；近π旋转损失最佳，单平面上较JPmHC提升4.5倍；范数保持强，平均偏差0.001；诊断反射探针中0.96的取反余弦对齐；层数减少33%。

Conclusion: EΔ-MHC-Geo通过混合方法成功访问O(n)的两个连通分量（旋转与反射），在更少层数下实现了卓越的长程稳定性、旋转损失和范数保持，为深度残差网络提供了更完整的正交群建模能力。

Abstract: We present the E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Transformer, a novel architecture that unifies Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC), Deep Delta Learning (DDL), and the Cayley transform to obtain input-adaptive, unconditionally orthogonal residual connections. Unlike DDL, whose Householder operator is orthogonal only at $β\in \{0,2\}$, our Data-Dependent Cayley rotation $Q(x)=(I+(β/2)A(x))^{-1}(I-(β/2)A(x))$ preserves orthogonality for all $β$ and all inputs. To handle negation, an eigenvalue $-1$ case that Cayley provably excludes, we introduce the E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Hybrid, which combines Cayley rotation with Householder reflection via a learned operator-selection gate $X'=γ(X)Q(X)X+(1-γ(X))H_2(X)X$. A midpoint-collapse regularizer, $4γ(1-γ)$, encourages boundary gate decisions, where each selected component is orthogonal. In matched-parameter comparisons, with approximately 1.79M parameters per model and mean +/- standard deviation over 3 seeds, against four baselines including the concurrent JPmHC, E$Δ$-MHC-Geo achieves the best long-horizon stability, 1.9x over JPmHC and 3.8x over GPT; the best near-$π$ rotation loss, 4.5x over JPmHC on single-plane; strong norm preservation, with 0.001 mean deviation; and 0.96 negation cosine alignment in a diagnostic reflection probe, all with 33% fewer layers. While JPmHC's wider representation excels on pure rotation, its finite Cayley residual mixer excludes an exact $λ=-1$ operator and has no reflection branch, motivating our hybrid approach for accessing both connected components of $O(n)$.

</details>


### [179] [Dr. Post-Training: A Data Regularization Perspective on LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07063)
*Pingbang Hu,Xueshen Liu,Z. Morley Mao,Jiaqi W. Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dr. Post-Training框架，将通用训练数据重新定义为防止过拟合的正则化器而非选择池，通过将目标数据的更新方向投影到通用数据构建的可行集上，实现了比传统数据选择方法更优的偏差-方差权衡和性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法难以有效利用稀缺的高保真目标数据和丰富但质量参差不齐的通用训练数据，传统框架将通用数据视为选择池存在局限性。需要新方法来平衡目标数据的精确性与通用数据的泛化能力，避免过拟合并提升模型性能。

Method: 提出Dr. Post-Training框架，将通用训练数据重构为数据诱导的正则化器：在每个训练步骤中，利用通用数据构建模型更新方向的可行集，将稀缺目标数据指定的更新方向投影到该可行集上。标准训练和现有数据选择方法作为不同正则化强度的特例，形成偏差-方差谱上的不同点。针对LLM规模应用设计了系统优化以实现最小开销。

Result: 在SFT、RLHF和RLVR任务上的广泛实验表明，该方法持续优于最先进的数据选择基线方法；系统基准测试确认了方法的高效性，能够在最小额外开销下实现性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将通用数据重新概念化为正则化器而非选择池，Dr. Post-Training提供了更丰富的设计空间和更灵活的偏差-方差权衡，为LLM后训练中的数据利用提供了新的理论视角和实践方案，在多种任务上取得了优越性能。

Abstract: Data selection methods address a critical challenge in LLM post-training: effectively leveraging scarce, high-fidelity target data alongside abundant but imperfectly aligned general training data. In this work, we move beyond the data-selection framing and introduce Dr. Post-Training (Data-Regularized Post-Training), a novel framework that reconceptualizes general training data as a data-induced regularizer that prevents overfitting to the scarce target objective, rather than serving as a pool for selection. Specifically, our framework proposes that at each training step, construct a feasible set of model update directions using the general training data, and project the model update direction specified by the scarce target data onto that feasible set. Standard training and existing data selection methods arise as special cases with different choices of the data-induced regularizer, and these methods correspond to different points on a bias--variance spectrum with different regularization strength. Building on this view, we propose a family of methods offering a richer design space and more flexible bias--variance tradeoffs. For practical LLM-scale use, we introduce careful system optimizations that realize these methods with minimal overhead. Extensive experiments across SFT, RLHF, and RLVR show that our methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art data selection baselines, and system benchmarks confirm their efficiency.

</details>


### [180] [PolarAdamW: Disentangling Spectral Control and Schur Gauge-Equivariance in Matrix Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07067)
*Haozhou Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解耦了Muon优化器中的两个机制：极谱范数控制和Schur规范等变性。提出的PolarAdamW在标准Transformer上超越Muon，但在SO(3)等变任务上表现相反，证明了两种机制的不同适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器同时耦合了两种效应：通过极映射的谱控制和多重性空间基变换下的等变性（Schur规范等变性）。作者希望分离这两种效应，理解它们各自的贡献和适用场景。

Method: 提出PolarAdamW算法，将Muon的Newton-Schulz极映射应用于AdamW预条件方向而非原始动量，保留了极谱范数控制但破坏了规范等变性。通过理论证明Muon的极步骤具有Schur规范等变性，而AdamW的坐标级步骤则不具有。

Result: 在DeiT-Tiny（ImageNet-1k的100类子集）上，PolarAdamW平均比Muon高+1.93 pp，比AdamW高+9.5 pp；在SO(3)等变3D点云回归任务中，Muon在所有容量下都优于PolarAdamW，且差距随容量增大。两者均优于AdamW。

Conclusion: 实现了谱控制与Schur规范等变性的双分离：前者与AdamW预条件在标准Transformer上配合良好，后者在多重性基自由度具有结构性意义的任务中变得重要。这为不同任务选择合适的优化策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Muon's matrix-level update couples two distinct effects: spectral control via a polar map, and equivariance under orthogonal changes of multiplicity-space basis (Schur gauge-equivariance). We separate them with PolarAdamW, a controlled hybrid that preserves Muon's polar spectral-norm control but breaks the gauge-equivariance, since AdamW's coordinatewise preconditioner is basis-dependent. Algorithmically, PolarAdamW applies Muon's Newton-Schulz polar map to AdamW's preconditioned direction rather than to raw momentum, at per-iteration wall-time comparable to Muon. We prove that Muon's polar step is Schur gauge-equivariant on multiplicity matrices while AdamW's coordinatewise step is not.
  On DeiT-Tiny trained from scratch on four independently sampled 100-class subsets of ImageNet-1k, where multiplicity-basis freedom is trivial, PolarAdamW outperforms Muon by +1.93 pp in test accuracy on average and AdamW by +9.5 pp; under the 300-epoch DeiT-style recipe, it remains ahead of Muon by +1.37 pp and AdamW by +5.80 pp on average. On SO(3)-equivariant 3D point-cloud regression, where multiplicity-basis freedom is non-trivial, the ordering reverses: Muon outperforms PolarAdamW at every audited capacity, and the gap widens with capacity. Both matrix-polar optimisers continue to outperform AdamW. This double dissociation separates spectral control from Schur gauge-equivariance: the first composes well with AdamW preconditioning on standard transformers, while the second becomes consequential when multiplicity-basis freedom is structurally non-trivial.

</details>


### [181] [Less Random, More Private: What is the Optimal Subsampling Scheme for DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07072)
*Andy Dong,Ayfer Özgür*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了结构化的平衡迭代采样(BIS)在差分隐私机器学习中比默认的泊松采样具有更强的隐私放大效果，推翻了“采样随机性越强隐私保护越好”的传统直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私机器学习中默认使用的泊松采样虽然具有易于分析的优点，但其非结构化随机性导致每个样本参与的训练迭代次数存在显著差异(参与方差)。作者发现这种方差不仅是实际缺陷，更是导致次优隐私放大的根本原因。

Method: 提出平衡迭代采样(BIS)方案，确保每个样本精确参与固定次数的迭代；证明BIS在噪声谱两端(σ→0和σ→∞)均为最优；开发近精确蒙特卡洛计数器以消除现有RDP和基于组合的PLD分析的松弛。

Result: 理论证明BIS比泊松采样具有更强的隐私放大效果，其核心是消除参与方差同时保持各迭代的均匀边际参与率；在60多个实际DP-SGD配置的评估中，BIS在最相关的高效用低噪声场景下持续优于泊松采样，所需噪声乘数最多可降低9.6%。

Conclusion: 研究表明最大化采样随机性并非隐私放大的关键，而消除参与方差才是本质；在DP-SGD中，结构化参与方案既更实用又更具隐私性，这颠覆了传统认知。

Abstract: Poisson subsampling is the default sampling scheme in differentially private machine learning, largely because its unstructured randomness yields tractable privacy amplification analyses. Yet this same randomness introduces substantial participation variance: each sample appears in very different numbers of training iterations. In this work, we show that this variance is not merely a practical artifact to be tolerated, but a fundamental source of suboptimal privacy amplification. We prove that Balanced Iteration Subsampling (BIS), a structured scheme in which each sample participates in exactly a fixed number of iterations, achieves stronger privacy amplification than Poisson subsampling and is optimal at both extremes of the noise spectrum ($σ\to 0$ and $σ\to \infty$). Our analysis reveals that the privacy-noise tradeoff is governed not by maximizing randomness, but by eliminating participation variance while preserving uniform marginal participation across iterations. To translate this asymptotic theory into finite-noise guarantees, we introduce a practical near-exact Monte Carlo accountant for BIS, which removes the analytical slack of existing RDP and composition-based PLD analyses. Evaluations across more than 60 practical DP-SGD configurations show that BIS consistently outperforms Poisson subsampling in the low-noise regimes most relevant for high-utility private training, reducing the required noise multiplier by up to $9.6\%$. These results overturn the common intuition that more sampling randomness necessarily yields stronger privacy amplification: in DP-SGD, structured participation can be both more practical and more private. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/dong-xin-ao-andy/bis-mc-accountant.

</details>


### [182] [ModelLens: Finding the Best for Your Task from Myriads of Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07075)
*Rui Cai,Weijie Jacky Mo,Xiaofei Wen,Qiyao Ma,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Muhao Chen,Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ModelLens框架，通过学习公开排行榜交互数据构建性能感知的隐空间，无需在新数据集上运行候选模型即可完成模型推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源模型生态中模型数量庞大且新模型与新数据集持续涌现，现有方法（AutoML、可迁移性估计、模型路由）无法有效处理开放环境下的模型选择问题，需要一种能够应对未知模型和未知数据集的推荐方法。

Method: 从分散且含噪声的公开排行榜交互数据中学习模型-数据集-指标三元组的性能感知隐空间表示，利用该隐空间直接对未见模型在未见数据集上的性能进行排序预测，无需在目标数据集上运行候选模型。

Result: 在包含1.62M评估记录（覆盖47K模型和9.6K数据集）的新基准上，ModelLens超越了依赖元数据或需要在目标数据集上运行候选模型的基线方法；其推荐的Top-K候选池使多种代表性路由方法的性能提升高达81%。

Conclusion: ModelLens成功实现了开放环境下的统一模型推荐，在QA基准及最新发布的文本和视觉-语言任务基准上均展现出良好的泛化能力，证明了利用排行榜交互数据学习隐式能力图谱的有效性。

Abstract: The open-source model ecosystem now contains hundreds of thousands of pretrained models, yet picking the best model for a new dataset is increasingly infeasible: new models and unbenchmarked datasets emerge continuously, leaving practitioners with no prior records on either side. Existing approaches handle only fragments of this in-the-wild setting: AutoML and transferability estimation select models from small predefined pools or require expensive per-model forward passes on the target dataset, while model routing presupposes a given candidate pool. We introduce ModelLens, a unified framework for model recommendation in the wild. Our key insight is that public leaderboard interactions, though scattered and noisy, collectively trace out an implicit atlas of model capabilities across heterogeneous evaluation settings, a signal rich enough to learn from directly. By learning a performance-aware latent space over model--dataset--metric tuples, ModelLens ranks unseen models on unseen datasets without running candidates on the target dataset. On a new benchmark of 1.62M evaluation records spanning 47K models and 9.6K datasets, ModelLens surpasses baselines that either rely on metadata alone or require running each candidate on the target dataset. Its recommended Top-K pools further improve multiple representative routing methods by up to 81% across diverse QA benchmarks. Case studies on recently released benchmarks further confirm generalization to both text and vision-language tasks.

</details>


### [183] [Test-Time Compositional Generalization in Diffusion Models via Concept Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07078)
*Zekun Wang,Anant Gupta,Tianyi Zhu,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种测试时组合生成方法，使预训练扩散模型能够从其时间索引分数几何中发现查询特定的概念并组合它们，无需预定义的概念库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组合生成方法通常假设相关概念或条件信号已经可用。本文探索预训练扩散模型是否能够从其学习到的时间索引分数中发现查询特定的概念，并在测试时进行组合。

Method: 对单个分布外查询，在多个噪声时间步上对分数进行梯度上升以恢复局部密度模式，将这些模式映射到干净空间的高斯分布，使用子模块似然目标贪婪选择相关原型，并将它们组合成具有解析分数的专家乘积(PoE)教师模型。该教师模型可直接通过无分类器指导采样，或用于生成样本池来训练新的类别嵌入和低秩适配器。

Result: 在基于ColorMNIST和CelebA构建的保留组合基准测试上，解析PoE采样器和低秩适配模型均优于仅查询和最近训练类别基线。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的时间索引分数几何包含可重用的密度模式概念，能够在没有预定义概念库的情况下支持测试时组合生成。

Abstract: Compositional generalization requires models to produce novel configurations from familiar parts. In diffusion models, prior compositional generation methods typically assume that the relevant concepts or conditioning signals are already available. We instead ask whether a pretrained diffusion model can discover query-specific concepts from the time-indexed scores it learns for the noisy marginals $p_t(x_t)$ and compose them at test time. Given a single out-of-distribution query, our method performs gradient ascent on $s_θ(x_t,t) \approx \nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)$ at multiple noising timesteps to recover local density modes, maps these modes into clean-space Gaussians, greedily selects relevant prototypes with a submodular likelihood objective, and combines them into a product-of-experts (PoE) teacher model with an analytic score. This teacher model can be sampled directly through classifier-free guidance or used to generate a sample pool for training a new class embedding and low-rank adapter. On held-out composition benchmarks built from ColorMNIST and CelebA, both the analytic PoE sampler and the low-rank adapted model outperform query-only and nearest trained-class baselines. These results suggest that the time-indexed score geometry of the diffusion model contains reusable density-mode concepts that support test-time compositional generation without a predefined concept library.

</details>


### [184] [Actor-Critic with Active Importance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07094)
*Majid Molaei,Gabor Paczolay,Matteo Papini,Alberto Maria Metelli,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AISAC算法，通过优化行为策略来降低策略梯度估计的方差，在保持无偏估计的同时提高了学习速度、样本效率和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Actor-Critic框架中，策略梯度估计存在高方差问题，导致学习效率和稳定性受限。作者希望通过优化行为策略来降低方差并保持无偏性，从而提升强化学习的性能。

Method: 提出了Active-Importance-Sampling Actor-Critic (AISAC)算法，利用重要性采样原理优化行为策略以最小化梯度方差，同时保持无偏的梯度估计。针对连续动作空间，采用高斯行为策略并通过交叉熵最小化进行优化，使数据采集分布与目标策略梯度对齐。

Result: 理论分析证明了方差降低和无偏性。在Inverted Pendulum和Half Cheetah任务上的实验表明，相比标准Actor-Critic方法，AISAC提高了学习速度、样本效率和训练稳定性，加速了收敛，并改善了目标策略更新和critic估计的准确性。

Conclusion: 优化行为策略能够有效提升Actor-Critic方法的性能，AISAC算法为实际应用提供了更高效的强化学习解决方案。未来工作将把该方法与Soft Actor-Critic和TD3等先进算法集成，以应用于更复杂的环境。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Active-Importance-Sampling Actor-Critic (AISAC) algorithm, an extension of the Actor-Critic framework for reducing variance in policy gradient estimation. AISAC optimizes the behavior policy to minimize gradient variance while preserving unbiased gradient estimates. Using importance sampling principles, the algorithm adapts the behavior policy toward efficient data collection distributions aligned with target policy gradients.
  For continuous action spaces, AISAC employs Gaussian behavior policies optimized through cross-entropy minimization. We provide theoretical analysis demonstrating variance reduction and unbiasedness. Experiments on Inverted Pendulum and Half Cheetah tasks show improved learning speed, sample efficiency, and training stability compared to standard Actor-Critic methods.
  Results indicate that optimizing the behavior policy improves both target policy updates and critic estimation accuracy across different hyperparameter settings. AISAC accelerates convergence and stabilizes reinforcement learning training, making it promising for real-world applications. Future work includes integration with advanced algorithms such as Soft Actor-Critic and TD3 for more complex environments.

</details>


### [185] [Query-efficient model evaluation using cached responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07096)
*Hayden Helm,Ben Johnson,Carey Priebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据核透视空间（DKPS）的方法，利用已缓存的历史模型响应对新模型进行基准测试性能预测，在保证预测精度的同时大幅减少了查询成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代评估框架中，对新模型进行全面基准测试的成本极为高昂。然而，以往评估过的模型响应通常会被缓存，这一潜在信息资源尚未被有效利用来降低新模型评估所需的查询数量。

Method: 引入了数据核透视空间（DKPS）方法来量化黑盒设置下模型之间的关系，并基于此提出了利用缓存模型响应预测新模型性能的方法。该方法在理论层面证明了在特定条件下的查询高效性，并提出了一种离线查询选择方法，通过最大化参考模型上的拟合优度来优化查询集合的选择。

Result: 实验结果表明，DKPS方法在相同平均绝对误差下，相比基线方法能显著降低查询预算。所提出的离线查询选择方法相比随机选择，能进一步提高预测准确性。

Conclusion: DKPS方法为利用历史缓存信息高效评估新模型提供了理论基础和实证支持，能够有效降低基准测试成本，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Evaluating a new model on an existing benchmark is often necessary to understand its behavior before deployment. For modern evaluation frameworks, generating and evaluating a response for all queries can be prohibitively expensive. In practice, responses from previously-evaluated models are often cached -- creating a potential opportunity to use this additional information to decrease the number of queries required to accurately evaluate a new model. In this paper, we introduce an approach for predicting benchmark performance that leverages cached model responses based on the Data Kernel Perspective Space (DKPS), a method for quantifying the relationship between models in the black-box setting. Theoretically, we show that DKPS-based methods are query-efficient under certain conditions. Empirically, we demonstrate that DKPS-based methods achieve the same mean absolute error as baselines with a substantially decreased query budget. We conclude by proposing an offline method for selecting a set of queries that maximizes the goodness-of-fit on reference models, improving prediction accuracy over random query selection.

</details>


### [186] [CarCrashNet: A Large-Scale Dataset and Hierarchical Neural Solver for Data-Driven Structural Crash Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07098)
*Mohamed Elrefaie,Dule Shu,Matthew Klenk,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了CarCrashNet，一个高质量开源车辆碰撞仿真基准数据集（含14,000+部件级和825个整车仿真），并提出了机器学习模型CrashSolver用于整车碰撞预测，验证了其在结构仿真和AI驱动虚拟碰撞测试中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞仿真是现代车辆开发的核心环节，可减少物理原型成本、加速安全设计迭代并支持虚拟测试流程。然而，结构碰撞力学建模极具挑战性，涉及非线性接触、大变形、材料塑性、失效以及高分辨率有限元网格上的复杂多体相互作用，需要可靠的数据驱动方法和基准数据集来推动该领域研究。

Method: 构建CarCrashNet开源基准数据集，包含14,000多个保险杠梁柱碰撞仿真（变化几何、材料和边界条件）和825个整车碰撞仿真（基于Dodge Neon、Toyota Yaris和Chevrolet Silverado三种车型）。基于OpenRadioss建立开源有限元工作流，并通过实验碰撞数据和商业求解器Ansys LS-DYNA进行验证。提出CrashSolver机器学习模型用于从高分辨率有限元碰撞数据进行整车碰撞预测，并在数据集上与最先进的几何深度学习和基于Transformer的神经求解器进行广泛基准测试。

Result: 成功构建了多模态高保真碰撞仿真数据集，验证了开源有限元工作流相对于实验数据和商业求解器的可靠性。CrashSolver在整车碰撞预测任务上表现出色，通过与现有几何深度学习和Transformer方法的对比评估，确立了CarCrashNet作为结构仿真、耐撞性建模和AI驱动虚拟碰撞测试可重现研究基础的地位。

Conclusion: CarCrashNet为结构碰撞仿真领域提供了首个大规模高质量开源基准，填补了该领域数据驱动研究的空白。该工作为可重现的结构仿真研究、耐撞性建模和AI驱动的虚拟碰撞测试奠定了基础，相关数据集已在GitHub开源，有望推动该领域的快速发展。

Abstract: Crash simulation is a cornerstone of modern vehicle development because it reduces the need for costly physical prototypes, accelerates safety-driven design iteration, and increasingly supports virtual testing workflows. At the same time, modeling structural crash mechanics remains exceptionally challenging: the response is governed by nonlinear contact, large deformation, material plasticity, failure, and complex multi-body interactions evolving over space and time on high-resolution finite-element meshes. In this work, we introduce \textsc{CarCrashNet}, a public high-fidelity open-source benchmark for data-driven structural crash simulation. \textsc{CarCrashNet} combines component-scale and full-vehicle simulations in a multi-modal format, including more than 14{,}000 bumper-beam pole-impact simulations with varying geometry, materials, and boundary conditions, together with 825 full-vehicle crash simulations built from three industry-standard vehicle models of increasing structural complexity: Dodge Neon, Toyota Yaris, and Chevrolet Silverado. To establish the reliability of the benchmark, we validate our open-source finite-element workflow based on OpenRadioss against both experimental crash data and the commercial solver Ansys LS-DYNA. We also introduce \textsc{CrashSolver}, a machine-learning model designed for full-vehicle crash prediction from high-resolution finite-element crash data. We further perform extensive benchmarking across the released datasets and evaluate \textsc{CrashSolver} against state-of-the-art geometric deep learning and transformer-based neural solvers. Our results position \textsc{CarCrashNet} as a foundation for reproducible research in structural simulation, crashworthiness modeling, and AI-driven virtual crash testing. The dataset is available at https://github.com/Mohamedelrefaie/CarCrashNet.

</details>


### [187] [Almost Sure Convergence Rates of Stochastic Approximation and Reinforcement Learning via a Poisson-Moreau Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07104)
*Xinyu Liu,Zixuan Xie,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对马尔可夫噪声下的随机近似和强化学习算法，建立了几乎必然收敛速率。对于幂律学习率，收敛速率接近o(n^{1-2η})；对于调和学习率，收敛速率接近o(n^{-1})，接近迭代对数定律给出的最优速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在马尔可夫噪声下建立随机近似和强化学习算法的几乎必然收敛速率是一个基础性的理论挑战。由于许多强化学习算法（如Q-learning和线性时序差分学习）都涉及期望更新为压缩映射的随机近似，因此研究此类算法的收敛速率具有重要理论意义。

Method: 针对期望更新为压缩映射的一类随机近似算法，提出了一种新颖的李雅普诺夫漂移构造方法。该方法将基于泊松方程的马尔可夫噪声修正应用于成熟的压缩映射Moreau包络平滑技术。通过这种构造，分析了幂律学习率O(n^{-η})(η∈(1/2,1))和调和学习率O(n^{-1})两种情形。

Result: 对于幂律学习率O(n^{-η})且η∈(1/2,1)的情况，获得了接近o(n^{1-2η})的几乎必然收敛速率。对于调和学习率O(n^{-1})的情况，获得了接近o(n^{-1})的几乎必然收敛速率。后者是一个强结果，因为它接近于迭代对数定律给出的最优速率O(n^{-1}log log n)（针对i.i.d.噪声的特殊情况）。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了马尔可夫噪声下随机近似和强化学习算法几乎必然收敛速率的理论难题，所提出的李雅普诺夫漂移构造方法为分析此类问题提供了新的技术工具。研究表明，在适当的学习率选择下，可以逼近理论最优收敛速率。

Abstract: Establishing almost sure convergence rates for stochastic approximation and reinforcement learning under Markovian noise is a fundamental theoretical challenge. We make progress towards this challenge for a class of stochastic approximation algorithms whose expected updates are contractive, a setting that arises in many reinforcement learning algorithms such as $Q$-learning and linear temporal difference learning. Specifically, for a power-law learning rate $O(n^{-η})$ with $η\in (1/2, 1)$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{1 - 2η})$. For a harmonic learning rate $O(n^{-1})$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{-1})$, which we argue is a strong result because it is close to the optimal rate $O(n^{-1}\log\log n)$ given by the law of the iterated logarithm (for a special case of i.i.d. noise). Key to our analysis is a novel Lyapunov drift construction that applies a Poisson-equation based correction for Markovian noise to the well-established Moreau-envelope smoothing for the contractive mapping.

</details>


### [188] [Theoretical Limits of Language Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07105)
*Lucas Monteiro Paes,Natalie Mackraz,Barry-John Theobald,Federico Danieli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了KL正则化对齐的信息论极限，发现最优奖励提升由Jeffreys散度决定而非√KL，并证明最佳N选一接近理论极限，而PPO和GRPO显著次优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习和最佳N选一是主流的语言模型对齐方法，但在固定KL散度预算下奖励提升的基本极限仍未被充分理解，需要从信息论角度刻画对齐性能的理论上界。

Method: 推导固定KL预算下最大可期望奖励增益的闭式表达式；将表达式重构为基模型下的协方差形式以构建实用估计器；扩展分析到代理奖励设置并研究奖励集成对缓解奖励黑客的理论作用；在安全性和摘要任务上实证计算KL-奖励Pareto前沿。

Result: 发现最优奖励提升由Jeffreys散度项控制而非先前的√KL；代理对齐与理想对齐的差距随奖励误差增大和KL惩罚因子减小而扩大；奖励集成可有效缓解奖励黑客；实证显示最佳N选一接近理论极限，而PPO和GRPO显著次优。

Conclusion: 理论结果揭示了最优对齐的信息论极限并为奖励黑客等经验现象提供解释，表明当前对齐算法仍需改进以在不增加推理成本的情况下实现最优对齐。

Abstract: Language model (LM) alignment improves model outputs to reflect human preferences while preserving the capabilities of the base model. The most common alignment approaches are (i) reinforcement learning, which maximizes the expected reward under a KL-divergence constraint, and (ii) best-of-$N$ alignment, which selects the highest-reward output among $N$ independent samples. Despite their widespread use, the fundamental limits of reward improvement under a KL budget remain poorly understood. We characterize the information-theoretic limits of KL-regularized alignment by deriving the maximum achievable expected reward gain for a fixed KL-divergence budget. Our first result provides a closed-form expression for the optimal reward improvement, governed by a Jeffreys divergence term rather than the $\sqrt{\texttt{KL}}$ used in prior analyses. We further reformulate this expression as a covariance under the base model, yielding a practical estimator that predicts achievable alignment gains from base model samples alone. We extend our analysis to the proxy reward setting, showing that the gap between ideal and proxy alignment (reward hacking) grows with the magnitude of reward error and when the KL penalty factor decreases. We then prove that reward ensembling mitigates reward hacking, providing a theoretical justification for this technique used in practice. Empirically, we compute the KL-reward Pareto frontier for two tasks for LMs, safety and summarization, and show that best-of-$N$ closely approaches the theoretical limit, while PPO and GRPO remain substantially suboptimal. Our theoretical results shed light on several empirically observed phenomena in the alignment literature and suggest that algorithmic improvements are needed to achieve optimal alignment without high inference costs.

</details>


### [189] [Solving Max-Cut to Global Optimality via Feasibility-Preserving Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07113)
*Hao Chen,Chendi Qian,Christopher Morris,Andrea Lodi,Can Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对Max-Cut问题的图神经网络，作为半定规划（SDP）求解器的轻量级神经代理，可直接集成到精确分支定界框架中，在保证解有效性的前提下将边界计算成本降低至多10.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确求解困难的组合优化问题通常依赖于强凸松弛，但在分支定界算法中重复求解这些松弛（如Max-Cut中的SDP松弛）计算代价过高，亟需一种高效且保持有效性的替代方法。

Method: 设计了一种Max-Cut专用的图神经网络架构，其更新步骤复杂度为O(n²+ne)，能够预测SDP的原偶可行解，并通过Goemans-Williamson算法获得可行的Max-Cut解。模型采用自监督训练方式，无需预先求解的SDP松弛作为标签。

Result: 实验结果表明，与使用最先进的SDP求解器Mosek相比，该架构可将精确Max-Cut求解中的边界计算成本降低至多10.6倍。

Conclusion: 研究展示了学习型、保持有效性的代理模型在加速基于结构化凸松弛的精确优化方面的潜力，为解决组合优化中的计算瓶颈提供了新思路。

Abstract: Exact solution of hard combinatorial optimization problems often relies on strong convex relaxations, but solving these relaxations repeatedly inside a branch-and-bound algorithm can be prohibitively expensive. Hence, we consider this challenge for Max-Cut, where branch and bound commonly uses semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations to bound subproblems. We propose a Max-Cut-specific graph neural network that serves as a principled, lightweight neural proxy for these SDP solvers and can be plugged directly into an exact branch-and-bound framework. The proposed architecture has update steps of complexity $\mathcal{O}(n^2 + ne)$, and predicts both primal- and dual-feasible SDP solutions. The primal SDP solutions yield feasible Max-Cut solutions via the Goemans--Williamson algorithm. In addition, it is trained in a self-supervised fashion without requiring solved SDP relaxations as labels. Empirically, we show that our architecture can substantially reduce the cost of bounding in exact Max-Cut solving by up to $10.6 \times$ compared with using the state-of-the-art SDP solver Mosek. Our work highlights the potential of learned, validity-preserving surrogates for accelerating exact optimization over structured convex relaxations.

</details>


### [190] [Where to Spend Rollouts: Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation for Group-Based RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07114)
*Tao Wang,Shuo Li,Yan Sun,Dongsheng Ding,Edgar Dobriban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HORA方法，通过自适应重分配rollout预算来优化RLVR中的计算资源利用，在保持Pass@1的同时提升Pass@K性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组的强化学习方法（如GRPO）为每个prompt分配固定数量的rollout，这种均匀分配效率低下：对已经饱和的prompt过度分配计算资源，而对可能通过额外采样获得正确轨迹的prompt探索不足。

Method: 引入hit utility概念（在提议的额外分配中至少有一个rollout正确的后验概率），提出HORA——一种免学习的rollout分配策略。HORA在每个分配批次内最大化总后验hit utility，自适应地重新分配rollout预算，同时保持下游奖励评估和基于组的优势估计器不变。

Result: 在四个数学推理基准和三个模型规模上，HORA在十二个模型-基准配置中的十个上保持了相当的Pass@1并提升了Pass@K（相比计算匹配的GRPO），一处平局，一处饱和异常。该方法与其他基于组的估计器（如RLOO）兼容。消融实验表明HORA使用的均匀先验与五种prompt条件学习先验方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: HORA提供了一种简单、高效且即插即用的方法来优化RLVR中的rollout分配，无需额外学习即可显著提升计算效率和模型性能，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Group-based policy optimization methods, such as GRPO, typically allocate a fixed number of rollouts to every prompt. This uniform allocation can be inefficient: it over-allocates compute to prompts whose sampled groups are already saturated while under-exploring prompts for which additional samples may reveal useful correct trajectories. To address this limitation, we introduce hit utility, the posterior probability that at least one rollout in a proposed additional allocation for a prompt will be correct. Building on this notion, we propose Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation (HORA), a learning-free rollout allocation policy that maximizes total posterior hit utility within each allocation batch. HORA adaptively reallocates rollout budgets while leaving the downstream reward evaluation and group-based advantage estimator unchanged. Across four mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three model scales, HORA preserves comparable Pass@1 and improves Pass@K over compute-matched GRPO in ten of twelve model--benchmark configurations, with one tie and one saturated exception. It is also drop-in compatible with other group-based estimators such as RLOO. Ablation studies indicate that the uniform prior used by HORA is competitive with five prompt-conditioned learned-prior alternatives.

</details>


### [191] [Conformal-Style Quantile Analyses for Stochastic Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07115)
*Chengyu Du,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究关注上尾表现的随机老虎机问题，提出ACP-UCB1算法，实现对数级别的上分位数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统老虎机算法基于均值奖励分析，但许多实际问题更偏好具有强上尾表现的动作臂。自然上尾目标是中央预测区间的上端点，该目标可能导致与均值排序不同的动作臂排序，从而产生与传统老虎机目标的中央错配问题。

Method: 提出ACP-UCB1算法——一种共形风格策略，结合上端点的自适应共形估计与UCB类型乐观奖励。关键技术是通过奖励-分位数集中性、重新计算得分分位数的扰动论证以及自适应水平的确定性定位来控制端点估计。核心挑战在于ACP-UCB1所使用的一致性得分是从不断演化的经验分位数估计中重新计算的，并在自适应水平上进行评估。

Result: ACP-UCB1实现对数级上分位数遗憾，每个动作臂的贡献为O(log n/Δ_j^ACP)。提供与UCB1比较的度量特定遗憾分解，数值实验验证了算法的性能表现和改进效果。

Conclusion: ACP-UCB1成功解决了上尾偏好场景下的老虎机问题，在保持对数遗憾的同时更准确地识别具有强上尾表现的动作臂，为相关应用场景提供了有效的算法选择。

Abstract: Stochastic bandit algorithms are usually analyzed under a mean-reward criterion, yet many problems favor arms with strong upper-tail performance, which we study herein. For a fixed miscoverage level \(α\), the natural upper-tail target of arm \(j\) is the upper endpoint \(F_j^{-1}(1-α/2)\) of a central prediction interval. This target can rank arms differently from their means, creating a central mismatch with the classical bandit objective. To this end, we propose ACP-UCB1, a conformal-style policy that combines an adaptive conformal estimate of the upper endpoint with a UCB-type optimism bonus. The technical challenge is that the conformity scores used by ACP-UCB1 are recomputed from evolving empirical quantile estimates and evaluated at an adaptive level. We control this endpoint through reward-quantile concentration, a perturbation argument for recomputed score quantiles, and deterministic localization of the adaptive level. ACP-UCB1 achieves logarithmic upper-quantile regret with per-arm contribution \(O(\nicefrac{\log n}{Δ_j^{\mathrm{ACP}}})\). We also provide metric-specific regret decompositions comparing ACP-UCB1 with UCB1 and use numerical experiments to validate performance and improvement.

</details>


### [192] [Stabilized neural Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman solvers: Error analysis and applications in model-based reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07116)
*Minseok Kim,Yeongjong Kim,Namkyeong Cho,Yeoneung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合神经求解器的误差理论，用于基于模型的连续时间强化学习。该方法结合了有限差分HJB方程与神经网络表示，证明了策略评估的稳定性估计，并在多个基准测试上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经求解器在连续时间模型强化学习中具有潜力，其实际实现通常介于经典网格方法和连续PDE PINN之间：价值函数由神经网络表示，有限差分HJB策略评估算子通过网络查询在偏移点处进行评估，残差通过随机连续配点最小化。这种混合机制保留了稳定的有限差分策略评估结构，同时避免了基于网格的价值未知量。然而，该混合机制缺乏系统的误差理论支持。

Method: 将有限差分解释为作用于神经网络的移位算子，证明了具有学习动力学的一步策略评估的总体L²稳定性估计。该界限分离了残差误差、初始和外部边界不匹配、策略不匹配以及模型辨识误差，并为学习动力学提供了显式的梯度放大因子。进一步给出了有限样本配点证书和通过贪婪策略改进的条件多步传播结果。

Result: 理论分析表明，基础的线性评估稳定性避免了隐式逆粘性爆裂。在多达64维的紧凑控制LQR、Allen-Cahn控制、摆锤、Hopper和3D四旋翼基准测试上进行实验，与代表性的基于模型和无模型强化学习基线进行比较，验证了预测的残差、策略不匹配和学习模型误差趋势。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了混合神经求解器机制的误差理论框架，为物理信息神经网络在连续时间强化学习中的应用提供了理论保证。实验结果证实了理论预测，表明该方法能够有效处理高维控制问题，为后续研究提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural solvers offer a promising route to model-based reinforcement learning in continuous time, where optimal feedback synthesis is governed by Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) equations. Practical implementations often occupy a regime that is neither a classical grid method nor a continuous-PDE PINN: the value function is represented by a neural network, finite-difference HJB policy-evaluation operators are evaluated by network queries at shifted points, and residuals are minimized by random continuous collocation. This regime preserves the stabilized finite-difference policy-evaluation structure while avoiding grid-based value unknowns. We develop an error theory for this hybrid regime. Interpreting finite differences as shift operators acting on neural networks, we prove a population $L^2$ stability estimate for one policy-evaluation step with learned dynamics. The bound separates residual error, initial and exterior-collar mismatch, policy mismatch, and model-identification error, with an explicit gradient amplification factor for learned dynamics, while the underlying linear evaluation stability remains free of hidden inverse-viscosity blow-up. We further give a finite-sample collocation certificate and a conditional multi-step propagation result through greedy policy improvement. Experiments on compact-control LQR upto 64 dimensions, Allen--Cahn control, pendulum, Hopper, and 3D quadrotor benchmarks compare against representative model-based and model-free RL baselines, demonstrating the predicted residual, policy-mismatch, and learned-model error trends.

</details>


### [193] [When Symbol Names Should Not Matter: A Logistic Theory of Fresh-Symbol Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07120)
*Wenjie Guan,Jelena Bradic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer在固定标签分类任务中是否使用抽象符号进行推理，发现通过正则化核逻辑分类可以学习到对符号重命名不变的决策规则，并提供了基于碰撞图的泛化性理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板任务是验证Transformer是否使用抽象符号而非具体token名称进行推理的测试平台。研究者希望理解在训练和测试样本共享潜在模板但使用不相交词汇表的固定标签分类场景中，模型如何学习对符号重命名不变的决策规则。

Method: 在transformer-kernel机制下分析正则化核逻辑分类，将学习到的预测器分解为理想的模板级分类器和由训练数据中偶然token重叠引起的有限样本扰动。使用着色碰撞图编码这些重叠，并证明新鲜符号分类的高概率边界转移保证。

Result: 研究发现词汇表大小控制碰撞的平均速率，而碰撞几何结构控制理想分类边界是否被保留。边界与碰撞准则可用于识别提示策略何时改善新鲜符号泛化，该准则适用于抽象增强输入。

Conclusion: 通过碰撞图理论分析，本文成功扩展了基于模板的分析到逻辑分类，并细化了标量多样性条件，为理解Transformer抽象推理能力提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Template tasks have emerged as a clean testbed for asking whether transformers reason with abstract symbols rather than concrete token names. We study the fixed-label classification version of this problem, where train and test examples share latent templates but may use disjoint vocabularies. Unlike next-token prediction, the model need not emit unseen symbols; it must learn a decision rule invariant to symbol renaming. We analyze regularized kernel logistic classification in the transformer-kernel regime. Our main result decomposes the learned predictor into an ideal template-level classifier and a finite-sample perturbation caused by accidental token overlaps in the training data. We encode these overlaps by a colored collision graph and prove high-probability margin-transfer guarantees for fresh-symbol classification. This perspective extends template-based analyses to logistic classification and refines scalar diversity conditions: vocabulary size controls the average rate of collisions, but collision geometry controls whether the ideal classification margin is preserved. More broadly, the same perturbation framework applies to abstraction-augmented inputs, yielding a general margin-versus-collision criterion for identifying when prompting strategies improve fresh-symbol generalization. Synthetic template experiments illustrate the predicted roles of regularization, sample size, and transformer-kernel structure.

</details>


### [194] [Convergence and Emergence of In-Context Reinforcement Learning with Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07123)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上揭示了Chain-of-Thought (CoT)生成与上下文强化学习(ICRL)的内在联系：在特定参数下，线性Transformer中的CoT过程等价于时序差分学习，且其误差随CoT长度几何级数下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管近期经验研究表明Chain-of-Thought (CoT)生成能够增强上下文强化学习(ICRL)能力，但缺乏对其背后机制的理论解释。本文旨在填补这一空白，从理论上阐明CoT如何与ICRL相互作用。

Method: 在线性Transformer的策略评估框架下进行研究。首先证明存在特定的Transformer参数配置，使得CoT生成过程等价于重复执行时序差分(TD)学习更新。随后进行有限样本收敛分析，并证明这些期望参数是预训练损失的全局最小化解。

Result: 1. CoT生成过程在特定参数下可视为时序差分学习的迭代实现；2. 策略评估误差随CoT长度呈几何级数下降，最终收敛于由上下文长度决定的统计误差下界；3. 所需的Transformer参数是预训练损失的全局最小化点，从理论上解释了这些参数的经验涌现现象。

Conclusion: 本文建立了CoT与ICRL之间坚实的理论桥梁，证明了CoT本质上是在执行时序差分学习，这一发现不仅解释了CoT增强ICRL能力的现象，也为其在强化学习领域的应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) refers to the ability of RL agents to adapt to new tasks at inference time without parameter updates by conditioning on additional context. Recent empirical studies further demonstrate that Chain-of-Thought (CoT) generation can amplify this ICRL capability. This paper is the first to provide a theoretical understanding on how CoT interacts with ICRL. We conduct our analysis in a policy evaluation setup with linear Transformer. We prove that with specific Transformer parameters, the CoT generation process is equivalent to repeatedly executing temporal difference learning updates. Additionally, we provide finite sample convergence analysis showing that the policy evaluation error decreases geometrically with CoT length and eventually saturates at a statistical floor determined by the context length. We also prove that the desired Transformer parameters are a global minimizer of the pretraining loss, providing a theoretical understanding on the empirical emergence of those parameters.

</details>


### [195] [The Position Curse: LLMs Struggle to Locate the Last Few Items in a List](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07127)
*Zhanqi Zhang,Hua-Dong Xiong,Robert C. Wilson,Mikio Aoi,Marcelo G. Mattar,Li Ji-An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLMs存在的“位置诅咒”现象：模型能从海量数据中精准定位单个事实，却难以检索短列表末尾的几个项目。通过构建PosBench数据集进行LoRA微调可以改善这一问题，但性能仍未达到饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型在“大海捞针”任务中表现优异，但在简单的短列表位置检索任务中却频频失败（例如难以识别代码片段中的倒数第二行）。作者旨在系统性地研究这一被称为“位置诅咒”的现象，因为对于在大型代码库上操作的LLM编码智能体而言，精确的索引能力对于代码理解和编辑至关重要。

Method: 1. 设计了两组互补的查询任务进行评估：给定位置检索对应项目，以及给定项目返回其位置。
2. 位置通过相对于锚点（列表端点或列表中其他项目）的前向或后向偏移量来指定。
3. 构建了专注于位置检索的训练数据集PosBench。
4. 使用LoRA微调技术对模型进行后训练。

Result: 1. 在开源和闭源前沿模型中，后向检索能力显著落后于前向检索能力。
2. LoRA微调同时提升了前向和后向检索性能，并能泛化到留出的代码理解基准测试（PyIndex），但绝对性能距离饱和状态仍相去甚远。

Conclusion: 位置检索能力是LLMs的关键短板，也是未来预训练目标和模型设计中需要重点关注的要素，特别是随着LLM编码智能体越来越多地在大型代码库上运行，精确的位置索引能力变得不可或缺。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) can find a needle in a haystack (locating a single relevant fact buried among hundreds of thousands of irrelevant tokens) with near-saturated accuracy, yet fail to retrieve the last few items in a short list. We call this failure the Position Curse. For instance, even in a two-line code snippet, Claude Opus 4.6 misidentifies the second-to-last line most of the time. To characterize this failure, we evaluated two complementary queries: given a position in a sequence (of letters or words), retrieve the corresponding item; and given an item, return its position. Each position is specified as a forward or backward offset from an anchor, either an endpoint of the list (its start or end) or another item in the list. Across both open-source and frontier closed-source models, backward retrieval substantially lags forward retrieval. To test whether this capability can be rescued by post-training, we constructed PosBench, a position-focused training dataset. LoRA fine-tuning improves both forward and backward retrieval and generalizes to a held-out code-understanding benchmark (PyIndex), yet absolute performance remains far from saturated. As LLM coding agents increasingly operate over large codebases where precise indexing becomes essential for code understanding and editing, position-based retrieval emerges as a key capability for future pretraining objectives and model design.

</details>


### [196] [Simple KNN-Based Outlier Detection Achieves Robust Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07130)
*Tianle Jiang,Yufa Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在合理的假设下，简单的K近邻距离剔除异常点方法能够为鲁棒k-Means聚类问题提供常数因子近似保证，并在实验中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒k-Means问题（带异常点的k-Means）在实际应用中至关重要，但经典异常点检测启发式方法在该问题上的理论保证和实证效果仍缺乏充分研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究简单KNN方法在鲁棒聚类中的有效性。

Method: 作者采用基于K近邻距离的简单启发式方法：计算每个点的K近邻距离并剔除距离较大的点作为异常点，将剩余问题转化为标准k-Means问题求解。该方法不需要引入额外中心点或剔除额外异常点，与现有复杂方法形成对比。

Result: 在最优聚类规模的合理假设下，该方法可将鲁棒k-Means问题以常数因子约减到标准k-Means问题，提供了理论近似保证。在真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在聚类成本和运行时间方面优于或媲美多种更复杂的算法。

Conclusion: 简单的KNN启发式方法对鲁棒聚类具有惊人的有效性，为桥接异常点检测技术和聚类方法提供了新的机遇，表明经典异常点检测方法在理论保证和实践效果上都具有重要价值。

Abstract: Being robust to the presence of outliers is crucial for applying clustering algorithms in practice. In the $\textit{robust $k$-Means}$ problem (i.e., $k$-Means with outliers), the goal is to remove $z$ outliers and minimize the $k$-Means cost on the remaining points. Despite the close connection between robust $k$-Means and outlier detection, both theoretical and empirical understanding of the effectiveness of $\textit{classic outlier detection heuristics}$ for robust $k$-Means remains limited. In this paper, we prove that under a practical assumption on the optimal cluster sizes, simply removing points with large $K$-Nearest-Neighbor distances achieves performance comparable to prior work in terms of approximation guarantees: it yields a constant-factor reduction from robust $k$-Means to standard $k$-Means, without introducing additional centers or discarding extra outliers, as is commonly required by existing approaches. Empirically, experiments on real-world datasets show that our method outperforms or matches several more sophisticated algorithms in terms of clustering cost and runtime. These results demonstrate that simple KNN-based heuristics can be surprisingly effective for robust clustering, highlighting new opportunities to bridge techniques from outlier detection and clustering.

</details>


### [197] [GAD in the Wild: Benchmarking Graph Anomaly Detection under Realistic Deployment Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07133)
*Jingjing Zhou,Shiyu Huang,Qing Qing,Zuquan Yuan,Huafei Huang,Ziqi Xu,Mingliang Hou,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Ivan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多维度图异常检测（GAD）基准测试，在百万规模图、极端异常稀疏和缺失节点属性三种现实挑战下系统评估模型，发现现有方法在大规模真实场景中存在严重的可扩展性和鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图异常检测基准测试多局限于小规模、人工标注且异常比例相对均衡的图数据，导致学术评估与实际部署之间存在巨大鸿沟。为了弥合这一差距，需要构建更贴近现实部署场景的大规模、极端异常稀疏且属性不完整的基准测试。

Method: 提出了一个多维度基准测试框架，系统性地评估GAD模型在三种部署相关挑战下的表现：百万规模图、极端异常稀缺性以及缺失节点属性。从五个不同图数据中衍生出一系列受控基准变体，其中包括两个原生工业级数据集（节点数超过370万），并对九种代表性GAD模型进行了广泛评估。

Result: 评估揭示了三个主要局限性：（1）大多数基于GNN的方法由于内存需求过高，无法扩展到百万节点规模的图；（2）在现实异常比例（如0.1%）下，检测性能急剧下降，常导致零召回率；（3）基于重构的模型对属性填充策略高度敏感。实验表明实验室环境下的优异性能并不能保证生产环境中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究强调了当前GAD模型在真实大规模、不完美图数据上的不足，实验室表现不能代表实际部署效果。研究者发布了该基准测试和实证评估作为诊断测试平台，旨在促进开发能够应对实践中大规模、不完美图数据的鲁棒且可扩展的GAD系统。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is a critical task in graph machine learning with vital applications in financial fraud detection and social platform governance. However, existing GAD benchmarks are often restricted to small-scale, curated graphs with relatively balanced anomaly ratios, leaving a substantial gap between academic evaluation and real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-dimensional benchmark that systematically evaluates GAD models under three deployment-relevant challenges: million-scale graphs, extreme anomaly scarcity, and missing node attributes. We derive a family of controlled benchmark variants from five diverse graphs, including two native industrial-scale datasets with over 3.7 million nodes. Our extensive evaluation of nine representative GAD models reveals three major limitations: (1) most GNN-based methods fail to scale to million-node graphs due to prohibitive memory requirements; (2) detection performance drops sharply under realistic anomaly ratios (e.g., 0.1\%), often resulting in zero recall; and (3) reconstruction-based models are highly sensitive to attribute imputation strategies. Our findings suggest that strong performance in laboratory settings does not guarantee robustness in production environments. We release this benchmark and empirical evaluation as a diagnostic testbed to promote the development of robust and scalable GAD systems for large-scale, imperfect graphs encountered in practice. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Benchmark_GAD-E7A3.

</details>


### [198] [Adaptive Negative Reinforcement for LLM Reasoning:Dynamically Balancing Correction and Diversity in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07137)
*Yash Ingle,Jaival Chauhan,Ankit Yadav,Sudhakar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出A-NSR和CW-NSR两种改进方法，通过动态惩罚调度和置信度加权优化负样本强化学习，在数学推理数据集上提升LLM推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有负样本强化学习(NSR)方法存在两个局限性：一是训练过程中使用固定惩罚规则，二是对所有错误响应同等对待。这限制了模型的学习效率和性能提升空间。

Method: 提出两种扩展方法：1) 自适应负样本强化学习(A-NSR)：使用时间依赖调度函数，训练初期重点纠错以稳定模型，后期转向更精细的更新；2) 置信度加权负强化(CW-NSR)：根据模型归一化序列似然分配惩罚权重，对高置信度错误给予更大惩罚，对探索性不确定错误轻惩罚。该方法实现了先验引导的概率重分布并防止过拟合。

Result: 在MATH、AIME 2025和AMC23等高难度推理数据集上，使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B架构进行了实验验证，证明了所提方法的有效性。理论分析表明该方法能精确控制token级更新。

Conclusion: A-NSR和CW-NSR通过动态惩罚调度和置信度敏感的错误权重分配，有效解决了传统NSR方法的局限性，为LLM推理能力提升提供了更精细和自适应的训练框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a highly effective method for improving the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Recent research shows that Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR) -- which focuses on penalizing incorrect steps rather than simply rewarding correct ones -- can match or even exceed the performance of more complex frameworks like PPO and GRPO across the entire Pass@k spectrum. However, current NSR techniques usually apply a fixed penalty throughout the training process and treat every incorrect response with the same weight.
  To address these limitations, we propose two extensions to the NSR framework: Adaptive Negative Sample Reinforcement. Rather than using a fixed update rule, A-NSR uses time-dependent scheduling functions. In the initial training phases, the system focuses heavily on correcting errors to stabilize the model. As training continues, it shifts toward more subtle and controlled updates. We also introduce Confidence-Weighted Negative Reinforcement, which operates on the principle that different mistakes carry different levels of importance. CW-NSR assigns specific penalty weights based on the model's normalized sequence likelihood. If the model is highly confident in a wrong path, it receives a larger penalty and for uncertain errors -- where the model is effectively exploring -- are penalized less strictly. Our formal analysis shows how these mechanisms govern token-level updates, allowing the model to leverage prior-guided probability redistribution while providing a natural defense against overfitting. We evaluated these methods on difficult reasoning datasets, including MATH, AIME 2025, and AMC23, using the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B architecture.

</details>


### [199] [Regret-Oracle Complexity Tradeoffs in Agnostic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07155)
*Idan Attias,Steve Hanneke,Arvind Ramaswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对不可知在线学习中的预言机复杂度问题，提出了一种基于弱一致性预言机的自适应动态归约算法，将查询复杂度从双重指数级$O(T^{2^{O(d_{LD})}})$大幅降低至多项式级$O(T^{d_{VC}+1})$，同时保持近最优期望悔界，并给出了悔界与查询复杂度之间权衡的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的不可知在线学习通过归约到可实现情形并使用Littlestone的标准最优算法（SOA）来求解。然而，SOA即使在单轮也具有计算不可行性。近期工作使用离线经验风险最小化（ERM）预言机替代SOA，虽能获得近最优期望悔界，但预言机复杂度高达双重指数级$O(T^{2^{O(d_{LD})}})$。本文旨在显著降低该复杂度，且仅依赖更弱的基元——弱一致性预言机。

Method: 提出一种自适应且动态的不可知到可实现归约方法，利用弱一致性预言机（仅判断给定标签数据集是否可实现）主动即时剪除非可实现标签序列。通过使用VC维度$d_{VC}$来界定动态维护的活跃路径数量，有效控制查询开销并实现内存优化。同时形式化量化悔界与预言机复杂度之间的权衡关系。

Result: 算法将总查询复杂度降低至$O(T^{d_{VC}+1})$（从双重指数级降至多项式级），同时完美保持近最优期望悔界；动态剪枝带来内存消耗降低；给出了悔界与查询复杂度权衡的上界；证明任何限制于$Q = o(\sqrt{T})$次查询的学习器必然承受$Ω(T/Q)$的期望悔界下界。

Conclusion: 本文通过弱一致性预言机和动态剪枝策略，成功突破了不可知在线学习中预言机复杂度的关键瓶颈，实现了从双重指数到多项式的复杂度飞跃，同时维持近最优性能，为在线学习算法的实际可计算性提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Agnostic online learning is classically solved via a reduction to the realizable setting, utilizing Littlestone's Standard Optimal Algorithm (SOA) as a base learner. However, the SOA is computationally intractable to execute even for a single round. To overcome this barrier, recent work in oracle-efficient online learning replaces the SOA with a realizable base learner that accesses the concept class exclusively through an offline empirical risk minimization (ERM) oracle. While such agnostic learners achieve near-optimal expected regret, they suffer from a doubly-exponential oracle complexity of $O\big(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}}\big)$, where $d_\mathrm{LD}$ is the Littlestone dimension and $T$ is the number of rounds. In this work, we significantly improve this oracle complexity while relying on an even weaker primitive: a weak-consistency oracle, which merely decides whether a given labeled dataset is realizable. At the core of our approach is an adaptive and dynamic agnostic-to-realizable reduction that actively prunes non-realizable label sequences on the fly. By using the VC dimension ($d_\mathrm{VC}$) to bound the number of dynamically maintained active paths, our algorithm reduces the total query complexity down to $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$ while perfectly preserving near-optimal expected regret. Crucially, this dynamic pruning also yields a memory reduction over the standard reduction. Furthermore, we formally quantify the regret--oracle complexity tradeoff, providing upper bounds that smoothly interpolate between restricted query budgets and attainable expected regret. We complement these with lower bounds proving that any learner restricted to $Q = o(\sqrt{T})$ queries must suffer an expected regret of $Ω(T/Q)$.

</details>


### [200] [Learned Lagrangian Models of PDEs via Euler-Lagrange Residual Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07157)
*Lyra Zhornyak,Eric Forgoston,M. Ani Hsieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个直接利用学习到的连续拉格朗日量来预测偏微分方程系统动力学的方法，通过基于优化的无网格近辛积分器，实现了稳定的长期预测，且具有线性扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对学习模型的积分器存在全局耦合问题，导致时空步进速度缓慢且学习过程复杂，同时难以解耦模型误差（相位误差）与积分误差（守恒误差）。本研究旨在利用系统固有的守恒结构，开发一种能够克服上述局限、实现稳定长期预测的方法。

Method: 作者开发了一种基于优化的积分器，该方法在局部时空块上通过无网格的近辛构造，最小化欧拉-拉格朗日残差的平方。通过依赖优化而非时间步进，该方法避免了固定离散化固有的全局耦合，对学习网络无结构要求，并可通过雅可比迭代实现线性扩展。

Result: 该方法在双摆、一维波动方程和二维波动方程的学习表示上进行了验证。实验结果显示，其误差与经典辛方法相当，且能够泛化到空间变化的动力学和任意边界条件，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 该方法成功利用学习到的连续拉格朗日量实现了对偏微分方程系统的稳定长期预测，在保持精度的同时解决了传统方法的耦合与扩展性问题，为物理引导的机器学习领域提供了一种高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present the first method to directly use a learned continuous Lagrangian to forecast the dynamics of systems governed by partial differential equations, exploiting the inherent conservative structure to achieve stable long-range predictions. We develop an optimization-based integrator that minimizes the squared Euler--Lagrange residual via a mesh-free near-symplectic construction on local space-time patches. Different from integrators for analytical models, integrators for learned models should decouple model error (phase error) from integration error (conservation error). By relying on optimization rather than time-stepping, we bypass the global coupling inherent to fixed discretizations, which slows time- and space-stepping and complicates learning. Our method scales linearly with domain size via Jacobi iteration, and places no structural requirements on the learned network, allowing it to be coupled with existing physics-guided machine learning (ML) methods. We validate our approach on a learned representation of a double pendulum, a one-dimensional wave equation, and a two-dimensional wave equation. Our method achieves error comparable to classical symplectic methods while generalizing to spatially varying dynamics and arbitrary boundary conditions without retraining.

</details>


### [201] [Neurosymbolic Imitation Learning with Human Guidance: A Privileged Information Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07166)
*Nikhilesh Prabhakar,Varun Balaji,Athresh Karanam,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号模仿学习方法，结合了神经网络处理高维数据的优势和符号方法的泛化能力，利用训练时的特权信息（注视数据）来提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯神经方法虽能处理高维数据但需大量样本且易过拟合，纯符号方法泛化能力强但不擅处理高维数据，作者旨在结合二者优势解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出一种神经符号（Neurosymbolic）方法，利用仅在训练阶段可用的特权信息（Privileged Information，具体为注视数据/Gaze data）来增强学习过程。

Result: 实证评估表明，该方法在有效性、效率以及泛化能力方面均表现良好，成功结合了神经方法与符号方法的优势。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能够在模仿学习中有效处理高维数据同时实现良好的泛化，充分利用训练时的特权信息是提升性能的关键。

Abstract: Imitation learning is widely used for learning to act in complex environments. While pure neural-based methods handle high dimensional data effectively, they suffer from the requirement of large number of samples and are prone to overfitting. Pure symbolic approaches, while generalize well, do not handle high-dimensional data effectively. We propose a neurosymbolic approach that achieves the best of both worlds, i.e, handling high-dimensional data while achieving generalization. The key advantage of our approach is that it can effectively exploit additional privileged information that is available only during training (in our case, gaze data). Our empirical evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency and the generalization capability of our proposed approach.

</details>


### [202] [Cost-Ordered Feasibility for Multi-Armed Bandits with Cost Subsidy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07171)
*Ishank Juneja,Carlee Joe-Wong,Osman Yağan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带成本补贴的多臂老虎机问题（MAB-CS），目标是满足未知最优奖励约束的前提下最小化成本。提出了实例相关的下界，设计了COF算法，并证明了其在累积成本和质量遗憾上的优越性能，理论和实验均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典多臂老虎机问题关注在不确定性下最大化奖励，但实际应用中往往需要在满足最低奖励约束的前提下最小化成本。现有研究未充分解决奖励约束相对于未知最优奖励定义且各臂成本已知的场景，因此需要新的理论分析与高效算法。

Method: 1. 推导实例相关的下界，刻画任何策略所需期望次优样本数量，揭示问题本质并提供理论基准；2. 提出Cost-Ordered Feasibility（COF）算法，利用理论洞察，智能组合所有臂的样本以评估廉价臂的可行性；3. 分析COF，建立其在期望累积成本和质量遗憾上的实例相关上界。

Result: 1. 获得了严格推广现有结果的实例相关下界；2. COF算法在理论上具有更优的遗憾上界；3. 在MovieLens、Goodreads数据集及合成实例上，COF相比文献基线展现出显著改进的实证性能。

Conclusion: 本文为MAB-CS问题提供了更深入的理论理解，所提出的COF算法在理论上具有更优的遗憾保证，在实际数据集上也表现出更优的实证效果，证明了该方法的有效性与实用价值。

Abstract: The classic multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem tackles the challenge of accruing maximum reward while making decisions under uncertainty. However, in applications, often the goal is to minimize cost subject to a constraint on the minimum permissible reward, an objective captured by multi-armed bandits with cost-subsidy (MAB-CS). Of interest to this paper is the setting where the quality (reward) constraint is specified relative to the unknown best reward and the cost of each arm is known. We characterize the expected sub-optimal samples required by any policy by proving instance-dependent lower bounds that offer new insight into the problem and are a strict generalization of prior bounds. Then, we propose an algorithm called Cost-Ordered Feasibility (COF) that leverages our insight and intelligently combine samples from all arms to gauge the feasibility of a cheap arm. Thereafter, we analyze COF to establish instance-dependent upper bounds on its expected cumulative cost and quality regret, i.e., relative to the cheapest feasible arm. Finally, we empirically validate the merits of COF, comparing it to baselines from the literature through extensive simulation experiments on the MovieLens and Goodreads datasets as well as representative synthetic instances. Not only does our paper develop qualitatively better theoretical regret upper bounds, but COF also convincingly demonstrates improved empirical performance.

</details>


### [203] [Learning Multi-Relational Graph Representations for DNA Methylation-Based Biological Age Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07175)
*Qing Qing,Xikun Zhang,Zhongyuan Zhang,Jiarui Liu,Xingtong Yu,Xiaotao Shen,Ziqi Xu,Qixin Zhang,Zhe Wang,Renqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RelAge-GNN，一种基于多关系图神经网络的DNA甲基化年龄预测框架。通过构建三种互补图来捕捉CpG位点间的复杂关系，实验显示该方法在预测准确性和疾病检测灵敏度方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DNA甲基化年龄预测方法大多将CpG位点视为独立特征，忽略了它们之间复杂且异质的生物学关系。然而，CpG位点之间存在着共甲基化模式、基因组共定位和基因水平关联等重要关系，这些信息对于准确预测生物学年龄至关重要。

Method: 提出RelAge-GNN多关系图神经网络框架：1）构建三个互补图分别捕捉CpG位点的共甲基化模式、基因组共定位和基因水平关联；2）每个图由独立的GNN分支建模；3）使用可学习的门控机制自适应融合各分支的表示。此外，通过事后可解释性分析量化不同关系结构和CpG位点的贡献。

Result: 在大规模数据集上的实验表明：1）RelAge-GNN实现了与最先进方法相比具有竞争力的准确性；2）与实际年龄的相关性更强；3）在不同疾病队列中检测年龄加速的敏感性提高，显示出更好的疾病表征能力。

Conclusion: RelAge-GNN成功地将CpG位点间的多种生物学关系整合到年龄预测中，不仅提高了预测性能，还增强了疾病检测的敏感性。可解释性分析提供了具有生物学意义的见解，为衰老相关研究指明了潜在方向，证明了该方法在健康评估和疾病分析中的应用价值。

Abstract: Aging clocks aim to estimate biological age, a measure of physiological state distinct from chronological age, from observable biomarkers, and are widely used for health assessment and disease analysis. DNA methylation is a particularly informative biomarker due to its stability and strong association with aging, and recent learning-based approaches have improved predictive performance. However, most existing methods treat CpG sites as independent features, overlooking the complex and heterogeneous biological relationships among them. We propose RelAge-GNN, a multi-relational graph neural network framework for DNA methylation-based age prediction. Our method constructs three complementary graphs capturing co-methylation patterns, genomic co-localization, and gene-level associations among CpG sites. Each graph is modeled by an independent GNN branch, and a learnable gating mechanism adaptively fuses the resulting representations. Experiments on large-scale datasets show that RelAge-GNN achieves competitive accuracy and stronger correlation with chronological age compared to state-of-the-art methods. Moreover, the model exhibits improved sensitivity in detecting age acceleration across diverse disease cohorts, highlighting its potential utility for disease characterization. Finally, through post hoc interpretability analyses, we quantify the contributions of different relational structures and CpG sites, providing biologically meaningful insights and suggesting potential directions for aging-related research. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RelAge-GNN-F1E3/.

</details>


### [204] [HyperEyes: Dual-Grained Efficiency-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Parallel Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07177)
*Guankai Li,Jiabin Chen,Yi Xu,Xichen Zhang,Yuan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyperEyes，一种并行多模态搜索智能体，通过并发查询将准确率提升9.9%，同时将工具调用轮数减少5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态搜索智能体对目标实体进行顺序处理，每次工具调用仅处理一个实体。当查询可分解为多个独立子检索时，这种方式会导致冗余的交互轮次。作者认为高效的多模态智能体应“横向搜索而非纵向搜索”，即在单轮内并发调度多个基于视觉定位的查询。

Method: 提出HyperEyes并行多模态搜索智能体，将视觉定位与检索融合为单一原子操作。训练分两阶段：1) 冷启动监督阶段，设计并行友好数据合成流水线，通过渐进式拒绝采样生成面向效率的轨迹；2) 提出双层粒度效率感知强化学习框架——宏观层面设计TRACE轨迹级奖励，通过单调收紧参考基准来抑制冗余工具调用；微观层面采用在策略蒸馏，为失败轨迹注入密集的token级校正信号，缓解稀疏结果奖励的信用分配不足问题。同时构建IMEB基准，联合评估搜索能力与效率。

Result: 在六个基准测试中，HyperEyes-30B相比最强可比开源智能体准确率提升9.9%，工具调用轮数平均减少5.3倍。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了并行查询调度的有效性，将推理效率作为一等训练目标是提升多模态搜索智能体性能的关键。HyperEyes在保持高准确率的同时显著降低了推理成本，为构建高效多模态智能体提供了新思路。

Abstract: Existing multimodal search agents process target entities sequentially, issuing one tool call per entity and accumulating redundant interaction rounds whenever a query decomposes into independent sub-retrievals. We argue that effective multimodal agents should search wider rather than longer: dispatching multiple grounded queries concurrently within a round. To this end, we present HyperEyes, a parallel multimodal search agent that fuses visual grounding and retrieval into a single atomic action, enabling concurrent search across multiple entities while treating inference efficiency as a first-class training objective. HyperEyes is trained in two stages. For cold-start supervision, we develop a Parallel-Amenable Data Synthesis Pipeline covering visual multi-entity and textual multi-constraint queries, curating efficiency-oriented trajectories via Progressive Rejection Sampling. Building on this, our central contribution, a Dual-Grained Efficiency-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework, operates at two levels. At the macro level, we propose TRACE (Tool-use Reference-Adaptive Cost Efficiency), a trajectory-level reward whose reference is monotonically tightened during training to suppress superfluous tool calls without restricting genuine multi-hop search. At the micro level, we adapt On-Policy Distillation to inject dense token-level corrective signals from an external teacher on failed rollouts, mitigating the credit-assignment deficiency of sparse outcome rewards. Since existing benchmarks evaluate accuracy as the sole metric, omitting inference cost, we introduce IMEB, a human-curated benchmark of 300 instances that jointly evaluates search capability and efficiency. Across six benchmarks, HyperEyes-30B surpasses the strongest comparable open-source agent by 9.9% in accuracy with 5.3x fewer tool-call rounds on average.

</details>


### [205] [Star Elastic: Many-in-One Reasoning LLMs with Efficient Budget Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07182)
*Ali Taghibakhshi,Ruisi Cai,Saurav Muralidharan,Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas,Aditya Vavre,Ameya Sunil Mahabaleshwarkar,Bilal Kartal,Sheldon Liang,Marcin Chochowski,Zijia Chen,Akhiad Bercovich,Ran Zilberstein,Ran El-Yaniv,Yonatan Geifman,Daniel Korzekwa,Yoshi Suhara,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Ashwath Aithal,Nima Tajbakhsh,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Star Elastic方法，通过一次后训练在父模型中嵌套N个子模型，实现360倍训练成本降低，并支持弹性推理预算控制，在Nemotron Nano模型上实现最高16%准确率提升和1.9倍延迟降低。


<details>
