  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法训练大型语言模型家族（从零训练或迭代压缩）成本高昂且效率低下，每个模型需单独训练；且现有推理架构具有刚性，无法根据token难度动态分配资源。

Method: 1）沿SSM、嵌入通道、MoE和FFN轴进行嵌套；2）通过端到端可训练路由器学习嵌套子模型；3）基于课程的知识蒸馏；4）扩展至量化感知蒸馏（QAD）支持NVFP4和FP8量化。

Result: 从Nemotron Nano v3（30B/3.6A）生成23B（2.8A）和12B（2.0A）变体，所有嵌套模型性能匹配或超越独立训练基线；相比从零预训练减少360倍成本，相比SOTA压缩减少7倍成本；弹性预算控制实现最高16%准确率提升和1.9倍延迟降低。

Conclusion: Star Elastic成功实现了高效模型家族训练与弹性推理的统一，显著降低训练成本的同时突破准确率-延迟帕累托前沿，为LLM的高效部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Training a family of large language models (LLMs), either from scratch or via iterative compression, is prohibitively expensive and inefficient, requiring separate training runs for each model in the family. In this paper, we introduce Star Elastic, a novel LLM post-training method that adds N nested submodels to a given parent reasoning model using the compute of one run (N-fold savings) via a single post-training job. Beyond reducing training costs, Star Elastic also addresses a fundamental limitation of efficient reasoning: the rigidity of static architectures, which forces the allocation of constant resources regardless of token difficulty. By unlocking elastic budget control, Star Elastic enables a novel inference scheme that uses different submodels for each reasoning phase (thinking and answering). Star Elastic supports (1) nesting along the SSM, embedding channel, MoE, and FFN axes, (2) learning nested submodels via an end-to-end trainable router, and (3) curriculum-based knowledge distillation. Building on the Nemotron Elastic framework, we apply Star Elastic to the NVIDIA Nemotron Nano models, with a particular focus on hybrid Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures: from Nemotron Nano v3 (30B/3.6A), we generate 23B (2.8A) and 12B (2.0A) variants with 160B training tokens. All nested models match or outperform independently trained baselines of comparable size and achieve a 360x reduction versus pretraining from scratch and a 7x reduction over state-of-the-art compression. Crucially, elastic budget control advances the accuracy-latency Pareto frontier, achieving up to 16% higher accuracy and 1.9x lower latency via dynamic per-phase model selection. We further extend Star Elastic to quantized regimes via Quantization-Aware Distillation (QAD), producing nested NVFP4 and FP8 elastic checkpoints that preserve zero-shot slicing while delivering smaller deployment footprints.

</details>


### [206] [Coupling Models for One-Step Discrete Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07193)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Avishek Joey Bose,Anru R. Zhang,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Coupling Models，一种单步离散生成模型，通过学习离散序列与高斯潜变量之间的直接耦合，实现了单步生成。实验表明，该方法在文本生成、生物序列设计和图像生成等多个领域均显著优于现有的最强单步基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散结构生成模型通常依赖于自回归解码或迭代优化，生成过程往往是顺序且多步的，效率较低。尽管已有蒸馏方法试图将多步采样压缩为少步，但仍未实现真正的单步高效生成。因此，本文旨在探索如何实现高效的单步离散生成。

Method: 提出Coupling Models，这是一种专为单步生成设计的模型。与将预训练多步采样器蒸馏压缩的方法不同，该方法训练了一个专用解码器来反演离散序列与高斯潜变量之间的耦合，从而实现单步生成。同时，该模型避免了在单纯形上进行复杂的连续流计算以及手工指定的数据-噪声耦合方式。

Result: 在多个领域的实验中，Coupling Models显著提升了最强单步基线的性能：在LM1B文本生成任务上，将困惑度降低了33%；在果蝇大脑增强子设计任务上，FBD指标降低了18%；在MNIST-Binary图像生成任务上，FID降低了46%。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了有效的单步离散生成在很大程度上取决于解码前数据与噪声的耦合方式。Coupling Models通过建立直接耦合并使用专用解码器反演，为实现高质量、高效率的单步离散生成提供了一种新的有效路径。

Abstract: Generative modeling over discrete structures underpins applications across deep learning, from biological sequence design and code generation to large language models, yet generation often remains sequential, relying on autoregressive decoding or iterative refinement. In this work, we introduce Coupling Models(Coupling Models), a one-step discrete generative model that learns a direct coupling between discrete sequences and Gaussian latents. Unlike recent distillation methods that compress a pretrained multi-step sampler into a few steps, Coupling Model trains a purpose-built decoder to invert this coupling and generate samples in a single step. The model also avoids complex continuous flows over the simplex and hand-specified data-to-noise couplings. Empirically,Coupling Model improves the strongest one-step baselines in each domain: it reduces LM1B text-generation perplexity by 33% at its lowest-perplexity operating point, Fly Brain enhancer-design FBD by 18%, and MNIST-Binary FID by 46%. These results suggest that effective one-step discrete generation depends strongly on how data and noise are coupled before decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/Coupling-Models.

</details>


### [207] [Arrow: A Foundation Model for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07204)
*Ryan Thompson,He Zhao,Daniel M. Steinberg,Edwin V. Bonilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Arrow，一个用于表格数据零样本因果发现的基础模型。通过将有向无环图分解为无向骨架和拓扑排序来保证无环性，在合成数据上预训练后，能以极低的推理成本在多种数据集上匹配或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果发现方法通常计算成本高昂，且针对每个新数据集都需要重新运行算法。本文旨在利用大规模预训练技术，开发一个零样本的因果发现基础模型，使其能够快速、准确且可复用地处理新的观测表格数据集。

Method: Arrow采用基于Transformer的架构来捕捉变量在样本内和样本间的上下文信息。其核心方法是将有向无环图（DAG）分解为无向骨架和拓扑排序，从而在构造上保证无环性。模型在有地面真值的合成数据集上进行有监督训练，利用由骨架-排序分解诱导的端到端可微有向边复合似然函数进行优化，训练数据覆盖了多样的图族、函数形式、噪声模型和数据集形态。

Result: 在分布内外的合成数据、半合成数据及真实数据上的实验表明，Arrow在准确性上匹配或优于现有的因果发现方法，同时其推理成本显著低于有竞争力的替代方案。

Conclusion: 大规模合成数据预训练可以产生快速、准确且可复用的零样本因果发现模型，证明了基础模型范式在因果发现领域的有效性和应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Arrow, a foundation model for zero-shot causal discovery on observational tabular data. Arrow factorizes a directed acyclic graph into an undirected skeleton and a topological order, guaranteeing acyclicity by construction. Given a new dataset, it uses a transformer-based architecture to contextualize variables within and across observations, then predicts skeleton edge probabilities and node order scores that together define a graph. Arrow is trained in a supervised fashion on synthetic datasets with ground-truth graphs, using an end-to-end differentiable directed edge composite likelihood induced by the skeleton-order factorization. The training distribution spans diverse graph families, functional forms, noise models, and dataset shapes. Across in- and out-of-distribution synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real datasets, Arrow matches or outperforms existing causal discovery methods at substantially lower inference cost than competitive alternatives. Our results demonstrate that large-scale pretraining on diverse synthetic data can yield zero-shot causal discovery models that are fast, accurate, and reusable on new datasets.

</details>


### [208] [FAME: Forecasting Academic Impact via Continuous-Time Manifold Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07208)
*Jianrong Ding,Jianyuan Zhong,Zhengyan Shi,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FAME框架，通过时空建模预测学术论文影响力，显著优于现有LLM评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在评估研究想法时难以可靠区分高影响力论文与普通论文，且静态文本评估不足以完成科学评价任务。需要一种可验证的代理任务来预测论文的真实影响力。

Method: 提出FAME（通过连续时间流形演化预测学术影响力）框架，这是一个时空建模框架。将论文投影到由文本特征和验证的知识流图构建的动态潜在空间中，学习几何约束，使高影响力论文与其领域的前进动力对齐。

Result: 在3,200篇arXiv论文（跨越三个快速发展的子领域）的实验中，FAME在前瞻性多维影响力预测任务上持续且显著地优于最先进的LLM评估器。将FAME的动态几何信号集成到LLM中可显著提升其预测性能。

Conclusion: 论文影响力预测是一个有用且可测量的代理基准，FAME为自动化科学评估提供了强大的、具有轨迹感知能力的基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to brainstorm and evaluate research ideas, yet assessing such judgments is fundamentally difficult because the true impact of a new idea may take years to emerge. We address this challenge by using the impact forecasting of human-authored manuscripts as a verifiable proxy task. In a prospective forecasting study, we find that frontier LLMs fail to reliably distinguish high-impact papers from ordinary publications, suggesting that static text-based judging is insufficient for scientific evaluation. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{FAME}$ ($\underline{\text{F}}$orecasting $\underline{\text{A}}$cademic Impact via Continuous-Time $\underline{\text{M}}$anifold $\underline{\text{E}}$volution), a spatiotemporal framework for modeling the dynamic trajectories of scientific topics. FAME projects papers into a dynamic latent space informed by textual features and a verified knowledge-flow graph, learning geometric constraints that align impactful manuscripts with the forward momentum of their fields. Experiments on 3,200 arXiv papers across three fast-evolving subfields show that FAME consistently and substantially outperforms state-of-the-art LLM evaluators in prospective multidimensional impact forecasting. Furthermore, integrating FAME's dynamic geometric signals into LLMs significantly improves their forecasting performance. These results support manuscript impact forecasting as a useful, measurable proxy benchmark and position FAME as a strong, trajectory-aware foundation for automated scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [209] [HARMONY: Bridging the Personalization-Generalization Gap by Mitigating Representation Skew in Heterogeneous Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07211)
*Jiseok Youn,You Rim Choi,Goodsol Lee,Sangtae Ha,Hyung-Sin Kim,Saewoong Bahk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HARMONY，这是首个支持异构客户端架构的混合分割联邦学习框架。通过改进元学习模拟多样化的特征提取器，并利用服务器端对比学习解决表示偏斜问题，在IDD和OOD测试中分别将准确率提升了43.0%和28.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备面临资源约束和非独立同分布数据挑战，现有的混合分割联邦学习在客户端架构异构时存在“表示偏斜”问题：定制化特征提取器产生的特征在共享空间无法对齐，导致负责分布外(OOD)预测的服务器模型性能急剧下降。

Method: 提出HARMONY框架：1) 改进元学习以模拟跨参数和架构的多样化特征提取器，学习个性化能力；2) 引入服务器端对比学习来对齐提取的特征，既不牺牲客户端个性化，也不共享原始标签，从而缓解表示偏斜。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型家族上的实验表明，与最先进方法相比，HARMONY在无OOD/有OOD场景下测试准确率分别提升高达43.0%和28.3%，同时保持可接受的延迟。

Conclusion: HARMONY成功解决了混合分割联邦学习中客户端架构异构导致的表示偏斜问题，有效平衡了本地分布内类别的快速推理与客户端特定的分布外类别的远程支持需求，证明了在异构环境下提升联邦学习性能的可行性。

Abstract: Mobile devices face diverse resource constraints and non-IID data class distributions, requiring fast on-device inference for local in-distribution (ID) classes and on-demand remote support for client-specific out-of-distribution (OOD) classes. Hybrid split federated learning (Hybrid SFL) couples personalized client-side front ends (supporting early exit) with a generalized server-side backend for fallback inference, balancing accuracy and cost. However, under client architectural heterogeneity, the existing hybrid SFL suffers from representation skew, where features from customized extractors fail to align in the shared space, leading to a sharp degradation in the server model responsible for OOD prediction. We propose HARMONY, the first hybrid SFL framework to support heterogeneous client architectures. HARMONY modifies meta-learning to simulate diverse extractors across parameters and architectures, and to learn to personalize. To mitigate representation skew, HARMONY conducts server-side contrastive learning to align extracted features, neither sacrificing clients' personalization nor sharing raw labels. Compared to the state of the art across multiple datasets and model families, HARMONY improves test accuracy by up to 43.0%/28.3% without/with OOD, respectively, while maintaining acceptable latency.

</details>


### [210] [Same Brain, Different Prediction: How Preprocessing Choices Undermine EEG Decoding Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07212)
*Dengzhe Hou,Zihao Wu,Lingyu Jiang,Zirui Li,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori D. Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现EEG深度学习模型对预处理流程极其敏感（预测翻转率高达42%），提出了三种工具来量化并降低这种不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG深度学习研究通常在单一、未报告的预处理流程下训练和评估模型，忽略了预处理选择对预测结果的影响。作者旨在揭示预处理变化导致的预测不稳定性问题，这种变异性是标准不确定性方法无法显式量化的。

Method: 1. 将预处理选择形式化为反事实干预空间；
2. 使用Walsh-Hadamard分解分析2^7管道空间，发现敏感性在实际中近似可加；
3. 提出预处理不确定性(PU)作为逐试验诊断工具；
4. 引入图结构正则化方法NA-PGI，利用预处理干预的组合结构来缓解不稳定性。

Result: 在跨越四种范式、六个数据集的实验中，仅改变预处理流程就有高达42%的试验级预测发生翻转。敏感性分析表明在二元干预设计下敏感性近似可加，使得逐步优化成为可能。预处理不确定性与模型置信度呈互补关系。

Conclusion: EEG预测在预处理干预空间中存在显著不稳定性，研究提供了使其可测量、可分解、可降低的框架和工具，为EEG深度学习模型的可靠性评估和改进提供了新视角。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a cornerstone of brain-computer interfaces and clinical neuroscience, yet deep learning models are typically trained and evaluated under a single, unreported preprocessing pipeline. We formalize preprocessing choices as a counterfactual intervention space and show that EEG predictions are surprisingly unstable under this space: across six datasets spanning four paradigms, up to 42% of trial-level predictions flip when only the preprocessing changes, a variability that standard uncertainty methods do not explicitly quantify because they condition on a fixed preprocessing pipeline. We provide three tools to make this instability measurable, decomposable, and reducible. First, a Walsh-Hadamard decomposition of the 2^7 pipeline space reveals that sensitivity is near-additive in practice under the binary intervention design, enabling efficient step-by-step optimization. Second, we introduce Preprocessing Uncertainty (PU), a per-trial diagnostic that captures a dimension of instability complementary to model-based confidence. Third, we study Normalized Adaptive PGI (NA-PGI), a graph-structured regularizer that exploits the compositional structure of preprocessing interventions as one mitigation strategy with clear scope conditions.

</details>


### [211] [Improved Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Smooth Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07218)
*Kun Long,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核平滑的在线强化学习方法，通过引入Bernstein风格的探索奖励，在Lipschitz连续MDP假设下改进了有限时域设置中的最先进遗憾界，特别是对时域的依赖性有所改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对连续状态-动作空间场景，经典强化学习理论主要关注低秩马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs），虽然提供了样本效率保证，但需要限制性的结构假设。核平滑模型方法作为一种有前景的替代范式，利用MDP的平滑性并采用非参数核平滑估计来建模转移动态。

Method: 在Lipschitz连续性假设下，提出了一种新的基于核平滑的有限时域在线强化学习方法。该方法在核平滑框架中引入了Bernstein风格的探索奖励（exploration bonus），并深入分析了Bernstein风格奖励与核平滑之间的协同作用。

Result: 该方法实现了改进的遗憾界，特别是在对时域（horizon）的依赖性方面优于现有的最先进遗憾界。理论进展依赖于一个新的紧凑型Bernstein类鞅浓度不等式。

Conclusion: 通过精细分析Bernstein风格奖励与核平滑之间的协同作用，成功改进了有限时域设置下的遗憾界。其中提出的新紧凑型Bernstein类鞅浓度不等式可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: For continuous state-action space scenarios, classical reinforcement learning (RL) theory predominantly focuses on low-rank Markov decision processes (MDPs), which provide sample-efficient guarantees at the expense of restrictive structural assumptions. Kernel smoothing model-based approaches offer a promising alternative paradigm that instead leverages the smoothness of the MDP and employs non-parametric kernel smoothing estimates of transition dynamics. This paper proposes a new kernel-smoothing model-based approach for online reinforcement learning in finite-horizon settings under Lipschitz continuity assumptions on the MDP. By incorporating a Bernstein-style exploration bonus into the kernel smoothing framework, our method achieves a regret bound which improves upon the state-of-the-art regret bound in its dependence on the horizon. The theoretical advancement relies on a delicate analysis of the synergy between Bernstein-style bonuses and kernel smoothing, where a new tight Bernstein-type concentration inequality for martingales may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [212] [On the Robustness of Distribution Support under Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07220)
*Ruijia Cao,Yuchen Wu,Nisha Chadramoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上解释了扩散引导（Diffusion Guidance）为何能生成高质量样本：在精确分数函数条件下，引导过程几乎总是生成保持在目标支持集附近的样本，从而避免了结构不合理的样本。该分析覆盖DDIM和DDPM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散引导在可控与高保真样本生成方面表现优异，但其理论基础与高质量生成的原因尚未被深入理解。作者希望从理论上阐释其有效性。

Method: 提出并证明了“支持的稳健性（robustness of support）”性质：在可精确获取分数函数的前提下，引导扩散过程几乎总是生成紧邻目标支持集的样本。分析涵盖DDIM与DDPM，并适用于由指数积分器导出的多种离散化方案。

Result: 严格证明了引导扩散过程的样本不会远离目标支持集；该性质可避免生成结构不合理的样本，保障了下游任务的有效性。

Conclusion: 为理解扩散引导为何能生成物理意义明确且结构合理的样本提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Diffusion guidance is a powerful technique that enables controllable and high-fidelity sample generation with diffusion models. At a high level, it modifies the score function by incorporating a guidance term that steers the generative process toward a desired condition. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical properties of diffusion guidance remain largely unexplored, and it is not well understood why it consistently produces high-quality samples.
  In this work, we explain the effectiveness of diffusion guidance by establishing a \emph{robustness of support} property. Specifically, we show that, given exact access to the score functions, guided diffusion processes almost always generate samples that remain close to the target support. This property is particularly desirable, as samples that lie off the support are often structurally implausible and may adversely affect downstream tasks. Our analysis covers both Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models (DDIM) and Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM), and applies to a wide range of discretization schemes induced by exponential integrators. Our results provide a rigorous foundation for understanding why diffusion guidance produces physically meaningful and structurally plausible samples.

</details>


### [213] [Don't Learn the Shape: Forecasting Periodic Time Series by Rank-1 Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07222)
*Takato Honda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FLAIR方法，证明了在周期性时间序列预测中，简单的低秩近似方法（仅需28-57个参数）能够达到与复杂模型PatchTST相当的性能，挑战了“更多参数更好”的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对周期性时间序列预测任务，探索究竟需要多少参数才能实现有效预测。作者观察到小时级电力序列可以重塑为24行矩阵，近似秩为1（日形状被日水平调制），这引发了对是否需要学习复杂形状的质疑。

Method: 提出FLAIR方法：(1)将时间序列重塑为周期结构矩阵；(2)对每个候选周期执行一次SVD；(3)使用GCV平均的Ridge回归；(4)无需GPU、无需预训练、无需逐任务调参。在GIFT-Eval的97个配置上测试了8种替代方法（傅里叶、EWMA、James-Stein、秩-r SVD等）。

Result: 在所有97个GIFT-Eval配置上：(1)8种替代方法均未显著超越简单的冻结基线（Holm校正后）；(2)两种方法显著更差；(3)FLAIR在聚合GIFT-Eval上匹配PatchTST性能（relMASE 0.838 vs 0.849）；(4)参数极其紧凑：小时级28个标量，周级57个标量；(5)计算快速：MacBook Pro单核CPU仅需22分钟。

Conclusion: 在高秩-1、多周期的情况下，额外的灵活性实际上是估计噪声。简单的周期性建模方法在参数效率、计算速度和预测准确性方面都能与复杂深度学习模型竞争，证明了“少即是多”的原则在此类任务中成立。

Abstract: How few parameters do we really need to forecast a periodic time series? An hourly electricity series, reshaped as a 24-row matrix with one column per day, is approximately rank-1: a daily shape modulated by a daily level (median centered rank-1 energy 0.82 on GIFT-Eval). Should we learn the shape? Smoothing, shrinkage, and low-rank fits all seem like obvious upgrades over the simple average of the last K=2 cycles. On all 97 GIFT-Eval configurations, we tested 8 such alternatives (e.g., Fourier, EWMA, James-Stein, rank-r SVD): none significantly beats the frozen baseline under Holm correction; two are significantly worse. The resulting method, FLAIR, is (a) Effective: matches PatchTST on aggregate GIFT-Eval (relMASE 0.838 vs 0.849); (b) Compact: 28 scalars for hourly, 57 for weekly; (c) Fast: 22 minutes on one CPU core of a MacBook Pro; (d) Closed-form & Hands-Off: one SVD per period candidate, GCV-averaged Ridge, no GPU, no pre-training, no per-task tuning. In the high-rank-1, many-cycle regime, extra flexibility is estimation noise.

</details>


### [214] [Experience Sharing in Mutual Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07244)
*Xiaoze Liu,Dhananjay Ram,Yuting Zhang,Zhaoyang Zhang,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了互强化学习框架，使异构大模型能在保持参数、目标和分词器独立的前提下交换类型化经验进行并发RL后训练。实验表明，在稳定性-支持性权衡中，结果层面的成功迁移（SGT）占据最有利位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习后训练方法通常针对单一模型，而不同模型家族之间存在分词器异构、参数独立等障碍，难以直接共享训练经验。作者希望构建一个统一的框架，使异构大模型能够并发训练并有效交换经验，从而提升各自的训练效率和效果。

Method: 提出互强化学习框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)共享经验交换机制(SEE)用于异构策略间的经验交换；(2)多工作者资源分配(MWRA)管理并发训练资源；(3)分词器异构层(THL)实现跨不兼容词表的文本重分词和token级轨迹对齐。在GRPO基础上实现了三种探测方法：数据级rollout共享(PRP)、价值级优势共享(XGRPO)和结果级成功迁移(SGT)，并通过上下文赌博机分析刻画了它们在稳定性-支持性权衡中的结构位置。

Result: 通过理论分析和实验评估，发现三种共享策略在稳定性-支持性权衡中呈现不同特征：PRP需要承担密度比方差和THL残差成本；XGRPO在改变标量基线的同时保持同策略演员支持；SGT提供朝向验证同伴成功的救援集分数方向。在评估范围内，结果层面的共享(SGT)占据了这一权衡中最有利的位置。

Conclusion: 互强化学习框架成功实现了异构大模型间的经验共享，证明了在保持模型独立性的前提下进行并发RL后训练的可行性。研究识别出结果层面的成功迁移策略在稳定性-支持性权衡中具有最优表现，为未来多模型协作训练提供了理论指导和实践基础。

Abstract: We introduce Mutual Reinforcement Learning, a framework for concurrent RL post-training in which heterogeneous LLM policies exchange typed experience while keeping separate parameters, objectives, and tokenizers. The framework combines a Shared Experience Exchange (SEE), Multi-Worker Resource Allocation (MWRA), and a Tokenizer Heterogeneity Layer (THL) that retokenizes text and aligns token-level traces across incompatible vocabularies. This substrate makes the experience-sharing design question operational across model families. We instantiate three controlled probes on top of GRPO: data-level rollout sharing via Peer Rollout Pooling (PRP), value-level advantage sharing via Cross-Policy GRPO Advantage Sharing (XGRPO), and outcome-level success transfer via Success-Gated Transfer (SGT). A contextual-bandit analysis characterizes their structural positions on a stability-support trade-off: PRP pays density-ratio variance and THL residual costs, XGRPO preserves on-policy actor support while changing scalar baselines, and SGT supplies a rescue-set score direction toward verified peer successes. In the evaluated regime, outcome-level sharing occupies the favorable point of this trade-off.

</details>


### [215] [Modulated learning for private and distributed regression with just a single sample per client device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07233)
*Praneeth Vepakomma,Amirhossein Reisizadeh,Samuel Horváth,Munther Dahleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中每个客户端仅持有一个数据样本的场景，提出了一种新的隐私保护学习方法，通过注入校准噪声扰动样本并共享转换表示，实现无偏梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习范式在每客户端仅有一个样本时失效，因为基于单点的本地更新在早期轮次中对模型系数估计作用甚微，且隐私保护噪声进一步削弱其效用。现实应用中存在大量此类场景（如健身追踪器、可穿戴传感设备等），需要解决这些数据极其有限的设备如何协同学习全局模型的问题。

Method: 在每个客户端对单个样本注入一个精心校准的噪声扰动进行转换，然后进行后处理得到表示并共享给服务器。服务器聚合这些表示后进行处理，获得无偏梯度更新，该更新在期望上匹配非私有集中式梯度，同时保护数据隐私。这与传统私有联邦学习不同，后者通信负载涉及模型系数而非私有转换的数据样本。

Result: 所提方法能够使仅有单个样本的设备协同学习准确且隐私保护的模型，在期望上实现了与非私有集中式梯度匹配的无偏梯度更新。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了数据极其有限的客户端参与联邦学习的挑战，无需大型本地数据集或牺牲个人隐私即可实现准确的隐私保护模型学习。

Abstract: This work focuses on the question of learning from a large number of devices with each device holding only a single sample of data. Several real-world applications exist to this one sample per client setup up including learning from fitness trackers, data/app usage aggregators, body-worn sensing devices, and daily event monitors to name a few. When a client has only one sample, the standard federated learning paradigm breaks down as a local update based on that single point is far from being useful, especially in the earlier rounds for estimation of the model coefficients. This utility is further weakened by the privacy-inducing noise applied at every round. This work caters to this problem to enable such clients to collaboratively contribute to effectively learn a global model without leaking the privacy of their data. The proposed approach injects a single, carefully calibrated noisy perturbation to transform the sample at each client, followed by a post-processed representation which is shared with the server. These representations aggregated at the server are processed to obtain an unbiased gradient update that in expectation matches the non-private centralized gradient while preserving data privacy. This approach is different than traditional private federated learning, where the communication payloads involve model coefficients as opposed to privately transformed data samples. This method enables devices with extremely limited data to collaborate and learn accurate, privacy-preserving models without requiring large local datasets or sacrificing individual privacy.

</details>


### [216] [Bifurcation Models: Learning Set-Valued Solution Maps with Weight-Tied Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07277)
*Caleb Jore,Jialin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分岔模型，通过不同初始化收敛到不同稳定平衡态，学习问题的多解集合而非单一标签，在Ising模型上超越单分支监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与组合问题存在多个正确解，传统监督学习通过任意选择单一解作为目标，导致隐藏的选择器不连续且难以学习，而底层解集本身可能更易表示。

Method: 提出分岔模型，采用权重共享的动力学视角，使不同初始化可收敛至不同稳定平衡态，将模型表示为吸引子景观而非单一分支；证明了具有局部Lipschitz分支的广泛集值映射可由正则平衡动力学表示，且诱导的选择器几乎处处正则。

Result: 在受挫Ising模型实验中，该方法无需分支标签即可发现多个有效平衡态，性能优于单分支监督；Allen-Cahn实验表明多样性非自动获得，需显式鼓励但存在精度-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 分岔模型能有效表示多解问题的解集结构，诱导的选择器具有良好正则性，为处理多解问题提供了比传统监督学习更优的框架，但需在精度与多样性间权衡。

Abstract: Many scientific and combinatorial problems admit multiple correct solutions, not a single label. Standard supervised learning resolves this ambiguity by choosing one solution as the target, but this hidden selector can be arbitrary, discontinuous, and harder to learn than the underlying solution set. We study bifurcation models, a weight-tied dynamical view in which different initializations can converge to different stable equilibria, so the model represents an attractor landscape rather than one chosen branch. We prove that broad set-valued maps with locally Lipschitz branches can be represented by regular equilibrium dynamics and that the induced selectors are almost everywhere regular, while manual selectors can be arbitrarily irregular. Experiments on frustrated Ising models show that such dynamics can discover multiple valid equilibria without branch labels and outperform single-branch supervision. Allen--Cahn experiments further show that diversity is not automatic: it can be encouraged explicitly, but with an accuracy--diversity tradeoff.

</details>


### [217] [Sample Complexity of Stochastic Optimization with Integer Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07239)
*Hongyu Cheng,Yinghao Zheng,Marco Molinaro,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了整数随机优化的样本复杂度理论，发现其与连续优化相比可能更高或更低，具体取决于目标和约束的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究整数随机优化问题的样本复杂度，并理解其与相应的连续优化问题在样本需求上的差异。

Method: 针对三类不同的优化场景进行理论分析：1）L∞球上的Lipschitz目标；2）L2球上的Lipschitz目标；3）强凸光滑目标。通过建立样本复杂度下界和上界来进行比较分析。

Result: 得到三个主要结果：1）在L∞球上，混合整数非线性非凸优化的统计复杂度与仅含边界约束的随机线性优化相同；2）在L2球上，整数优化在某些情况下所需样本数严格少于连续优化；3）对于强凸光滑目标，整数优化需要Ω(1/ε²)样本，而连续优化仅需O(1/ε)样本。

Conclusion: 整数随机优化的样本复杂度并非总是高于连续优化，其与连续优化的相对复杂度取决于问题的几何结构和目标函数性质，这为选择合适的优化方法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We establish sample complexity results for stochastic optimization over the integers, especially with a view to understand the complexity with respect to the corresponding continuous optimization problem. We show that integer optimization can sometimes require strictly more samples and sometimes strictly smaller number of samples, depending on the structure of the objective and constraints. 1. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_\infty$ ball, the statistical complexity of general stochastic mixed-integer, nonlinear, nonconvex optimization is exactly the same as stochastic linear optimization with just bound constraints. 2. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_2$ ball, we show that integer optimization can require strictly *smaller* sample size compared to the continuous setting in a certain regime. To get to this result, we also establish tight sample complexity results for nonconvex continuous stochastic optimization which, to the best of our knowledge, do not appear in prior work. 3. For strongly convex, smooth objectives, integer optimization has high statistical complexity compared to the continuous setting. In particular, we show that integer optimization requires $Ω(1/ε^2)$ samples to report an $ε$-approximate solution, compared to the well-known $O(1/ε)$ sample complexity from the continuous optimization literature.

</details>


### [218] [Predictive but Not Plannable: RC-aux for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07278)
*Wenyuan Li,Guang Li,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RC-aux辅助目标，通过多 horizon 开环预测和预算条件可达性监督，解决潜在世界模型中时空不匹配问题，显著提升了目标条件控制任务中的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型虽然能实现准确的短期预测，但其潜在空间与规划任务存在时空不匹配问题：模型通常使用局部预测监督训练，但部署时用于长视距目标导向搜索，此时欧氏距离可能无法反映有限动作预算内的可达性，导致潜在空间与规划不对齐。

Method: 提出可达性校正辅助目标(RC-aux)，保持世界模型主干不变，沿两个轴添加规划对齐的监督：(1)时间轴：多视距开环预测训练模型超越单步一致性；(2)空间轴：预算条件可达性监督结合时序硬负样本，鼓励潜在空间区分最终可达状态与当前规划视距内可达状态。测试时，学习到的可达性信号可用于可达性感知规划器。

Result: 在LeWorldModel上实例化RC-aux，在目标条件像素控制任务和LIBERO-Goal扩展任务上进行评估。在续训练和从头训练两种设置下，RC-aux均以适度的额外成本提升了LeWM风格规划的性能。

Conclusion: 基于潜在世界模型的规划不仅依赖于预测准确性，更关键在于学习到的表征是否编码了下游搜索所需的时序和几何结构。RC-aux为解决时空不匹配问题提供了轻量有效的解决方案。

Abstract: A latent world model may achieve accurate short-horizon prediction while still inducing a latent space that is poorly aligned with planning. A key issue is spatiotemporal mismatch: these models are often trained with local predictive supervision, but deployed for long-horizon goal-directed search in latent spaces where Euclidean distance may not reflect what is reachable within a finite action budget. We present the Reachability-Correction auxiliary objective (RC-aux), a lightweight correction for this mismatch in reconstruction-free latent world models. RC-aux keeps the world-model backbone unchanged and adds planning-aligned supervision along two axes. Along the time axis, multi-horizon open-loop prediction trains the model beyond one-step consistency. Along the space axis, budget-conditioned reachability supervision, together with temporal hard negatives, encourages the latent space to distinguish states that are eventually reachable from those reachable within the current planning horizon. At test time, the learned reachability signal can also be used by a reachability-aware planner to favor trajectories that are both goal-directed and attainable under the available budget. We instantiate RC-aux on LeWorldModel and evaluate it under both continuation-training and matched-from-scratch settings. Across goal-conditioned pixel-control tasks and a LIBERO-Goal extension, RC-aux improves LeWM-style planning with modest additional cost. These results suggest that planning with latent world models depends not only on predictive accuracy, but also on whether the learned representation encodes the temporal and geometric structure required by downstream search. The code is available at https://github.com/Guang000/RC-aux.

</details>


### [219] [Mask2Cause: Causal Discovery via Adjacency Constrained Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07280)
*Omar Muhammad,Pasupuleti Dhruv Shivkant,Deepak N. Subramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Mask2Cause框架，通过端到端方式直接在预测过程中恢复因果图，在多个基准测试上实现了最先进的因果发现性能，并将预测模型的参数量平均减少了70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经因果发现方法主要依赖逐组件架构，无法捕获共享系统动态，或采用解耦的后验图提取方法，容易过拟合到虚假相关性，因此需要一种能够直接在预测过程中恢复潜在因果图的端到端框架。

Method: 提出了Mask2Cause端到端框架，引入了倒置变量嵌入和邻接约束掩码注意力机制，并使用同方差或异方差目标函数进行训练，以捕获均值和方差中的因果影响。

Result: 在从合成混沌动力学到真实生物模拟的多种基准测试中，展示了最先进的因果发现性能，与标准基线相比显著降低了参数复杂度；推断出的因果结构可将预测模型的参数量平均减少70%以上，同时保持预测准确性。

Conclusion: Mask2Cause有效地解决了现有神经因果发现方法的关键局限性，实现了高效的因果发现和模型压缩，证明了因果结构推断对提升预测模型效率的价值。

Abstract: Leveraging deep learning for causal discovery in time series remains challenging because existing neural methods predominantly rely on component-wise architectures that fail to capture shared system dynamics or employ decoupled post-hoc graph extraction that risks overfitting to spurious correlations. We propose $\textbf{Mask2Cause}$, an end-to-end framework that recovers the underlying causal graph directly during the forecasting forward pass. Our approach introduces an Inverted Variable Embedding and an Adjacency-Constrained Masked Attention mechanism, trained with homoscedastic or heteroscedastic objectives to capture causal influences in both mean and variance. Empirical results on diverse benchmarks, from synthetic chaotic dynamics to realistic biological simulations, demonstrate state-of-the-art causal discovery with significantly reduced parameter complexity compared to standard baselines. We further show that inferred causal structures can be used to reduce parameter count of forecasting models by more than 70% on average while maintaining predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [220] [When Are Experts Misrouted? Counterfactual Routing Analysis in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07260)
*Youngsik Yoon,Siwei Wang,Wei Chen,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE模型的路由器在困难推理任务的脆弱token上存在路由分配不当问题，仅通过微调最后一层路由器即可显著提升数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型的路由决策是否最优缺乏直接评估。标准训练仅评估执行的路由，导致路由器可能在关键推理步骤上选择次优专家组合，但这一假设未被验证。

Method: 在固定模型参数的情况下，对比标准路由与等计算量替代路由，通过验证推理轨迹中已实现token的下一词元概率来评分路由质量。进一步提出最小化方案：仅更新最后一层路由器参数，冻结所有专家及其他路由器。

Result: 标准路由器在置信token上与路由效用对齐良好，但在驱动困难推理的脆弱token上表现不佳，存在未被选择的更低损失替代路由。该现象在Qwen3-30B-A3B、GPT-OSS-20B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite和OLMoE-1B-7B等多个模型中普遍存在。仅微调路由器即可提升AIME 2024+2025和HMMT 2025的pass@K性能。

Conclusion: MoE模型的路由失效至少部分源于路由器可达的误分配而非仅专家容量限制，标准训练范式（仅评估执行路由、负载均衡依赖聚合统计）结构性地导致了该问题。简单的路由器更新即可改善推理性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models route each token to a small subset of experts, but whether the routes selected by a trained top-$k$ router are good ones is rarely evaluated directly. Holding the model fixed, we compare each standard route against sampled equal-compute alternatives for the same token and score each by the next-token probability it assigns to the realized token in a verified reasoning trajectory. The result is sharply token-conditional: the standard router is well-aligned with route utility on confident tokens but uninformative on the fragile tokens that drive hard reasoning, where lower-loss equal-compute routes consistently exist inside the frozen model but are not selected. The same pattern holds across Qwen3-30B-A3B, GPT-OSS-20B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and OLMoE-1B-7B, and follows structurally from how standard top-$k$ training evaluates routing decisions: the language modeling loss scores only the executed route, and load balancing depends only on aggregate routing statistics. A minimal router-only update to the final-layer router, leaving every expert and every other router frozen, is sufficient to shift pass@K on AIME 2024+2025 and HMMT 2025 for both Qwen3-30B-A3B and GPT-OSS-20B, suggesting that at least part of the failure reflects router-reachable misallocation rather than expert capacity alone.

</details>


### [221] [Generative Modeling with Flux Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07319)
*Peter Pao-Huang,Xiaojie Qiu,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Flux Matching，一种新的生成建模范式，通过放宽向量场约束条件，允许设计非保守向量场，解锁了更快采样、可解释模型等新应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于分数的生成模型要求模型必须拟合数据的分数，这限制了向量场的选择，使得模型无法直接融入归纳偏置、结构先验或特定的动力学属性。作者希望提出一种更灵活的框架，放宽这一严格约束，从而解锁新的生成模型设计空间。

Method: 提出了Flux Matching目标函数，它不再要求模型向量场必须等同于数据分数（即保守场），而是施加一个更弱的条件：仅要求向量场的平稳分布为数据分布。该条件允许存在无穷多个满足要求的非保守向量场，使得研究者可以根据具体任务需求，将归纳偏置、结构先验或动力学性质直接作为设计选择融入向量场中。

Result: Flux Matching在高维图像数据集上表现出色。更重要的是，这种额外的自由度解锁了一系列新应用：实现了更快的采样速度、构建了具有可解释性和机制性的模型，以及能够编码变量间有向依赖关系的动力学系统。

Conclusion: Flux Matching通过将向量场本身转变为一个可设计的选择而非固定目标，为生成建模开辟了一个新的维度，拓展了生成模型的设计空间与应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Flux Matching, a new paradigm for generative modeling that generalizes existing score-based models to a broader family of vector fields that need not be conservative. Rather than requiring the model to equal the data score, the Flux Matching objective imposes a weaker condition that admits infinitely many vector fields whose stationary distribution is the data. This flexibility enables a class of generative models that cannot be learned under score matching, in which inductive biases, structural priors, and properties of the dynamics can be directly imposed or optimized. We show that Flux Matching performs strongly on high-dimensional image datasets and, more importantly, that our added freedom unlocks a range of applications including faster sampling, interpretable and mechanistic models, and dynamics that encode directed dependencies between variables. More broadly, Flux Matching opens a new dimension in generative modeling by turning the vector field itself into a design choice rather than a fixed target. Code is available at https://github.com/peterpaohuang/flux_matching.

</details>


### [222] [PerCaM-Health: Personalized Dynamic Causal Graphs for Healthcare Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07267)
*Elahe Khatibi,Ziyu Wang,Saba A. Farahani,Di Huang,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PerCaM-Health框架，通过结合群体先验知识与患者特定时序证据，学习个性化动态因果图，以解决现有方法在个体化因果发现中不可靠的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时序因果发现方法存在根本性缺陷：群体级模型缺乏个性化，而个体级发现因轨迹短、噪声大、不规则和非平稳而不可靠，无法满足个性化医疗决策对患者特定、时变因果机制的需求。

Method: 首先学习知识引导的群体时序图，然后利用患者特定的时序证据和滚动窗口更新对其进行保守适应与演化，生成可解释、可审计的图序列，并结合时序结构方程支持患者层面的反事实查询。

Result: 在半合成动态健康基准测试中，PerCaM-Health在图恢复、动态边跟踪和干预方向准确性方面均优于群体级、个体级和非个性化时序基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模个性化与时序演化能够产生更可靠的因果结构和干预推理，填补了群体因果建模与患者特定机制之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Personalized healthcare decisions require reasoning about how physiological and behavioral variables influence an individual patient over time. Existing temporal causal discovery methods are poorly matched to this setting: cohort-level models provide stable but non-personalized structures, while per-patient discovery is unreliable because individual trajectories are short, noisy, irregular, and non-stationary. This creates a fundamental gap between population-level causal modeling and the patient-specific, time-varying mechanisms needed for intervention reasoning. We introduce PerCaM-Health, a framework for learning personalized dynamic causal graphs from longitudinal health data. The framework learns a knowledge-guided population temporal graph, then conservatively adapts and evolves it using patient-specific temporal evidence and rolling-window updates, producing interpretable and auditable graph sequences. By coupling these graphs with temporal structural equations, the framework enables patient-level counterfactual queries, such as estimating short-horizon outcome changes under hypothetical behavioral interventions. Experiments on a semi-synthetic dynamic health benchmark show that PerCaM-Health improves graph recovery, dynamic edge tracking, and intervention direction accuracy compared to cohort-level, per-patient, and non-personalized temporal baselines. These results demonstrate that jointly modeling personalization and temporal evolution yields more reliable causal structure and intervention reasoning.

</details>


### [223] [bispectrum: Selective $G$-Bispectra Made Practical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07270)
*Johan Mathe,Adele Myers,Simon Mataigne,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了bispectrum，一个开源的PyTorch库，为七种群变换实现了可微分的选择性G-双谱模块，大幅降低了计算复杂度，并在深度学习架构中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器学习任务对群变换具有不变性，如平移、旋转等。G-双谱是一种原理性的完备不变量，但其应用受到高计算成本和缺乏统一实现的限制。现有的实现是针对特定群体的碎片化方案，亟需一个统一、高效的解决方案。

Method: 开发了bispectrum PyTorch库，实现了七个不同群作用的选择性G-双谱作为可微分模块。针对有限群，通过选择性机制将计算复杂度从O(|G|²)降至O(|G|)；针对平面旋转采用圆盘双谱；针对球面3D旋转引入增强选择性双谱，将代价从O(L³)降至Θ(L²)系数。实现了多种计算优化。

Result: 库在GPU上实现亚毫秒级的选择性G-双谱计算（达到常用带宽限制），提供近似精确的G-不变性。在三个经典基准数据集上，将G-双谱作为池化层融入深度学习架构，与多种池化方法对比，在低数据、中等容量场景下，G-双谱始终优于其他方案。

Conclusion: bispectrum库成功解决了G-双谱的计算效率和可用性问题，提供了一个经过充分测试、可直接集成到机器学习管道的工具，证明了G-双谱在构建群不变深度学习架构中的实用价值和性能优势。

Abstract: Many machine learning tasks are invariant under the action of a group $G$ of transformations: signal classification can be invariant under translations, image classification under 2D rotations, and spherical-image classification under 3D rotations. The $G$-bispectrum is a principled complete invariant of a signal (retaining all all signal's information up to the group action) with proven benefits in machine learning and as a pooling layer in deep networks. However, its deployment has been hampered by high computational cost and a patchwork of group-specific implementations. We present bispectrum, an open-source, fully unit-tested PyTorch library that implements selective $G$-bispectra for seven different group actions, as differentiable modules that can be directly incorporated into machine learning pipelines and deep learning architectures. For finite groups $G$, selectivity reduces the computational cost from $O(|G|^2)$ to $O(|G|)$. For planar rotations, we leverage the disk bispectrum. For spherical 3D rotations, we introduce an augmented selective bispectrum at band-limit $L$ which reduces the cost from $O(L^3)$ to $Θ(L^2)$ coefficients. We profile the entire library (for which we implemented various compute optimizations), showing that it delivers near-exact $G$-invariance with its selective $G$-bispectra computed in sub-millisecond time on GPU (up to commonly used bandlimits). We evaluate the benefits of incorporating $G$-bispectra as pooling layers into deep learning architectures on three classical benchmark datasets --comparing against norm pooling, gated pooling, Fourier-ELU pooling, max pooling, and (non-equivariant) data-augmented convolutional baselines. Results show that $G$-bispectra consistently outperform alternatives in the low-data, moderate-capacity regime.

</details>


### [224] [Rethinking Importance Sampling in LLM Policy Optimization: A Cumulative Token Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07331)
*Yuheng Zhang,Chenlu Ye,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Wei Xiong,Saurabh Sahu,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CTPO算法，通过累积token重要性采样比率解决了强化学习中偏差-方差权衡问题，在数学推理任务上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型强化学习方法在重要性采样比率设计上面临偏差-方差困境：token级IS比率（如PPO、GRPO）忽略前缀状态分布不匹配导致偏差，完整序列比率虽然准确但方差过高，GSPO通过长度归一化提升稳定性但偏离了精确校正。需要一种理论上更优的解决方案。

Method: 识别累积token IS比率（到位置t的token比率乘积）作为理论上的最优解，证明其在token级策略梯度下提供无偏前缀校正且方差严格低于完整序列比率；在此基础上提出CTPO算法，结合累积token IS比率与位置自适应裁剪，裁剪边界按累积对数比率的√t自然增长进行缩放，实现跨token位置的一致性正则化。

Result: 在工具集成推理设置下的多个挑战性数学推理基准测试中，CTPO在两种模型规模下均实现了相比强基线GRPO和GSPO的最优平均性能。

Conclusion: 累积token IS比率在理论上解决了偏差-方差困境，为off-policy策略梯度估计提供了无偏且低方差的解决方案；CTPO通过位置自适应裁剪实现了更一致的正则化，证明了该方法在大语言模型强化学习后训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning, including reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), has emerged as a powerful approach for LLM post-training. Central to these approaches is the design of the importance sampling (IS) ratio used in off-policy policy-gradient estimation. Existing methods face a fundamental bias-variance dilemma: token-level IS ratios, as adopted by PPO (Schulman et al., 2017) and GRPO (Shao et al., 2024), introduce bias by ignoring prefix state distribution mismatch; full sequence ratios provide exact trajectory-level correction but suffer from high variance due to the multiplicative accumulation of per-token ratios, while GSPO (Zheng et al., 2025) improves numerical stability via length normalization at the cost of deviating from the exact full-sequence IS correction. In this work, we identify the cumulative token IS ratio, the product of per-token ratios up to position $t$, as a theoretically principled solution to this dilemma. We prove that, under the token-level policy-gradient formulation, this ratio provides an unbiased prefix correction for each token-level gradient term and has strictly lower variance than the full sequence ratio. Building on this insight, we propose CTPO (Cumulative Token Policy Optimization), which combines the cumulative token IS ratio with position-adaptive clipping that scales log-space clip bounds according to the natural $\sqrt{t}$ growth of the cumulative log-ratio. This yields more consistent regularization across token positions. We implement and evaluate CTPO in the tool-integrated reasoning setting on several challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, achieving the best average performance across both model scales compared with strong GRPO and GSPO baselines. Code will be available at https://github.com/horizon-llm/CTPO.

</details>


### [225] [Mage: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLM-Generated Executable Game Scenes Beyond Compile-Pass Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07342)
*Hugh Xuechen Liu,Kıvanç Tatar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在领域特定代码生成中，编译通过率可能与功能正确性呈反相关；仅依赖编译率作为评估指标会产生误导，需要多轴评估协议来揭示真实性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译通过率是LLM代码生成的主导评估信号，但在多组件领域特定工件（如可执行游戏场景）中，该指标可能具有误导性，无法真实反映生成质量。

Method: 提出名为'Mage'的四轴评估协议（编译成功、运行时成功、结构保真度、机制遵循度），对4个开源LLM（7B-30B）、26个Unity目标模式和两种IR粒度级别的858次生成尝试进行系统评估。

Result: 直接NL-to-C#生成运行时通过率最高（43%均值），但结构空洞（机制F1≈0.12）；结构IR条件化使运行时率减半，但恢复了领域忠实结构（F1高达1.00）；行为级与全场景粒度在统计上无显著差异（McNemar p=1.0），表明输入级粒度饱和。

Conclusion: 编译通过率在此领域与功能正确性呈反相关，单轴评估无法检测这种分歧，多轴评估对于准确评估领域特定代码生成质量是必要的。发布了基准、回放日志和指标供独立验证。

Abstract: Compile-pass rate is the dominant evaluation signal for LLM code generation, yet for multi-component domain-specific artifacts it can be actively misleading. We demonstrate this on executable game scene synthesis with a four-axis evaluation protocol (named `Mage') -- compile success, runtime success, structural fidelity, and mechanism adherence -- applied to 858 generation attempts across four open-weight LLMs (7B--30B), 26~hand-crafted Unity goal pattern playable concepts, and two automatically extracted IR granularity levels. Direct NL-to-C\# generation achieves the highest runtime-pass rate (43\% mean) yet produces structurally vacuous scenes (mechanism $F_1 \approx 0.12$). Structural IR conditioning halves the runtime rate but recovers domain-faithful structure ($F_1$ up to 1.00). Within IR conditioning, behavior-only and full-scene granularity are statistically indistinguishable (McNemar $p = 1.0$), indicating input-level granularity saturation. These results show that compile rate is anti-correlated with functional correctness in this domain and that multi-axis evaluation is necessary to detect the divergence. We release the benchmark, replay logs, and per-record metrics for independent verification.

</details>


### [226] [MISA: Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07363)
*Ruijie Zhou,Fanxu Meng,Yufei Xu,Tongxuan Liu,Guangming Lu,Muhan Zhang,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MISA通过混合专家路由机制动态选择少量索引头，在保持性能的同时大幅降低了DeepSeek稀疏注意力(DSA)的推理成本，实现3.82倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA通过多索引头设计保持表达能力，但这也导致索引器成为长上下文推理的主要计算瓶颈。每个查询需要用所有头(如64个)对每个前缀token进行评分，计算开销巨大。

Method: 提出MISA（混合索引器稀疏注意力），将DSA的索引头视为专家池。轻量级路由器基于廉价的块级统计信息选择查询相关的少量活跃头，仅这些头执行昂贵的token级评分。进一步引入层次化变体，先用路由过程保留扩大的候选集，再用原始DSA索引器重新排序以精确恢复最终选中的token。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.2和GLM-5的LongBench测试中，仅使用8个活跃头（分别为原来的1/8和1/4）且无需额外训练，MISA匹配密集DSA索引器性能并超越HISA。在128K token上下文的Needle-in-a-Haystack测试中保持全绿热力图，每层恢复超过92%的DSA选中token。TileLang内核在单张H200 GPU上实现约3.82倍加速。

Conclusion: MISA作为DSA索引器的即插即用替代方案，成功在保持稀疏注意力表达能力和选择质量的同时，显著降低了计算成本，为长上下文LLM推理提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) sets the state of the art for fine-grained inference-time sparse attention by introducing a learned token-wise indexer that scores every prefix token and selects the most relevant ones for the main attention. To remain expressive, the indexer uses many query heads (for example, 64 on DeepSeek-V3.2) that share the same selected token set; this multi-head design is precisely what makes the indexer the dominant cost on long contexts. We propose MISA (Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention), a drop-in replacement for the DSA indexer that treats its indexer heads as a pool of mixture-of-experts. A lightweight router uses cheap block-level statistics to pick a query-dependent subset of only a few active heads, and only those heads run the heavy token-level scoring. This preserves the diversity of the original indexer pool while reducing the per-query cost from scoring every prefix token with every head to scoring it with only a handful of routed heads, plus a negligible router term computed on a small set of pooled keys. We further introduce a hierarchical variant of MISA that uses the routed pass to keep an enlarged candidate set and then re-ranks it with the original DSA indexer to recover the final selected tokens almost exactly. With only eight active heads and no additional training, MISA matches the dense DSA indexer on LongBench across DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5 while running with eight and four times fewer indexer heads respectively, and outperforms HISA on average. It also preserves fully green Needle-in-a-Haystack heatmaps up to a 128K-token context and recovers more than 92% of the tokens selected by the DSA indexer per layer. Our TileLang kernel delivers roughly a 3.82 times speedup over DSA's original indexer kernel on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU.

</details>


### [227] [Unsolvability Ceiling in Multi-LLM Routing: An Empirical Study of Evaluation Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07395)
*Saloni Garg,Amit Sagtani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有LLM路由系统的“不可解性”上限被高估，主要源于评估偏差；本文揭示了三大评估伪影，并提出了修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为路由系统的性能上限受限于“不可解性天花板”（即没有任何模型能解决的查询），但作者质疑这一结论是否真实，怀疑其可能源于评估过程中的系统性偏差。

Method: 进行大规模多层级LLM路由研究（20.6万对查询-模型数据，覆盖6个基准测试和Gemma 4、Llama 3.1模型家族）；通过双模型验证和精确匹配锚定识别评估伪影；引入失败归因分解框架；使用随机特征和打乱标签对照组验证路由器训练问题。

Result: 发现大量报告的“不可解性”源于三种评估伪影： verbosity偏差、生成预算截断、输出格式不匹配；修正后显著降低了测量到的不可解性；这些伪影导致标准路由器退化为多数类预测（约79%倾向于最小层级），造成13-17个百分点的机会成本损失。

Conclusion: 现有路由系统的性能空间估计被严重夸大，强调了在多LLM系统中建立可靠评估协议的必要性；提出了双评估验证、精确匹配锚定和成本敏感目标等可操作建议。

Abstract: Efficient routing across multiple LLMs enables cost-quality tradeoffs by directing queries to the cheapest capable model. Prior work attributes routing headroom to an "unsolvability ceiling", queries no model in the pool can solve. We present a large-scale study of multi-tier LLM routing with 206,000 query-model pairs across six benchmarks (MMLU, MedQA, HumanEval, MBPP, Alpaca, ShareGPT) using the Gemma 4 and Llama 3.1 families. Evaluating with both LLM-as-a-judge and exact-match metrics, we show that a substantial portion of reported unsolvability stems from evaluation artifacts: (i) systematic judge biases favoring verbosity over correctness, (ii) truncation under fixed generation budgets, and (iii) output format mismatches. Through dual-judge validation and exact-match grounding, we reduce measured unsolvability across tasks. We introduce a decomposition framework attributing failures to these artifacts, revealing consistent patterns across domains and model families. These artifacts also distort router training signals: standard routers collapse to majority-class prediction (~79% smallest-tier optimal), confirmed via random-feature and shuffled-label controls, incurring a 13-17 percentage point opportunity cost. We provide actionable recommendations including dual-judge validation, exact-match anchoring, and cost-sensitive objectives. Our findings suggest existing routing headroom estimates are substantially inflated, underscoring the need for reliable evaluation protocols in multi-LLM systems.

</details>


### [228] [The Convergence Gap: Instruction-Tuned Language Models Stabilize Later in the Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07282)
*Yifan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出“收敛差距”诊断方法，发现指令微调模型相比预训练模型在Transformer堆叠中更晚收敛到最终预测，且后期MLP层是影响这一延迟的关键计算模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对模型最终输出如何隐藏在检查点的下一词预测中缺乏深入理解，特别是预训练与指令微调模型在预测动态上的差异尚未被系统研究。作者希望揭示指令微调对模型内部预测收敛过程的影响机制。

Method: 提出收敛差距（convergence gap）这一模型差分诊断方法，通过解码每一层的下一词分布并测量其与模型最终分布的距离来量化收敛过程。在六对预训练和指令微调检查点上进行实验，采用原生提示机制，并进行端点匹配、同历史检查和固定历史模板重放等多重控制实验。使用匹配前缀干预方法识别关键层，包括后期MLP移植和置换实验。

Result: 指令微调检查点在堆叠的更深层才接近最终预测，该效应在端点匹配、无端点同历史检查和固定历史模板重放下持续存在。后期MLP窗口是最大的杠杆点：将指令微调后期层移植到预训练主机使后期KL散度增加+0.34 nats；将预训练后期层换入指令微调主机减少-0.51 nats。匹配随机后期扰动仅产生+0.003变化，而真实后期移植产生+0.327变化。

Conclusion: 识别出训练后阶段的鲁棒预测动态特征：发布的指令跟随检查点倾向于更晚收敛，后期MLP计算是在匹配历史条件下影响该延迟的最强双向操控点。这一发现为理解指令微调如何影响模型内部表示演化提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Final outputs hide when a checkpoint commits to its next-token prediction. We introduce the convergence gap, a model-diffing diagnostic that decodes each layer's next-token distribution and measures its distance to the model's own final distribution. Across six paired pretrained and instruction-tuned checkpoints in native prompting regimes, instruction-tuned checkpoints remain farther from their final predictions later into the stack. The effect persists under endpoint-matched raw and tuned readouts, endpoint-free same-history checks, and fixed-history template replay. Matched-prefix interventions identify late MLP windows as the largest tested leverage point: late IT grafts into PT hosts increase late KL by +0.34 nats, while PT-late swaps into IT hosts reduce it by -0.51 nats; matched random late perturbations give only +0.003 versus +0.327 for the true late graft. A preselected Gemma case study provides behavior-facing plausibility for the same late swap, without serving as a benchmark claim. These results identify a robust predictiondynamics signature of post-training: released instruction-following checkpoints tend to settle later, and late MLP computation is the strongest tested bidirectional handle on that delay under matched histories.

</details>


### [229] [Rubric-based On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07396)
*Junfeng Fang,Zhepei Hong,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Gengsheng Li,Houcheng Jiang,Dan Zhang,Haiyun Guo,Xiang Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ROPD框架，利用结构化语义评分标准替代教师模型logits，实现了仅依赖教师生成回复的黑盒兼容策略蒸馏方法，在样本效率上提升高达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的策略蒸馏方法依赖教师模型的logits输出，限制了其只能在白盒场景下应用。为了突破这一限制，本文探索利用结构化语义评分标准作为教师logits的可扩展替代方案，使策略蒸馏能够在仅有教师生成回复的黑盒场景下实现。

Method: 提出ROPD（Rubric-based On-Policy Distillation）框架：首先从师生对比中归纳出提示特定的评分标准，然后利用这些评分标准对学生模型的生成结果进行打分，最后基于评分进行策略优化。该方法无需访问教师模型的内部logits，仅需教师生成的回复即可完成蒸馏。

Result: 实验表明，ROPD在大多数场景下优于先进的基于logits的策略蒸馏方法，样本效率提升高达10倍。该方法在不同规模的LLM上均表现良好，证明了其有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 基于评分标准的策略蒸馏为现有的基于logits的方法提供了一种灵活、黑盒兼容的替代方案，为跨专有和开源LLM的可扩展蒸馏提供了简单而强有力的基线方法，具有重要的实践意义。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a powerful paradigm for model alignment, yet its reliance on teacher logits restricts its application to white-box scenarios. We contend that structured semantic rubrics can serve as a scalable alternative to teacher logits, enabling OPD using only teacher-generated responses. To prove it, we introduce ROPD, a simple yet foundational framework for rubric-based OPD. Specifically, ROPD induces prompt-specific rubrics from teacher-student contrasts, and then utilizes these rubrics to score the student rollouts for on-policy optimization. Empirically, ROPD outperforms the advanced logit-based OPD methods across most scenarios, and achieving up to a 10x gain in sample efficiency. These results position rubric-based OPD as a flexible, black-box-compatible alternative to the prevailing logit-based OPD, offering a simple yet strong baseline for scalable distillation across proprietary and open-source LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Peregrine123/ROPD_official.

</details>


### [230] [Instruction Tuning Changes How Upstream State Conditions Late Readout: A Cross-Patching Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07284)
*Yifan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出first-divergence cross-patching方法，发现指令微调模型的晚期层效果依赖于其自身的上游状态，而数学领域微调模型则可移植基础模型的上游状态，揭示了早期计算与晚期层之间的协作机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究主要定位模型行为（如拒绝回答、人格特征）发生在中晚期层，但尚不清楚早期计算与晚期层如何协作将这些差异转化为下一个token的预测优势。作者旨在探究早期层状态与晚期层堆栈之间的交互作用，以理解不同训练方法下行为差异的形成机制。

Method: 提出first-divergence cross-patching方法：在预训练基础模型(PT)和指令微调模型(IT)首次产生预测分歧的token位置，交叉交换两个模型的早期层状态与晚期层堆栈，构建四种组合来分离训练方法的影响。该方法在五个稠密模型家族（4B-32B参数）上进行验证。

Result: 发现两类训练方法的差异：(1)同源的指令微调模型（如助手训练）的晚期层效果依赖于自身的早期层状态；(2)OpenMath2数学微调和验证领域学习的代码/生物医学CPT控制组的晚期层效果可从基础模型的早期层状态移植。IT晚期层从PT上游获得+0.76 logits，从IT上游获得+2.44 logits，产生+1.68的正向交互作用。最终MLP层的稀疏特征部分介导这一效果并受上游补丁驱动。强制token评分显示局部token选择可改变后续精确答案的成功率。

Conclusion: 晚期层堆栈确实存在真实的PT上游效应，但其更大的IT效果仅在读取自身的后训练上游状态时才显现。存在从早期状态到最终层特征激活再到IT token优势的传递机制。操作上，将差异定位到晚期层的配对检查点研究应测试该差异是否在其他检查点的上游状态下仍然存在，而非将晚期层视为自包含系统。

Abstract: Recent interpretability work has identified model-internal handles on post-trained behavior, including refusal directions, assistant/persona axes, and sparse chat-tuning features. These results localize where behaviors can be read out or controlled, often in middle-to-late layers. We ask how earlier computation and the late stack cooperate to turn those differences into next-token margins. To test this, we introduce first-divergence cross-patching: at the first token where pretrained base (PT) and instruction-tuned (IT) checkpoints disagree, we cross each model's earlier-layer state with each model's late stack. The diagnostic separates training recipes: same-base instruction-following descendants show late effects that depend on their own earlier-layer state, while OpenMath2 math-domain SFT and controlled code/biomed CPT controls with verified domain learning do not; for OpenMath2, the late effect is already largely portable from base earlier-layer state. Across five dense families (4B-32B), the IT late stack adds +0.76 logits from PT upstream and +2.44 from IT upstream, giving a +1.68 interaction that is positive in every family. Thus the late stack has a real PT-upstream effect, but its larger effect in the IT checkpoint appears only when it reads its own post-trained upstream state. Sparse features in final MLP layers partially mediate the effect and are driven by upstream patches, supporting a handoff from earlier state to final-layer feature activation to IT-token margin. Forced-token scoring shows that the local token choice can change later exact-answer success. Operationally, paired-checkpoint studies that localize a difference to late layers should test whether it survives under the other checkpoint's upstream state before treating the late stack as self-contained.

</details>


### [231] [Tracking Large-scale Shared Bikes with Inertial Motion Learning in GNSS Blocked Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07412)
*Feng Liu,Kejia Li,Zhiwei Yang,Chunwei Yang,Qun Li,Guobin Wu,Qiang Ni,Ruipeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合自行车机械约束与混合专家模型的惯性跟踪框架，利用骑行行为与加速度的关系进行动态校准，在共享单车真实数据上相比基线精度提升至少12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在城市峡谷等复杂骑行环境中GNSS信号受限，而仅依赖低成本惯性传感器的定位存在累积漂移和鲁棒性差的问题；视觉和激光雷达等传感器虽可靠但不适合大规模部署。

Method: 提出一种惯性跟踪框架，集成自行车机械约束与混合专家模型（Mixture-of-Experts）。具体包括：(1) 利用多个专家模块捕获共享表示并通过门控机制加权，提升多任务学习性能并实现不确定性感知的轨迹估计；(2) 基于自行车踏板与后轮之间的机械传动关系，探索骑行者周期性踩踏行为与加速度变化的内在联系，将其转换为车轮速度用于动态校准。

Result: 基于滴滴出行平台共享单车的真实骑行数据实验表明，该系统相比基线方法精度提升至少12%，95分位数的车轮速度误差低于0.5 m/s。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了低成本惯性传感器在复杂环境下的定位漂移问题，证明了结合机械约束与混合专家模型的可行性，为共享单车等大规模应用提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Although Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) provide a general solution for bike tracking outdoors, there still exist complex riding environments where only inertial navigation systems work, such as urban canyons. Despite decades of research, localization using only low-cost inertial sensors still faces challenges such as cumulative drifts and poor robustness caused by filtering methods. Furthermore, sensors such as visual and LiDAR could provide reliable measurements, but they are not suitable for large-scale deployment. In this paper, we propose an inertial tracking framework that integrates bicycle mechanical constraints with a mixture-of-experts model. Specifically, we leverage multiple expert modules to capture shared representations and weight them through the gating mechanism, thus improving multi-task learning performance and enabling uncertainty-aware trajectory estimation. Furthermore, based on the mechanical transmission between the pedal and the rear wheel of a bike, we explore the intrinsic relationship between the rider's periodic pedalling behaviors and acceleration variations, and convert such patterns into bike's wheel speed for dynamic calibration. Experiments with real-world riding data from shared bikes of the DiDi ride-hailing platform demonstrate that our system improves the accuracy of baselines by at least 12%, with wheel speed errors below 0.5 m/s at 95-percentile.

</details>


### [232] [Pretraining Induces a Reusable Spectral Basis for Downstream Task Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07302)
*Junjie Yu,Yue Wang,Zihan Deng,Yan Zhu,Wenxiao Ma,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 预训练模型的主奇异向量在微调过程中保持高度稳定，编码了可迁移的结构知识，基于此提出的谱系数微调方法仅需0.2%的可训练参数即可在GLUE上达到竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调发生在参数空间的低维子空间中。现有工作主要关注描述该优化子空间，却忽略了互补问题：为何某些方向在微调中未被探索？这些稳定方向是与下游任务无关，还是已编码了无需调整的任务相关结构？回答此问题对理解预训练知识如何迁移至关重要。

Method: 通过对视觉和语言模型进行系统谱分析，研究预训练权重矩阵的主奇异向量在微调过程中的稳定性，并分析其在不同下游任务间的共享性。基于发现，提出一种参数高效方法：冻结预训练奇异向量，仅优化主谱系数。

Result: 预训练模型的主奇异向量在微调下保持高度稳定，且在无关下游任务间共享，揭示预训练建立了可复用的谱坐标系。在更大数据集上预训练的模型在分布变化或任务变化时表现出更强的谱稳定性。所提方法在GLUE上以0.2%的可训练参数实现了竞争性能。

Conclusion: 稳定方向编码的是可迁移结构而非无关噪声。成功的预训练发现了下游任务继承并在其中操作的谱基，预训练规模与几何可迁移性直接相关。

Abstract: Finetuning pretrained models occurs in a low-dimensional subspace of the full parameter space. Prior work has focused on characterizing this optimization subspace, but largely ignored the complementary question: why do certain directions remain unexplored during finetuning? Are these stable directions irrelevant to downstream tasks, or do they already encode task-relevant structure that requires no further adjustment? Answering this question is central to understanding how pretrained knowledge transfers. Through systematic spectral analysis across vision and language models, we show that the leading singular vectors of pretrained weight matrices remain highly stable under finetuning and are shared across unrelated downstream tasks, revealing that pretraining establishes a reusable spectral coordinate system. Models pretrained on larger datasets exhibit greater spectral stability under distribution shift or task change, directly linking pretraining scale to geometric transferability. Motivated by these findings, we propose a parameter-efficient method that freezes pretrained singular vectors and optimizes only leading spectral coefficients, achieving competitive performance on GLUE with 0.2% trainable parameters. Our results reveal that the stable directions encode transferable structure rather than irrelevant noise: successful pretraining discovers spectral bases that downstream tasks inherit and operate within.

</details>


### [233] [Latent Order Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07304)
*Emil Carlsson,Newton Mwai,Fredrik D. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在序老虎机（LOB），放宽了潜在老虎机对奖励分布的强假设，仅需已知各状态下动作偏好的偏序关系，从而提高了对实例间奖励尺度差异的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在老虎机算法依赖对奖励与潜在状态后验分布的准确建模，且潜在状态数量有限时难以刻画所有问题实例，导致模型获取困难、适用性受限。

Method: 提出潜在序老虎机（LOB）框架，仅要求同状态实例共享动作偏序关系；设计了上置信界算法并提供后悔上界，同时提出后验采样算法以提升实证性能。

Result: 理论证明了LOB上置信界算法的次线性后悔上界；实验表明，当同状态实例共享奖励参数时，LOB与全先验潜在老虎机性能相当；当奖励尺度不同时，LOB显著优于后者。

Conclusion: LOB通过放宽潜在老虎机的强假设，在保持理论保证的同时，增强了对奖励分布变化的鲁棒性，为跨实例结构利用提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Bandit algorithms solve diverse sequential decision-making problems, but are often too sample-inefficient for from-scratch personalization. To substantially reduce exploration times, latent bandit algorithms exploit cross-instance structure implied by discrete latent states, provided that the posterior distribution of rewards and latent states is known and accurate. However, obtaining an accurate model of this structure is difficult, and a small number of latent states may be insufficient to characterize the reward distributions in all problem instances. We propose latent order bandits (LOB), relaxing the assumptions of latent bandits to require only prior knowledge of a partial order of action preferences in each state. This allows instances of the same state to vary in reward distributions, as long as the partial order of actions is shared. For example, groups of users on a streaming service may agree on which movie genres are the best but rate experiences on different scales. We give an upper-confidence bound procedure for the LOB problem, applicable to both total and partial latent orders, and give an upper bound on its regret. To improve empirical performance, we propose a posterior-sampling algorithm and show, in a suite of experiments, that both are competitive with full-prior latent bandits when same-state instances share reward parameters, and preferable to them when reward scales differ between instances with the same latent state.

</details>


### [234] [Physical Simulators as Do-Operators: Causal Discovery under Latent Confounders for AI-for-Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07467)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CFM-SD方法，利用物理模拟器作为干预算子，在存在潜在混淆变量的情况下从仿真数据中学习因果结构，在合成与真实科学数据上均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的干预式因果发现方法（如IGSP、DCDI、ENCO）假设无潜在混淆变量且依赖合成模拟器中的虚拟干预，但AI-for-Science场景（如分子设计、材料科学）中潜在混淆变量普遍存在，且真实干预（如基于物理的仿真）单次需数小时至数天，现有方法难以适用。

Method: 提出CFM-SD（基于仿真数据的因果流匹配）方法，将第一性原理物理模拟器作为Pearl干预演算中的do算子，同时处理潜在混淆变量和真实干预数据。

Result: 理论上证明在物理可实现性约束下，d变量因果结构可用O(d)次单变量干预识别（达最小值）；在合成数据上F1达0.800，基线仅为0.127-0.562；在真实科学数据上，分子毒性预测和电池电解质优化的偏差降低57-58%。

Conclusion: CFM-SD为存在潜在混淆变量且干预成本高昂的科学领域提供了理论可识别性保证与实际有效的因果发现方法。

Abstract: Existing interventional causal discovery methods -- IGSP, DCDI, ENCO -- assume causal sufficiency (no latent confounders) and rely on virtual interventions in synthetic simulators. In AI-for-Science settings such as molecular design and materials science, latent confounders are ubiquitous and real interventions (e.g., physics-based simulations) require hours to days per data point. We propose CFM-SD (Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data), which uses first-principles physical simulators as do-operators in Pearl's interventional calculus to simultaneously handle latent confounders and real interventional data. Theoretically, $d$-variable causal structure is identifiable with $O(d)$ single-variable interventions -- the minimum under physical realizability constraints. In Intrinsic Evaluation on synthetic data ($γ=0.2$--$0.8$), CFM-SD achieves average F1$=0.800$ vs. F1$=0.127$--$0.562$ for all baselines. In Extrinsic Evaluation on real scientific data, CFM-SD achieves 57--58\% bias reduction in molecular toxicity prediction and battery electrolyte optimization, demonstrating practical value beyond synthetic benchmarks.

</details>


### [235] [SHRED: Retain-Set-Free Unlearning via Self-Distillation with Logit Demotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07482)
*Zizhao Hu,Ameya Godbole,Johnny Tian-Zheng Wei,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SHRED方法，一种无需保留数据集的大语言模型遗忘方法，通过区分高/低信息token实现遗忘与效用的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸遗忘方法依赖保留数据集以维持模型效用，这增加了数据依赖性和部署复杂性。需要一种无需保留数据集的方法来降低部署难度。

Method: SHRED分两阶段：(1)选择阶段：对遗忘集进行前向传播，选择概率最低（信息量最高）的token作为遗忘位置，其余作为良性锚点；(2)训练阶段：构建修改的KL目标，在遗忘位置降低被记忆token的logit，在良性位置保留原始分布，通过单一KL自蒸馏目标同时实现遗忘和效用保持。

Result: 在四个标准遗忘基准上，SHRED建立了遗忘效果与模型效用之间的新帕累托最优权衡，优于依赖保留数据集的方法；对重学习攻击和成员推断攻击具有鲁棒性，多次顺序遗忘后仍保持稳定效用。

Conclusion: SHRED证明了无需保留数据集的遗忘方法不仅能简化部署，还能实现更优的遗忘-效用权衡，为大语言模型的安全可控遗忘提供了新思路。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to selectively remove memorized content such as private data, copyrighted text, or hazardous knowledge, without costly full retraining. Most existing methods require a retain set of curated examples to prevent catastrophic degradation of general model utility, creating an extra data dependency that complicates deployment. We propose SHRED (Self-distillation via High-surprisal-only Retain-set-free Entropy Demotion), a retain-set-free unlearning method built on a key insight: not all tokens within a forget set instance carry memorized information equally. High-information tokens concentrate the model's memorized knowledge, while low-information tokens reflect general language competence. SHRED operates in two stages. (1) Selection: We perform a forward pass on a forget set instance, collect per-token autoregressive probabilities, and select the bottom (lowest probability, highest Shannon information) as forget positions; the remaining positions are retained as benign anchors. (2) Training: We construct modified KL targets that demote the memorized token's logit at forget positions while preserving the original distribution at benign positions. The model is then trained via a single top KL self-distillation objective that simultaneously drives forgetting and utility preservation. We evaluate SHRED across four standard unlearning benchmarks and demonstrate that it establishes a new Pareto-optimal trade-off between forget efficacy and model utility, outperforming retain-set-dependent methods. Our analysis shows that SHRED is robust against relearning attacks and membership-inference attacks, and it maintains stable utility even after many sequential unlearning runs.

</details>


### [236] [Does Your Neural Network Extrapolate? Feature Engineering as Identifiability Bias for OOD Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07483)
*Leonel Aguilar,Jan Nagler,Christoph Hoelscher,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DNN在OOD泛化上的成功或失败取决于结构承诺（特征图、标签图和模型类），而非仅依靠ID数据；正确的表示可使OOD误差降低约520倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释深度神经网络为何在分布外（OOD）泛化时有时失败：仅从单一训练窗口学习时，OOD外推是不可识别的——存在无限多个在训练数据上观察等价但在外部任意发散的数据生成过程（DGP），且没有任何ID准则能可靠地打破这一僵局。

Method: 将特征学习与DGP可识别性解耦，形式化结构承诺$(\varphi, \psi, \mathcal{M})$作为决定OOD泛化的关键；通过理论分析识别成功/失败条件，并在三个自然科学场景（质量作用化学、开普勒第三定律系外行星预测、跨物种编码DNA检测）和264组位置编码实验（Transformer、Mamba、S4D）中进行验证。

Result: 仅改变表示（架构和ID损失不变）可使OOD误差相差约520倍；当结构承诺正确且可识别时，OOD误差趋零（如傅里叶坐标将周期外推转化为$\mathbb{S}^1$上的插值）；正确特征是必要但不充分条件——模型类须能表达目标，且变换后的训练数据须覆盖相关表示空间。

Conclusion: OOD成功的关键在于结构承诺的正确注入：当架构、预训练、增强、输入格式或领域知识隐式注入正确的结构承诺时模型成功；当模型无法从ID证据推断OOD相关结构时则失败。这为理解和改进OOD泛化提供了理论框架和实践指导。

Abstract: Successful deep neural networks discover salient features of data. We show when and why they fail to learn out-of-distribution (OOD)-relevant representations from an in-distribution (ID) training window. This requires decoupling feature learning from data-generating-process (DGP) identifiability. From a single training window, OOD extrapolation is non-identifiable: infinitely many DGPs are $\varepsilon$-observationally equivalent on the training data but diverge arbitrarily outside it, and no in-distribution criterion alone reliably breaks the tie. A structural commitment, the feature map, label map, and model class $(\varphi, ψ, \mathcal{M})$, dictates the assumed DGP and governs OOD generalization while leaving ID performance essentially unchanged. When architecture, pretraining, augmentation, input formats, or domain knowledge implicitly inject the missing commitment, the model succeeds. When it cannot infer OOD-relevant structure from ID evidence, it fails. Changing only the representation can make the same architecture, at the same in-distribution loss, differ by ${\sim}520\times$ out of distribution. When the commitment is correct and identifiable, OOD error vanishes. For example, Fourier coordinates turn periodic extrapolation into interpolation on $\mathbb{S}^1$. The same mechanism predicts outcomes in three natural-science settings (mass-action chemistry; Kepler's-third-law exoplanet prediction, $n=2{,}362$; and cross-species coding-DNA detection) and in a 264-run positional-encoding study across Transformer, Mamba, and S4D. Finally, a controlled study shows: correct features are necessary but not sufficient. The model class must express the target, and the transformed training data must cover the relevant representation space.

</details>


### [237] [Excluding the Target Domain Improves Extrapolation: Deconfounded Hierarchical Physics Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07485)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去混淆层次门控机制（DHG），通过消除温度混淆变量并采用从粗到细的物理约束，显著提升了物理约束深度生成模型在分布外条件下的外推性能。同时发现预训练中排除目标域数据反而能获得更好的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理约束深度生成模型在外推到分布外条件时面临挑战。现有方法将物理约束作为单一静态正则化项统一应用于生成过程，既未考虑物理定律的层次结构，也未解决混淆变量问题。这导致模型在温度等条件变化时的外推性能受限。

Method: 提出去混淆层次门控（DHG）机制作为诊断和控制机制：(1)识别温度混淆何时及如何污染各约束层级；(2)结合do-算子的反事实估计与后门调整来消除混淆；(3)应用从粗到细（Coarse-to-Fine）的渐进式物理约束。层次门控反映内在物理不一致性而非虚假温度效应。

Result: 在锂离子电池温度外推基准测试（训练温度24℃，评估温度4.0-43.0℃）上，RMSE达到0.215，相比无约束基线（纯CFM：0.397）提升46%。发现反直觉现象：预训练时排除目标域数据比包含目标域数据的外推性能高39%（RMSE 0.224 vs 0.324），因为FNO学习的领域无关物理模式在目标域被保留时迁移更有效。

Conclusion: DHG通过识别和消除温度混淆、利用物理定律层次结构、采用渐进式约束策略，有效解决了分布外外推问题。研究揭示了预训练策略中目标域数据保留与物理模式迁移之间的权衡关系，为物理约束生成模型的训练策略提供了新见解。

Abstract: Extrapolation to out-of-distribution conditions is a fundamental challenge for physics-constrained deep generative models. Existing methods apply physical constraints as a single static regularization term uniformly across the generation process, and address neither the hierarchical structure of physical laws and the confounding variable problem. We propose the Deconfounded Hierarchical Gate (DHG), which serves as a diagnostic and control mechanism: it identifies when and how strongly temperature confounding contaminates each constraint level, so that hierarchical gates reflect intrinsic physical inconsistency rather than spurious temperature effects. DHG combines counterfactual estimation via the do-operator with backdoor adjustment to remove confounding, then applies Coarse-to-Fine physical constraints progressively. We report a counter-intuitive finding in pretraining: excluding the target-domain data from pretraining outperforms including it by 39% in extrapolation performance (RMSE 0.224 vs. 0.324). This occurs because FNO learns domain-agnostic physical patterns that transfer more effectively when the target domain is withheld. On a lithium-ion battery temperature extrapolation benchmark (trained at 24 degrees Celsius, evaluated at 4.0--43.0 degrees Celsius), our method achieves RMSE = 0.215, a 46% improvement over the unconstrained baseline (Pure CFM: 0.397).

</details>


### [238] [Beyond Linear Attention: Softmax Transformers Implement In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07333)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在不依赖线性注意力简化的情况下，为上下文强化学习（ICRL）提供了理论分析。研究发现，带有Softmax注意力的Transformer前向传播等价于一种新的加权Softmax时序差分（TD）学习算法，并证明了在特定条件下评估误差随层数衰减，且相关参数是预训练损失的全局最小化点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ICRL理论分析主要依赖于线性注意力假设（将标准Softmax替换为恒等映射），这在实际应用中是不现实的。作者旨在填补这一空白，对实际中使用的标准Softmax注意力进行理论分析，以提供更贴近实际的理论支撑。

Method: 作者分析了使用标准Softmax注意力的Transformer模型。理论证明发现，在特定参数配置下，该Transformer的逐层前向传播过程等价于一种新提出的“加权Softmax时序差分（TD）学习”算法的迭代更新。该算法在核空间中进行策略评估，并将线性TD和表格型TD作为特例包含其中。

Result: 1. 建立了标准Softmax注意力Transformer前向传播与加权Softmax TD算法之间的等价关系。
2. 证明了在特定收缩条件下，策略评估误差随Transformer层数的增加而衰减。
3. 证明了所识别的特定参数是预训练损失的全局最小化点，解释了这些参数在数值实验中的涌现现象。

Conclusion: 本文打破了ICRL理论中对线性注意力的依赖，揭示了标准Softmax注意力Transformer执行ICRL的内在机制——其本质是在执行加权Softmax TD学习。这不仅为理解ICRL提供了新的理论视角，也解释了模型参数在预训练后的涌现行为，为设计和优化ICRL算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) studies agents that, after pretraining, adapt to new tasks by conditioning on additional context without parameter updates. Existing theoretical analyses of ICRL largely rely on linear attention, which replaces the softmax function in the standard attention with an identity mapping. This paper provides the first theoretical understanding of ICRL without making the unrealistic linear attention simplification. In particular, we consider the standard softmax attention used in practice. We show that, with certain parameters, the layerwise forward pass of a Transformer with such softmax attention is equivalent to iterative updates of a weighted softmax temporal difference (TD) learning algorithm. Here, weighted softmax TD is a new RL algorithm that performs policy evaluation in kernel space and adopts both linear TD and tabular TD as special cases. We also prove that under a certain contraction condition, the policy evaluation error decays as the number of layers grows, with the identified parameters above. Finally, we prove that those parameters are a global minimizer of a pretraining loss, explaining their emergence in our numerical experiments.

</details>


### [239] [Why Self-Inconsistency Arises in GNN Explanations and How to Exploit It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07527)
*Wenxin Tai,Yaqian Liu,Ting Zhong,Fan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自解释图神经网络中自不一致现象的成因，并提出了一种无需训练、模型无关的后处理方法（Self-Denoising），仅增加约4-6%的计算开销即可有效提升解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现自解释图神经网络（SI-GNNs）存在自不一致现象：将模型应用于其自身生成的解释子图时，可能会产生不同的解释。然而，这种现象为何产生目前尚不清楚，缺乏深入的理论理解。

Method: 首先识别出重解释诱导的上下文扰动是分数变化的直接原因；提出潜在信号分配假说来解释为何仅部分边对扰动敏感；分析简洁性正则化如何影响潜在信号分配；基于这些发现，提出Self-Denoising（SD）方法，这是一种模型无关、无需训练的后处理策略，仅需一次额外前向传播来校准解释。

Result: 实验验证了所提假说的正确性；在代表性SI-GNN框架、骨干架构和基准数据集上，SD方法一致性地提升了解释质量；实际应用中仅增加约4-6%的计算开销。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅从理论上阐明了SI-GNNs自不一致现象的产生机制，还提供了一种实用高效的解决方案，为提升图神经网络解释的稳定性和可靠性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent work has observed that explanations produced by Self-Interpretable Graph Neural Networks (SI-GNNs) can be self-inconsistent: when the model is reapplied to its own explanatory graph subset, it may produce a different explanation. However, why self-inconsistency arises remains poorly understood. In this work, we first identify re-explanation-induced context perturbation as the direct cause of score variation. We then introduce a latent signal assignment hypothesis to explain why only some edges are sensitive to this perturbation, and analyze how conciseness regularization affects latent signal assignment. Given that self-inconsistent edges do not provide stable evidence for the model's prediction, we propose Self-Denoising (SD), a model-agnostic and training-free post-processing strategy that calibrates explanations with only one additional forward pass. Experiments across representative SI-GNN frameworks, backbone architectures, and benchmark datasets support our hypothesis and show that SD consistently improves explanation quality while adding only about 4--6\% computational overhead in practice.

</details>


### [240] [CellScientist: Dual-Space Hierarchical Orchestration for Closed-Loop Refinement of Virtual Cell Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07335)
*Mengran Li,Bo Li,Jiaying Wang,Wenbin Xing,Yixuan Dong,Chengyang Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Yuzhong Peng,Jinlin Wu,Bob Zhang,Bingo Wing-Kuen Ling,Fuji Yang,Zhen Lei,Jiebo Luo,Zelin Zang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CellScientist双空间层次化框架，通过耦合高层假设空间与低层可执行实现空间，解决了虚拟细胞建模中的细化路由问题，实现了模型假设与实现之间的闭环迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助的虚拟细胞建模工作流面临细化路由问题：虽然可以通过可执行实现观察到预测偏差，但相关修订可能涉及建模假设、表示设计、实现或任务约束等多个层面。缺乏跨层级结构化反馈传播机制导致迭代细化可能仅修复代码而未能修正产生偏差的根本假设。

Method: CellScientist是一个双空间层次化框架，包含：1）高层假设空间与低层可执行实现空间的耦合；2）将建模决策表示为结构化状态；3）在任务和接口约束下将其实现为可接纳程序；4）将执行偏差路由回目标假设或实现更新。该框架构建了假设->实现->假设的闭环循环，将失败转化为模型细化的结构化信号而非调试事件。

Result: 在形态学和转录组学基准测试以及单细胞扰动评估中，CellScientist选择的最终可执行模型在固定划分和评估协议下优于参考基线，同时工作流产生了可审计的细化轨迹。

Conclusion: CellScientist成功实现了建模假设与实现之间的结构化反馈传播，使预测失败成为指导模型细化的有用信号，提升了虚拟细胞建模的性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Virtual Cell Modeling (VCM) requires models that not only predict perturbation responses, but also support targeted revision when predictions fail. Current LLM-assisted modeling workflows face a refinement-routing problem: prediction discrepancies are observed through executable implementations, but the relevant revision may involve the modeling assumption, representation design, implementation, or task constraint. Without structured feedback propagation across these levels, iterative refinement may repair code while failing to revise the assumption responsible for the discrepancy. We propose CellScientist, a dual-space hierarchical framework that couples a high-level hypothesis space with a low-level executable implementation space. CellScientist represents modeling decisions as structured states, realizes them as admissible programs under task and interface constraints, and routes execution discrepancies back to targeted hypothesis or implementation updates. This enables a closed Hypothesis -> Implementation -> Hypothesis loop where failures become structured signals for model refinement rather than debugging events. Across morphology and transcriptomic benchmarks, with additional single-cell perturbation evaluations, the final executable models selected by CellScientist improve over reference baselines under fixed split and evaluation protocols, while the workflow produces auditable refinement traces.

</details>


### [241] [ProteinJEPA: Latent prediction complements protein language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07554)
*Dan Ofer,Dafna Shahaf,Michal Linial*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出masked-position MLM+JEPA方法，在匹配计算预算下，相比纯MLM在下游任务中取得更多胜利（10-11胜/2-3负），表明JEPA与MLM结合具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究潜在空间预测（latent-space prediction）是否能在匹配计算预算下补充蛋白质语言模型中的token级目标函数（MLM），探索比纯MLM更有效的预训练方法。
