Method: 提出masked-position MLM+JEPA方法：仅在掩码位置预测潜在目标，同时保留MLM交叉熵损失。在35M-150M参数的预训练和随机初始化蛋白质序列编码器上进行实验，使用16个下游任务进行评估。

Result: Masked-position MLM+JEPA在预训练模型上表现优异：ESM2-35M上10胜/3负/3平，ESM2-150M上11胜/2负/3平。在11个任务上取得增益，包括稳定性、β-内酰胺酶适应性、变异效应等。全位置MLM+JEPA与纯MLM持平，纯JEPA（无MLM）在几乎所有实验中失败。

Conclusion: JEPA与MLM结合时具有竞争力，在预训练和持续训练中能够超越纯MLM，即使在匹配计算预算条件下也有效。关键在于仅在掩码位置进行潜在预测而非全位置预测。

Abstract: Protein language models are trained primarily with masked language modeling (MLM), which predicts amino-acid identities at masked positions. We ask whether latent-space prediction can complement these token-level objectives under matched wall-clock budget. Across pretrained and random-init protein sequence encoders at 35--150M parameters, we find that the best protein-JEPA design is not all-position latent prediction but a variant: predicting latent targets only at masked positions, and retaining the MLM cross-entropy. We call this recipe masked-position MLM+JEPA. On a 16-task downstream suite (15 frozen linear probes plus SCOPe-40 zero-shot fold retrieval), under matched wall-clock budgets, this recipe wins more tasks than it loses against MLM-only continuation: 10 wins / 3 losses / 3 ties (hereafter W/L/T) on pretrained ESM2-35M, 11/2/3 on ESM2-150M while results in pretraining from scratch are mixed (6/8/2). Gains are seen for multiple models on 11 of 16 tasks, including stability, \b{eta}β\b{eta}-lactamase fitness, variant effect, intrinsic disorder, remote homology, enzyme classification, and SCOPe-40 fold retrieval. Tasks with more losses than wins are Fluorescence (TAPE) and Peptide-HLA Binding. All-position MLM+JEPA matches MLM-only overall but does not reproduce the masked-position gains. JEPA-only (no MLM) collapses in nearly every experiment. We conclude that JEPA, when combined with MLM, is competitive and can outperform pure MLM in pretraining and continued training, even under matched wall-clock budgets.

</details>


### [242] [Ensemble Distributionally Robust Bayesian Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07565)
*Tigran Ramazyan,Denis Derkach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的集成分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化算法，在处理连续上下文时保持计算可行性，并获得了改进的理论次线性遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在零阶优化问题中，上下文分布的不确定性是一个常见挑战。现有的贝叶斯优化方法在面对复杂数据和噪声时鲁棒性不足，虽然集成模型可以缓解单一模型的缺陷，但如何在连续上下文场景下保持计算可行性仍是一个待解决的问题。

Method: 提出了一种新的集成分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化算法，该算法能够在管理连续上下文的同时保持计算上的可处理性。使用集成模型作为代理模型来提高对数据复杂性和噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 获得了理论上的次线性遗憾界，改进了当前最先进的结果。实验表明方法的实际表现与其理论保证相一致。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了能够在连续上下文场景下高效运行的集成分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化算法，在理论和实践层面都取得了突破性进展，为零阶优化问题提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: We study zeroth-order optimisation under context distributional uncertainty, a setting commonly tackled using Bayesian optimisation (BO). A prevailing strategy to make BO more robust to the complex and noisy nature of data is to employ an ensemble as the surrogate model, thereby mitigating the weaknesses of any single model. In this study, we propose a novel algorithm for Ensemble Distributionally Robust Bayesian Optimisation that remains computationally tractable while managing continuous context. We obtain theoretical sublinear regret bounds, improving current state-of-the-art results. We show that our method's empirical behaviour aligns with its theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [243] [Your Language Model is Its Own Critic: Reinforcement Learning with Value Estimation from Actor's Internal States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07579)
*Yunho Choi,Jongwon Lim,Woojin Ahn,Minjae Oh,Jeonghoon Shim,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POISE利用策略模型前向传播中已有的内部信号（隐藏状态和熵统计）轻量级预测基线值，替代了PPO需要的大型评论家模型和GRPO需要的多样本采样，在保持梯度无偏性的同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在基线估计上存在高昂的计算开销：PPO需要与策略模型规模相当的评论家网络，而GRPO需要为每个提示进行多次采样以维持经验组均值的稳定性。这些方法在计算效率和训练稳定性之间面临权衡，限制了大规模推理模型的强化学习训练。

Method: 提出POISE（Policy Optimization with Internal State Value Estimation）方法：1) 设计轻量级探针，从策略模型的隐藏状态、已生成轨迹和token熵统计中预测期望可验证奖励；2) 引入交叉采样构建机制，使用独立采样的内部状态预测当前采样的价值，确保梯度无偏性；3) 仅需单次采样即可估计提示价值，支持更高的提示多样性。探针与策略模型在线联合训练。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B模型上的数学推理基准测试中，POISE在较少计算量下达到与DAPO相当的性能。其价值估计器与独立的LLM规模价值模型表现相似，并能泛化到多种可验证任务。通过减少梯度方差，实现了更稳定的学习过程。

Conclusion: POISE成功利用策略模型自身的内部表示进行高效基线估计，在计算效率、训练稳定性和性能表现三方面取得了良好的平衡，为大型推理模型的强化学习提供了一种更高效的优化方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for Large Reasoning Models hinges on baseline estimation for variance reduction, but existing approaches pay a heavy price: PPO requires a policy-model scale critic, while GRPO needs multiple rollouts per prompt to keep its empirical group mean stable. We introduce Policy Optimization with Internal State Value Estimation), which obtains a baseline at negligible cost by using the policy model's internal signals already computed during the policy forward pass. A lightweight probe predicts the expected verifiable reward from the hidden states of the prompt and generated trajectory, as well as token-entropy statistics, and is trained online alongside the policy. To preserve gradient unbiasedness despite using trajectory-conditioned features, we introduce a cross-rollout construction that predicts each rollout's value from an independent rollout's internal states. Because POISE estimates prompt value using only a single rollout, it enables higher prompt diversity for a fixed compute budget during training. This reduces gradient variance for more stable learning and also eliminates the compute overhead of sampling costs for detecting zero-advantage prompts. On Qwen3-4B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B across math reasoning benchmarks, POISE matches DAPO while requiring less compute. Moreover, its value estimator shows similar performance to a separate LLM-scale value model and generalizes to various verifiable tasks. By leveraging the model's own internal representations, POISE enables more stable and efficient policy optimization.

</details>


### [244] [FlightSense: An End-to-End MLOps Platform for Real-Time Flight Delay Prediction via Rotation-Chain Propagation Features and Agentic Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07364)
*Aditi J. Shelke,Renuka J. Shelke,Yash M. Kamerkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlightSense，一个端到端的MLOps平台，用于实时航班延误预测。该系统通过三版本渐进式特征工程框架，将AUC从基线的0.732提升至0.879，并部署了包含天气感知对话式AI接口的生产级系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航班延误每年给美国经济造成数十亿美元损失，并在互联的飞机轮转系统中产生级联运营和财务负担。现有机器学习方法虽预测性能较强，但大多将上游延误视为静态输入变量，未能显式建模延误如何在飞机轮转链中动态传播，且缺乏与实时天气感知对话式AI界面的结合部署。

Method: 构建FlightSense端到端MLOps平台，采用三版本渐进式特征工程框架：版本1使用XGBoost分类器在1107万条BTS 2018准点性能记录上训练11个基于时刻表的特征建立基线；版本2通过尾号追踪从飞机轮转链中提取11个延误传播特征；版本3整合10个美国主要机场的5个NOAA气象特征。系统部署为生产级AWS MLOps管道，包含Lambda实时天气摄入、SageMaker实时推理、Streamlit交互式仪表板，以及Amazon Bedrock Nova Micro对话助手。

Result: 版本1建立基线ROC AUC为0.732；版本2引入延误传播特征后AUC从0.732提升至0.875，实现了最主要的性能提升，超越了Zhou (2025)报告的单阶段XGBoost基线；版本3整合气象特征后最终测试集AUC达到0.879。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模飞机轮转链中的延误传播机制，FlightSense实现了显著的预测性能提升，证明了延误传播特征的重要性。系统成功部署了包含实时天气摄入、推理引擎、可视化仪表板和自然语言查询接口的完整生产级MLOps管道，为终端用户提供了实用的实时延误预测服务。

Abstract: Flight delays impose cascading operational and financial burdens across the aviation network, costing the U.S. economy billions of dollars annually by disrupting interconnected aircraft rotation systems. While prior machine learning approaches have demonstrated strong predictive performance, most treat upstream delays as static input variables rather than explicitly modeling how delays propagate dynamically through aircraft rotation chains, and none have deployed such systems alongside a live weather-aware conversational AI interface for end-user interaction. This paper presents FlightSense, an end-to-end MLOps platform for real-time flight delay prediction built through a progressive three-version feature engineering framework. Version 1 trains an XGBoost classifier on 11 schedule-based features establishing a baseline ROC AUC of 0.732 on 7.07 million BTS 2018 On-Time Performance records. Version 2 introduces 11 delay propagation features derived from aircraft rotation chains via tail-number tracking, yielding the dominant performance gain (AUC 0.732 to 0.875) and surpassing the single-stage XGBoost baseline reported by Zhou (2025). Version 3 integrates five NOAA meteorological features across 10 major U.S. airports, achieving a final test set AUC of 0.879. FlightSense is deployed as a production AWS MLOps pipeline incorporating live weather ingestion via Lambda, real-time SageMaker inference, an interactive Streamlit dashboard, and an Amazon Bedrock Nova Micro conversational assistant answering natural-language delay queries via a tool-use architecture.

</details>


### [245] [Revisiting Transformer Layer Parameterization Through Causal Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07588)
*Jin Xu,Camille Couturier,Victor Rühle,Saravan Rajmohan,James Hensman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了因果能量最小化（CEM）框架，将Transformer层重新诠释为对条件能量函数的优化步骤。该视角揭示了多头注意力和门控MLP的能量基础，识别出包括层内权重共享等在内的新设计空间。实验表明，尽管参数化受限，CEM推导出的层仍能稳定训练并匹配基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模块中多头注意力与门控MLP的组合及其参数化选择主要依赖经验，缺乏理论指导。作者旨在通过能量最小化的视角，为Transformer层的参数化提供理论基础，并以此探索新的架构设计空间。

Method: 提出因果能量最小化（CEM）框架，将Transformer层重新表述为对条件能量函数的优化步骤。该框架将权重绑定的MHA推导为交互能量上的梯度更新，将具有共享上/下投影的门控MLP视为元素级能量的优化。基于此视角，识别出包含层内权重共享、对角加低秩交互、轻量预条件器和递归更新的设计空间。

Result: 在数亿参数规模的语言建模实验中评估了CEM推导出的层。结果显示，尽管这些层具有受限的参数化形式，它们仍能稳定训练，并且性能可以匹配相应的Transformer基线模型。

Conclusion: CEM为理解Transformer层参数化提供了有用的视角，成功将Transformer架构与基于能量的模型联系起来，证明了能量引导的层设计具有进一步探索的价值。

Abstract: Transformer blocks typically combine multi-head attention (MHA) for token mixing with gated MLPs for token-wise feature transformation, yet many choices in their parameterization remain largely empirical. We introduce Causal Energy Minimization (CEM), a framework that recasts Transformer layers as optimization steps on conditional energy functions while explicitly accounting for layer parameterization. Extending prior energy-based interpretations of attention, CEM shows that weight-tied MHA can be derived as a gradient update on an interaction energy, and that a gated MLP with shared up/down projections can be viewed through an element-wise energy. This perspective identifies a design space for Transformer layers that includes within-layer weight sharing, diagonal-plus-low-rank interactions, lightweight preconditioners, and recursive updates. We evaluate CEM-derived layers in language-modeling experiments at the moderate hundred-million-parameter scale. Despite their constrained parameterizations, these layers train stably and can match corresponding Transformer baselines. Overall, our results suggest that CEM provides a useful lens for understanding Transformer layer parameterization, connecting Transformer architectures to energy-based models and motivating further exploration of energy-guided layer designs.

</details>


### [246] [QuadNorm: Resolution-Robust Normalization for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07375)
*Bum Jun Kim,Makoto Kawano,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了正交归一化（QuadNorm）系列方法，通过用数值求积替代均匀平均，解决了神经算子中归一化层对离散化依赖的问题，实现了跨分辨率的鲁棒迁移，并在Darcy流和真实数据基准上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子中的归一化层通常通过对离散网格值进行均匀平均来计算统计量，这使得归一化本身依赖于离散化方式，从而成为不同分辨率或网格间迁移误差的来源。作者旨在解决这一被忽视的分辨率依赖性问题。

Method: 提出了正交归一化家族（QuadNorm和BlendQuadNorm），用数值求积方法替代现有归一化层中的均匀平均。在包含端点的均匀网格上，所提出的求积矩在离散化间具有O(h²)的一致性，即跨分辨率失配随网格间距二次衰减。推导了迁移误差界来预测归一化引起的失配如何随分辨率差距和网络深度变化。

Result: 实验验证了迁移误差界预测的差距和深度缩放趋势。在Darcy流问题上，QuadNorm在64²到256²的每个测试目标分辨率上都提供了最佳的跨分辨率性能；在真实数据基准上，配备QuadNorm的Transolver实现了近乎分辨率不变的迁移。在非周期PDE和非谱架构上取得了最大收益，同时原生分辨率性能也有提升。BlendQuadNorm验证表明其行为接近LayerNorm，可作为周期FNO设置的保守默认选择。

Conclusion: 这些结果识别出归一化是神经算子中一个此前被忽视的分辨率依赖性来源，证明了通过正交归一化可以有效解决该问题，为设计真正网格无关的神经算子提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Normalization layers in neural operators usually compute statistics by uniformly averaging discrete grid values, making the normalization itself discretization-dependent and thereby a source of transfer error across different resolutions or meshes. To enable discretization robustness, we introduce a quadrature normalization family that replaces existing uniform averaging in normalization layers with numerical quadrature: QuadNorm and BlendQuadNorm. On endpoint-inclusive uniform grids, the proposed quadrature moments are $O(h^2)$-consistent across discretizations, meaning that their cross-resolution mismatch decays quadratically with grid spacing. A transfer-error bound then predicts how normalization-induced mismatch scales with both the resolution gap and network depth. The experiments show the same gap- and depth-scaling trends predicted by the transfer-error bound. On Darcy, QuadNorm delivers the best cross-resolution performance at every tested target resolution from $64^2$ to $256^2$; on real-data benchmarks, Transolver with QuadNorm achieves nearly resolution-invariant transfer. The largest gains appear on nonperiodic PDEs and nonspectral architectures, where native-resolution improvements also emerge. We also validate BlendQuadNorm, which stays close to LayerNorm behavior and serves as a conservative default for periodic FNO settings. These results identify normalization as a previously overlooked source of resolution dependence in neural operators.

</details>


### [247] [Mathematical Reasoning via Intervention-Based Time-Series Causal Discovery Using LLMs as Concept Mastery Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07600)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CIKA框架，利用大模型本身作为干预模拟器来识别对正确答案有因果贡献的概念，在无需更新权重的情况下显著提升了数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM数学推理改进方法（如基于MCTS的测试时搜索或因果图引导的知识注入）无法识别哪些概念对正确答案有因果贡献，因为观察到的关联可能是虚假的，受到问题难度等混杂因素的干扰。

Method: 提出CIKA（Causal Intervention for Knowledge Activation）框架，使用LLM本身作为干预模拟器：通过提示将概念状态设为“掌握”，观察正确性变化来估计因果效应。将这一指标形式化为干预能力探测（ICP），用于诊断LLM是否能使用特定概念。由于干预外生地设置概念状态且独立于问题难度，ICP能够分离观测方法无法处理的混杂因素。

Result: 在67个筛选问题上，顶级概念的ICP（+0.219）显著大于负对照组（+0.039，配对t检验p<10^-6，Cohen's d=0.86）。601个Omni-MATH问题分析显示，已解决问题比未解决问题的平均处理效应（ATE）高6.1倍。在完全冻结权重的7B参数LLM上，CIKA在无污染的Omni-MATH-Rule基准上达到69.7%（o1-mini为60.5%），GSM8K上达97.2%，AIME 2024-2026上达46-50%。因果知识激活组件在基础模型单独失败的问题上贡献了33.8%的正确答案。

Conclusion: CIKA成功识别出对正确答案有因果贡献的概念，证明了LLM已经拥有但未激活相关知识。该方法无需更新模型权重即可显著提升数学推理性能，为理解和改进LLM的推理能力提供了新的因果视角。

Abstract: Recent methods for improving LLM mathematical reasoning, whether through MCTS-based test-time search or causal graph-guided knowledge injection, cannot identify which concepts causally contribute to a correct answer, as the observed association may be spurious, driven by confounders such as problem difficulty.
  We propose CIKA (Causal Intervention for Knowledge Activation), a framework that uses the LLM itself as an interventional simulator: a prompt sets the concept state to ``mastered'' and the correctness change estimates the causal effect. We formalize this quantity as an Interventional Capability Probe (ICP), which diagnoses whether the LLM can use a given concept -- distinct from merely possessing knowledge. Because the intervention exogenously sets the concept state independently of problem difficulty, ICP separates confounding that observational methods cannot.
  On 67 screened problems, the ICP of the top-ranked concept (+0.219) is significantly larger than that of the negative control (+0.039; paired $t$-test, $p < 10^{-6}$, Cohen's $d = 0.86$), confirming that the probe discriminates causally relevant concepts from irrelevant ones. Analysis of 601 Omni-MATH problems further shows that solved problems have 6.1$\times$ higher ATE than unsolved ones (0.338 vs. 0.055), confirming that ICP is predictive of problem-solving success. With a 7B-parameter LLM whose weights are entirely frozen, CIKA achieves 69.7\% on the contamination-free Omni-MATH-Rule benchmark and 64.0\% overall, compared to 60.5\% for o1-mini, and 97.2\% on GSM8K, 46--50\% on AIME 2024--2026, and 46.2\% on MathArena. The Causal Knowledge Activation component contributes 33.8\% of correct answers on problems where the base model alone fails, demonstrating that the LLM already possessed but had not activated the requisite knowledge.

</details>


### [248] [Zero-Shot Neural Network Evaluation with Sample-Wise Activation Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07378)
*Yameng Peng,Andy Song,HaythamM. Fayek,Vic Ciesielski,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SWAP-Score，一种通用的零样本评估指标，可在无需训练的情况下高效预测CNN和Transformer在各类任务中的性能，并基于此实现了快速的神经架构搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本指标与真实性能相关性弱、泛化能力差，且大多仅适用于CNN或Transformer其中一类架构，缺乏跨架构和跨任务域的通用性。

Method: 提出样本级激活模式（SWAP）及其衍生指标SWAP-Score，通过测量神经网络在一个mini-batch样本上的表达能力来评估其性能。该指标具有标签无关性，可应用于语言模型的预训练阶段。

Result: SWAP-Score在CNN和Transformer上均优于现有零样本指标。在DARTS CNNs（CIFAR-10）上Spearman相关系数达0.93，在FlexiBERT Transformers（GLUE）上达0.71。基于SWAP的NAS（SWAP-NAS）在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上仅需约6分钟和9分钟GPU时间即可达到竞争性能。

Conclusion: SWAP-Score是一种高效、通用且标签无关的零样本指标，能够跨架构（CNNs和Transformers）和跨任务域（CV和NLP）准确预测网络性能，显著降低了神经架构搜索的计算开销。

Abstract: Zero-shot proxies, also known as training-free metrics, are widely adopted to reduce the computational overhead in neural network evaluation for scenarios such as Neural Architecture Search (NAS), as they do not require any training. Existing zero-shot metrics have several limitations, including weak correlation with the true performance and poor generalisation across different networks or downstream tasks. For example, most of these metrics apply only to either convolutional neural networks (CNNs) or Transformers, but not both. To address these limitations, we propose Sample-Wise Activation Patterns (SWAP), and its derivative, SWAP-Score, a novel and highly effective zero-shot metric. SWAP-Score is broadly applicable across both architecture families and task domains, demonstrating strong predictive performance in the majority of tasks. This metric measures the expressivity of neural networks over a mini-batch of samples, showing a high correlation with the neural networks' ground-truth performance. For both CNNs and Transformers, the SWAP-Score outperforms existing zero-shot metrics across computer vision and natural language processing tasks. For instance, Spearman's correlation coefficient between the SWAP-Score and CIFAR-10 validation accuracy for DARTS CNNs is 0.93, and 0.71 for FlexiBERT Transformers on GLUE tasks. Moreover, SWAP-Score is label-independent, hence can be applied at the pre-training stage of language models to estimate their performance for downstream tasks. When applied to NAS, SWAP-empowered NAS, SWAP-NAS can achieve competitive performance using only approximately 6 and 9 minutes of GPU time, on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet respectively. Our code is available at: https://github.com/pym1024/SWAP_Universal

</details>


### [249] [An Efficient Hybrid Sparse Attention with CPU-GPU Parallelism for Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07719)
*Feiyu Yao,Zhixiong Niu,Xiaqing Li,Yongqiang Xiong,Juan Fang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fluxion提出了一种针对CPU驻留KV缓存的长上下文推理系统，通过输出感知的KV预算分配、头特定和粒度感知的稀疏配置以及跨设备协同执行，在保持模型质量的同时实现了1.5×-3.7×的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中，KV缓存常驻CPU内存（由于GPU显存不足或预填充-解码分离架构），但现有方案存在瓶颈：纯GPU设计受限于PCIe带宽和元数据开销，CPU-GPU混合设计存在GPU空闲时间、CPU端top-k选择和稀疏注意力计算瓶颈，仅依靠块稀疏注意力无法实现端到端效率。

Method: Fluxion基于三个核心洞察构建：(1)输出感知的KV预算分配；(2)头特定且粒度感知的稀疏配置；(3)跨设备协同执行。系统包含轻量级头属性预测器、粒度-预算选择器和基于优先级的调度器，联合优化预算分配、稀疏配置和CPU-GPU执行重叠。

Result: 在2个模型、3个基准和40个任务上，Fluxion相比全量注意力最差平均质量下降仅-0.26，相比最强固定稀疏混合基线（KV预算仅0.05）实现1.5×-3.7×加速。

Conclusion: Fluxion通过系统级协同设计，成功解决了CPU驻留KV缓存场景下稀疏注意力的准确性与系统效率权衡问题，为长上下文推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference increasingly operates over CPU-resident KV caches, either because decoding-time KV states exceed GPU memory capacity or because disaggregated prefill-decode systems place KV data in host memory. Although block-sparse attention reduces attention cost in this setting, sparsity alone is insufficient for end-to-end efficiency. GPU-only designs remain constrained by PCIe bandwidth and metadata memory overhead, while CPU-GPU hybrid designs still suffer from substantial GPU idle time and bottlenecks in CPU-side top-k selection and sparse attention computation.
  Fluxion is built on three key insights: output-aware KV budgeting, head-specific and granularity-aware sparse configuration, and cross-device coordinated execution for sparse attention over CPU-resident KV caches. Guided by these insights, Fluxion combines a lightweight head-property predictor, a granularity-budget selector, and a priority-based scheduler to jointly optimize budget allocation, sparse configuration, and CPU-GPU execution overlap. This co-design enables hybrid sparse attention to achieve both accuracy and system efficiency in long-context inference. Across 2 models, 3 benchmarks, and 40 tasks, Fluxion preserves quality well -- the worst average degradation is only -0.26 relative to FULL, while delivering 1.5$\times$-3.7$\times$ speedup over the strongest fixed sparse hybrid baseline, whose KV budget is only 0.05.

</details>


### [250] [StreamPhy: Streaming Inference of High-Dimensional Physical Dynamics via State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07384)
*Panqi Chen,Yifan Sun,Shikai Fang,Xiao Fu,Lei Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了StreamPhy框架，用于从稀疏测量中实时流式推断高维物理场，相比扩散模型准确度提升至少48%，推理速度提升20-100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非规则稀疏测量实时推断高维多模态物理场是科学与工程领域的基础挑战。现有方法（如扩散生成模型和函数张量方法）通常在离线设置下运行、依赖完整时间观测或产生大量推理成本，无法满足实时流式推断需求。

Method: 提出StreamPhy端到端框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 数据自适应观测编码器，对任意观测模式具有鲁棒性；(2) 结构化状态空间模型，支持非规则时间间隔下的内存高效在线更新；(3) 表达性强的函数张量特征线性调制(FT-FiLM)解码器，用于连续场生成。理论证明FT-FiLM比函数Tucker模型更具表达能力。

Result: 在三个代表性物理系统上的实验表明，StreamPhy在具有挑战性的采样模式下持续优于最先进基线方法。相比扩散方法，准确度提升至少48%，推理速度快20-100倍。

Conclusion: StreamPhy成功实现了从稀疏测量到物理场的实时流式推断，在保持高准确性的同时大幅降低了推理成本，为科学工程中的实时物理场推断提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring the evolution of high-dimensional and multi-modal (e.g., spatio-temporal) physical fields from irregular sparse measurements in real time is a fundamental challenge in science and engineering. Existing approaches, including diffusion-based generative models and functional tensor methods, typically operate in offline settings, depend on full temporal observations, or incur substantial inference cost. We propose StreamPhy, an end-to-end framework that enables efficient and accurate streaming inference of full-field physical dynamics from incoming irregular sparse measurements. The framework integrates a data-adaptive observation encoder that is robust to arbitrary observation patterns, a structured state-space model that supports memory-efficient online updates across irregular time intervals, and an expressive Functional Tensor Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FT-FiLM) decoder for continuous-field generation. We prove that FT-FiLM is more expressive than the functional Tucker model, admitting a richer function class for handling complex dynamics. Experiments on three representative physical systems under challenging sampling patterns show that StreamPhy consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with at least 48\% improvement in accuracy and up to 20--100X faster inference than diffusion-based methods.

</details>


### [251] [Curated Synthetic Data Doesn't Have to Collapse: A Theoretical Study of Generative Retraining with Pluralistic Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07724)
*Ali Falahati,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Kate Larson,Lukasz Golab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对生成模型递归重训练中的模式坍缩问题，提出利用多个奖励函数进行数据筛选可以有效避免坍缩，并证明模型最终收敛到一个保持多样性的稳定分布，该分布满足加权纳什议价解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，当基于单一固定奖励信号筛选合成数据进行递归训练时，生成模型倾向于坍缩到一组狭窄的输出，过度优化该目标，且普遍认为除非引入真实数据，否则这种坍缩不可避免。本文旨在从对齐视角重新审视这一结论，探索不引入真实数据的情况下缓解坍缩的可能性。

Method: 作者提出基于多个奖励函数（异质偏好）进行数据筛选的方法，形式化了在异质偏好下的递归训练动力学，并建立了相应的理论分析框架。

Result: 理论证明表明，在特定条件下，模型会收敛到一个稳定的分布，该分布将概率质量分配到相互竞争的高奖励区域，从而保持输出多样性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了无需引入真实数据即可缓解递归重训练中的模式坍缩问题。极限分布满足加权纳什议价解，为合成重训练循环中的价值聚合提供了形式化的理论解释。

Abstract: Recursive retraining of generative models poses a critical representation challenge: when synthetic outputs are curated based on a fixed reward signal, the model tends to collapse onto a narrow set of outputs that over-optimize that objective. Prior work suggests that such collapse is unavoidable without adding real data into the mix. We revisit this conclusion from an alignment perspective and show that collapse can be mitigated through curation based on multiple reward functions. We formalize the dynamics of recursive training under heterogeneous preferences and prove that, under certain conditions, the model converges to a stable distribution that allocates probability mass across competing high-reward regions. The limiting distribution preserves diversity and provably satisfies a weighted Nash bargaining solution, offering a formal interpretation of value aggregation in synthetic retraining loops.

</details>


### [252] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07386)
*Karthick Krishna M.,Haricharan Balasundaram,Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究可行集嵌套演化的凸优化问题，提出了针对一般凸、强凸及α-sharp损失函数的在线算法，并证明了算法的近似最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可行集随时间嵌套收缩的在线凸优化问题，目标是在保证每个时刻可行性的前提下，同时最小化后悔值和移动成本。这是对经典的嵌套凸体追逐问题的优化导向推广。

Method: 针对一般凸损失函数提出懒惰算法（lazy-algorithm）；针对强凸或α-sharp损失函数提出Frugal算法。

Result: 懒惰算法在任意β∈(0,1]下同时达到O(T^{1-β})后悔和O(T^β)移动成本；Frugal算法同时实现零后悔和O(log T)移动成本；并证明任何o(T)后悔的在线算法的移动成本下界为Ω(log T)。

Conclusion: Frugal算法在强凸和α-sharp情形下达到了最优的后悔与移动成本权衡。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} is considered where the objective function $f$ remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence $S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T$. The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known nested convex body chasing problem. When the loss function is convex, we propose a lazy-algorithm and show that it achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost for any $β\in (0,1]$, over a time horizon of $T$. When the loss function is strongly convex or $α$-sharp, we propose an algorithm Frugal that simultaneously achieves zero regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with $o(T)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω(\log{T})$ for both cases, proving optimality of Frugal.

</details>


### [253] [Drifting Field Policy: A One-Step Generative Policy via Wasserstein Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07727)
*Juil Koo,Mingue Park,Jiwon Choi,Yunhong Min,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了漂移场策略（DFP），一种基于漂移模型范式的非ODE单步生成策略，在操控任务上超越了现有的ODE方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有生成策略中ODE方法推理步骤多、计算成本高的问题，探索非ODE参数化在策略优化中的潜力，以实现高效的单步生成。

Method: 将策略更新构建为向软目标策略的逆KL Wasserstein-2梯度流，分解为朝向高动作价值区域的上升和与锚点策略的分数匹配（作为置信域）。推导出一个简单可处理的替代损失函数，类似于在评议员选出的Top-K动作上进行行为克隆。

Result: DFP在Robomimic和OGBench上的多项操控任务中达到了最先进的性能，推理仅需一步，显著优于基于ODE的策略。

Conclusion: 非ODE参数化与所提出的梯度流机制相结合，为生成策略提供了一种高效且性能优越的新范式，证明了漂移模型骨干在策略学习中的独特优势。

Abstract: We propose Drifting Field Policy (DFP), a non-ODE one-step generative policy built on the drifting model paradigm. We frame the policy update as a reverse-KL Wasserstein-2 gradient flow toward a soft target policy, so that each DFP update corresponds to a gradient step in probability space. By construction, this gradient is decomposed into an ascent toward higher action-value regions and a score matching with the anchor policy as a trust region. We further derive a simple, tractable surrogate of the otherwise intractable update loss, akin to behavior cloning on top-K critic-selected actions. We find empirically that this mechanism uniquely benefits the drifting backbone owing to its non-ODE parameterization. With one-step inference, DFP achieves state-of-the-art performance on several manipulation tasks across Robomimic and OGBench, outperforming ODE-based policies.

</details>


### [254] [Intelligent Truck Matching in Full Truckload Shipments using Ping2Hex approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07733)
*Srinivas Kumar R,Jose Mathew,Ankit Singh Chauhan,Dinesh Rajkumar,Aravind Manoj,Mohit Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ITM 2.0系统，通过将卡车-货物匹配建模为概率排序问题，利用H3空间索引和LightGBM模型，在北美和欧洲分别实现了26和14个百分点的精度提升，覆盖率翻倍，有效解决了因车辆标识符缺失导致的供应链可视性缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在整车运输供应链中，基于GPS数据的精准卡车-货物匹配是实现实时跟踪和准确预计到达时间（ETA）预测的基础。然而，车辆标识符的缺失或损坏使传统匹配方法失效，导致部分货物失去可视性，这是行业亟待解决的关键痛点。

Method: 该方法将匹配问题表述为概率排序问题。技术路线上，首先利用Uber H3六边形空间索引将GPS离散点离散化为路径相似性特征，并结合时间信息；随后应用LightGBM梯度提升模型进行训练，并采用基于阈值的后处理策略。研究经过了离线模型选择（对比SVM、XGBoost、LightGBM）、全面的消融实验以及生产环境影子测试。

Result: 与基于规则的基线相比，ITM 2.0在北美实现了26个百分点的精度提升，在欧洲实现了14个百分点的提升，同时将覆盖率翻倍。系统已部署于Project44的生产环境，表现出对高达1公里的地理编码错误、多候选卡车及稀疏Ping数据的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ITM 2.0系统成功填补了车辆标识符缺失导致无法匹配的技术空白，显著提升了整车运输供应链的可视性、精度与覆盖率，证明了机器学习方法在解决复杂物流匹配问题上的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate truck-to-shipment matching using GPS data is foundational for full truckload supply chain visibility, enabling real-time tracking and accurate estimated time of arrival (ETA) predictions. However, missing or corrupted vehicle identifiers prevent traditional matching approaches, leaving shipments without visibility. This paper presents Intelligent Truck Matching (ITM) 2.0, a machine learning system that addresses this critical gap by formulating matching as a probabilistic ranking problem. Our approach leverages Uber H3 hexagonal spatial indexing to discretize GPS pings into route similarity features, combined with temporal information, then applies LightGBM gradient boosting with threshold-based post-processing. Through rigorous evaluation including offline model selection (SVM, XGBoost, LightGBM), comprehensive ablation studies, and production shadow testing, we demonstrate substantial gains over rule-based baselines. ITM 2.0 achieves 26 percentage point precision improvement in North America and 14 points in Europe, while doubling coverage. Deployed in production at Project44 handling full truckload shipments, the system demonstrates robustness to geocoding errors up to 1 km, multiple candidate trucks, and sparse pings.

</details>


### [255] [When Losses Align: Gradient-Based Composite Loss Weighting for Efficient Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07756)
*Ivan Karpukhin,Andrey Savchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于梯度的双层优化方法，在线学习预训练损失权重，将超参数调优的计算开销降低至单次训练的130%左右，且性能匹配或优于精心调优的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度模型在大规模无标签数据预训练中常使用复合目标函数，其多个损失项的相对权重作为超参数需要调优。传统的随机搜索或贝叶斯优化方法需要多次独立训练运行，计算成本极高，亟需一种高效的超参数调优方法。

Method: 提出一种基于梯度的双层优化方法，通过将复合预训练梯度与下游目标对齐来在线学习预训练损失权重。该方法利用损失函数的结构特性，避免了截断反向传播通常需要的多次反向传递，从而显著降低计算开销。

Result: 在事件序列建模和自监督计算机视觉任务上的评估表明，该方法在匹配或优于精心调优的基线模型性能的同时，与随机搜索或贝叶斯优化相比大幅降低了超参数调优成本，额外开销仅为单次训练的30%左右。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了复合预训练目标中损失权重调优的高成本问题，提供了一种高效、自动化的超参数学习方案，为大规模预训练模型的超参数优化提供了新的思路和实用工具。

Abstract: Modern deep models are often pretrained on large-scale data with missing labels using composite objectives, where the relative weights of multiple loss terms act as hyperparameters. Tuning these weights with random search or Bayesian optimization is computationally expensive, as it requires many independent training runs. To address this, we propose a gradient-based bilevel method that learns pretraining loss weights online by aligning the composite pretraining gradient with a downstream objective. By exploiting the structure of the loss, the method avoids the multiple backward passes typically required by truncated backpropagation through the full model, reducing the overhead of hyperparameter tuning to approximately 30% above a single training run. We evaluate the approach on event-sequence modeling and self-supervised computer vision, where it matches or improves upon carefully tuned baselines while substantially reducing the cost of hyperparameter tuning compared to random or Bayesian search.

</details>


### [256] [Have Graph -- Will Lift? The Case for Higher-Order Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07397)
*Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文观点性论文指出几何深度学习领域存在模型丰富但合适基准数据集稀缺的不平衡现状，呼吁社区除提升现有图数据外，还应主动采集新的数据集，以夯实拓扑深度学习的研究基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何与拓扑学在机器学习中已站稳脚跟，消息传递是几何深度学习的核心驱动力，层等抽象概念也被用作新颖的归纳偏置。然而，模型架构的多样性与合适基准数据集的稀缺形成鲜明对比。研究人员常通过提升现有图数据集来引入高阶信息，但这不足以支撑该领域的长远发展。

Method: 本文是一篇观点性论文，未提出具体技术方法。作者通过分析当前拓扑深度学习领域的研究现状，指出模型发展与数据集建设之间的失衡问题，并对社区提出建议。

Result: 识别出拓扑深度学习领域的一个关键瓶颈：模型多样化发展与基准数据集供应不足之间的矛盾。发现当前研究过度依赖对现有图数据集的“提升”操作来获取高阶信息，而缺乏原生适合的高阶结构数据集。

Conclusion: 呼吁拓扑深度学习社区在继续改进现有数据使用方式的同时，应更加重视主动采集和构建新的、原生包含高阶信息的数据集，以加强该研究领域的理论基础，促进其健康发展。

Abstract: After a somewhat rocky start, geometry and topology have established a foothold in machine learning. Message passing, either on graphs or higher-order complexes, is one of the main drivers of geometric deep learning, and paradigms that were once considered to be firmly in the realm of the abstract-like sheaves-have been "tamed" to serve as novel inductive biases for model architectures in topological deep learning. The veritable diversity of models, however, is in stark contrast to the scarcity of suitable benchmark datasets. As a result, researchers often resort to lifting existing graph datasets to include higher-order information. In this opinion paper, I want to encourage the community to also source new datasets, which may be used to prop up the foundations of our research field.

</details>


### [257] [POETS: Uncertainty-Aware LLM Optimization via Compute-Efficient Policy Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07775)
*Nicolas Menet,Andreas Krause,Abbas Rahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出POETS框架，通过策略集成隐式编码奖励函数来量化认知不确定性，实现了高效的KL正则化Thompson采样，在LLM场景下采用共享主干+独立LoRA分支的架构，在科学发现和强化学习任务中达到SOTA样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在序列决策和黑盒优化中平衡探索与利用是核心挑战。现有方法需要训练不确定性感知的奖励模型再单独拟合策略，这种复杂的嵌套过程计算昂贵且难以扩展至大语言模型。需要一种能直接量化不确定性并指导策略优化的统一框架。

Method: POETS利用KL正则化策略隐式编码奖励函数的洞见，直接训练策略集成来捕获认知不确定性，将隐式奖励函数与在线自助法数据匹配。针对LLM的计算和内存限制，采用共享预训练主干、独立LoRA分支的高效架构，既保持集成多样性又降低开销。理论上证明了该方法隐式执行KL正则化Thompson采样。

Result: 理论分析证明POETS继承Thompson采样的强累积遗憾界O(√(Tγ_T))。实验方面：在蛋白质搜索和量子电路设计等科学发现领域达到SOTA样本效率；在强化学习中改善优化轨迹，特别是在经验回放的离策略设置和小数据集场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: POETS成功地将不确定性量化与策略优化统一到一个框架中，避免了传统方法的复杂嵌套训练，通过高效的架构设计使其适用于大语言模型，理论保证和实验验证了其在探索-利用平衡上的有效性和广泛适用性。

Abstract: Balancing exploration and exploitation is a core challenge in sequential decision-making and black-box optimization. We introduce POETS ($\textbf{Po}$licy $\textbf{E}$nsembles for $\textbf{T}$hompson $\textbf{S}$ampling), a novel framework that bridges uncertainty quantification and policy optimization. Our approach is grounded in the insight that policies trained with Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization implicitly encode an underlying reward function. Building on this, POETS bypasses the complex, nested process of training an uncertainty-aware reward model and separately fitting a policy to this model. Instead, we directly train a policy ensemble to capture epistemic uncertainty by matching implicitly encoded reward functions to online, bootstrapped data. To overcome the prohibitive compute and memory constraints of ensembling Large Language Models (LLMs), POETS utilizes an efficient architecture: the ensemble shares a pre-trained backbone while maintaining diversity through independent Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) branches. Theoretically, we prove that POETS implicitly conducts KL-regularized Thompson sampling and thus inherits strong cumulative regret bounds of ${\mathcal O}(\sqrt{T γ_T})$. Empirically, we demonstrate that POETS achieves state-of-the-art sample efficiency across diverse scientific discovery domains, including protein search and quantum circuit design. Furthermore, it improves the optimization trajectories of reinforcement learning, proving particularly robust in off-policy settings with experience replay or in small dataset regimes.

</details>


### [258] [Emergent Symbolic Structure in Health Foundation Models: Extraction, Alignment, and Cross-Modal Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07407)
*Gajendra Katuwal,Advait Koparkar,Salar Abbaspourazad,Anshuman Mishra,Sarvesh Kirthivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种后训练框架，通过分解冻结嵌入为可解释的符号方向，实现跨模态知识迁移，在健康基础模型中保留了95%以上的域内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康基础模型虽然能从可穿戴传感器学习有用表示，但理解其编码内容并在训练后跨模态迁移知识仍然困难。

Method: 提出一种后训练框架，将冻结嵌入分解为可解释的符号方向，并利用这些符号对齐嵌入空间，无需重新训练。在三个PPG和加速度计数据的基础模型上进行评估，这些模型独立预训练于约172K参与者的2000万分钟无标签数据，并在30K受试者的留出队列上进行分析。

Result: 提取的符号与健康状况和生理属性选择性关联，这些关联在模态和架构间部分共享；跨模态迁移保留了95%以上的域内性能，在域方向上几乎对称，且在有限配对数据下饱和，表明对齐恢复了一个富含生理信息的共享低维子空间。

Conclusion: 健康基础模型嵌入包含可解释的符号组织，这种组织在模态间共享，并支持无需联合训练的跨域迁移。

Abstract: Health foundation models (FMs) learn useful representations from wearable sensors, but interpreting what they encode and transferring that knowledge across modalities after training remains difficult. We present a post-training framework that decomposes frozen embeddings into interpretable directions, referred to as symbols, and use these symbols to align the embedding spaces without retraining. We evaluate the framework on three FMs for photoplethysmography (PPG) and accelerometer data, independently pretrained on ~20M minutes of unlabeled data from ~172K participants, and analyzed on a held-out cohort of 30K subjects. We find that extracted symbols associate selectively with health conditions and physiological attributes, and these associations are partially shared across modalities and architectures. Cross-modal transfer via symbols retains more than 95% of in-domain performance, is nearly symmetric across domain directions, and saturates with limited paired data, together indicating that alignment recovers a shared low-dimensional subspace rich in physiological information. Overall, these results suggest that health FM embeddings contain an interpretable symbolic organization that is shared across modalities and supports cross-domain transfer without joint training.

</details>


### [259] [Tracing Uncertainty in Language Model "Reasoning"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07776)
*Nils Grünefeld,Bertram Højer,Philipp Mondorf,Barbara Plank,Anna Rogers,Christian Hardmeier,Stefan Heinrich,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不确定性量化的新方法来研究语言模型的“推理”过程。通过将推理轨迹视为演化的模型状态，并提取不确定性轨迹特征，作者发现这些特征能有效预测推理结果的正确性（AUROC最高达0.807），且在生成早期即可检测错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的“推理”（如思维链或测试时扩展）虽能提升基准测试性能，但其底层动态过程仍未被充分理解。作者旨在通过不确定性量化的视角来深入探究这一动态过程。

Method: 作者将语言模型生成的中间token序列（推理轨迹）视为演化的模型状态，为每条轨迹总结出一个“不确定性轨迹特征”，包含描述不确定性信号形状的一小组特征（如斜率和线性度）。研究在五个语言模型上使用GSM8K和ProntoQA数据集进行了评估。

Result: 提出的特征能预测轨迹是否产生正确答案，AUROC最高达0.807，显著优于近期相关工作。仅使用完整轨迹的前几百个token即可达到AUROC 0.801，表明错误可在生成早期被检测。对比分析显示，正确轨迹的不确定性下降更陡峭且线性度更低。

Conclusion: 研究结果表名，这种基于不确定性决策理论的方法为研究语言模型“推理”背后的生成过程提供了一个有原则的视角。

Abstract: Language model (LM) "reasoning", commonly described as Chain-of-Thought or test-time scaling, often improves benchmark performance, but the dynamics underlying this process remain poorly understood. We study these dynamics through the lens of uncertainty quantification by treating the "reasoning" traces, the intermediate token sequences generated by LMs, as evolving model states. We summarize each trace by an uncertainty trace profile: a small set of features describing the shape of the uncertainty signal over its trace, such as its slope and linearity. We find that across five LMs evaluated on GSM8K and ProntoQA, these profiles predict whether a trace yields a correct final answer with AUROC up to 0.807, improving markedly on recent related work. We reach AUROC 0.801 using only the first few hundred tokens of full traces, suggesting that errors can be detected early in the generation. A detailed comparison of correct and incorrect traces further reveals qualitatively distinct uncertainty profiles, with correct traces showing a steeper and less linear decline in uncertainty. Together, the results suggest that our method, grounded in decision-making under uncertainty, provides a principled lens for studying the generative process underlying LM "reasoning".

</details>


### [260] [Neural Operators as Efficient Function Interpolators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07792)
*Vasilis Niarchos,Angelos Sirbu,Sokratis Trifinopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用神经算子（NOs）进行有限维函数插值的新框架，在多个基准测试中优于MLP和KAN，并成功应用于核质量模型修正，实现了198.2 keV的高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经算子设计用于学习无限维函数空间之间的映射。本文旨在重新定义其用途，探索其在有限维函数插值任务中的潜力，以期在保持高参数效率和短训练时间的同时，实现比现有网络架构更高的精度。

Method: 引入了一个辅助基空间，将任意有限维函数视为作用于该基空间函数的组合算子。通过解析函数基准测试验证了该方法的有效性，并构建了二维张量化傅里叶神经算子（TFNO）集成模型，将其应用于核图表，学习对最先进核质量模型的修正。

Result: 在复杂度和维度递增的解析函数基准测试中，NOs在精度上匹配或优于标准多层感知机（MLP）和Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN），且所需参数显著更少、训练时间更短。在核质量预测的实际应用中，TFNO集成模型在保留集上实现了198.2 keV的均方根误差，跻身近期最佳神经网络方法之列。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了神经算子作为一个可扩展的框架，不仅适用于无限维问题，也能高效解决从解析基准到结构化科学数据的有限维函数插值问题，兼具高精度与高效率。

Abstract: Neural operators (NOs) are designed to learn maps between infinite-dimensional function spaces. We propose a novel reframing of their use. By introducing an auxiliary base-space, any finite-dimensional function can be viewed as an operator acting by composition on functions of the base-space. Through a range of benchmarks on analytic functions of increasing complexity and dimensionality, we demonstrate that NOs can match or outperform standard multilayer perceptrons and Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks in accuracy while requiring significantly fewer parameters and training time. As a real-world application, we apply a two-dimensional Tensorized Fourier Neural Operator (TFNO) to the nuclear chart, learning a correction to state-of-the-art nuclear mass models as a partially observed residual field. A TFNO ensemble reaches a held-out root-mean-square error of 198.2 keV, placing it among the best recent neural-network approaches while retaining high parameter efficiency and short training times. More broadly, these results introduce NOs as a scalable framework for finite-dimensional function interpolation, from analytic benchmarks to structured scientific data.

</details>


### [261] [Risk-Consistent Multiclass Learning from Random Label-Subset Membership Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07413)
*Jiaxu Su,Junpeng Li,Changchun Hua,Yana Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对标签获取困难的问题，提出了一种基于随机标签子集查询的多分类学习框架，通过建立查询-响应观测的数据生成模型，推导出无偏风险估计器及修正风险估计器，解决了弱监督学习中的负经验风险与过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，获取准确的类别标签往往成本高昂、不可靠或受隐私等条件限制。相比于让标注者提供精确类别，询问真实标签是否属于某个标签子集通常更为容易。这种查询-响应形式定义了一种独特的弱监督机制，但目前缺乏对直接由此类查询响应观测生成的弱监督学习的系统性刻画。

Method: 提出了一种基于随机标签子集查询的多分类学习框架。具体包括：1）对查询-响应观测的数据生成分布进行建模；2）在经验风险最小化（ERM）框架下推导目标风险的无偏估计器；3）针对负经验风险及相关过拟合问题，引入基于非负校正和绝对值校正的修正风险估计器。

Result: 理论分析建立了无偏估计器的条件泛化界和过量风险界，并证明了修正风险估计器的偏差与一致性结果。在匹配随机查询机制下的实验验证了直接查询-响应学习的可行性，并展示了风险校正的稳定化效果。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了标签子集查询这一弱监督场景下的系统性理论空白，提出的无偏与修正风险估计器为解决此类问题提供了有效方法，实验证明了框架的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Obtaining accurate class labels is often costly or unreliable, and may also be limited by privacy or other practical conditions. Compared with asking an annotator to provide the exact class, it is often easier to ask whether the true label belongs to a certain label subset. This query-response form defines a distinct weak-supervision mechanism: weak supervision information is generated through feedback on a label subset. Although weakly supervised learning has studied many learning frameworks, most existing work starts from established weak label objects. A systematic characterization is still lacking for weakly supervised learning generated directly by such query response observations. This paper proposes a multiclass learn ing framework under random label-subset queries. We model the data-generating distribution of query-response observations and derive an unbiased estimator of the target risk under the empirical risk minimization (ERM) framework. To address negative empirical risk and the associated overfitting problem, we introduce corrected risk estimators based on non-negative and absolute-value corrections. Theoretical analysis establishes a conditional generalization and excess-risk bound for the unbiased estimator, and a bias-and-consistency result for the corrected risk estimator. Experiments under the matched random-query mechanism demonstrate the feasibility of direct query-response learning and the stabilization effect of risk correction.

</details>


### [262] [Toward Privileged Foundation Models:LUPI for Accelerated and Improved Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07799)
*Xueying Ding,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PIQL框架，首次系统性地利用特权信息（PI）来同时加速表格基础模型（TFMs）的训练收敛并提升泛化能力，在减少数据和计算需求方面效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的训练通常计算开销巨大且收敛缓慢。现有的表格基础模型（TFMs）面临这一瓶颈，亟需一种既能加速训练又能提升模型性能的方法。

Method: 提出了PIQL框架，构建了两种互补的特权信息形式：(i) 数据集级别的聚合统计信息，用于减轻上下文学习负担；(ii) 底层数据生成程序的编码，提供超越观测数据的知识。设计了特定架构，通过学习在推理时从观测上下文中重建这些仅训练时可用的特权信息，实现信息的有效迁移。同时提供了理论分析，刻画了特权信息减少近似误差并加速收敛的条件。

Result: 实验结果表明，PIQL使TFMs能够实现更快的收敛速度、更低的最终损失以及更好的泛化性能，从而有效降低了对数据量和计算资源的需求。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了基于特权信息引导的预训练作为一种原则性且实用的范式，能够有效提升基础模型的效率和性能。

Abstract: Training foundation models is computationally intensive and often slow to converge.We introduce PIQL,Privileged Information for Quick and Quality Learning, the first framework to systematically integrate privileged information (PI) to simultaneously accelerate learning and improve generalization in tabular foundation models (TFMs). We construct two complementary forms of PI: (i) aggregate dataset-level statistics that reduce the burden on in-context learning, and (ii) encodings of the underlying data-generating program, providing knowledge beyond observable data. We further design an architecture that effectively transfers the train-time-only PI by learning to reconstruct it from observed context at inference. We provide a theoretical analysis characterizing conditions under which PI reduces the population-level approximation gap and accelerates convergence in finite-data regimes. Empirical evidence shows that PIQL enables TFMs to achieve faster convergence, lower final loss, and better generalization, in effect, reducing data and compute requirements. Our work establishes PI-guided pretraining as a principled and practical paradigm for improving the efficiency and performance of foundation models.

</details>


### [263] [SR$^2$-LoRA: Self-Rectifying Inter-layer Relations in Low-Rank Adaptation for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07420)
*Fengqiang Wan,Yipeng Lin,Kan Lv,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SR²-LoRA方法，通过约束层间关系漂移来缓解类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，理论和实验均证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管参数高效微调（PEFT）在类增量学习中表现良好，但适应新任务时仍存在灾难性遗忘。作者通过分析层间关系漂移（即学习新任务时层间表示关系的渐进性破坏），发现这种漂移会降低已学任务的分类间隔，从而导致性能退化。

Method: 提出自校正层间关系低秩适应方法（SR²-LoRA）。该方法通过构建前模型和当前模型在当前任务样本上的关系矩阵，并对齐相应的奇异值来约束层间关系漂移。理论分析表明，相比于直接的逐元素对齐，奇异值对齐对估计扰动具有更强的鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准CIL基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SR²-LoRA能有效缓解灾难性遗忘。随着任务数量的增加，该方法的优势更加明显。

Conclusion: SR²-LoRA通过约束层间关系漂移成功缓解了类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，为理解CIL中的遗忘现象提供了新的视角，并在实验中展现了优越的性能，特别是在长任务序列场景下。

Abstract: Pre-trained models with parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) have demonstrated promising potential for class-incremental learning (CIL), yet catastrophic forgetting still persists when adapting models to new tasks. In this paper, we present a novel perspective on catastrophic forgetting through the analysis of inter-layer relation drift, i.e., the progressive disruption of relationships among layer-wise representations during the learning of new tasks. We theoretically show that the increase of such drift reduces the classification margins of previously learned tasks, thereby degrading overall model performance. To address this issue, we propose \underline{S}elf-\underline{R}ectifying inter-layer \underline{R}elation Low-Rank Adaptation~(SR$^2$-LoRA), a simple yet effective method that mitigates catastrophic forgetting by constraining inter-layer relation drift. Specifically, SR$^2$-LoRA constructs the relation matrices induced by the previous and current models on current-task samples, and aligns the corresponding singular values. We further theoretically show that this alignment exhibits greater robustness to estimation perturbations than direct entry-wise alignment. Extensive experiments on standard CIL benchmarks demonstrate that SR$^2$-LoRA effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting, with its advantages becoming more pronounced as the number of tasks increases. Code is available in the \href{https://github.com/FqWan24/SR-2-LoRA}{repository}.

</details>


### [264] [Prune-OPD: Efficient and Reliable On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07804)
*Zhicheng Yang,Zhijiang Guo,Yifan Song,Minrui Xu,Yongxin Wang,Yiwei Wang,Xiaodan Liang,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prune-OPD框架，通过实时监测师生预测的局部兼容性来检测前缀漂移，动态截断不可靠的训练轨迹并重新分配计算资源，在保持或提升性能的同时显著减少训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长序列任务中，传统的策略蒸馏（OPD）存在关键缺陷：随着学生模型生成的前缀与教师思维过程的偏离，教师的密集奖励失去局部可利用性，导致奖励质量下降并造成巨大的计算浪费。

Method: 提出Prune-OPD框架，通过持续监测学生与教师预测之间的局部兼容性（如top-k重叠度）实时检测前缀漂移事件。当检测到严重漂移时，单调降低后续不可靠奖励的权重并触发动态rollout截断，使训练过程能够终止无效生成并将计算重新分配给可靠的教师监督。

Result: 在多样化的师生组合中，Prune-OPD能够将计算与监督可靠性对齐。当前缀漂移导致教师密集奖励不可靠时，训练时间减少37.6%--68.0%，同时在AMC、AIME、HMMT等挑战性基准测试上保持或提升性能。当师生兼容性保持较高时，系统自动扩展训练窗口以保留长上下文监督。

Conclusion: Prune-OPD通过将计算重新分配给局部可利用的教师奖励来改进OPD，而非盲目缩短rollouts，实现了计算效率与监督质量的动态平衡。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) leverages dense teacher rewards to enhance reasoning models. However, scaling OPD to long-horizon tasks exposes a critical flaw: as the student's generated prefix inevitably diverges from the teacher's thought process, the teacher's dense reward loses local exploitability. Continuing to generate and evaluate tokens on these ``drifted'' trajectories not only degrades reward quality but also incurs massive computational waste. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Prune-OPD}, a framework that dynamically aligns training budgets with supervision quality. By continuously monitoring the local compatibility between student and teacher predictions (e.g., via top-$k$ overlap), Prune-OPD detects prefix-drift events in real time. Upon detecting severe drift, it monotonically down-weights subsequent unreliable rewards and triggers dynamic rollout truncation. This allows the training process to halt futile generation and reallocate compute strictly to reliable teacher supervision. Across diverse teacher-student combinations, Prune-OPD consistently aligns computation with supervision reliability. When prefix drift makes dense teacher rewards unreliable, it reduces training time by 37.6\%--68.0\% while preserving, and often improving, performance on challenging benchmarks (AMC, AIME, HMMT). When student-teacher compatibility remains high, it automatically preserves long-context supervision by expanding the training window. These results suggest that Prune-OPD improves OPD not by blindly shortening rollouts, but by reallocating computation toward locally exploitable teacher rewards.

</details>


### [265] [A Flexible Adaptive Stable Clustering Algorithm for Archive-Scale Online Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07424)
*Shao Shi,Xin Yang,Huiran Feng,Jianhuai Ye,Tianlong Hu,Yaling Zeng,Tzung-May Fu,Lei Zhu,Huizhong Shen,Chen Wang,Shu Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了FASC（灵活自适应稳定聚类）框架，解决了传统聚类方法在可扩展性、度量灵活性和算法稳定性之间的妥协问题，成功应用于2500万条大气气溶胶质谱数据的大规模分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代在线质谱技术产生TB级数据流，对理解地球环境系统至关重要。然而现有聚类方法在可扩展性、度量灵活性和算法稳定性之间存在计算瓶颈，阻碍了从海量数据中提取可操作的化学见解。

Method: 提出FASC（灵活自适应稳定聚类）动态系统框架，通过架构上解耦相似性核与严格优化逻辑，采用密度增强相似性选择规则和几何约束，保证确定性的、与顺序无关的收敛性，克服了传统启发式方法的随机漂移和算法混合问题。

Result: 在经典机器学习基准数据集上验证，FASC实现了>99.5%的聚类纯度和0.99的调整兰德指数。在2500万条大气气溶胶质谱数据上部署，展现出严格的线性经验运行时间复杂度O(N)，自主绘制了二次无机气溶胶的大气老化路径，同时分离出丰度<0.2%的超稀有工业示踪剂。

Conclusion: FASC框架成功解决了聚类分析中的核心约束问题，为环境大数据挖掘提供了可扩展的基础设施，在保证算法稳定性和度量灵活性的同时实现了线性可扩展性，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Modern online mass spectrometry generates multi-terabyte data streams critical for understanding Earth's environmental systems. However, extracting actionable chemical insights from these repositories is impeded by a computational bottleneck: existing clustering methods force a compromise among scalability, metric flexibility, and algorithmic stability. Here, we introduce Flexible Adaptive Stable Clustering (FASC), a dynamical systems framework that resolves these constraints by architecturally decoupling the similarity kernel from rigorous optimization logic. Unlike legacy heuristics that suffer from stochastic drift and algorithmic blending, FASC employs a Density-Augmented Similarity Selection rule and geometric constraints to guarantee deterministic, order-independent convergence. After validating FASC on canonical machine-learning ground truths (achieving >99.5% cluster purity and 0.99 Adjusted Rand Index), we deployed the framework on 25 million mass spectra of atmospheric aerosols. Demonstrating strictly linear empirical runtime scaling (O(N)), FASC autonomously mapped atmospheric aging pathways of secondary inorganic aerosols while isolating ultra-rare industrial tracers (<0.2% abundance), providing a scalable infrastructure for mining environmental big data.

</details>


### [266] [Approximation-Free Differentiable Oblique Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07837)
*Subrat Prasad Panda,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DTSemNet，一种将硬倾斜决策树(DT)语义等价且可逆地表示为神经网络的新方法，通过标准梯度下降实现端到端训练，无需软化边界或直通估计器(STE)等近似，在分类和回归任务上均优于现有可微分决策树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树在医疗诊断等安全关键领域应用广泛，但训练精确的倾斜决策树面临优化景观复杂和过拟合风险。现有可微分方法依赖概率软化边界或量化梯度(STE)等近似，限制了性能。

Method: 提出DTSemNet，将硬倾斜决策树语义等价且可逆地表示为神经网络，支持标准梯度下降进行端到端训练。针对回归任务中内部节点与叶回归器的联合优化难题，分析STE局限性并提出退火Top-k方法提供无近似的精确梯度信号。

Result: 在分类和回归基准测试上的大量实验表明，DTSemNet训练的倾斜决策树优于最先进的可微分决策树方法。此外，DTSemNet可作为强化学习环境中的程序化决策树策略，扩展了其适用性。

Conclusion: DTSemNet成功解决了可微分决策树训练中的近似问题，实现了分类和回归任务的端到端优化，在多个基准上达到最先进性能，并拓宽了决策树在强化学习等领域的应用前景。

Abstract: Decision Trees (DTs) are widely used in safety-critical domains such as medical diagnosis, valued for their interpretability and effectiveness on tabular data. However, training accurate oblique DTs is challenging due to complex optimization landscapes and overfitting risks, particularly in regression. Recent advances have introduced differentiable formulations that enable gradient-based training and joint optimization of decision boundaries and leaf regressors. Yet, existing approaches typically rely on approximations, either through probabilistic softening of boundaries (soft DTs) or quantized gradients such as the Straight-Through Estimator (STE). To overcome these limitations, we propose DTSemNet, a novel, semantically equivalent, and invertible representation of hard oblique DTs as neural networks. DTSemNet enables end-to-end training with standard gradient descent, eliminating the need for approximations in both classification and regression. While classification aligns naturally with this formulation, regression remains challenging due to the joint optimization of internal nodes and leaf regressors. To address this, we analyze the limitations of STE and introduce an annealed Top-k method that provides accurate gradient signals without approximation. Extensive experiments on classification and regression benchmarks show that DTSemNet-trained oblique DTs outperform state-of-the-art differentiable DTs. Furthermore, we demonstrate that DTSemNet can serve as programmatic DT policies in reinforcement learning environments, thereby broadening their applicability.

</details>


### [267] [GameGen-Verifier: Parallel Keypoint-Based Verification for LLM-Generated Games via Runtime State Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07442)
*Chaobo Jia,Ruipeng Wan,Ting Sun,Weihao Tan,Borui Wan,Yuxuan Tong,Guangming Sheng,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GameGen-Verifier框架，通过将游戏规范分解为可验证关键点并转化为独立验证单元，实现了对LLM生成游戏的高效自动化验证，准确率提升至92.2%，时间缩短16.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM游戏生成的自动化验证缺乏可靠方法。游戏正确性需在长期交互中验证，可能存在状态更新、交互规则和阶段转换等核心机制的违规。现有Agent-as-a-Verifier方法将验证过程与开放性游戏玩法合并，导致判定结果受可达性约束、耗时、覆盖率有限且受Agent游戏能力影响。

Method: 提出GameGen-Verifier范式：1) 将规范分解为可验证关键点；2) 将关键点转化为独立验证单元；3) 每个单元将游戏运行时修补至具体目标状态，执行有界交互，并根据关键点断言判断结果。实现GGV-Harness系统，提供并发管理、运行时隔离和故障恢复能力。

Result: 在包含7种类型100个游戏的VeriGame数据集上，GameGen-Verifier相对于人类判断达到92.2%准确率，而覆盖率增强的Agent-as-a-Verifier基线仅为58.8%；同时将实际运行时间缩短达16.6倍。

Conclusion: GameGen-Verifier通过结构化分解和独立验证单元的设计，有效解决了LLM生成游戏验证中的长期交互挑战，在准确性和效率上均显著优于现有方法，为游戏生成的自动化验证提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based game generation promises to turn natural-language specifications into executable games, but progress is limited by the lack of reliable automated verification. Unlike conventional code generation, game correctness is defined over long-horizon interaction: a game may appear correct while violating core mechanics such as state updates, interaction rules, and phase transitions. Existing Agent-as-a-Verifier approaches collapse verification into open-ended gameplay, making verdicts reachability-bound, time-consuming, coverage-limited, and sensitive to the agent's gameplay ability.
  We present GameGen-Verifier, an automated verification paradigm for LLM-generated games that decomposes a specification into verifiable keypoints and grounds them into independent verification units. Each unit patches the game runtime into a concrete target state, executes a bounded interaction, and judges the outcome against the keypoint assertion. We implement GGV-Harness, a scalable agentic harness providing concurrency management, runtime isolation, and fault recovery.
  On VeriGame, our dataset of 100 games across seven genres, GameGen-Verifier achieves up to 92.2% accuracy against human judgments versus 58.8% for the coverage-enforced Agent-as-a-Verifier baseline, while reducing wall-clock time by up to 16.6x.

</details>


### [268] [VNN-LIB 2.0: Rigorous Foundations for Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07451)
*Ann Roy,Allen Antony,Andrea Gimelli,Matthew L. Daggitt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了VNN-LIB 2.0的理论基础，通过引入"网络理论"概念解决了1.0版本缺乏精确语义等问题，建立了形式化的语法、类型系统和语义，并在Agda中完成机械化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VNN-LIB 1.0作为神经网络验证标准存在严重缺陷：缺乏精确的语法、语义和类型系统，表达能力有限，且依赖外部定义的ONNX模型（其语义非形式化且不断演变）。这与SMT-LIB等成熟标准不同，后者具有自包含查询和固定语义。这些问题阻碍了VNN-LIB成为可靠的形式化基础。

Method: 1) 引入"网络理论"(network theory)概念，抽象刻画神经网络模型格式所需的最小语义接口，使VNN-LIB独立于特定ONNX版本；2) 基于此建立更丰富查询语言的形式化语法；3) 设计基于网络理论数值域的类型系统；4) 给出形式化语义；5) 在Agda定理证明器中对标准进行机械化实现以确保内部一致性。

Result: 成功构建了VNN-LIB 2.0的完整理论框架，包括独立于ONNX版本的抽象语义接口、形式化语法、类型系统和语义定义，并通过Agda机械化验证确保了标准的一致性。

Conclusion: VNN-LIB 2.0为可信神经网络验证提供了坚实严谨的理论基础，解决了前一版本的关键缺陷，同时保持了与不断演进的模型表示的兼容性，为神经网络验证生态系统的互操作性提供了可靠保障。

Abstract: Neural network verification is an active and rapidly maturing research area, with a growing ecosystem of solvers and tools. The VNN-LIB standard was introduced to support interoperability in this ecosystem, but Version~1.0 has several serious short-comings as a formal foundation: it lacks a precise syntax, semantics, and type system, offers limited expressivity, and relies on externally defined ONNX models whose semantics are informal and constantly evolving. The latter distinguishes VNN-LIB from established standards such as SMT-LIB, where queries are self-contained and have fixed semantics.
  In this paper we address these challenges by developing the theoretical foundations of VNN-LIB~2.0. Our key contribution is the introduction of the notion of a \emph{network theory}, which abstractly characterises the minimal semantic interface required from a neural network model format. This abstraction enables VNN-LIB to be defined independently of any specific ONNX version while remaining compatible with evolving model representations. Building on this foundation, we present a formal syntax for a more expressive query language, a type system for it over the numeric domains provided by the network theory, and finally a formal semantics. To ensure internal consistency, the standard is mechanised in the Agda theorem prover. VNN-LIB~2.0 therefore provides robust and rigorous foundations for trustworthy neural network verification.

</details>


### [269] [On the Tradeoffs of On-Device Generative Models in Federated Predictive Maintenance Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07860)
*Usevalad Milasheuski,Piero Baraldi,Enrico Zio,Stefano Savazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在联邦学习环境下系统比较了VAE、GAN和扩散模型三种生成式模型用于时间序列异常检测和预测性维护的表现，发现部分联邦（仅共享解码器等组件）在非独立同分布和带宽受限场景下可优于全联邦方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能在物联网环境中保护数据隐私，但现有工作多聚焦判别式模型。生成式模型（VAE、GAN、扩散模型）在时间序列无监督异常检测方面潜力巨大，尤其在关键基础设施的预测性维护中。目前缺乏对这些生成式模型在联邦环境下的系统性对比分析，特别是在模型组件共享策略方面的研究空白。

Method: 1. 在联邦预测性维护场景下系统分析VAE、GAN和扩散模型（DM）三种生成式模型的性能；
2. 对比全联邦与部分联邦两种设置，后者仅共享模型的部分组件；
3. 提出联邦生成式模型的新分类法，将部分组件共享形式化为模型个性化的机制；
4. 在真实世界时间序列数据集上进行实验，评估模型效用、稳定性、可扩展性及通信开销。

Result: 1. GAN全联邦配置相比独立本地训练提升了训练稳定性，但鲁棒性仍不及VAE和DDPM方案；
2. 对于扩散模型，部分联邦（特别是仅共享解码器）在带宽受限和非独立同分布（non-IID）设置下可超越全联邦方案；
3. 不同模型在异构性和带宽受限的联邦设置中呈现出模型效用、稳定性和可扩展性之间的显著权衡。

Conclusion: 联邦生成式模型的设计需要根据具体场景权衡全联邦与部分联邦策略。本研究提出的部分组件共享分类法为联邦生成式模型的个性化提供了原则性框架，特别是在带宽受限和数据非独立同分布的实际工业场景中具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for preserving client data ownership and control over distributed Internet of Things (IoT) environments. While discriminative models dominate most FL use cases, recent advances in generative models -- such as Variational Autoencoders (VAE), Generative Adversarial Networks (GAN), and Diffusion Models (DM) -- offer new opportunities for unsupervised anomaly detection in time series analysis, with relevant applications in predictive maintenance (PdM) in critical industrial infrastructures. In this work, we present a comprehensive analysis of VAEs, GANs, and DMs in the context of federated PdM. We analyze their performance and communication overhead under both full and partial federation setups, where only subsets of model components are shared. Building on this analysis, the paper proposes a novel taxonomy for federated generative models that formalizes partial component sharing as a principled mechanism for model personalization. Our experiments over a real-world time series dataset reveal distinct trade-offs in model utility, stability, and scalability, especially in heterogeneous and bandwidth-constrained FL settings. For the evaluated GAN-based configurations, full federation improves training stability relative to independent local training, although the model remains less robust than the VAE- and DDPM-based alternatives. For DMs, however, partial federation -- especially decoder sharing -- can outperform full federation in bandwidth-constrained, non-IID settings.

</details>


### [270] [Inference-Time Attribute Distribution Alignment for Unconditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07456)
*Hao Luan,See-Kiong Ng,Chun Kai Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种即插即用的最优控制算法，用于在推理时对齐无条件扩散模型的属性分布，无需重新训练即可使生成样本群体满足多样化的目标分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时可控生成技术主要关注单个样本，难以满足现实应用中对样本群体属性分布（如人口统计平衡或语义比例）的特定要求。

Method: 将推理时的属性分布对齐问题建模为逆向扩散过程的最优控制问题，将扩散过程视为动态系统的展开，通过求解时变加性扰动作为控制量，在优化可微分分布匹配目标的同时惩罚控制代价以保持数据保真度。

Result: 图像生成实验表明，该方法能在不重新训练或微调预训练扩散模型的情况下，相比基线方法更好地将属性分布对齐到多样化且灵活的测试时目标。

Conclusion: 所提出的最优控制框架成功解决了无条件扩散模型的推理时属性分布对齐问题，为需要群体属性分布控制的实际应用提供了一种有效的免训练解决方案。

Abstract: Inference-time controllable generation is essential for real-world applications of unconditional diffusion models. However, most existing techniques focus on individual samples, struggling in applications that require the sample population to follow specific attribute distributions (e.g., demographic balance or semantic proportions). We formalize this setting as the inference-time attribute distributional alignment problem for pretrained unconditional diffusion models. To address this, we cast inference-time attribute distributional alignment as an optimal control problem over the reverse diffusion process, viewing the process as the rollout of a dynamical system and augmenting it with additive, time-dependent perturbations as control. We solve for the perturbations using an optimal-control-based algorithm to optimize a differentiable distribution-matching objective while penalizing control effort to preserve data fidelity. Experiment results in image generation demonstrate that our proposed plug-and-play approach can better align attribute distributions to diverse and flexible test-time targets compared to baselines, without retraining or finetuning the pretrained diffusion model.

</details>


### [271] [KL for a KL: On-Policy Distillation with Control Variate Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07865)
*Minjae Oh,Sangjun Song,Gyubin Choi,Yunho Choi,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出vOPD方法，通过引入控制变量基线（形式为已知的逐token负反向KL散度）来稳定在策略蒸馏训练，在不增加额外开销的情况下显著降低梯度方差，性能媲美全词表方法且计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在策略蒸馏（OPD）方法在实践中存在训练不稳定问题，根本原因在于其单样本蒙特卡洛估计器具有高梯度方差，而现有的稳定训练方案要么计算开销大，要么会引入偏差，缺乏成熟的解决方案。

Method: 将OPD重新表述为策略梯度强化学习问题，引入控制变量基线进行方差缩减。理论推导证明OPD的价值函数具有闭式解，即学生模型与教师模型之间的逐token负反向KL散度，该值可直接从前向传播中获得而无需额外训练评论家网络。通过减去该基线保持梯度无偏的同时降低方差，并进一步提出top-k近似基线以降低计算成本。

Result: 在数学和科学推理基准测试中，vOPD一致性地优于普通OPD方法，性能匹配计算开销最大的全词表基线方法，同时保持了轻量级的单样本估计器架构。top-k近似基线在降低计算成本的同时不损害性能表现。

Conclusion: vOPD通过将强化学习中成熟的控制变量方差缩减技术引入在策略蒸馏，实现了原理性的训练稳定化，为大型语言模型的后训练提供了一个高效、稳定且无需额外评论家网络的解决方案。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a dominant post-training paradigm for large language models, especially for reasoning domains. However, OPD remains unstable in practice due to the high gradient variance of its single-sample Monte Carlo estimator, and recipes for stable training are still immature. We propose vOPD (On-Policy Distillation with a control variate baseline), which casts OPD as policy-gradient RL and stabilizes it by introducing a control variate baseline-canonically a value function -- from the RL literature. We show that the OPD value function admits a closed form as the per-token negative reverse KL divergence between the student and the teacher, available directly from the already-computed forward pass with no additional critic or inference. Existing stabilization methods either compute the full token-level reverse KL over the entire vocabulary, adding significant overhead, or restrict it to a top-k support, biasing the objective. vOPD instead preserves the lightweight single-sample estimator, subtracting the value function as a detached baseline to keep the gradient unbiased while reducing variance. Furthermore, we show that a top-k approximation of the baseline further lowers cost without compromising performance. Across mathematical and scientific reasoning benchmarks, vOPD consistently outperforms vanilla OPD and matches the most expensive full-vocabulary baseline, offering an efficient stabilization of On-Policy Distillation through principled RL variance reduction.

</details>


### [272] [Estimation of Motor Unit Parameters from Surface Electromyograms using an Informed Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07458)
*Kaja Balzereit,Malte Mechtenberg,Axel Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息自编码器的方法，能够从非侵入式表面肌电信号中同时估计多个受试者特定的运动单元参数，在合成数据上取得了较高的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动单元参数（如神经支配区中心、电位传导速度）可提高神经力学模型的保真度，但现有非侵入式估计方法主要依赖白箱建模，需要大量手动建模工作，且难以应对参数随肌肉收缩变化的挑战。

Method: 开发了一种结合物理约束的知情自编码器，通过重构表面肌电信号，在潜在空间中学习参数，并遵循参数与肌电信号之间的物理规律，从而解决非线性损失函数的逆问题。

Result: 在合成数据实验中，神经支配区中心的平均绝对误差为2.5989 mm，电位传导速度的平均绝对误差为0.1697 m/s，验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了多个运动单元参数的联合估计，并通过集成数据驱动的机器学习减少了手动建模工作量，为神经力学模型的参数化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Motor unit parameters such as the innervation zone centre or the conduction velocity of the electrical potential harbour the potential to improve the fidelity of neuromechanical models used for movement and force prediction. Determining these parameters in a non-invasive way is challenging, as they are subject-specific and may vary with muscle contraction. Existing work on the estimation of motor unit parameters mainly relies on white-box modelling and therefore requires substantial manual modelling effort. This work targets the simultaneous estimation of multiple subject-specific motor unit parameters from electromyography (EMG) recordings measured non-invasively at the skin surface. This results in an inverse problem with a nonlinear loss function. To address this problem, an informed autoencoder is developed. This autoencoder reconstructs the surface EMG recordings while learning the parameters in its latent space and adhering to physical laws that relate the parameters to the EMG signals. In experiments on synthetic data, innervation zone centres are estimated with a mean absolute error of 2.5989 $\mathrm{mm}$, and conduction velocities of the electric potential are estimated with a mean absolute error of 0.1697 $\mathrm{m}\mathrm{s}^{-1}$. These results demonstrate the plausibility of this novel approach, which enables the simultaneous estimation of several motor unit parameters while reducing manual modelling effort through the integration of data-driven machine learning.

</details>


### [273] [Trajectory as the Teacher: Few-Step Discrete Flow Matching via Energy-Navigated Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07924)
*Amin Karimi Monsefi,Dominic Culver,Nikhil Bhendawade,Manuel R. Ciosici,Yizhe Zhang,Irina Belousova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-DFM方法，通过在训练轨迹中引入轻量级能量指南针来引导中间步骤的选择，解决了离散流匹配蒸馏中学生模型性能瓶颈的问题，在8步情况下实现了比1024步教师模型低32%的困惑度，同时速度提升128倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散流匹配方法在蒸馏过程中，当学生模型表现不佳时，通常认为原因是学生模型容量不足。作者提出相反观点：瓶颈在于训练轨迹本身。原始轨迹通过盲目随机跳跃构建，缺乏对序列质量的评估，早期中间点的错误决策会传播到后续步骤，而学生模型必须模仿这些有缺陷的结果。

Method: 提出轨迹形状离散流匹配（TS-DFM），用引导导航替代盲目跳跃：在训练的每个中间点，使用轻量级能量指南针评估候选延续，选择最连贯的选项。所有引导操作仅用于训练阶段，推理成本保持不变。

Result: 在170M参数语言建模任务上，经过轨迹塑造的8步学生模型实现了比1024步教师模型低32%的困惑度，同时速度提升128倍。这些增益在不同源分布和三个不同规模评估器上保持一致。TS-DFM在所有离散生成基线中达到最佳困惑度，包括那些使用6倍数据量或5倍模型规模训练的方法。

Conclusion: 证明了蒸馏性能瓶颈在于训练轨迹质量而非学生模型容量。通过轨迹塑造可以显著提升学生模型性能，在保持推理成本不变的情况下实现更好的生成质量和效率，为离散流匹配蒸馏提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching generates text by iteratively transforming noise tokens into coherent language, but may require hundreds of forward passes. Distillation uses the multi-step trajectory to train a student to reproduce the process in a few steps. When the student underperforms, the usual explanation is insufficient capacity. We argue the opposite: the trajectory is the bottleneck, not the student. Each training trajectory is built through a chain of blind stochastic jumps with no evaluation of sequence quality; a single bad decision at an early midpoint propagates through subsequent steps, yet the student must imitate the result. Trajectory-Shaped Discrete Flow Matching (TS-DFM) replaces these blind jumps with guided navigation: a lightweight energy compass evaluates candidate continuations at each midpoint, selecting the most coherent. All shaping is training-only; inference cost is unchanged. On 170M-parameter language modeling, the shaped student at 8 steps achieves 32% lower perplexity than the 1,024-step teacher while being 128x faster, with gains consistent across source distributions and three evaluators of increasing scale. TS-DFM achieves the best perplexity of any discrete-generation baseline we compare against, including methods trained on 6x more data or using 5x larger models.

</details>


### [274] [Learning Minimal-Deviation Corrections for Multi-Dimensional Mismodelling in HEP Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07460)
*Matthias Schott,Lucie Flek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经网络的方法，能够在仅有一维目标分布数据的情况下，对高能物理蒙特卡洛模拟进行多维校正，同时保持原始模拟的全局相关性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中的蒙特卡洛模拟在复杂场景下常无法准确复现观测数据。实际实验信息往往仅限于一维分布，而模拟误差源于多维特征空间。传统的一维加权方法忽略了相关性，而全多维方法需要大量目标数据集，两者在受限信息下均存在不足。

Method: 提出一种基于神经网络的方法，学习模拟事件的变换，使其在符合可用一维目标分布的同时，尽可能接近原始模拟。这种最小偏差原则旨在保留基线模型的全局相关性结构，并实现对建模不准确特征的针对性校正。研究通过模拟伪数据的对照实验进行验证。

Result: 在受控的伪数据研究中，该方法成功改善了与目标分布的一致性，并在有限信息的约束下保持了连贯的多维结构。

Conclusion: 该方法专为传统技术不足以应对的复杂、高维分析设计，为在有限信息条件下增强蒙特卡洛模拟精度提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate Monte Carlo (MC) modelling in high-energy physics is challenging, particularly in complex scenarios where simulations fail to reproduce observed data. In practice, experimental information is often limited to one-dimensional (1D) distributions, while mismodelling arises in a multidimensional feature space. This restricts traditional correction methods, as one-dimensional reweighting ignores correlations and fully multidimensional approaches require large target datasets. We propose a neural network-based method that operates under these constraints by learning a transformation of simulated events that reproduces the available 1D target distributions while remaining close to the original simulation. This minimal-deviation principle preserves the global correlation structure of the baseline model while enabling targeted corrections of mismodelled features. Using controlled studies with simulated pseudo-data, we show that the method improves agreement with target distributions and maintains a consistent multidimensional structure. The approach is designed for complex, high-dimensional analyses where traditional techniques are insufficient, providing a scalable way to enhance MC modelling under limited information.

</details>


### [275] [INO-SGD: Addressing Utility Imbalance under Individualized Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07930)
*Xiao Tian,Jue Fan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对个性化差分隐私(IDP)中因不同隐私需求导致的数据代表性失衡问题，提出了INO-SGD算法，通过策略性降权来提升高隐私要求数据的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习中，数据所有者开始自主设定隐私需求，特别是敏感数据所有者（如被污名化疾病的阳性病例）会设置更强的隐私要求。然而现有IDP算法会导致效用失衡问题：强隐私要求数据在训练中被严重低估，导致模型在此类数据上性能下降。

Method: 提出INO-SGD算法，在每个批次中策略性地降低数据权重，在所有迭代过程中改善更高隐私要求数据的性能表现。该算法专门设计以满足IDP要求，而现有的解决效用失衡的技术既不满足IDP也无法轻易适配。

Result: 通过实证研究证明了INO-SGD方法的可行性，有效解决了个性化差分隐私中的效用失衡问题。

Conclusion: INO-SGD算法成功解决了个性化差分隐私场景下的效用失衡问题，在满足不同数据所有者隐私需求的同时，保证了模型在高隐私要求数据上的性能表现。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) is widely employed in machine learning to protect confidential or sensitive training data from being revealed. As data owners gain greater control over their data due to personal data ownership, they are more likely to set their own privacy requirements, necessitating individualized DP (IDP) to fulfil such requests. In particular, owners of data from more sensitive subsets, such as positive cases of stigmatized diseases, likely set stronger privacy requirements, as leakage of such data could incur more serious societal impact. However, existing IDP algorithms induce a critical utility imbalance problem: Data from owners with stronger privacy requirements may be severely underrepresented in the trained model, resulting in poorer performance on similar data from subsequent users during deployment. In this paper, we analyze this problem and propose the INO-SGD algorithm, which strategically down-weights data within each batch to improve performance on the more private data across all iterations. Notably, our algorithm is specially designed to satisfy IDP, while existing techniques addressing utility imbalance neither satisfy IDP nor can be easily adapted to do so. Lastly, we demonstrate the empirical feasibility of our approach.

</details>


### [276] [Approximation Error Upper and Lower Bounds for Hölder Class with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07463)
*Xin He,Yuling Jiao,Xiliang Lu,Jerry Zhijian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对标准Transformer架构建立了Hölder函数类的逼近误差上下界，证明了达到ε精度所需的网络块数分别在O(ε^{-d_0/α})和Ω(ε^{-d_0/(4α)})量级，并将理论结果扩展到回归任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Transformer在各类任务中表现卓越，但其理论表达能力的研究仍不充分，特别是针对具有特定平滑性质的函数类（如Hölder类）的逼近误差上下界缺乏精确的理论刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，为Transformer的理论基础提供严谨的数学证明。

Method: 1) 针对配备Softmax算子、ReLU激活函数和残差连接的标准Transformer架构，推导逼近误差上界；
2) 利用VC维上界技术，首次严格证明逼近误差下界；
3) 将理论结果推广至一般回归任务，建立对应的超额风险率。

Result: 1) 上界：最多O(ε^{-d_0/α})个Transformer块即可逼近任意有界Hölder函数（d_0维输入，平滑度α∈(0,1]）达到ε精度；
2) 下界：实现ε逼近精度至少需要Ω(ε^{-d_0/(4α)})个块；
3) 回归任务超额风险率成功建立，从理论角度解释了Transformer在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次为标准Transformer架构提供了Hölder函数类的精确逼近误差上下界，上下界在ε指数项上仅相差常数因子，表明所获结果具有紧致性。研究不仅深化了对Transformer表达能力的理论理解，还通过回归任务的扩展为实际应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We explore the expressive power of Transformers by establishing precise approximation error upper and lower bounds for Hölder class. Specifically, a new approximation upper bound is derived for the standard Transformer architecture equipped with Softmax operators, ReLU activation functions, and residual connections. We prove that a Transformer network composed of at most $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-{d_{0}}/α})$ blocks can approximate any bounded Hölder function with $d_{0}$-dimensional input and smoothness $α\in(0,1]$ under any accuracy $\varepsilon>0$. In the case of approximation lower bounds, leveraging the VC-dimension upper bound, we are the first to rigorously prove that Transformers demand for at least $Ω(\varepsilon^{-{d_{0}}/({4α})})$ blocks to achieve the $\varepsilon$ approximation accuracy. As a final step, we extend the derived results for standard Transformers to a general regression task and establish the corresponding excess risk rates demonstrating Transformers' empirical effectiveness in real-world settings.

</details>


### [277] [It Just Takes Two: Scaling Amortized Inference to Large Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07972)
*Antoine Wehenkel,Michael Kagan,Lukas Heinrich,Chris Pollard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种解耦表示学习与后验建模的策略，通过在小规模集合上训练Mean-pool Deep Set编码器并泛化到任意规模，实现了计算成本与数据集规模解耦的高效神经后验估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际应用中，条件变量是观测集合，其元素不仅依赖于目标参数，还依赖于集合间共享的未知因素。最优推断需要联合处理整个集合，但在部署规模的集合上训练估计器会导致内存和计算成本急剧增加，变得不可行。

Method: 提出一种解耦策略：1) 在最大规模为2的集合上训练Mean-pool Deep Set，使编码器能够泛化到任意集合规模；2) 在预聚合的嵌入上微调推断头，使训练成本基本独立于部署集合规模N。

Result: 在标量、图像、多视角3D、分子和高维条件生成基准测试中（N达到数千），该方法以极小部分的计算成本匹配或优于标准基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了神经后验估计中集合规模带来的计算瓶颈问题，在保持性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，为大规模集合条件下的推断提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Neural posterior estimation has emerged as a powerful tool for amortized inference, with growing adoption across scientific and applied domains. In many of these applications, the conditioning variable is a set of observations whose elements depend not only on the target but also on unknown factors shared across the set. Optimal inference therefore requires treating the set jointly, which in turn requires training the estimator at the deployment set size -- a regime where memory and compute quickly become prohibitive. We introduce a simple, theoretically grounded strategy that decouples representation learning from posterior modeling. Our method trains a mean-pool Deep Set on sets of size at most two, producing an encoder that generalizes to arbitrary set sizes. The inference head is then finetuned on pre-aggregated embeddings, making training cost essentially independent of the deployment set size N. Across scalar, image, multi-view 3D, molecular, and high-dimensional conditional generation benchmarks with N in the thousands, our approach matches or outperforms standard baselines at a fraction of the compute.

</details>


### [278] [Where's the Plan? Locating Latent Planning in Language Models with Lightweight Mechanistic Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07984)
*Nicole Ma,Nick Rui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型中的规划站点形成现象。线性探测显示未来押韵信息在行边界处可解码且随规模增强；激活修补发现仅Gemma-3-27B依赖此编码，其他模型均直接依赖押韵词。通过两阶段路径修补将Gemma-3-27B的转换定位到5个注意力头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在前向传播中是否会形成对未来结构约束token的内部表示（规划站点），以及这些表示是否因果地驱动生成过程，以理解模型是否存在前瞻性规划机制。

Method: 以对韵句完成为测试任务，在Qwen3、Gemma-3和Llama-3三个模型家族的十余种规模上应用线性探测和激活修补两种轻量级方法。通过两阶段路径修补技术对Gemma-3-27B中的机制转换进行精确定位。

Result: 线性探测表明未来押韵信息在行边界处线性可解码，信号强度随规模增加而增强。激活修补揭示仅Gemma-3-27B因果依赖此编码，在第30层附近出现因果驱动源从押韵词迁移到行边界的手递现象，且可定位到5个注意力头。其他模型全程依赖押韵词，行边界处因果效应接近零。

Conclusion: 规划站点在语言模型中确实存在，但其因果作用因模型架构和规模而异。强探测信号不等同于因果作用，大多数模型采用即时条件生成策略而非前瞻规划，仅特定配置的模型展现出规划驱动的生成模式。
