Abstract: We study planning site formation in language models -- where internal representations of structurally-constrained future tokens form during the forward pass, and whether they causally drive generation. Using rhyming-couplet completion as a clean test of forward-looking constraint, we apply two lightweight methods (linear probing and activation patching) across Qwen3, Gemma-3, and Llama-3 at more than ten scales. Probing shows that future-rhyme information is linearly decodable at the line boundary, with signal that strengthens with scale in all three families. Activation patching reveals that only Gemma-3-27B causally relies on this encoding, exhibiting a handoff in which the causal driver migrates from the rhyme word to the line boundary around layer 30. Every other model we test conditions on the rhyme word throughout generation, with near-zero causal effect at the line boundary despite strong probe signal. We localize the Gemma-3-27B handoff to five attention heads through two-stage path patching that recover ~90% of the rhyme-routing capacity at the newline.

</details>


### [279] [Uncovering Hidden Systematics in Neural Network Models for High Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07470)
*Lucie Flek,Philipp Alexander Jungs,Akbar Karimi,Timo Saala,Alexander Schmid,Matthias Schott,Philipp Soldin,Christopher Wiebusch,Ulrich Willemsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文受对抗攻击研究启发，揭示了神经网络在高能物理分析中对输入观测量的实验不确定性范围内的微小扰动具有隐藏敏感性，并提出了一个量化框架来评估和控制这种系统不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络虽然在处理高能物理分析中复杂的非线性关系方面表现出色，但其灵活性使其对输入的微小变化高度敏感。当前的系统不确定性传播与估计方法（如在控制区推导或对输入特征进行名义变化）可能低估了真实的模型不确定性，导致潜在的偏差未被考虑，这成为神经网络在物理分析中应用的一个公开挑战。

Method: 受机器学习中对抗攻击研究的启发，作者探索了在保持一维及相关输入分布几乎不变的情况下，与输入观测量的实验不确定性完全一致的细微扰动如何导致神经网络输出的显著变化。研究涵盖了典型的高能物理任务（如事件分类和对象识别），并在多种网络架构上进行了测试。基于观察到的网络在允许的不确定性包络内可能被“愚弄”的现象，引入了一个量化框架。

Result: 研究表明，神经网络在允许的不确定性包络内可以被系统性地以显著比率“愚弄”，即输入的细微扰动可导致输出发生实质性变化。这证明了网络对现实实验变化存在隐藏敏感性。通过所提出的量化框架，能够探测并测量这种隐藏敏感性。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了神经网络在高能物理应用中存在被忽视的系统不确定性风险，并成功构建了一个实用的框架，为在物理分析中评估和控制神经网络模型的系统不确定性提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: Neural networks (NNs) are inherently multidimensional classifiers that learn complex, non-linear relationships among input observables. While their flexibility enables unprecedented performance in high-energy physics (HEP) analyses, it also makes them sensitive to small variations in their inputs. Consequently, the propagation and estimation of systematic uncertainties in NN-based models remain an open challenge. There are indications that uncertainties derived in control regions or from nominal variations of input features can underestimate the true model uncertainty, potentially leaving biases unaccounted for. Inspired by insights from adversarial-attack studies in machine learning, we explore how subtle perturbations, fully consistent with the experimental uncertainties on the input observables, can lead to substantial changes in NN outputs, while keeping the one-dimensional and correlated input distributions nearly unchanged. Using a set of representative HEP tasks, including event classification and object identification, and testing across a variety of network architectures, we demonstrate that networks can be systematically "fooled" at significant rates within the allowed uncertainty envelopes. Building on this observation, we introduce a quantitative framework to probe and measure the hidden sensitivity of neural networks to realistic experimental variations, providing a practical path to evaluate and control their systematic uncertainty in physics analyses.

</details>


### [280] [Transfer Learning Across Fast- and Full-Simulation Domains in High-Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07471)
*Matthias Schott,Lucie Flek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究表明，在高能物理任务中，利用快模拟数据预训练模型并迁移至全模拟数据，能显著降低对目标域训练数据的统计量需求（约减少一半），且表现优于独立训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中的机器学习模型通常依赖模拟数据训练。全模拟样本计算成本高昂，而快模拟虽然能提供大统计量样本，但真实度较低。因此，有必要研究如何通过迁移学习，利用易获取的快模拟数据来缓解全模拟数据的稀缺与高成本问题。

Method: 在现实的LHC环境下，系统研究了快模拟与全模拟数据集之间的迁移学习。针对信号-背景分类、夸克-胶子喷注标记、缺失横向能量重建三项任务，采用了稠密神经网络、图神经网络及Transformer架构。模型首先在类ATLAS快模拟数据上预训练，随后适应至类CMS快模拟数据及ATLAS全模拟开放数据。

Result: 在所有任务中，预训练模型的表现均优于独立训练的基准模型，且对目标域训练数据的统计量需求显著降低，通常可将所需样本量减少约一半。

Conclusion: 快模拟数据可用于学习鲁棒且可复用的特征表示，研究支持将训练好的模型作为可复用的科学资产发布，其价值超越了大型基础模型本身。

Abstract: Machine-learning models in high-energy physics are often trained on simulated data, where fully simulated samples are computationally expensive while fast simulation provides large statistics at reduced realism. In this work, we systematically study transfer learning between fast-simulated and fully simulated datasets in a realistic LHC environment. We consider three representative tasks, signal-background classification, quark-gluon jet tagging, and missing transverse energy reconstruction, using dense neural networks, graph neural networks, and transformer-based architectures. Models are pretrained on ATLAS-like fast simulation and adapted to CMS-like fast simulation and to fully simulated ATLAS Open Data. Across all tasks, pretrained models consistently outperform independently trained baselines and require significantly less target-domain training data, typically reducing the needed statistics by about a factor of two. These results demonstrate that fast simulation can be used to learn robust, reusable representations and motivate publishing trained models as reusable scientific assets beyond large foundation models.

</details>


### [281] [Graph-Structured Hyperdimensional Computing for Data-Efficient and Explainable Process-Structure-Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07999)
*Jingzhan Ge,Ajeeth Vellore,Ajinkya Palwe,Ahsan Khan,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,SeungYeon Kang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PSP-HDC框架，一种基于图结构超维计算的方法，用于解决多光子光还原制造中稀疏异构数据下的工艺-结构-性能预测问题，在3D平台片阻预测任务上准确率达0.910，并具备内在的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光子光还原技术虽能制造复杂3D微结构，但工艺-结构-性能（PSP）预测面临重大挑战：可用数据稀疏、异构且存在强烈交互作用。在此情况下，传统特征向量模型统计上欠定，易产生虚假相关性、迁移能力差且解释不稳定；而机理模型在早期工艺开发阶段缺乏必要的标定子模型。

Method: 提出PSP-HDC图结构超维计算框架，将有向PSP图编码为内部先验。核心方法包括：(1) 可训练标量-超向量编码器学习参数特定嵌入；(2) 通过图对齐的绑定和捆绑操作沿有向PSP依赖关系组合样本表示；(3) 通过联想记忆检索与类别原型进行预测。相同的原型记忆同时支持决策和归因分析。

Result: 在3D平台的片阻区间预测任务上，PSP-HDC在1000次随机划分中达到0.910±0.077的准确率，在工艺折叠泛化测试中达到0.896准确率，均优于强基线方法。框架能够在参数、组和组内三个层面提供内在解释，并通过记忆对齐和分离量化训练过程中的原型形成。

Conclusion: PSP-HDC成功解决了稀疏异构数据下的PSP预测难题，通过图结构先验和超维计算实现了高精度预测与内在可解释性的统一，为早期工艺开发阶段的可靠预测提供了有效解决方案。该方法在制造工艺优化和材料设计领域具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Multiphoton photoreduction enables high-fidelity fabrication of complex 3D microstructures, yet reliable process-structure-property (PSP) prediction remains difficult because the available data are sparse, heterogeneous, and interaction-dominated. In this regime, conventional feature-vector models are statistically underdetermined, making them prone to spurious correlations, poor regime transfer, and unstable post hoc explanations, whereas mechanistic pipelines depend on calibrated submodels that are rarely available during early process development. We present PSP-HDC, a graph-structured hyperdimensional computing framework that encodes a directed PSP graph as an internal prior for representation, inference, and explanation. A trainable scalar-to-hypervector encoder learns parameter-specific embeddings on a fixed hyperdimensional basis to accommodate heterogeneous scales and noise. Sample representations are then composed through graph-aligned binding and bundling along directed PSP dependencies, and prediction is performed by associative-memory retrieval against class prototypes. Because the same prototype memories support both decision making and attribution, PSP-HDC provides intrinsic explanations at the parameter, group, and within-group levels, while memory alignment and separation quantify prototype formation during training. On sheet-resistance regime prediction for the 3D platform, PSP-HDC achieves an accuracy of 0.910 +/- 0.077 over 1000 random splits and 0.896 under process-fold generalization, outperforming strong baselines.

</details>


### [282] [NPMixer: Hierarchical Neighboring Patch Mixing for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07476)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NPMixer模型，通过可学习平稳小波变换和邻域混合块有效解决多变量时间序列预测中的局部动态和全局依赖问题，在71.4%的实验设置上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测因局部时间动态的复杂性和跨变量的全局依赖关系而具有挑战性，现有方法难以同时有效捕获这两个方面。

Method: 提出NPMixer分层架构：1) 可学习平稳小波变换自适应学习滤波系数，将信号分解为趋势和细节分量；2) 邻域混合块通过分层MLP在非重叠补丁上捕获局部时间动态；3) 通道混合编码器应用于高频分量以学习通道相关性同时保持全局趋势稳定性。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，NPMixer在28个实验设置中的20个（71.4%）上取得更好的MSE性能，持续优于最先进模型。

Conclusion: NPMixer通过数据驱动的方式自适应分解信号并分层建模局部和全局模式，为多变量时间序列预测提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting remains a challenge due to the complexity of local temporal dynamics and global dependencies across multiple variables.
  In this paper, we propose \textbf{N}eighboring \textbf{P}atching \textbf{Mixer} (\textbf{NPMixer}), a hierarchical architecture featuring a Learnable Stationary Wavelet Transform that adaptively learns filter coefficients to decompose signals into trend and detail components in a data-dependent manner.
  Our framework introduces a Neighboring Mixer Block that captures local temporal dynamics through a series of hierarchical MLP layers operating on non-overlapping patches.
  Specifically, the mixer block utilizes MLPs to learn temporal patterns within and across these patches, expanding the receptive field to capture multi-scale dependencies.
  A Channel-Mixing Encoder is applied to high-frequency components to learn channel correlations while preserving the stability of the underlying global trend.
  Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate that NPMixer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, achieving better performance in 20 out of 28 ($71.4\%$) evaluated experimental setups for MSE.

</details>


### [283] [Position: Mechanistic Interpretability Must Disclose Identification Assumptions for Causal Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08012)
*Zezheng Lin,Fengming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审计发现机制可解释性论文普遍缺乏因果识别假设的明确声明，常用验证指标替代因果支持，提出了包含五项要求的披露规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性论文越来越多地使用因果词汇（如电路、中介变量、因果抽象、单义性），但这些因果声明需要明确的识别假设。作者旨在调查现有文献是否正确声明了这些假设。

Method: 对跨越四种方法论流派的10篇论文进行目的性审计，随后在30篇论文上进行双人编码审计，检查是否存在专门的识别假设部分以及验证指标被用作因果支持的模式。

Result: 审计发现：1）没有论文包含专门的识别假设部分；2）普遍存在将保真度、完整性、单义性、对齐或消融效应等验证指标作为因果支持报告，而未陈述使其具有识别性的假设。

Conclusion: 验证不等于识别。作者提出披露规范：声明主张是否为因果性的、指明识别策略、列举假设、强调至少一个假设，并解释假设失效时结论如何变化。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability papers increasingly use causal vocabulary: circuits, mediators, causal abstraction, monosemanticity. Such claims require explicit identification assumptions. A purposive audit of 10 papers across four methodological strands finds no dedicated identification-assumptions section and a recurring pattern: validation metrics such as faithfulness, completeness, monosemanticity, alignment, or ablation effects are reported as causal support without stating the assumptions that make them identifying. A two-human-coder audit on $n=30$ reproduces the direction of the main finding: dedicated identification sections are absent, and validation-metric substitution is common, though exact Dim B/D counts are coding-rule sensitive. The paper proposes a disclosure norm: state whether the claim is causal, name the identification strategy, enumerate assumptions, stress at least one, and explain how conclusions shift if assumptions fail. Validation is not identification.

</details>


### [284] [Beyond Pairs: Your Language Model is Secretly Optimizing a Preference Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08037)
*Ning Liu,Chuanneng Sun,Kristina Klinkner,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GraphDPO，将 DPO 从成对偏好扩展到有向无环偏好图结构，通过利用传递性和等价类划分有效聚合多响应监督信号，在推理和程序合成任务上表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中每个提示常对应多个响应并形成丰富偏好结构，但传统成对 DPO 将其坍缩为独立样本对，忽略了传递性，引入冗余或冲突监督，导致优化不稳定。

Method: 将响应排序建模为有向无环偏好图，边表示支配关系；优化基于 Plackett–Luce 启发的图结构目标，在图邻域聚合监督并强化传递性。引入等价类构建使同层等价响应间损失为零以避免伪梯度；通过高效 log-sum-exp 聚合保持线性复杂度。可选地引入真值锚点与退火调度以稳定早期训练并逐步放松先验监督。

Result: 在推理与程序合成任务上的实验表明，GraphDPO 性能优于成对及列表式对齐目标，验证了图结构偏好建模的可扩展性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GraphDPO 是 DPO 的有原则推广，有效利用多响应偏好结构与传递性，同时保持高效性，为语言模型对齐提供可扩展、鲁棒的新范式。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) aligns language models using pairwise preference comparisons, offering a simple and effective alternative to Reinforcement Learning (RL) from human feedback. However, in many practical settings, training data consists of multiple rollouts per prompt, inducing rich preference structure that pairwise DPO fails to exploit. Collapsing such data into independent pairs discards transitivity, introduces redundant or conflicting supervision, and can lead to unstable optimization. We propose Graph Direct Preference Optimization (GraphDPO), a principled generalization of DPO that operates over directed acyclic preference graphs induced by rollout rankings. GraphDPO encodes dominance relations as edges and optimizes a graph-structured Plackett--Luce-inspired objective that aggregates supervision over graph neighborhoods, enforcing transitivity while recovering standard DPO as a special case. To handle discrete or sparse signals, we introduce an equivalence-class construction where responses with identical preferences form graph layers, and intra-layer edges contribute zero loss, preventing spurious gradients. Despite leveraging full graph structure, GraphDPO maintains linear per-prompt complexity via efficient log-sum-exp aggregation. We further incorporate optional ground-truth anchoring by inserting verified solutions as dominant nodes and applying an annealed schedule that stabilizes early training while gradually relaxing oracle supervision. Experiments on reasoning and program synthesis tasks demonstrate superior performance, suggesting that graph-structured preference modeling is a scalable and robust alternative to pairwise and listwise alignment objectives.

</details>


### [285] [ExpThink: Experience-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Chain-of-Thought Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07501)
*Tingcheng Bian,Yuzhe Zhang,Jing Jin,Jinchang Luo,MingQuan Cheng,Haiwei Wang,Wenyuan Jiang,Miaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ExpThink框架，通过经验引导的奖励塑形和难度自适应优势两种机制，在多个数学推理基准上将平均响应长度减少高达77%，同时提高准确率，准确率-效率比达到基线的3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型(LRMs)通过扩展思维链(CoT)推理获得强性能，但存在token消耗过多和推理延迟高的问题。现有基于强化学习的CoT压缩方法依赖统一、静态的长度惩罚，忽略了模型能力动态和问题级难度变化。

Method: 提出ExpThink框架，包含两种互补机制：1）经验引导的奖励塑形，跟踪每个问题目前找到的最短正确解，应用三层奖励（简洁正确给满分，冗长正确给折扣分，错误给零分），阈值随模型改进自动收紧形成自进化课程；2）难度自适应优势，用正确计数归一化替代标准差归一化，产生单调难度缩放的梯度，放大难问题学习以保持准确率，抑制易问题梯度以鼓励简洁。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上，ExpThink将平均响应长度减少高达77%，同时提高准确率；准确率-效率比（准确率除以平均token数）比原始基线高3倍，在两个指标上都优于现有基于RL的压缩方法。

Conclusion: ExpThink通过自进化课程和难度自适应梯度缩放，成功实现了准确率优先、压缩其次的训练目标，证明了动态适应模型能力和问题难度的强化学习方法在CoT压缩任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) achieve strong performance via extended chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, yet suffer from excessive token consumption and high inference latency. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) approaches for CoT compression rely on uniform, static length penalties that neglect model capability dynamics and problem-level difficulty variation. We propose \textbf{ExpThink}\xspace, an RL framework that addresses both dimensions through two complementary mechanisms. First, \emph{experience-guided reward shaping} tracks the shortest correct solution found so far for each problem and applies a three-tier reward: full credit for concise correct responses, discounted credit for verbose correct ones, and zero for incorrect ones. The threshold tightens automatically with model improvement, forming a self-evolving curriculum that requires no manual scheduling. Second, \emph{difficulty-adaptive advantage} replaces standard deviation normalization with correct-count normalization, yielding monotonically difficulty-scaled gradients that amplify learning on hard problems to preserve accuracy while suppressing gradients on easy ones to encourage brevity. Together, these mechanisms enforce an accuracy-first, compression-second training objective. Experiments on multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that \textbf{ExpThink}\xspace reduces average response length by up to 77\% while simultaneously improving accuracy, achieving up to $3\times$ higher accuracy-efficiency ratio (accuracy divided by average token count) than the vanilla baseline and outperforming existing RL-based compression methods on both metrics.

</details>


### [286] [Tessellations of Semi-Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07513)
*Emile Pierret,Johannes Hertrich,Samuel Hurault,Julie Delon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在半离散设定下研究了流匹配，发现目标点的终端分配区域是开且单连通的，但可能与最优传输中的拉盖尔胞不同，表现为非凸、弯曲边界等特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半离散设定是流匹配用于生成建模的理论基础，其中目标分布由有限数据集表示。然而，现有研究缺乏对该设定下流匹配诱导几何结构的理论理解。作者旨在独立于优化和近似效应，分析精确半离散流匹配速度场诱导的终端流映射的几何性质。

Method: 利用半离散设定下精确流匹配速度场具有闭式解的优势，研究终端分配区域（即终端流下目标原子的原像）的几何性质。通过理论证明分析这些区域的拓扑特性，并构造平面四点反例对比分析其与半离散最优传输中拉盖尔胞的差异。

Result: 证明了终端分配区域是开集且单连通的；在附加假设下，这些区域同胚于单位球。同时，通过平面四点反例揭示了这些胞与拉盖尔胞存在显著差异：它们可能是非凸的、具有弯曲边界，并表现出不同的有界性和邻接模式。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了在神经网络近似介入之前，精确半离散流匹配目标内在诱导的几何结构，为理解生成建模中流匹配的理论性质提供了重要见解。

Abstract: We study Flow Matching in a semi-discrete setting where a Gaussian source is transported toward a discrete target supported on finitely many points. This semi-discrete regime is the theoretical setting behind the use of Flow Matching for generative modeling, where the target distribution is represented by a finite dataset. In this semi-discrete regime, the exact Flow Matching velocity field is available in closed form, which makes it possible to analyze the geometry induced by the terminal flow map independently of optimization and approximation effects. We investigate the terminal assignment regions, namely the preimages of the target atoms under the terminal flow. We show that these regions are open, simply connected and, under an additional assumption, homeomorphic to the unit ball. At the same time, a planar four-point example shows that these cells can differ sharply from Laguerre cells arising in semi-discrete optimal transport: they may be non-convex, have curved boundaries, and exhibit different boundedness and adjacency patterns. These results clarify the geometry intrinsically induced by the exact semi-discrete Flow Matching objective before neural approximation enters the picture.

</details>


### [287] [SGD for Variational Inference: Tackling Unbounded Variance via Preconditioning and Dynamic Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07531)
*Hippolyte Labarrière,Cesare Molinari,Silvia Villa,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对黑盒变分推断（BBVI）中随机梯度方差无界的问题，在椭圆位置-尺度分布族下，证明了ELBO解的存在性，并为动态批处理和预条件的小批量投影SGD提供了完整的收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BBVI方法依赖SGD优化ELBO，但其随机梯度具有无界方差，违反了标准收敛假设。虽然实际中满足较弱的Blum-Gladyshev（BG）条件（方差随距最优点的距离二次增长），但理论与实际应用之间存在差距，缺乏针对该条件的系统性收敛保证。

Method: 1. 聚焦椭圆位置-尺度参数化分布族；
2. 首先证明ELBO解的存在性（文献中通常作为先验假设）；
3. 在BG条件下，为配备动态批处理和预条件技术的Minibatch投影SGD（PSGD）建立理论框架，推导有限时间和渐近收敛保证。

Result: 1. 证明了ELBO解的存在性，填补了理论基础空白；
2. 建立了涵盖有限时间和渐近区域的完整收敛保证；
3. 理论框架表明动态批处理结合预条件技术能在复杂设置下系统性地提供严格保证；
4. 数值实验验证了方法在现代推断任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功弥合了随机优化理论与BBVI实际应用之间的差距，证明在BG条件下，通过动态批处理和预条件技术的组合，可以为BBVI提供系统性的理论保证，为现代推断任务奠定了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Black-Box Variational Inference (BBVI) typically relies on Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) to optimize the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO). However, the stochastic gradients in BBVI inherently exhibit unbounded variance, violating standard assumptions and instead satisfying the weaker Blum-Gladyshev (BG) condition, where variance grows quadratically with distance from the optimum. In this paper, we bridge the gap between stochastic optimization theory and the practical instances of BBVI. Focusing on the broad elliptic location-scale family of parameterized distributions, we offer two main contributions. First, we prove the existence of an ELBO solution, a foundational property usually assumed a priori in the literature. Second, we establish comprehensive convergence guarantees spanning finite-time and asymptotic regimes for Minibatch Projected SGD (PSGD) equipped with dynamic batching and preconditioning under the BG condition. Our theoretical framework demonstrates that dynamic batching combined with preconditioning systematically enables rigorous guarantees even in complex settings. We illustrate our theoretical findings with numerical results, highlighting the efficacy of our approach for modern inference tasks.

</details>


### [288] [On the Invariance and Generality of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07546)
*Xing Han,Ziyin Liu,Suchi Saria,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可泛化的神经扩展定律，通过信息分辨率ρ这一单一轴，实现了扩展定律在不同领域间的迁移，在跨领域预测中将数据扩展指数的误差控制在3%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经扩展定律虽然能为资源分配提供关键指导，但为新模型-任务对拟合扩展定律需要昂贵的计算开销，而这恰恰违背了扩展定律旨在节约计算资源的初衷。本文旨在解决如何将在资源充足的源领域拟合的扩展定律可靠地迁移到无法进行完整计算扫描的新领域这一根本问题。

Method: 识别出了正确的恒定性质：扩展定律在数据的双射（信息保持）变换下保持不变，而在非双射变换下会以信息论为基础的可预测方式发生变化，这些变换会降低数据的信息分辨率ρ。信息分辨率ρ成为了将一个领域拟合的定律迁移到另一个领域的单一轴心。

Result: 在语言、视觉和语音三个模态上验证了该方法的有效性，并展示了两个跨领域应用：1）根据通用文本上拟合的定律预测电子健康记录上训练的语言模型的扩展性能；2）预测不同噪声注入水平下时间序列分类的扩展性能，成功将数据扩展指数的预测误差控制在3%以内。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了对扩展特性何时以及为何变化的基础性理解，证明了通过信息分辨率ρ这一信息论基础的概念，可以实现扩展定律的跨领域迁移，为在计算资源受限的新领域进行性能预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws establish a predictable relationship between model performance and data or compute, offering crucial guidance for resource allocation in new domains and tasks. Yet such laws are most needed precisely where they are hardest to obtain: fitting one for a new model task pair demands expensive sweeps that typically exhaust the very compute budget the law is meant to economize. This paper poses the research question of how to develop generalizable scaling laws: laws fit once on a well-resourced source domain and reliably transported to new domains where running a full sweep is infeasible, which requires a fundamental understanding of when and why scaling properties change. We address this by identifying the right invariants: scaling laws are preserved under bijective (information-preserving) transformations of the data and modified in predictable, information-theoretically grounded ways under non-bijective transformations that lower its information resolution $ρ$: a single axis along which a law fit in one domain can be transported to another. We validate this across language, vision, and speech, and demonstrate two cross-domain applications: predicting scaling for language models trained on electronic health records from laws fit on general text, and predicting time-series classification scaling under varying levels of noise injection, recovering the data-scaling exponents to within $3\%$ error.

</details>


### [289] [Disagreement-Regularized Importance Sampling for Adversarial Label Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07551)
*Csongor Horváth,Ida-Maria Sintorn,Prashant Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对标准重要性采样（IS）在标签损坏下失效的问题，提出基于代理集成分歧的正则化重要性采样方法（DR-IS），在理论保证和实验验证上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准重要性采样（IS）倾向于优先选择高范数样本以降低方差，但在标签损坏场景下，这些高范数样本往往是敌对异常值，导致采样失效。作者旨在解决这种采样优先级与样本质量之间的错配问题。

Method: 1. 使用ε-污染模型形式化描述上述错配问题。\n2. 提出基于分歧的正则化重要性采样（DR-IS）。该方法利用独立代理集成的损失排序差异进行子采样。\n3. 理论上推导了有限样本浓度界，证明了“大量”损坏样本与“边界”干净样本的经验排序分歧之间存在严格分离。

Result: 1. 理论上证明了经验排序分歧以速率$O(\\sqrt{\\log(N/δ)/K})$收敛，并在一定条件下验证了选定子集的污染率受控。\n2. 实验表明，在面对针对高范数样本的攻击时，DR-IS比基于幅度的方法（如EL2N）更具鲁棒性。\n3. DR-IS与训练动力学方法（如AUM）互补，在损失对齐机制下提供了更优的鲁棒性及明确的有限样本浓度保证。

Conclusion: DR-IS为标签损坏下的数据筛选提供了一种鲁棒且理论依据充分的新方法，它不仅有效抵抗针对高范数的攻击，弥补了幅度类方法的缺陷，还为后续研究提供了明确的统计保证。

Abstract: Standard Importance Sampling (IS) collapses under label corruption because high-norm examples, prioritized for variance reduction, are often adversarial outliers. We formalize this misalignment using an $\varepsilon$-contamination model and propose Disagreement-Regularized Importance Sampling (DR-IS), a sub-sampling method based on loss rank-disagreement across independent proxy ensemble. We prove finite-sample concentration bounds showing that the empirical rank disagreement of bulk corrupted examples is bounded above, and that of boundary-clean examples bounded below, both at rate $O(\sqrt{\log(N/δ)/K})$ with probability $1-δ$; when the structural expectation gap $Δ'$ between the two groups is positive and the boundary-clean set is at least as large as the selected subset, these bounds certify strict separation and control the contamination rate of the selected subset. Empirically, DR-IS remains robust under targeted high-norm attacks that break magnitude-based methods such as the Error $L_2$-norm (EL2N) on benchmark datasets. DR-IS complements training-dynamics approaches like Area Under the Margin ranking (AUM), offering improved robustness in the loss-aligned regime alongside explicit finite-sample concentration certificates and a contamination bound limiting noise leakage from the statistical tail of corrupted points.

</details>


### [290] [Beyond Distribution Estimation: Simplex Anchored Structural Inference Towards Universal Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07557)
*Yaxin Hou,Jun Ma,Hanyang Li,Bo Han,Jie Yu,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了通用半监督学习（UniSSL）框架，针对标签数据稀缺且无标签数据分布未知的现实场景，提出了SAGE方法，通过表征级别的结构推断和单纯形等角紧框架引导，在五个基准测试上平均准确率提升8.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督学习方法依赖于理想化的均匀无标签数据分布假设或需要足够的标签数据来估计分布，但在标签数据稀缺且无标签数据分布未知的现实场景中，这些依赖会导致大量错误的伪标签，从而引发表征混淆。因此，需要研究通用半监督学习这一关键但未被充分探索的范式。

Method: 提出Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition (SAGE)方法：1）利用表征捕获的样本间关系比伪标签更可靠这一洞察，转向表征级别的结构推断以规避分布估计；2）捕获高阶样本间依赖关系建立结构共识来指导表征学习；3）使用满足单纯形等角紧框架的向量作为坐标系引导类间表征分离以缓解表征混淆；4）引入基于分布无关度量的一致加权策略优先处理可靠伪标签，并使用辅助分支隔离潜在错误伪标签。

Result: 在五个标准基准测试上的评估表明，SAGE方法始终优于最先进方法，平均准确率提升达8.52%。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了通用半监督学习中因未知任意分布导致的伪标签错误和表征混淆问题，证明了表征级别的结构推断比传统的伪标签方法更可靠，为现实场景中的半监督学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning faces significant challenges in realistic scenarios where labeled data is scarce and unlabeled data follows unknown, arbitrary distributions. We formalize this critical yet under-explored paradigm as Universal Semi-supervised Learning (UniSSL). Existing methods typically leverage unlabeled data via pseudo-labeling. However, they often rely on the idealized assumption of a uniform unlabeled data distribution or require sufficient labeled data to estimate it. In the UniSSL setting, such dependencies lead to numerous erroneous pseudo-labels, thereby triggering representation confusion. Fortunately, we observe that inter-sample relations captured by representations are more reliable than pseudo-labels. Leveraging this insight, we shift our focus to representation-level structural inference to bypass distribution estimation. Accordingly, we propose Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition (SAGE), which captures high-order inter-sample dependencies to establish structural consensus for guiding representation learning. Meanwhile, to mitigate representation confusion, we employ vectors that satisfy a simplex equiangular tight frame to serve as a coordinate frame for guiding inter-class representation separation. Finally, we introduce a weighting strategy based on distribution-agnostic metrics to prioritize reliable pseudo-labels and an auxiliary branch to isolate potentially erroneous pseudo-labels. Evaluations on five standard benchmarks show that SAGE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with an average accuracy gain of \textbf{8.52\%}.

</details>


### [291] [Bilevel Graph Structure Learning, Revisited: Inner-Channel Origins of the Reported Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07577)
*Minkyoung Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现双层图结构学习的性能增益主要源于内层训练动态而非图重构本身，提出了frozen-φ控制方法来分解增益来源，并提出三预条件框架预测增益符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为双层图结构学习通过联合优化模型参数和学习图结构来提升GNN性能，并将增益归因于重构的邻接矩阵。作者质疑这种归因可能被高估，旨在揭示内层训练动态而非图重构本身才是增益的主要来源。

Method: 提出frozen-φ控制方法：冻结图结构但保留内层训练调度，将双层增益分解为内层通道（T步训练动态+隐式梯度正则化）和图通道（图重构本身）。此外提出三预条件框架预测增益符号，以及图蒸馏作为方法无关的补充。

Result: 在时空流预测任务中，内层通道达到或超过完整双层管道性能，贡献78-101%的增益；在节点分类任务中，在伯努利边级参数化下贡献37-44%的增益。验证了经典谱诊断可能与任务增益解耦。三预条件框架在全部六个基准上成功预测了双层增益的符号。

Conclusion: 双层图结构学习的增益主要来自内层训练动态效应而非图重构本身，现有归因被高估。建议将frozen-φ作为双层图结构学习的标准化诊断工具，配合图蒸馏使用。三预条件框架为理解和预测双层方法有效性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Bilevel graph structure learning is widely understood to improve graph neural networks by jointly optimizing model parameters and a learned graph structure, with the resulting performance gain attributed to the rewired adjacency. We find that this attribution may be overstated: training-dynamics effects in the inner loop, rather than the rewiring itself, capture a substantial share of the gain. To establish this, we introduce frozen-$φ$, a control that freezes the graph while retaining the inner-loop training schedule. This decomposes the bilevel gain into an inner channel of $T$-step training dynamics with implicit gradient regularization and a graph channel of the graph rewiring itself. On spatio-temporal flow forecasting the inner channel matches or exceeds the full bilevel pipeline, accounting for 78-101% of the gain; on node classification it accounts for 37-44% under a Bernoulli edge-level parameterization. We also verify that classical spectral diagnostics can dissociate from task gain. We propose frozen-$φ$ as a standardized diagnostic for bilevel graph structure learning, with graph distillation as a method-agnostic complement. A three-precondition framework further predicts the sign of the bilevel gain on all six benchmarks.

</details>


### [292] [Optimal Recourse Summaries via Bi-Objective Decision Tree Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07598)
*Ioannis Chatzis,Jason Liartis,Athanasios Voulodimos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SOGAR方法，将可操作追索摘要学习转化为最优决策树学习问题，通过寻找帕累托前沿有效解决了追索有效性与成本之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个体级行动追索方案难以扩展至全局审计和偏差检测，因为聚合局部行动成本高昂且往往不一致。虽然追索摘要通过为子群体分配共享行动解决了此问题，但现有方法未能妥善处理追索有效性与成本之间的基本权衡关系。

Method: 提出SOGAR（最优全局可操作追索摘要）方法，将追索摘要学习建模为最优决策树学习问题。该方法寻找帕累托前沿，即在两个目标之间权衡的完整解集，使得改进一个目标必然恶化另一个目标，从而无需重新训练即可事后选择所需的权衡。采用浅层轴平行决策树和稀疏叶节点行动来构建模型。

Result: SOGAR生成的追索摘要具有稳定性、低成本和高效性，在有效性和成本指标上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将追索摘要问题转化为决策树优化问题并探索帕累托前沿，SOGAR成功解决了追索有效性与成本之间的核心权衡，为机器学习模型的全局审计和偏差检测提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Actionable Recourse provides individuals with actions they can take to change an unfavorable classifier outcome. While useful at the instance level, it is ill-suited for global auditing and bias detection, since aggregating local actions is costly and often inconsistent. Recourse Summaries address this limitation by partitioning the population and assigning one shared action per subgroup, enabling comparison across subgroups. Designing summaries involves a fundamental trade-off between recourse effectiveness and recourse cost, which existing methods do not adequately address. We introduce Summaries of Optimal and Global Actionable Recourse (SOGAR), which formulates recourse summary learning as an optimal decision tree learning problem and finds the Pareto front -- the complete set of solutions where improving one objective necessarily worsens the other. SOGAR enables post-hoc selection of the desired trade-off without retraining. Using shallow axis-parallel decision trees and sparse leaf actions, SOGAR produces stable, low-cost, and effective recourse summaries that outperform existing approaches across effectiveness and cost metrics.

</details>


### [293] [Learning Large-Scale Modular Addition with an Auxiliary Modulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07648)
*Hanato Kikuchi,Ryosuke Masuya,Kazuhiko Kawamoto,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无协变量偏移的模加法学习方法，通过引入辅助模数$Kq$在保持训练测试分布一致的同时降低问题难度，在大规模、小样本场景下显著优于现有的稀疏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习奇偶函数和模加法是机器学习中的挑战性任务。现有方法通过在训练序列中增加零来控制训练难度，但这导致了训练和测试输入分布之间的协变量偏移，影响了模型性能。

Method: 提出了一种无协变量偏移的方法：在训练过程中引入辅助模数$Kq$。该方法通过减少回绕频率和问题难度来简化学习任务，同时保持训练和测试阶段的输入分布一致，避免了协变量偏移的副作用。

Result: 实验表明该方法具有强可扩展性和样本效率。在$N=64$和$q=974269$的设置下，使用100K样本训练即可达到$97.0\%$的$τ$-准确率（$τ=0.05$），而稀疏方法在相同数据规模下仅达到$9.5\%$，即使扩展到1M样本也只能达到$93.9\%$。

Conclusion: 所提出的无协变量偏移方法成功解决了现有稀疏方法在大输入长度、大模数和小数据集场景下的学习失败问题，为模加法学习提供了一种更有效、更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning parity functions, more general modular addition, is a challenging machine learning task due to its input sensitivity. A recent study substantially scaled modular addition learning in both the number of summands and the modulus. Its key idea is to increase zeros in training sequences, reducing the effective number of summands and thus controlling training difficulty; however, this induces covariate shift between training and test input distributions. This study theoretically and empirically analyzes this side effect and proposes a covariate-shift-free method for modular addition. Specifically, we introduce an auxiliary modulus $Kq$ during training, which reduces wrap-around frequency and problem difficulty while preserving the same input distribution across training and testing. Experiments show strong scalability and sample efficiency: even for large input length $N$, large modulus $q$, and small datasets -- where the sparse method fails to learn -- our method achieves equal or better match accuracy and relaxed $τ$-accuracy. For example, at $N=64$ and $q=974269$, our method trained on 100K samples achieves $97.0\%$ $τ$-accuracy at $τ=0.05$, while the sparse method achieves only $9.5\%$ with the same data size and $93.9\%$ even when extended to 1M samples.

</details>


### [294] [Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation for Fast Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07661)
*George Rapakoulias,Peter Garud,Lingjiong Zhu,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了STMD（随机转移映射蒸馏），一种无需教师的扩散模型加速框架，通过蒸馏完整的SDE转移映射，实现一步或少步随机采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然生成质量高、多样性强且分布覆盖好，但推理成本昂贵。现有方法难以在加速的同时保持采样的随机性和概率特性，而这对于下游任务如后验采样、逆问题和基于能量的微调至关重要。

Method: 提出STMD框架，不同于传统分数扩散模型对后验分布均值的参数化，该方法直接对采样随机微分方程（SDE）的完整转移映射进行蒸馏。采用条件Mean Flow模型参数化这些SDE转移，获得保留扩散过程转移结构的一步或少步随机采样器。无需预训练教师、双层优化或轨迹模拟与缓存。

Result: 在Wasserstein距离下推导出收敛界限，为方法提供坚实的理论基础。在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CelebA数据集上验证了STMD的有效性，实现了高效可扩展的训练。

Conclusion: STMD为扩散模型加速提供了一种新的视角，在保持随机性和概率特性的同时显著减少推理步数，特别适用于需要随机推理的下游任务，且具有理论保证和高效训练优势。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve strong generation quality, diversity, and distribution coverage, but their performance often comes with expensive inference. In this work, we propose Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation (STMD), a teacher-free framework for accelerating diffusion model inference while preserving probabilistic sample generation. In contrast to score-based diffusion models, whose denoising parametrization models the mean of the posterior distribution, STMD distills the full transition map associated with the sampling stochastic differential equation (SDE). We parameterize these SDE transitions with a conditional Mean Flow model, yielding a one- or few-step stochastic sampler that retains the transition structure of the underlying diffusion process. This perspective is especially useful for downstream tasks that require stochastic inference, such as diffusion posterior sampling, inverse problems, and energy-based fine-tuning. Compared to recent distillation methods, STMD requires no pretrained teacher, bi-level optimization, or trajectory simulation and caching, enabling efficient and scalable training. We derive convergence bounds for our method in the Wasserstein distance, providing a strong theoretical foundation for our approach, and validate STMD on various image generation examples on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CelebA datasets.

</details>


### [295] [Direction-Preserving Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07662)
*Bardia Zadeh,George A. Constantinides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度分析了低精度数格式对向量方向的表示能力，证明了标准格式（二进制补码、定点、浮点）在方向覆盖上存在可量化的次优性，并通过数值优化找到了更优的标量字母表，为NVIDIA NVFP4格式的优异性能提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习系统广泛使用低精度数格式以提高效率，而向量运算的准确性关键在于方向的精确表示。然而，现有标准格式（如二进制补码、定点、浮点）在方向表示上的性能极限和优化空间尚不明确，缺乏理论指导。本文旨在建立几何分析框架，量化现有格式的次优性，并探索更优的标量字母表设计。

Method: 1. 建立几何分析框架，研究有限字母表中选取标量元素对向量方向的覆盖能力。
2. 解析量化乘积结构码与球面码在低维和渐近高维下的次优性差距。
3. 证明二进制补码、定点和浮点等标准格式在乘积码类中的次优性，并给出可量化的差距。
4. 数值优化多种块维度下的标量字母表，优化目标为方向覆盖能力。
5. 通过实验对比标准格式与优化字母表的性能表现。

Result: 1. 给出了乘积结构码与球面码在方向表示上的次优性差距的解析表达式（适用于低维和高维场景）。
2. 证明了标准数格式（二进制补码、定点、浮点）在乘积码类中是次优的，并量化了差距。
3. 优化得到了多种块维度下的标量字母表，包括NVIDIA NVFP4格式使用的维度。
4. 实验发现：在4位精度下，NVIDIA的E2M1格式与优化字母表高度接近，这从几何角度解释了其在低精度机器学习任务中的优异性能。
5. 建立了E2M1优越性与块大小之间的解析联系。

Conclusion: 本文从几何角度为低精度数格式的方向表示能力提供了系统的理论分析，揭示了现有标准格式的次优性，指出了设计新型标量数格式的潜力。研究结果不仅解释了NVIDIA NVFP4格式（E2M1）的成功原因，还为未来低精度格式的优化设计提供了理论依据。所有定理均提供了Lean形式化证明和开源代码，增强了结论的可信度和可复现性。

Abstract: Low-precision number formats are widely used in modern machine learning systems due to their efficiency. Accurate direction representation is key to the accuracy of vector operations. This work precisely explores the extent to which the direction of a vector can be represented by selecting its scalar elements from a common finite alphabet of a given size. This is standard practice in machine learning, where low-precision significands may be narrow-width floating-point or integer values. A geometric framework is introduced for analyzing the directional coverage of such product-structured codes.
  This work analytically quantifies the suboptimality gap between such product-structured codes and spherical codes for the vector as a whole, in both low and asymptotically high dimensions. Furthermore, within the product code class, it is proven that the standard formats of two's complement, fixed-point, and floating-point are suboptimal, again with quantified gap, pointing to the potential to develop new scalar number formats.
  Such scalar alphabets are numerically optimized across multiple block dimensions for directional coverage, including the dimension used in NVIDIA's NVFP4 format. Experimental results are presented comparing the performance of standard formats and the optimized alphabet. We find that for four bits, NVIDIA's choice of E2M1 closely approximates the optimized alphabet, providing a geometric explanation for its strong performance in low-precision machine learning workloads and an analytical understanding of the link between that superiority and block size.
  We provide open-source formal proofs in Lean for the theorems in this work, along with the experimental code and the optimized alphabets obtained.

</details>


### [296] [Structured Coupling for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07676)
*Xavier Sumba,Carles Balsells-Rodas,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化耦合流匹配方法（SCFM），通过将流匹配与结构化潜在表示学习相结合，在保持生成质量的同时学习可解释的潜在结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配方法虽然扩展性良好，但依赖非结构化的源分布，限制了其学习可解释潜在结构的能力；而潜在变量模型虽能捕获结构，却往往牺牲生成质量。这两种方法之间存在能力差距。

Method: 提出结构化耦合流匹配（SCFM）框架，通过在源分布中引入结构化潜在变量和外生噪声，联合学习结构化先验（通过潜在变量建模）和连续传输映射（通过流匹配）。该方法使用共享的时间依赖识别网络，同时服务于潜在变量模型的变分推理和中间时刻流速度估计，实现了结构化信息驱动的无条件、免模拟流模型。

Result: SCFM在无监督潜在表示学习任务（聚类、解耦和下游任务）上表现良好，同时在样本质量上与流匹配方法保持竞争力，证明了可以在不牺牲生成保真度的前提下学习有意义的结构。

Conclusion: 该研究成功弥合了流匹配与潜在变量模型之间的差距，展示了通过结构化耦合可以在保持生成质量的同时实现有意义的潜在结构学习，为无监督表示学习和生成建模提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard flow matching scales well but typically relies on an unstructured source distribution, limiting its ability to learn interpretable latent structure. Latent-variable models, by contrast, capture structure but often sacrifice generative quality. We bridge this gap by proposing Structured Coupling for Flow Matching (SCFM), a cooperative framework that augments flow matching with structured latent representation learning. By introducing structured latent variables and exogenous noise into the source, SCFM jointly learns a structured prior (via latent variable modeling) and a continuous transport map (via flow matching). It uses a shared time-dependent recognition network for both latent variable model variational inference and intermediate-time flow velocity estimation. This yields a structurally informed yet unconditional, simulation-free flow model, where the latent variable model can also assist flow sampling. Empirically, SCFM facilitates unsupervised latent representation learning for clustering, disentanglement and downstream tasks, while remaining competitive with flow matching in sample quality, showing that meaningful structure can be learned without sacrificing generative fidelity.

</details>


### [297] [The Coupling Tax: How Shared Token Budgets Undermine Visible Chain-of-Thought Under Fixed Output Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07686)
*Wenhua Nie,Junlin Liu,Jianan Wu,Zijie Meng,Yilong Fan,Zhang Zijian,Haoran Zheng,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 思维链推理并非总是有益。本文发现“耦合税”现象：推理过程与最终答案共享输出token预算时，长推理链会挤占答案空间。在多个基准测试中，非思维模式在低预算下表现更优。通过预算分解理论预测了性能交叉点，并提出分离预算生成方法，在MATH-500上达到83.6%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“思维链推理通过延长思考时间单调提升模型准确性”的普遍假设。研究者观察到当推理轨迹与最终答案共享输出token预算时，过长的推理链可能反而损害最终答案的生成，这一“耦合税”现象需要被量化和解决。

Method: 1. 在GSM8K、MATH-500和五个BIG-Bench Hard任务上，使用三种规模的Qwen3模型进行实验，对比思维模式与非思维模式在不同token预算下的表现。
2. 推导“截断-浪费分解”公式 $\mathrm{Acc}_{\mathrm{think}}(b)=α_c F_L(b)+α_t(1-F_L(b))$，从链长分布和准确率统计预测性能交叉点。
3. 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B模型上复现实验以验证普适性。
4. 提出“分离预算生成”缓解策略，解耦推理链与答案的token预算，并设计IRIS方法及其变体（加强提取、SC+IRIS门控机制）。

Result: 1. 在GSM8K和MATH-500上，非思维模式在2048 token以内的所有预算中匹配或超越思维模式；更难的任务将性能交叉点推向更大预算。
2. 推导的分解公式成功预测了交叉点，并解释了Qwen模型家族中的逆缩放现象。
3. DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B复现了相同模式。
4. 缓解方法效果显著：在完整MATH-500上，IRIS达到74.0%准确率，加强提取变体达78.8%，固定非预言机SC+IRIS门控达83.6%。

Conclusion: 测试时推理应被评估为预算分配问题，而不仅仅是更长推理链是否可用的问题。分离预算生成是缓解耦合税的有效方法，能够显著提升模型在受限token预算下的表现。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning is often treated as a monotone way to improve language-model accuracy by letting a model think longer. We identify a countervailing effect, the coupling tax: when reasoning traces and final answers share one output-token budget, long traces can crowd out the answer they are meant to support. Across GSM8K, MATH-500, and five BIG-Bench Hard tasks with Qwen3 models at three scales, non-thinking mode matches or outperforms thinking mode on GSM8K and MATH-500 at every budget up to 2048 tokens, while harder tasks shift the crossover to larger budgets. We derive a truncation-waste decomposition, $\mathrm{Acc}_{\mathrm{think}}(b)=α_c F_L(b)+α_t(1-F_L(b))$, that predicts this crossover from chain-length and accuracy statistics and explains inverse scaling within the Qwen family. A DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B replication shows the same pattern under a different thinking interface. As a mitigation, split-budget generation decouples reasoning and answer budgets; on full MATH-500, IRIS reaches 74.0% accuracy, a strengthened extraction variant reaches 78.8%, and a fixed non-oracle SC+IRIS gate reaches 83.6%. The results show that test-time reasoning should be evaluated as a budget-allocation problem, not only as a question of whether longer traces are available.

</details>


### [298] [Gradient Starvation in Binary-Reward GRPO: Why Group-Mean Centering Fails and Why the Simplest Fix Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07689)
*Wenhua Nie,Jianan Wu,Junlin Liu,Ziwei Li,Zheng Lin,Zhang Zijian,Yilong Fan,Haoran Zheng,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO算法在二元奖励下存在梯度饥饿问题导致高退化率，提出的Sign优势函数通过pass@$G$下降机制，在GSM8K上相比标准方法提升45.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的GRPO算法使用组均值中心化的优势函数，在二元奖励场景下存在严重缺陷：当组内所有响应都正确或都错误时，中心化优势为零，导致策略无法获得学习信号（梯度饥饿）。作者发现实际退化率高于独立同分布伯努利预测，在Qwen3.5-9B GSM8K训练中退化率达0.69。

Method: 提出固定参考的Sign优势函数$A=2r-1$，替代原有的组均值中心化方法。该优势函数执行pass@$G$失败下降，即增加组内至少一个样本成功的概率。作者通过Jensen不等式证明了真实退化率总是超过i.i.d.伯努利预测，并在GSM8K测试集上使用七种随机种子进行对比实验，组大小设为四。

Result: 在完整GSM8K测试集上，Sign方法达到73.8%准确率，而标准归一化组均值DrGRPO仅为28.4%，提升45.4个百分点（p<0.0001）。在Llama-3.1-8B上效果方向一致，在MATH-500迁移测试上正向但统计功效不足。Pass@$k$分析表明主要收益来自搜索压缩而非大容量扩展。

Conclusion: Sign优势函数有效解决了GRPO在二元奖励下的梯度饥饿问题，显著提升了性能。该方法的理论机制与近期RLVR上限观察相吻合，主要通过搜索压缩实现增益。研究为可验证奖励强化学习提供了重要的算法改进方向。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a standard algorithm for reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards, but its group-mean-centered advantage can fail under binary rewards. The failure mode is gradient starvation: when every response in a group is correct or every response is wrong, the centered advantage is exactly zero and the policy receives no learning signal. We prove that the true degeneracy rate always exceeds the i.i.d. Bernoulli prediction by Jensen's inequality, and observe a 0.69 degeneracy rate at group size four in logged Qwen3.5-9B GSM8K training. We then show that the fixed-reference Sign advantage, $A=2r-1$, performs pass@$G$ failure descent by increasing the probability that at least one sample in the group succeeds. On the full GSM8K test set across seven seeds, Sign reaches 73.8% accuracy versus 28.4% for standard normalized group-mean DrGRPO at group size four, a 45.4 point gain with $p<0.0001$. The effect is directionally consistent on Llama-3.1-8B and positive but underpowered on a MATH-500 transfer check. Pass@$k$ analysis indicates that the main benefit is search compression rather than large capacity expansion, aligning the empirical gains with recent RLVR ceiling observations.

</details>


### [299] [Fortifying Time Series: DTW-Certified Robust Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07690)
*Shijie Liu,Tansu Alpcan,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为时间序列异常检测提出了基于动态时间规整（DTW）度量的可认证鲁棒防御方法，通过连接ℓ_p范数与DTW距离的下界变换，实现了在DTW度量下的可认证鲁棒性，实验显示在DTW对抗攻击下F1分数最高提升18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列异常检测在关键安全应用中至关重要，然而现有防御方法要么基于启发式，要么仅在ℓ_p范数约束下提供可认证鲁棒性，而ℓ_p范数无法捕捉时间序列的内在时间结构。DTW作为更适合时间序列的相似性度量，目前尚无可认证的鲁棒性结果，因此亟需开发在DTW度量下的可认证防御方法。

Method: 通过改进随机平滑范式，首次为时间序列异常检测开发了DTW可认证鲁棒防御。核心技术是通过下界变换将ℓ_p范数桥接到DTW距离，从而建立DTW度量下的可认证鲁棒性保证。该方法在理论框架内实现了对时间序列对抗操纵的防御能力。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型上进行了广泛实验验证，结果表明该方法在DTW对抗攻击下显著提升了性能。与传统可认证模型相比，F1分数最高提升达18.7%，证明了理论方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了首个基于DTW度量的可认证鲁棒防御方法，填补了时间序列异常检测领域在DTW可认证鲁棒性方面的空白，为高 stakes应用提供了更强的安全保障。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is critical for ensuring safety in high-stakes applications, where robustness is a fundamental requirement rather than a mere performance metric. Addressing the vulnerability of these systems to adversarial manipulation is therefore essential. Existing defenses are largely heuristic or provide certified robustness only under $\ell_p$-norm constraints, which are incompatible with time-series data. In particular, $\ell_p$-norm fails to capture the intrinsic temporal structure in time series, causing small temporal distortions to significantly alter the $\ell_p$-norm measures. Instead, the similarity metric \emph{Dynamic Time Warping} (DTW) is more suitable and widely adopted in the time-series domain, as DTW accounts for temporal alignment and remains robust to temporal variations. To date, however, there has been no certifiable robustness result in this metric that provides guarantees. In this work, we introduce the first \emph{DTW-certified robust defense} in time-series anomaly detection by adapting the randomized smoothing paradigm. We develop this certificate by bridging the $\ell_p$-norm to DTW distance through a lower-bound transformation. Extensive experiments across various datasets and models validate the effectiveness and practicality of our theoretical approach. Results demonstrate significantly improved performance, e.g., up to 18.7\% in F1-score under DTW-based adversarial attacks compared to traditional certified models.

</details>


### [300] [Toward Better Geometric Representations for Molecule Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07693)
*Shaoheng Yan,Zian Li,Cai Zhou,Qiaojing Huang,Kai Liu,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LENSES框架，通过三种互补机制（表示头、分子感知损失、节点级表示对齐损失）改进几何表示条件分子生成，在GEOM-DRUG数据集上达到97.28%有效性和98.51%分子稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的几何表示条件分子生成方法虽然通过解耦分子生成过程提升了效率和质量，但其有效性根本上受限于表示空间的质量。预训练的分子编码器（如UniMol）产生的表示存在非平滑问题，且在生成训练过程中未被充分利用。

Method: 提出LENSES框架，引入三种互补机制：（1）表示头：在生成任务中同时训练，从预训练编码器提取多层次表示；（2）分子感知损失：在语义信息表示空间中优化生成器；（3）节点级表示对齐（REPA）损失：显式对齐生成器隐藏状态与编码器表示，缩小预训练与生成之间的语义差距。

Result: 在GEOM-DRUG数据集上，LENSES实现97.28%有效性和98.51%分子稳定性，超越现有先进方法。Lipschitz常数降低4.6倍，QM9探测任务证明细化的表示更加平滑且信息丰富。

Conclusion: LENSES通过生成训练与对齐目标有效改进了分子表示质量，验证了该方法可作为分子编码器预训练的新范式，为表示条件分子生成提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Geometric representation-conditioned molecule generation provides an effective paradigm that decouples molecule representation modeling from structure generation. By decoupling molecule generation into two stages-first generating a meaningful molecule representation, and then generating a 3D molecule conditioned on this representation-the efficiency and quality of the generation process can be significantly enhanced. However, its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by the quality of the representation space: pretrained molecular encoders, such as UniMol, produce representations that are non-smooth and not fully exploited during the generative training process. In this work, we propose LENSEs, a framework that better exploits the potential of molecule representations in representation-conditioned generation methods. In particular, LENSEs introduces three complementary mechanisms: (1) a representation head, simultaneously trained during generative tasks, that extracts multi-level representations from the pretrained encoder; (2) a molecule perceptual loss that optimizes the generator in a semantic-informative representation space; and (3) a node-level representation alignment (REPA) loss that explicitly aligns the generator's hidden states with encoder representations, reducing the semantic gap between pretraining and generation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of these improvements through extensive molecule generation tasks. Specifically, on the challenging molecule generation dataset GEOM-DRUG, LENSEs achieves 97.28% validity and 98.51% molecule stability, surpassing existing advanced methods. Further analyses through Lipschitz constant reduction (4.6x) and QM9 probing tasks also demonstrate the smoother, more informative refined representations, establishing generative training with alignment objectives as a potential pretraining paradigm for molecular encoders.

</details>


### [301] [Future Validity is the Missing Statistic: From Impossibility to $Φ$-Estimation for Grammar-Faithful Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07698)
*Wenhua Nie,Zijie Meng,Kun Zou,Zheng Lin,Ziwei Li,Haoran Zheng,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了结合语法规格约束的推测解码实际上采样自局部投影分布而非期望的语法条件分布，并提出基于未来有效性函数的校正方法来修正这一分布偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语法约束生成方法结合局部词汇掩码和推测解码时，其采样分布并非用户期望的语法条件分布，而是局部投影分布。作者旨在揭示这一理论缺陷并提供解决方案。

Method: 识别未来有效性函数Φ作为缺失的校正统计量，提出FVO-Spec解码器：将目标分布表示为基模型的Doob变换。针对精确Φ难以计算的问题，设计了估计器层级结构，包括OneStep方法和精确动态规划方案。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B模型上的Dyck语法实验中，总变差差距可达0.996。OneStep方法以低于1%的吞吐量开销将Dyck总变差降低14%，精确动态规划降低97%，有限语言校正可将JSON差距收敛至数值精度。

Conclusion: 理论上扩展了GAD不可能性结果至推测解码场景；实践上提供了可行的校正方案，但所有保真度声明仅适用于可枚举语法和token trie结构。

Abstract: Grammar-constrained generation is often combined with local vocabulary masking and speculative decoding, but the resulting sampling law is not the grammar-conditional distribution users usually intend. We show that any speculative decoder with local mask access, Leviathan rejection, and rollback soundness samples from the locally projected distribution $μ^{\mathrm{proj}}$ rather than the grammar-conditional distribution $μ^\star$. This extends the GAD impossibility result to speculative decoding; on Dyck grammars with Qwen3-8B, the total-variation gap can reach 0.996. We identify the future-validity function $Φ_t(y)=\Pr_p[\mathrm{valid\ completion}\mid y]$ as the missing correction statistic. The target distribution is a Doob transform of the base model with $h=Φ$, while local masking corresponds to setting $h$ to one. With exact $Φ$, our oracle decoder FVO-Spec samples exactly from $μ^\star$; with approximate $Φ$, we bound the resulting total-variation error. Because exact future validity is hard for general context-free grammars, we evaluate estimator hierarchies on tractable Dyck and finite JSON languages. OneStep reduces Dyck TV by 14% with under 1% throughput overhead, exact dynamic programming reduces it by 97%, and finite-language correction closes JSON gaps to numerical precision. All fidelity claims are scoped to enumerable grammars and token tries.

</details>


### [302] [Bayesian Fine-tuning in Projected Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07706)
*Viktar Dubovik,Patryk Marszałek,Jacek Tabor,Tomasz Kuśmierczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的参数高效贝叶斯微调框架，证明了在极低维参数空间中即可实现有效的不确定性量化，在保持计算效率的同时改善了模型的校准和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的低秩自适应（LoRA）方法虽然实现了参数高效的微调，但缺乏不确定性量化机制，导致模型过度自信且校准不佳；现有的贝叶斯LoRA变体虽然解决了该问题，但显著增加了可训练参数数量，抵消了原本的效率优势，且训练难度大、收敛不稳定。

Method: 提出了一种参数高效贝叶斯微调的新框架，通过适当的权重空间投影，在极低维参数空间中对不确定性进行建模，利用权重协方差的低秩特性来实现高效的不确定性量化。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过适当的权重空间投影，不确定性可以在低维空间中被有效建模，权重协方差表现出低秩特性；该方法在保持计算效率的同时，实现了强大的性能表现，并改善了模型的校准和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在极低维参数空间中进行有效的不确定性量化是可行的，该方法成功平衡了不确定性量化能力与计算效率，为参数高效的贝叶斯微调提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large models by decomposing weight updates into low-rank matrices, significantly reducing storage and computational overhead. While effective, standard LoRA lacks mechanisms for uncertainty quantification, leading to overconfident and poorly calibrated models. Bayesian variants of LoRA address this limitation, but at the cost of a significantly increased number of trainable parameters, partially offsetting the original efficiency gains. Additionally, these models are harder to train and may suffer from unstable convergence. In this work, we propose a novel framework for parameter-efficient Bayesian fine-tuning, demonstrating that effective uncertainty quantification can be achieved in very low-dimensional parameter spaces. The proposed method achieves strong performance with improved calibration and generalization while maintaining computational efficiency. Our empirical findings show that, with the appropriate projection of the weight space uncertainty can be effectively modeled in a low-dimensional space, and weight covariances exhibit low ranks.

</details>


### [303] [Robust and Reliable AI for Predictive Quality in Semiconductor Materials Manufacturing with MLOps and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07752)
*Min Gao,Julia Maria Perathoner,Anton Ludwig Bonin,Steven Eulig,Gianni Klesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用五年制造数据对MLOps重训练策略进行基准测试，发现每5个批次进行固定重训练且无需超参数调优的策略性能最优，并结合共形预测实现了可靠的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造过程受工艺条件演变、设备老化和原材料变异影响，机器学习模型性能会随时间推移而下降，亟需确定最优的重训练策略以维持模型准确性，并引入不确定性量化以支持制造决策。

Method: 使用五年真实制造数据，评估了多种重训练频率和超参数优化策略，以控制限标准化残差作为关键性能指标；同时实施共形预测（Conformal Prediction）生成具有统计保证的预测置信区间。

Result: 结果表明，每5个生产批次进行一次固定重训练且不进行超参数重新调优的策略，在所有漂移条件下均取得最佳性能，同时显著降低了计算开销；该方法在突发工艺变化和渐进式设备老化中均能有效维持模型精度；共形预测成功实现了预测置信区间的生成。

Conclusion: 该研究为制造环境中实施稳健的MLOps策略提供了实用指南，证明了兼顾计算效率与可靠不确定性量化的重训练方案能够将传统的被动质量管理转化为预测性框架，对实现卓越运营至关重要。

Abstract: Semiconductor materials manufacturing presents unique challenges for machine learning deployment due to evolving process conditions, equipment degradation, and raw material variability that can cause model performance deterioration over time. This study benchmarks machine learning operations (MLOps) retraining strategies using five years of real manufacturing data to identify optimal retraining approaches for quality prediction. We evaluate various retraining frequencies and hyperparameter optimization strategies using control limit normalized residuals as key performance metric. Results demonstrate that a fixed retraining cadence every five production batches without hyperparameter retuning achieves superior performance across all drift conditions while significantly reducing computational overhead compared to strategies incorporating hyperparameter optimization. This approach effectively maintains model accuracy during both abrupt process changes and gradual equipment degradation patterns. To address the critical need for uncertainty quantification in manufacturing decision-making, we implement conformal prediction to generate prediction confidence intervals with strong statistical guarantees. This enables proactive quality control by identifying when prediction intervals fall within acceptable control limits, transforming traditional reactive quality management into a predictive framework. The findings provide practical guidelines for implementing robust MLOps strategies in manufacturing environments where computational efficiency and reliable uncertainty quantification are paramount for operational success.

</details>


### [304] [Rethinking State Tracking in Recurrent Models Through Error Control Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07755)
*Jiwan Chung,Heechan Choi,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了循环架构（如状态空间模型和线性注意力）在状态跟踪任务中失败的根本原因：虽然理论上具备表达能力，但由于仿射结构无法纠正状态区分方向上的误差，导致误差累积并最终破坏跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的循环架构状态跟踪理论主要关注表达能力（即架构是否能实现符号转换规则），而忽略了误差控制的重要性。作者认为误差控制——即控制区分符号状态的隐藏状态漂移动力学——同样关键，因为实际模型必须处理不可避免的数值误差。

Method: 1. 从理论上证明仿射循环网络（包括SSM和线性注意力）在保持状态表示时无法纠正状态分离子空间上的误差
2. 分析失效机制：建立可读性阈值理论，即跟踪仅在类内累积扩散小于初始类间分离时保持可读
3. 设计群组状态跟踪任务进行实证验证，测量可区分性比率与解码器可读性阈值的关系

Result: 1. 理论证明：仿射跟踪器无法学习鲁棒的状态跟踪，只能学习受状态相关累积误差支配的有限视界解
2. 实验验证：跟踪崩溃是可预测的——当可区分性比率跨越训练解码器的可读性阈值时，跟踪失效
3. 跨模型一致性：失效交叉点准确预测了下游精度失败的视界位置

Conclusion: 鲁棒的状态跟踪不仅取决于架构的理论表达性，更关键地取决于其误差控制能力。这一发现为理解和改进循环架构的状态跟踪能力提供了新的理论框架，表明未来研究应同时关注表达能力和误差控制两个维度。

Abstract: The theory of state tracking in recurrent architectures has predominantly focused on expressive capacity: whether a fixed architecture can theoretically realize a set of symbolic transition rules. We argue that equally important is error control, the dynamics governing hidden-state drift along the directions that distinguish symbolic states. We prove that affine recurrent networks, a class of models encompassing State-Space Models and Linear Attention, cannot correct errors along state-separating subspaces once they preserve state representations. Consequently, practical affine trackers do not learn robust state tracking; rather, they learn finite horizon solutions governed by accumulated state-relevant error. We characterize the mechanics of this failure, showing that tracking remains readable only while the accumulating within-class spread remains small relative to the initial between-class separation. We demonstrate empirically on group state-tracking tasks that this breakdown is predictable: tracking collapses when the distinguishability ratio crosses the readability threshold of the trained decoder. Across trained models, the point of this crossing predicts the horizon at which downstream accuracy fails. These results establish that robust state tracking is determined not only by an architecture's theoretical expressivity but crucially by its error control.

</details>


### [305] [Efficient Verification of Neural Control Barrier Functions with Smooth Nonlinear Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07757)
*Jun Zhang,Haibo Zhang,Chun Liu,Xiaofan Wang,Liang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LightCROWN方法，通过利用激活函数的解析特性计算更紧致的雅可比界，解决了现有CROWN方法在验证具有非线性激活的神经控制障碍函数(NCBF)时过于保守的问题，在多个控制系统中验证成功率提升至100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经控制障碍函数(NCBF)的形式化验证仍具挑战性，特别是对于包含tanh等非线性激活函数的神经网络。现有的基于CROWN的方法依赖保守的线性松弛来计算雅可比界，限制了其可扩展性和验证效率。

Method: 提出LightCROWN方法，通过挖掘和利用激活函数的解析性质，推导并计算更紧致的雅可比界。该方法是对现有CROWN框架的通用性改进，无需依赖过于保守的线性松弛近似。

Result: 在倒立摆、Dubins小车和平面四旋翼等非线性控制系统的实验中，LightCROWN将验证成功率提升至100%，同时在验证速度和可扩展性方面也表现出显著改善。

Conclusion: LightCROWN为基于CROWN的验证框架提供了一种通用且高效的改进方案，能够更高效地验证复杂的NCBFs，推动了神经网络控制在安全关键领域的应用。

Abstract: Formal verification of neural control barrier functions (NCBFs) remains challenging, especially for neural networks with nonlinear activations like \(\tanh\). Existing CROWN-based methods rely on conservative linear relaxations for Jacobian bounds, limiting scalability. We propose LightCROWN, which computes tighter Jacobian bounds by exploiting the analytical properties of activation functions. Experiments on nonlinear control systems including the inverted pendulum, Dubins car, and planar quadrotor demonstrate that LightCROWN improves verification success rates up to 100\%, while enhancing speed and scalability. Our approach provides a generalizable improvement for CROWN-based frameworks, enabling more efficient verification of complex NCBFs. The code can be found at github.com/Autonomous-Systems-and-Control-Lab/verify-neural-CBF.

</details>


### [306] [Pre-trained Tabular Foundation Models as Versatile Summary Networks for Neural Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07765)
*Elliot Pickens,Chiraag Gohel,Sidharth Satya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PFN-NPE方法，利用预训练的TabPFN作为固定摘要网络用于仿真贝叶斯推断（SBI），在多种任务中性能匹敌甚至超越现有方法，但在联合后验结构表示上存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于仿真的贝叶斯推断（SBI）中，后验估计依赖于从仿真观测中学习信息丰富的摘要统计量。TabPFN等表格基础模型在广泛的合成表格数据生成过程上进行了预训练，能够通过上下文学习在测试时适应新任务，这使其成为SBI中摘要网络的天然候选者。研究旨在探索TabPFN作为训练无关、模块化摘要网络在SBI中的有效性。

Method: 提出PFN-NPE框架：将预训练的TabPFN编码器作为固定摘要网络处理仿真器输出，然后将得到的摘要与下游推断头（默认使用归一化流）配对。该方法的核心在于TabPFN提供固定摘要，推断网络保持模块化和任务依赖性。通过诊断探测分析TabPFN派生摘要对后验位置和边缘信息的保留情况。

Result: PFN-NPE在多种SBI设置中与现有后验近似方法性能相当，有时甚至优于现有方法。诊断探测显示TabPFN派生摘要通常能够保留有用的后验位置和边缘信息。实验表明TabPFN能够在多样化的SBI设置中作为有效的摘要网络。但同时发现一个限制：即使边缘分布恢复良好，TabPFN派生摘要可能难以表示联合后验结构。

Conclusion: TabPFN可作为有效的摘要网络用于仿真贝叶斯推断，提供了训练无关、模块化的解决方案。虽然在联合后验结构表示方面存在局限，但该方法为SBI提供了新的思路，摘要网络与推断网络的解耦设计增强了方法的灵活性和适应性。

Abstract: In this work, we study TabPFN as a training-free, modular summary network for simulation-based Bayesian inference (SBI). Tabular foundation models such as TabPFN are pretrained on broad families of synthetic tabular data-generating processes and adapt at test time through in-context learning, making them natural candidates for SBI, where posterior estimation often depends on learning informative summaries of simulated observations. We propose PFN-NPE: a general recipe that uses a pretrained TabPFN encoder as a fixed summary network for simulator outputs, then pairs the resulting summaries with a downstream inference head chosen for the problem. With normalizing flows as the default inference head, PFN-NPE matches established posterior approximation methods and sometimes outperforms them. More importantly, diagnostic probes show that the TabPFN-derived summaries often preserve useful posterior location and marginal information. These analyses also reveal a limitation in that TabPFN-derived summaries may struggle to represent the joint posterior structure even when the marginals are well recovered. Still, our experiments show that TabPFN can serve as an effective summary network across a diverse set of SBI settings, with the inference network left modular and task-dependent.

</details>


### [307] [Training-Induced Escape from Token Clustering in a Mean-Field Formulation of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07772)
*Noboru Isobe,Daisuke Inoue,Masaaki Imaizumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究训练如何重塑Transformer的token聚类行为，发现了训练诱导的动力学新相位：token分布会在最后几层离开聚类区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer均值场理论主要将模型参数视为预设值，忽略了训练过程对token聚类的影响，无法解释训练如何重塑这一聚类图景。

Method: 研究一个噪声均值场Transformer，其中仅有参数线性的FFN在L2正则化下被训练；基于熵正则化交互能量进行数学分析，该能量刻画了注意力的聚类偏置。

Result: 发现了训练诱导的动力学相位：token分布初期遵循注意力驱动的聚类，但在接近最后几层时可以离开聚类区域。

Conclusion: 研究结果指向一种训练感知的Transformer动力学均值场理论，其中训练过程和推理动力学被统一处理。

Abstract: Transformers perform inference by iteratively transforming token representations across layers. This layerwise computation has been studied empirically, and recent mean-field theories of Transformer dynamics explain how attention can drive token distributions toward clustering. However, existing mean-field analyses largely treat model parameters as prescribed, leaving open how training reshapes this clustering picture. We study this question in a noisy mean-field Transformer in which only a parameter-linear FFN is trained under $L^2$ regularization. We find and analyze a training-induced phase in the dynamics: after initially following attention-driven clustering, the token distribution can leave the clustered regime near the final layers. Our mathematical analysis is based on an entropy-regularized interaction energy that captures the clustering bias of attention. More broadly, our results point toward a training-aware mean-field theory of Transformer dynamics, in which training and inference dynamics are treated together.

</details>


### [308] [Spectral Surgery: Class-Targeted Post-Hoc Rebalancing via Hessian Spike Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07790)
*Hugo Vigna,Samuel Bontemps*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“谱手术”（Spectral Surgery）的训练后优化方法，通过扰动Hessian矩阵尖峰特征向量来重新平衡各类别准确率，无需重新训练，在CIFAR-10和ISIC-2019数据集上取得了更好的平衡准确率和更低的标准差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明训练好的深度网络Hessian谱呈现出“连续近零特征值主体加少量大特征值尖峰”的结构，且尖峰数量与类别数减一相匹配。然而，现有工作仅描述了该现象，尚未有方法将其操作化以提升分类性能。本文旨在利用该谱结构，解决模型在不同类别上表现不平衡的问题。

Method: 提出了“谱手术”这一后处理优化方法，具体包含三个核心组件：（1）构建尖峰-类别敏感度矩阵，量化每个类别的准确率沿各尖峰特征向量方向的变化率；（2）在约束条件下优化扰动系数，定向提升弱类别的表现同时保持强类别的性能；（3）设计自适应幅度控制机制，根据迭代过程中的改进信号动态调整扰动幅度。该方法直接沿尖峰特征向量扰动模型权重，无需重新训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ISIC-2019数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在平衡准确率指标上取得了令人鼓舞的提升，同时降低了准确率的标准差，有效改善了各类别间的性能平衡。

Conclusion: 本文成功将深度网络Hessian谱的尖峰结构操作化，提出了一种无需重训练即可有效调节各类别性能的后处理方法，验证了利用谱结构改善分类任务中类别不平衡问题的可行性。

Abstract: The Hessian spectrum of trained deep networks exhibits a characteristic structure: a continuous bulk of near-zero eigenvalues and a small number of large outlier eigenvalues (spikes), confirming the relevance of Random Matrix Theory in deep learning. The spike count matches the number of classes minus one. While prior work has described this structure, no method has exploited it operationally to improve classification performance. We propose Spectral Surgery, a post-hoc optimization method that directly perturbs model weights along spike eigenvectors to rebalance per-class accuracy without retraining. We introduce (i) a spike-class sensitivity matrix that quantifies the directional derivative of each class's accuracy along each spike eigenvector, (ii) a constrained optimization of perturbation coefficients that targets weak classes while preserving strong ones, and (iii) an adaptive amplitude control that raises or lowers the perturbation budget based on iteration-level improvement signals. We obtain encouraging results on CIFAR-10 and ISIC-2019 on both balanced accuracy and standard deviation.

</details>


### [309] [Flexible Routing via Uncertainty Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07805)
*Charlotte Peale,Siddartha Devic,Parikshit Gopalan,Udi Wieder,Aravind Gollakota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于不确定性分解的新型模型路由器，能够区分可减少和不可减少的不确定性，从而在弱模型预测、调用昂贵模型和弃权之间做出最优决策，且无需针对不同损失函数重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型路由策略在处理具有固有歧义的查询时，往往会不必要地调用昂贵的模型（如大型预训练模型或人类专家），导致成本浪费。此外，传统路由器通常难以在不重新训练的情况下适应不同的损失函数和成本参数。

Method: 该方法利用高阶预测器将总不确定性分解为可减少不确定性（reducible uncertainty）和不可减少不确定性（irreducible uncertainty）。基于此分解，设计了一套统一的路由与弃权策略：当不确定性低时使用低成本模型预测；当可减少不确定性高时路由至昂贵模型；当不可减少不确定性高时选择弃权。该路由器具有强理论保障，能够界定相对于特定任务最优路由器的后悔值（regret）。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在合适的场景下（特别是当可减少不确定性与不可减少不确定性相关性较低时）表现优异。理论分析证明了该路由器相对于最优任务特定路由器的后悔值是有界的。

Conclusion: 所提出的不确定性感知路由器成功实现了在性能与成本之间的有效平衡，通过避免对固有歧义查询的不必要模型调用，为现代机器学习系统提供了一种高效、灵活且理论可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: A key strategy for balancing performance and cost in modern machine learning systems is to dynamically route queries to either a low-cost model or a more expensive oracle (such as a large pretrained model or human expert), an approach known as model routing. In this work we present a new uncertainty-aware router that (1) avoids unnecessary oracle calls on inherently ambiguous queries, and (2) adapts dynamically to different loss functions and cost parameters through simple hyperparameter changes, without retraining. Our method, applicable to any classification setting where multiple independent annotations per input are available, is based on decomposing total uncertainty into irreducible and reducible components using higher-order predictors [Ahdritz et al., 2025]. This enables a unified approach to both routing and abstention: predict with the weak model when uncertainty is low, route to the oracle when reducible uncertainty is high, and abstain when irreducible uncertainty is high. Our router comes with strong theoretical guarantees bounding regret relative to optimal task-specific routers. We conduct experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets that demonstrate the benefits of our approach in suitable regimes -- in particular, whenever reducible and irreducible uncertainty are not too correlated.

</details>


### [310] [The Minimax Rate of Second-Order Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07808)
*Kamil Ciosek,Banafsheh Rafiee,Sina Ghiassian,Nicolò Felicioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了二阶校准误差估计的最优速率Õ(1/√n)，通过多项式回归方法实现了对现有方法(O(n^{-1/4}))的定性改进，并提出了首个二阶Platt缩放的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶校准误差用于评估高阶预测器的认知不确定性估计是否匹配标签概率的条件方差，但现有基于分桶或核平滑的方法收敛速率较慢(O(n^{-1/4}))。本文旨在建立该问题的最小最大最优估计速率。

Method: 发现sech扰动核使校准函数在半宽度为hπ/2的带状区域内解析，据此采用多项式回归方法估计二阶校准误差，推导出具有显式常数的Õ(1/√n)收敛速率。同时建立了匹配的Ω(1/√n)下界，证明了最小最大最优性。

Result: （1）二阶校准误差估计的最小最大速率为Õ(1/√n)，较现有O(n^{-1/4})方法有定性改进；（2）首次给出二阶Platt缩放的有限样本保证，实现对任意高阶预测器均值预测和认知方差估计的后处理重校准；（3）提出无分桶的二阶校准定义并建立与分桶形式的定量联系。实验验证了理论速率和重校准不确定性质量。

Conclusion: 通过揭示sech核的解析性质，本文将多项式回归引入二阶校准误差估计，建立了该问题的最小最大最优理论框架。理论结果和实验验证表明该方法能有效提升不确定性估计质量。

Abstract: We characterize the minimax rate of estimating the second-order calibration error for binary classification, which quantifies whether a higher-order predictor's epistemic-uncertainty estimate matches the conditional variance of the label probability on its level sets. Our key observation is that the sech perturbation kernel, previously used only to enforce smoothness of calibration functions, in fact makes them analytic in a strip of half-width $hπ/2$. Polynomial regression then estimates the calibration error at rate $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$, with explicit constants, a qualitative improvement over the $O(n^{-1/4})$ rate achievable by bucketing or kernel smoothing. A matching $Ω(1/\sqrt{n})$ lower bound establishes minimax optimality up to logarithmic factors. As a corollary, we give the first finite-sample guarantee for second-order Platt scaling, yielding a post-hoc procedure that recalibrates both the mean prediction and the epistemic-variance estimate of any higher-order predictor. Along the way, we provide a bucket-free definition of second-order calibration and relate it quantitatively to the bucketed formulation of Ahdritz et al. [2025]. Our experiments confirm the predicted rate and the quality of the recalibrated uncertainties.

</details>


### [311] [OrScale: Orthogonalised Optimization with Layer-Wise Trust-Ratio Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07815)
*Yuxuan Lou,Yang You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OrScale通过引入信任比率扩展了Muon优化器，实现了层级自适应学习率缩放，在CIFAR-10和语言模型预训练任务上均取得了优于Muon和AdamW的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器通过正交化矩阵更新的方向来改进神经网络训练，但每个层的更新幅度仍主要由全局学习率控制，缺乏层级自适应能力，作者旨在解决这一局限性。

Method: 提出OrScale方法，使用Frobenius范数测量实际参数空间方向作为信任比率的分母，结合Moonlight形状缩放和一次性层级校准；分析并避免了Muon-LAMB混合方法的三个失败模式（形状退化分母、原始动量裁剪饱和、解耦权重衰减失控）。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/DavidNet上以94.05%验证top-1排名第一（Muon为93.70%）；OrScale-LM在125M至1.1B参数规模的FineWeb-Edu预训练中，四个规模中有三个优于Muon+Moonlight，所有规模均优于AdamW。

Conclusion: OrScale成功实现了Muon的层级自适应学习率扩展，理论保证包括O(1/√T)非凸收敛、严格层自适应下降增益和muP风格学习率迁移特性，实验验证了其在视觉和语言任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Muon improves neural-network training by orthogonalizing matrix-valued updates, but it leaves each layer's update magnitude controlled mostly by a global learning rate. We introduce OrScale, a trust-ratio extension of Muon built on a simple rule: the denominator of a layer-wise ratio should measure the Frobenius norm of the actual parameter-space direction that will be applied. This yields OrScale for general matrix layers and OrScale-LM for language models, where Moonlight shape scaling is combined with one-time per-layer calibration so every trust ratio starts at one. We analyze why three natural Muon-LAMB hybrids fail through shape-degenerate denominators, raw-momentum clip saturation, and decoupled weight-decay runaway, and show that the real-update-direction denominator with coupled weight decay avoids these failures. Theoretically, OrScale admits an O(1/sqrt(T)) nonconvex convergence guarantee in a nuclear-norm criterion, a strict layer-adaptive descent gain under measurable layer heterogeneity, and calibration properties that preserve muP-style learning-rate transfer at initialization. Empirically, OrScale ranks first on CIFAR-10/DavidNet across three seeds, improving Muon from 93.70% to 94.05% validation top-1, and OrScale-LM improves FineWeb-Edu pre-training versus Muon+Moonlight at three of four scales from 125M to 1.1B parameters while outperforming AdamW at every scale.

</details>


### [312] [Scaling Categorical Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07820)
*Oscar Davis,Anastasiia Filippova,Pierre Ablin,Victor Turrisi,Amitis Shidani,Marco Cuturi,Louis Béthune*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文成功将分类流匹配（CFM）方法扩展至17亿参数规模，在2.1T token上训练后仅需4步推理即可生成高质量文本，解决了该方法的可扩展性疑问，并提供了训练大规模模型的关键技术见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散和流匹配模型在语言建模中具有加速采样等优势，但现有基于分类流图（CFM）的方法仅在较小规模（<10亿参数）上得到验证，其可扩展性尚不明确。本文旨在验证该方法在十亿参数规模上的可行性。

Method: 1. 训练一个17亿参数的基础流模型（使用2.1T token）；2. 通过自蒸馏得到分类流图（CFM）；3. 引入半离散环境下的似然边界用于模型评估；4. 提出针对大规模训练的损失加权和时间调度策略。

Result: 1. CFM仅需4步推理即可生成多样化、高质量的文本，同时保持接近数据水平的token熵；2. 在标准LM基准测试中，评分结果与离散扩散方法处于同一水平；3. 揭示了大规模训练中的挑战并提供了相应的解决方案。

Conclusion: 分类流匹配方法可成功扩展至十亿参数规模，在极少推理步骤下实现具有竞争力的生成质量，证明了其作为自回归方法替代方案的潜力，同时为未来大规模训练提供了重要的技术指导。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion and flow matching models could represent a powerful alternative to autoregressive approaches for language modelling (LM), as they unlock a host of advantages currently reserved for continuous modalities, including accelerated sampling and tilting. Recently, several works have demonstrated the possibility of generating discrete data continuously by a simple flow matching process between a Gaussian and the one-hot encoded data distribution. They have further shown the feasibility of accelerated sampling via Categorical Flow Maps (CFMs), resulting in competitive sample quality in the few-step regime. However, this method had only been evaluated at relatively modest scales ($<1$B), leaving the question of its scalability completely open. In this article, we train a $1.7$B-parameter base flow model on $2.1$T tokens and self-distill it into a CFM that generates diverse, high-quality text in as few as $4$ inference steps while maintaining near-data-level token entropy. Furthermore, we introduce a likelihood bound for CFMs in the semi-discrete setting, and show that they can be used to score the model on standard LM benchmarks, achieving results in the same range as discrete diffusion methods. Finally, we uncover some of the challenges that arise from training these models at scale, and we provide prescriptive insights on loss weighting and time scheduling.

</details>


### [313] [RelAgent: LLM Agents as Data Scientists for Relational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07840)
*Xingyue Huang,Louis Tichelman,Jinwoo Kim,Krzysztof Olejniczak,İsmail İlkan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RelAgent，一个基于大语言模型的自主数据科学家，通过生成SQL特征程序和选择经典预测模型来解决关系学习问题，实现了快速、确定性且可解释的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系学习是一个具有挑战性的问题，现有的解决方案如图神经网络、表格基础模型和序列模型等各有优劣，缺乏一种能够结合可解释性、部署效率与预测性能的统一方法。

Method: 提出了一个名为RelAgent的基于LLM的智能体，分为两个阶段：在搜索阶段，LLM智能体利用数据库、验证和评估工具构建SQL特征程序并选择预测模型；在推理阶段，直接执行生成的程序而无需调用LLM。最终预测器由SQL查询和经典模型组成。

Result: 该方法生成的预测器具有快速、确定性和内在可解释性的特点。其特征是人类可读的SQL查询，预测仅依赖于查询定义的特征图，并支持使用标准数据库系统进行可扩展部署。

Conclusion: RelAgent成功地将大语言模型的推理能力与经典模型及数据库系统的优势相结合，为关系学习提供了一种高效、可解释且易于部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Relational learning is a challenging problem that has motivated a wide range of approaches, including graph-based models (e.g., graph neural networks, graph transformers), tabular methods (e.g., tabular foundation models), and sequence-based approaches (e.g., large language models), each with its own advantages and limitations. We propose RelAgent, an LLM-based autonomous data scientist for relational learning, which operates in two phases. In the search phase, an LLM agent uses database, validation, and evaluation workspace tools to construct SQL feature programs and select a predictive model. In the inference phase, the resulting program is executed without further LLM calls. The final predictor consists of SQL queries and a classical model, enabling fast, deterministic, and intrinsically interpretable predictions: features are human-readable queries, and predictions depend only on the resulting query-defined feature map, enabling scalable deployment using standard database systems.

</details>


### [314] [Distributional simplicity bias and effective convexity in Energy Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07844)
*Aurélien Decelle,Alfonso de Jesús Navas Gómez,Beatriz Seoane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过有效模型对能量模型（EBM）学习过程进行了动力学分析，揭示了固定点性质及低阶相互作用优先学习的机制，解释了分布简单性偏差现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量模型训练本质上的非凸性导致其对初始化敏感、易陷入局部最优以及梯度动态不稳定，目前缺乏对训练动力学及收敛特性的深入理论理解。

Method: 利用有效模型（可解释为含高阶相互作用的广义伊辛模型或能量的傅里叶展开）分析二值变量上严格正分布的梯度流；研究其不动点类型及其稳定性。

Result: 发现梯度流存在两类不动点：数据一致点（精确复现目标分布）和伪不动点（满足平稳性但不匹配目标分布）。数据一致点附近扰动稳定或中性，且梯度动态诱导学习层次结构——低阶相互作用先于高阶相互作用被学习。

Conclusion: 研究结果为分布简单性偏差提供了机理解释，并阐明了为何实践中不出现低阶非数据一致的伪不动点，加深了对能量模型训练动态的理解。

Abstract: Energy-based learning is a powerful framework for generative modelling, but its training is inherently non-convex, leading potentially to sensitivity to initialisation, poor local optima, and unstable gradient dynamics. We present a dynamical analysis of energy-based learning through the lens of the effective model, which can be interpreted as either a generalised Ising model with higher-order interactions or the Fourier expansion of the energy. Under sufficient expressivity, we show that the gradient flow induced by learning strictly positive distributions over binary variables admits two types of fixed points: data-consistent points, which exactly reproduce the target distribution, and spurious points, which satisfy stationarity without matching the target distribution. Around data-consistent points, we show that perturbations are either stable or neutral, with neutral directions leaving the effective model invariant. Finally, we show that gradient dynamics induce a hierarchy in which lower-order interactions are learned before higher-order ones. This provides a mechanistic explanation for the distributional simplicity bias and clarifies why fixed points that are not data-consistent at low orders are not observed in practice.

</details>


### [315] [Actor-Critic Algorithm for Dynamic Expectile and CVaR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07857)
*Yudong Luo,Erick Delage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需转移扰动的代理策略梯度方法，并结合可引出性开发了无模型的价值学习方法，构建了能够在动态风险优化中学习风险规避策略并优于现有方法的无模型离策略演员-评论家算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用随机策略优化动态风险时，现有的策略更新通常需要转移扰动，而价值学习往往依赖于有模型的方法，这两者在实际应用中均面临挑战。

Method: 在softmax策略参数化下提出了无需转移扰动的代理策略梯度；利用可引出性为动态期望分位数和条件风险价值开发了无模型价值学习方法；受Expected SARSA和Expected Policy Gradient启发，构建了无模型离策略演员-评论家算法。

Result: 在具有可验证风险规避行为的领域进行的实验表明，该算法能够学习到风险规避策略，并且表现持续优于其他现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功解决了动态风险优化中策略更新和价值学习的关键挑战，为无模型风险敏感强化学习提供了一种有效的新途径。

Abstract: Optimizing dynamic risk with stochastic policies is challenging in both policy updates and value learning. The former typically requires transition perturbation, while the latter may rely on model-based approaches.
