To address these challenges, we propose a surrogate policy gradient without transition perturbation under softmax policy parameterization. We further develop model-free value learning methods for dynamic expectile and conditional value-at-risk by leveraging elicitability. Finally, inspired by Expected SARSA and Expected Policy Gradient, a model-free off-policy actor-critic algorithm is constructed. Empirical results in domains with verifiable risk-averse behavior show that our algorithm can learn risk-averse policy and consistently outperforms other existing methods.

</details>


### [316] [ADKO: Agentic Decentralized Knowledge Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07863)
*Lucas Nerone Rillo,Zhanhong Jiang,Nastaran Saadati,Aditya Balu,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Chinmay Hegde,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADKO框架，通过在自主代理间交换压缩的“知识令牌”而非原始数据，实现了高效、隐私保护的协同黑盒优化，并首次给出了包含LM误差和压缩损失的理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决多智能体协同黑盒优化中存在的数据隐私泄露、通信开销大、目标异构以及样本效率低等关键挑战，需要一种既能保护隐私又能高效利用知识的去中心化优化方法。

Method: 提出ADKO框架，每个代理维护一个本地高斯过程(GP)代理模型，仅通过包含方向信号、优势分数及大语言模型(LM)见解的“知识令牌”进行通信，实现了GP-UCB、并行贝叶斯优化、去中心化学习与LM引导发现的统一；同时引入了基于保真度的令牌剪枝策略。

Result: 首次提供了双重信息损失（令牌压缩的互信息保真度损失与LM近似误差）的形式化分析，推导出累积遗憾分解公式并给出了次线性遗憾的充要条件；在神经架构搜索和科学发现任务上验证了理论并显示出优于强基线的性能。

Conclusion: ADKO框架在理论上证明了次线性遗憾的条件，在实践中展示了其在保护隐私的前提下实现高效协同优化的能力，为去中心化智能体协作提供了一个理论基础扎实且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present Agentic Decentralized Knowledge Optimization (ADKO), a framework for collaborative black-box optimization across autonomous agents that achieves sample efficiency, privacy preservation, heterogeneous-objective handling, and communication efficiency. Each agent maintains a private Gaussian Process (GP) surrogate trained on local data and communicates only through knowledge tokens-compact, lossy summaries containing directional signals, advantage scores, and optional language-model (LM) insights-without sharing raw data or model parameters. ADKO unifies GP-Upper Confidence Bound (GP-UCB), parallel Bayesian optimization, decentralized learning, and LM-guided discovery. We provide the first formal analysis of dual information loss: token compression, quantified via mutual-information-based fidelity, and LM approximation error, decomposed into bias and stochastic noise. Our main result shows cumulative regret decomposes into GP error, LM bias, LM noise, and compression loss, with necessary and sufficient conditions for sublinear regret. We also propose fidelity-aware token pruning to preserve high-information tokens under memory budget. Experiments on neural architecture search and scientific discovery validate the theory and show consistent improvements over strong baselines.

</details>


### [317] [Black-box model classification under the discriminative factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07878)
*Hayden Helm,Merrick Ohata,Carey Priebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出判别性分解框架，用于在黑盒模型分类任务中区分高质量和低质量查询集，证明了随机分类概率随查询预算呈指数衰减，并验证了该方法能有效预测查询集质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成系统通常以黑盒API形式提供服务，用户在推理时无法获知系统内部属性。现有研究表明，基于查询响应关系的低维模型表示可用于推断模型级属性，但这些表示的质量对查询集高度敏感，如何区分高质量和低质量查询集成为关键问题。

Method: 提出判别性分解框架，用于量化查询集在黑盒模型级分类任务中的质量。该框架下推导出随机分类概率随查询预算呈指数衰减的理论结果，并开发了估计判别场的方法用于选择查询集。

Result: 在三个审计任务上验证，估计的分解参数能准确预测经验性能衰减率。使用估计判别场选择的查询集能够复现oracle查询集的经验排序。

Conclusion: 判别性分解框架为黑盒模型审计中的查询集质量评估提供了理论基础和实用方法，所提出的判别场估计方法能够有效指导高质量查询集的选择。

Abstract: Access to modern generative systems is often restricted to querying an API (the ``black-box" setting) and many properties of the system are unknown to the user at inference time. While recent work has shown that low-dimensional representations of models based on the relationship between their embedded responses to a set of queries are useful for inferring model-level properties, the quality of these representations is highly sensitive to the query set. We introduce the \emph{discriminative factorization} to distinguish between high- and low-quality query sets in the context of black-box model-level classification. Under this framework, the probability of chance-level classification decays exponentially in the query budget. On three auditing tasks, estimated factorization parameters predict the empirical performance decay rate. We conclude by showing that query sets selected using the estimated discriminative field reproduce the empirical ordering of oracle query sets.

</details>


### [318] [Enhancing Federated Quadruplet Learning: Stochastic Client Selection and Embedding Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07888)
*Ozgu Goksu,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedQuad方法，通过显式约束类内表示紧凑和类间分离，有效解决了联邦学习中数据异构导致的全局模型泛化性能下降问题，在多个基准数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在分布式客户端训练中面临数据异构性挑战，特别是在数据有限和类别不平衡的情况下，导致全局模型的泛化性能显著下降。现有的方法难以有效处理客户端间的表示错位问题。

Method: 提出FedQuad方法，通过联合优化正样本对距离最小化和负样本对距离最大化，显式强制最小化类内表示距离并促进类间分离，从而缓解模型聚合过程中引入的表示错位问题。同时提供了基于度量学习方法在中心化和联邦环境下的综合分析。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet数据集上进行了广泛实验，采用多种非独立同分布设置和不同客户端数量，FedQuad相比现有基线方法取得了持续的性能提升。实验证明基于度量学习的方法能有效缓解异构数据分布下的表示崩塌问题。

Conclusion: FedQuad通过表示学习有效缓解了联邦学习中的数据异构性问题，为联邦环境下的度量学习方法提供了新的视角和解决方案，具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables decentralised model training across distributed clients without requiring data centralisation. However, the generalisation performance of the global model is usually degraded by data heterogeneity across clients, particularly under limited data availability and class imbalance. To address this challenge, we propose FedQuad, a novel method that explicitly enforces minimising intra-class representations while enabling inter-class splits across clients. By jointly minimising distances between positive pairs and maximising distances between negative pairs, the proposed approach mitigates representation misalignment introduced during model aggregation. We evaluate our method on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under diverse non-IID settings and varying numbers of clients, demonstrating consistent improvements over existing baselines. Additionally, we provide a comprehensive analysis of metric learning-based approaches in both centralised and federated environments, highlighting their effectiveness in alleviating representation collapse under heterogeneous data distributions.

</details>


### [319] [Adaptive Regularization for Sparsity Control in Bregman-Based Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07892)
*Ahmad Aloradi,Tim Roith,Emanuël A. P. Habets,Daniel Tenbrinck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对稀疏训练中正则化参数λ与目标稀疏度难以对应的问题，提出了一种自适应正则化方案，能够动态调整λ以精确达到预设稀疏度，并在说话人确认任务上实现了更快的收敛和相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏优化方法（如$\ell_1$惩罚项）只能通过正则化参数$\lambda$间接控制稀疏度，且两者之间的映射关系是非平凡的。特别是对于Bregman优化器，不同变体达到相同稀疏度所需的$\lambda$值可能相差两个数量级，需要昂贵的试错搜索才能达到用户指定的稀疏度，这限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出一种自适应正则化方案，根据模型当前稀疏度与目标稀疏度之间的差异动态更新正则化参数$\lambda$，从而直接且精确地控制最终的稀疏率。该方法被应用于基于Bregman的稀疏优化框架中。

Result: 在VoxCeleb和CNCeleb数据集上，使用ECAPA-TDNN和ResNet34进行自动说话人确认任务的实验表明：该方法能可靠地实现75%至99%范围内的稀疏度目标；在训练早期能比经过oracle调优的非自适应基线收敛更快；最终在等错误率（EER）指标上匹配或超越基线性能；同时继承了非自适应方法的关键特性，包括比稠密基线更好的分布外鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应正则化方案有效解决了稀疏训练中参数敏感性高、调参成本大的问题，能够精确达到预设稀疏度，同时保持甚至提升模型性能与鲁棒性，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Sparse training reduces the memory and computational costs of deep neural networks. However, sparse optimization methods, e.g., those adding an $\ell_1$ penalty, often control sparsity only indirectly through a regularization parameter $λ$, whose mapping to the final sparsity rate is non-trivial. In our experiments, we found this parameter sensitivity to be particularly pronounced for Bregman-based optimizers. Specifically, the two variants LinBreg and AdaBreg reach the same sparsity at $λ$ values that differ by up to two orders of magnitude, requiring expensive trial-and-error sweeps to achieve a user-specified sparsity. To address this, we propose an adaptive regularization scheme that updates $λ$ based on the difference between the model's current sparsity and the target sparsity. We analyze the resulting algorithm and evaluate it on automatic speaker verification with ECAPA-TDNN and ResNet34 on VoxCeleb and CNCeleb. The proposed method reliably achieves sparsity targets ranging between 75% and 99%. It also converges faster than the oracle-tuned non-adaptive baseline during early training and matches or surpasses its final performance in equal error rate. We further show that the adaptive scheme inherits key properties from its non-adaptive counterpart, including improved out-of-distribution robustness over the dense baselines.

</details>


### [320] [Curvature Beyond Positivity: Greedy Guarantees for Arbitrary Submodular Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07902)
*Yixin Chen,Alan Kuhnle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将曲率概念扩展至所有子模函数，提出了首个能够同时处理非单调性和负值的贪婪算法，突破了传统非负性限制，在特定条件下优于现有理论界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典子模函数优化理论主要局限于单调非负函数，然而实际应用中的目标函数常包含成本项导致出现负值。现有工作仅能针对特殊函数类提供加性界限或孤立处理非单调性，无法提供统一的乘性近似保证，且不适用于负值情况。

Method: 将曲率（衡量函数偏离线性程度的参数）从单调子模函数扩展至所有子模函数，利用这一经典概念同时刻画非单调性和负值性。提出带剪枝操作的贪婪算法，并设计了多线性扩展变体以处理一般组合约束。

Result: 算法对任意子模函数（含负值函数）均能提供由曲率控制的乘性近似比。在非单调情形（曲率1≤c_g<2.2）下，近似比严格优于已知最佳一致比0.401；对于单调函数，恢复经典的(1-e^{-c_g})/c_g保证。实验验证了算法在代价惩罚实验设计、覆盖、特征选择及Multi-News段落选择任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过曲率的推广，首次为负值子模函数提供了乘性近似保证，突破了单调性与非负性的理论限制，将经典结构理论扩展至更广泛的函数类别，为带成本的实际优化问题提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Submodular functions -- functions exhibiting diminishing returns -- are central to machine learning. When the objective is monotone and non-negative, the greedy algorithm achieves a tight $63\%$ approximation. But many practical objectives incorporate costs that make them negative on some inputs, and all existing multiplicative guarantees require non-negativity. Prior work handles negativity through additive bounds for the special class of decomposable functions and non-monotonicity through partial-monotonicity parameters, but these address each difficulty in isolation and neither extends the classical structural theory. We extend \emph{curvature} -- a parameter measuring how far a function deviates from linearity -- to all submodular functions, handling both non-monotonicity and negativity through a single classical concept. A greedy algorithm with pruning achieves a curvature-controlled multiplicative ratio for \emph{any} submodular function, including those taking negative values -- the first such guarantee beyond monotonicity and non-negativity. In the non-monotone regime $1 \le c_g < 2.2$, the bound strictly beats the best known uniform ratio of $0.401$ (for non-negative $f$), and it recovers the classical $(1-e^{-c_g})/c_g$ guarantee for monotone functions. A multilinear-extension variant extends the framework to general combinatorial constraints via multilinear relaxation. Experiments on cost-penalized experimental design, coverage, feature selection, and a curvature sweep on Multi-News passage selection support the theory.

</details>


### [321] [Flatness and Gradient Alignment Are Both Necessary: Spectral-Aware Gradient-Aligned Exploration for Multi-Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07914)
*Aristotelis Ballas,Christos Diou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE方法，同时优化损失景观的平坦度和梯度对齐性，解决了现有方法只关注单一几何特性的局限，在多分布学习任务中取得了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的锐度感知方法和平坦度方法各自仅关注损失景观的单一几何特性（前者关注平坦度，后者关注梯度对齐），这种遗漏在多分布学习场景下是结构上不可避免的。研究表明，单一指标无法保证低过量风险，因此需要同时考虑这两种特性。

Method: 1. 推导出过量风险分解公式，包含对齐项（由平均Hessian逆与梯度协方差的迹控制）和曲率项（由平均Hessian控制）两个加性主项；
2. 提出SAGE算法：曲率分量用每层梯度矩阵的极因子（通过Newton-Schulz迭代计算）替代SAM的梯度缩放扰动，使上升步以相似幅度探测所有方向；
3. 对齐分量在下降步注入各向同性噪声，噪声幅度随跨分布梯度分歧而变化。

Result: 在五个域泛化基准和两个多任务学习基准上的实验表明：该方法在DomainBed上建立了新的最优性能，作为通用改进可应用于基础MTL求解器，与现有最优方法保持竞争力甚至超越它们。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析揭示了平坦度和梯度对齐在多分布学习中的互补必要性，并提出统一的SAGE框架同时优化这两个目标，为多分布学习提供了一个有效的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware and gradient-alignment methods have been shown to improve generalization, however each family of methods targets a single geometric property of the loss landscape, while ignoring the other. In this paper, we show that this omission is structurally unavoidable and that both flatness and gradient alignment should be considered in multi-distribution learning settings. Specifically, we derive an excess-risk decomposition that yields two additive leading-order terms: (i) an alignment term, controlled by the trace of $\bar{H}^{-1}Σ_g$ and (ii) a curvature term, controlled by $\bar{H}$, where $\bar{H}$ is the average Hessian and $Σ_g$ is the covariance of the gradient across distributions. Notably, $\bar{H}$ appears inverted in one and non-inverted in the other. We further show, via a counterexample, that neither quantity bounds the other in general, so no algorithm targeting only one term can guarantee low excess risk. Motivated by this decomposition, we propose SAGE (Spectral-Aware Gradient-Aligned Exploration) that targets both terms. The curvature component replaces SAM's gradient-scaled perturbation with the polar factor of each layer's gradient matrix, computed via Newton-Schulz iteration, so that the ascent step probes all directions with similar magnitude. On the other hand, the alignment component injects isotropic noise at the descent step, the magnitude of which scales with cross-distribution gradient disagreement. Experiments on five domain-generalization and two multi-task learning benchmarks show that the proposed method establishes a new state-of-the-art on DomainBed and acts as a general-purpose improvement to base MTL solvers, remaining competitive with, or even surpassing, state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [322] [Tree SAE: Learning Hierarchical Feature Structures in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07922)
*Tue M. Cao,Hoang X. Nhat,Raed Alharbi,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tree SAE，通过结合激活约束和新型重构约束，有效学习稀疏自编码器中的层次化特征结构，在识别层次关系上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于激活覆盖的方法识别SAE特征层次关系时会产生大量假阳性（语义不相关的父子特征），仅依赖子特征在父特征激活时才激活的条件不足以准确刻画层次结构。

Method: 提出一种新型重构条件，强制层次级别间存在更深层的功能联系，将其与激活约束结合，设计出能够直接从特征集中学习层次结构的Tree SAE模型。

Result: Tree SAE在学习层次化特征对方面显著超越现有SAE方法，同时在多个关键基准测试上保持与最先进方法相当的竞争力；成功应用于映射子特征子空间几何结构和揭示大语言模型中复杂的层次化概念结构。

Conclusion: Tree SAE有效解决了现有方法在层次特征学习中的假阳性问题，为理解大语言模型内部表示的层次化结构提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Learning hierarchical features in Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) is essential for capturing the structured nature of real-world data and mitigating issues like feature absorption or splitting. Existing works attempt to identify hierarchical relationships within independent feature sets by relying on activation coverage, the assumption that child feature should only activate when its parent feature activates. However, we demonstrate that this condition alone is insufficient; that is, it often produces false positives where parent and child concepts are semantically unrelated. To address this, we introduce a novel reconstruction condition that enforces a deeper functional link between hierarchical levels. By combining both activation and reconstruction constraints, we propose the Tree SAE, a model designed to learn hierarchical structures directly from within the feature set. Our results demonstrate that Tree SAEs significantly surpass the existing SAEs at learning hierarchical pairs while maintaining competitive performance to the state-of-the-art on several key benchmarks. Finally, we demonstrate the practical utility of our Tree SAE in mapping the geometry of child feature subspaces and uncovering the complex hierarchical concept structures encoded within large language models.

</details>


### [323] [Prototype Guided Post-pretraining for Single-Cell Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07938)
*Sachini Weerasekara,Natasha Darras,Sagar Kamarthi,Colles Price,Jacqueline Isaacs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CellRefine方法，通过在预训练和微调之间引入后预训练阶段，利用标记基因集作为结构先验来优化单细胞基础模型的嵌入流形，在下游任务中提升高达15%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单细胞预训练模型将基因视为token、细胞视为句子，但受限于细胞类型分布的长尾特性，且在基因表达数据的协变量偏移下泛化能力不足，微调后性能仍受限。

Method: 提出CellRefine后预训练方法，位于预训练和微调之间，采用多面目标函数，整合标记基因集作为结构先验来引导后预训练过程，优化细胞的潜在嵌入流形。

Result: 在多个计算生物学任务中，CellRefine持续提升下游任务性能，性能增益最高达15%。

Conclusion: CellRefine有效解决了单细胞基础模型在长尾分布和协变量偏移下的泛化问题，显著提升了模型在下游任务中的表现。

Abstract: Single-cell representation learning (SCRL) from gene expression data offers a way to uncover the complex regulatory logic underlying cellular function. Inspired by large language models in natural language modeling, several single-cell pretrained models have recently been proposed that treat genes as tokens and cells as sentences. However, these models are fundamentally limited by the long-tailed nature of cell-type distributions and struggle to generalize under covariate shifts in gene expression data. While fine-tuning is often used to mitigate these issues, we observe that performance remains bounded. To address this challenge, we introduce CellRefine, a post-pretraining method that operates between the pretraining and fine-tuning stages of a single-cell foundation model. CellRefine uses a multi-faceted objective that incorporates marker-gene sets as structural priors to guide post-pretraining and refine the latent embedding manifold of cells. Across multiple computational biology tasks, empirical results show that CellRefine consistently improves downstream performance, yielding gains up to 15%.

</details>


### [324] [Slowly Annealed Langevin Dynamics: Theory and Applications to Training-Free Guided Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07950)
*Atsushi Nitanda,Dake Bu,Yueming Lyu,Tanya Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出慢退火朗之万动力学(SALD)用于跟踪移动目标分布路径，并引入速度感知版本(VA-SALD)实现无训练引导生成，提供了收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何跟踪移动目标分布的路径并近似终端目标分布，以及为基于预训练分数生成模型的无训练引导生成提供理论框架。

Method: 提出慢退火朗之万动力学(SALD)，通过时间减慢改进跟踪性能；建立KL散度微分不等式证明非渐近收敛性；引入速度感知SALD(VA-SALD)，显式结合预训练模型的边缘分布，利用减慢校正引导引起的额外偏差。

Result: 建立了SALD的非渐近收敛保证，证明减慢通过中间目标的收缩和路径复杂性改善跟踪性能；为扩散模型及相关生成模型族提供了收敛保证的理论框架，明确了中间函数不等式和引导偏差的作用。

Conclusion: SALD为跟踪移动目标分布提供了有效方法，VA-SALD为无训练引导生成建立了统一原理框架，理论分析揭示了减慢机制和引导偏差的关键作用。

Abstract: We study Slowly Annealed Langevin Dynamics (SALD), a sampler for tracking a path of moving target distributions and approximating the terminal target through time slowdown. We establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees via a KL differential inequality, showing that slowdown improves tracking through contraction of intermediate targets and the complexity of the path. Motivated by training-free guided generation with pretrained score-based generative models, we further introduce Velocity-Aware SALD (VA-SALD), which explicitly incorporates the underlying marginal distributions of the pretrained model and uses slowdown to correct the additional deviation induced by guidance. This yields a principled framework for training-free guided generation for diffusion-based and related generative model families, together with convergence guarantees that clarify the roles of intermediate functional inequalities and guidance bias. Code is available at https://github.com/anitan0925/sald.

</details>


### [325] [Convergent Stochastic Training of Attention and Understanding LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07959)
*Zhengkai Sun,Dibyakanti Kumar,Alejandro F Frangi,Anirbit Mukherjee,Mingfei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在无数据分布和架构规模假设的前提下，严格证明了注意力层和浅层神经网络LoRA微调的可训练性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在机器学习中广泛应用，且大规模模型常采用低秩自适应（LoRA）以实现精度与参数量的平衡，但此类模型在随机优化方法下的可训练性缺乏严格的理论保证。

Method: 建立统一理论框架，证明在任意温和正则化下，注意力层和浅层网络LoRA的经验回归损失对应的吉布斯测度均满足庞加莱不等式，并引用近期关于随机微分方程（SDE）的结论，证明模仿SGD的SDE能最小化对应损失。

Result: 证明了注意力层和浅层神经网络LoRA在随机方法下具有可训练性，且该结论不依赖于数据分布或模型架构规模的假设。

Conclusion: 该研究为注意力机制和LoRA微调的训练稳定性提供了首个通用理论保证，丰富了深度学习优化理论。

Abstract: Transformers have revolutionized machine learning and deploying attention layers in the model is increasingly standard across a myriad of applications. Further, for large models, it is common to implement Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA), whereby a factorized parameterization of them is trained, to achieve a surprisingly beneficial accuracy-size trade-off. In this work, via a unified framework we rigorously establish trainability of such models under stochastic methods. We prove that for any mild regularization, the empirical regression loss on a attention layer and LoRA on a shallow neural net, both induce Poincaré inequality for the corresponding Gibbs' measure. Then it follows via invoking recent results that a certain SDE, which mimics the SGD, minimizes the corresponding losses. In both the cases, our first-of-its-kind results of trainability on attention and nets, do not rely on any assumptions on the data or the size of the architecture.

</details>


### [326] [Aggregation in conformal e-classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07963)
*Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了交叉共形e预测器及其改进方法，这些改进方法在聚合共形e预测器时更简单灵活且不牺牲有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合共形预测器是平衡预测效率、计算效率并保持有效性的标准方法。共形e预测器的重要优势在于更容易在不牺牲有效性的情况下进行聚合。

Method: 对交叉共形e预测这一现有聚合方法进行实验研究，并探讨其在概念上更简单、更灵活的改进版本。

Result: 通过实验研究验证了交叉共形e预测及其改进方法的性能表现。

Conclusion: 共形e预测器的改进聚合方法能够在保持有效性的同时提供更简单灵活的实现方案。

Abstract: Aggregating conformal predictors is a standard way of balancing their predictive and computational efficiency while retaining their validity, at least approximately. An important advantage of conformal e-predictors is that they are easier to aggregate without sacrificing their validity. This paper studies experimentally cross-conformal e-prediction, which is an existing method of aggregating conformal e-predictors, and its modifications that are conceptually simpler and more flexible.

</details>


### [327] [When Diffusion Model Can Ignore Dimension: An Entropy-Based Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07969)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的扩散模型收敛性分析视角，证明了对于高斯混合目标分布，离散化误差由潜在混合成分的香农熵控制，而非环境维度，从而解释了扩散模型在高维数据上的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在高维数据（如图像）上表现出色，且仅需少量反向时间步。然而，现有的收敛理论无法完全解释为何此类采样器在高维空间中仍保持高效。许多先验的KL保证将离散化误差以环境维度为界，而其他改进结果则用内在维度或几何结构假设替代了这种依赖。作者希望从信息论角度提供新的理论解释。

Method: 作者开发了一种基于信息论的扩散采样器收敛分析框架。通过以香农熵为核心度量，针对高斯混合目标分布进行理论推导，证明离散化误差受控于潜在混合成分的香农熵。该分析还扩展至离散目标分布，表明相关复杂度取决于目标分布的熵而非嵌入空间的维度。

Result: 对于高斯混合目标分布，离散化误差由潜在混合成分的香农熵控制，而非环境维度；主要步数复杂度与潜在熵呈线性关系，且仅与数据的二阶矩呈对数依赖关系。对于离散目标分布，相关复杂度取决于目标分布的熵而非嵌入空间的维度。

Conclusion: 扩散采样在高维空间中可以保持高效，前提是数据分布具有紧凑的潜在表示。这一结论与自然图像广泛被认为具有潜在表示的观点相符，为扩散模型在高维数据上的成功提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Diffusion models perform remarkably well on high-dimensional data such as images, often using only a modest number of reverse-time steps. Despite this practical success, existing convergence theory does not fully explain why such samplers remain efficient in high dimensions. Many prior KL guarantees bound the discretization error in terms of the ambient dimension, while other improved results replace this dependence using intrinsic-dimensional or geometric structure assumptions. In this work, we develop an alternative information-theoretic perspective on diffusion sampler convergence. We prove that, for Gaussian mixture targets, the discretization error is controlled by the Shannon entropy of the latent mixture component rather than by the ambient dimension. Consequently, the leading step complexity scales linearly with latent entropy and depends only logarithmically on the second moment of the data. Our analysis also extends to discrete target distributions, where the relevant complexity is the entropy of the target rather than the dimension of the embedding space. These results suggest that diffusion sampling can remain efficient in high-dimensional spaces when the data distribution admits a compact latent representation, as is widely believed to be the case for natural images.

</details>


### [328] [Self-Play Enhancement via Advantage-Weighted Refinement in Online Federated LLM Fine-Tuning with Real-Time Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07977)
*Seohyun Lee,Wenzhi Fang,Dong-Jun Han,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPEAR，一种高效的联邦大语言模型在线微调算法，通过反馈引导的自我博弈循环构建对比样本对进行训练，无需昂贵的组生成和真实标签上下文，仅依赖部分非答案反馈即可在资源受限的边缘设备上实现在线学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于反馈的学习系统存在明显局限：需要离线设置、依赖特权的真实标签上下文进行训练，且缺乏对联邦学习（FL）的考虑。而联邦学习非常适合在大量终端用户网络中整合外部反馈，但要求方法在资源受限的边缘设备上保持高效。

Method: 提出SPEAR（基于优势加权精化的自我博弈增强）算法。该方法利用反馈引导的自我博弈循环，针对每个提示自然地构建对比样本对，训练包含两个部分：(i) 对正确补全进行标准最大似然训练；(ii) 对错误补全的尾部token进行置信度加权的非似然训练。该方法无需昂贵的组生成和真实标签上下文，仅需部分、非答案反馈即可实现资源高效的在线训练。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上验证了SPEAR的有效性，实验结果表明其性能优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: SPEAR成功解决了现有反馈学习方法的局限性，实现了高效的联邦LLM在线微调，为在资源受限环境下利用外部反馈进行模型自改进提供了可行方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent works have advanced feedback-based learning systems, whereby a foundation model is able to intake incoming feedback (e.g., a user) to self-improve, creating a self-loop system of training. However, existing works are limited in needing to consider an offline setup to allow for such feedback-based methods, and are further limited in the need of requiring privileged ground-truth contexts for training. Moreover, there is limited consideration of federated learning (FL), which is particularly well-suited for incorporating external feedback across large networks of end users, for example, but requires methods to be efficient for training on resource-constrained edge devices. Therefore, we introduce SPEAR (Self-Play Enhancement via Advantage-Weighted Refinement), an efficient online learning algorithm for federated LLM fine-tuning. SPEAR utilizes a feedback-guided self-play loop to construct naturally contrastive pairs per prompt which are utilized to be trained on (i) standard maximum likelihood on correct completions and (ii) confidence-weighted unlikelihood on tail tokens of incorrect completions. Without the need of expensive group generations and ground-truth contexts for training (i.e., only partial, non-answer feedback), in contrast with existing works, SPEAR can be trained both online and in a resource-efficient manner. We validate SPEAR across various benchmark datasets, demonstrating its superior performance in comparison to state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/lee3296/SPEAR.

</details>


### [329] [Susceptibilities and Patterning: A Primer on Linear Response in Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07980)
*Chris Elliott,Daniel Murfet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于统计力学的神经网络易感性理论框架，通过后验协方差定义了易感性矩阵，用于量化数据扰动对模型结构的影响，并为模式识别问题提供了线性化解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解释神经网络的内在机制，需要一个理论基础来量化数据扰动如何影响模型组件和结构，从而理解数据模式与模型结构之间的对应关系。

Method: 基于统计力学中的涨落-耗散定理，将可观测量φ对数据扰动的易感性定义为后验期望的导数，该导数等于后验协方差。通过选择不同的可观测量，构建了影响矩阵（对应每样本损失）和结构易感性矩阵（对应组件局部化可观测量），并建立易感性矩阵与损失景观几何的联系。

Result: 易感性矩阵（相差nβ因子）是从数据分布到结构坐标映射的雅可比矩阵；其伪逆为模式识别问题提供了线性化解，即找到能产生所需结构变化的数据扰动。不同可观测量选择产生不同对象：每样本损失生成影响矩阵，组件局部化可观测量生成结构易感性矩阵。

Conclusion: 易感性理论为神经网络解释提供了统一的统计力学框架，将数据分布变化与模型结构变化通过雅可比映射联系起来，为理解神经网络的内在工作机制提供了理论工具和实证估计方法。

Abstract: These notes introduce the theory of susceptibilities as developed in [arXiv:2504.18274, arXiv:2601.12703] for interpreting neural networks. The susceptibility of an observable $φ$ to a data perturbation is defined as a derivative of a posterior expectation, which by the fluctuation--dissipation theorem equals a posterior covariance. Different choices of $φ$ yield different objects: per-sample losses give the influence matrix (the Bayesian influence function of [arXiv:2509.26544]), while component-localized observables give the structural susceptibility matrix that pairs model components with data patterns. The susceptibility matrix is (up to a factor of $nβ$) the Jacobian of the map from data distributions to structural coordinates; its pseudo-inverse provides a linearized solution to the patterning problem of [arXiv:2601.13548]: finding data perturbations that produce a desired structural change. We motivate the theory from its statistical-mechanical foundations, then give a detailed exposition of susceptibilities, their empirical estimators, and their connection to the geometry of the loss landscape.

</details>


### [330] [Bayesian Sensitivity of Causal Inference Estimators under Evidence-Based Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07993)
*Nikita Dhawan,Daniel Shen,Leonardo Cotta,Chris J. Maddison*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因果推断敏感性分析中仅关注最坏情况存在局限性，提出了一种基于先验信息的贝叶斯敏感性值（BSV）新方法，以更现实地评估估计对假设违背的敏感程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在观察性研究的因果推断中，现有敏感性分析框架通常关注假设的最坏情况变化。作者认为这种悲观准则往往缺乏信息量，或导致与先验世界知识相矛盾的结论。因此，需要一种更合理的敏感性评估方法。

Method: 作者首先推广了s-value框架以估计因果推断中三种常见假设的敏感性；随后提出了贝叶斯敏感性值（BSV），该方法基于现实证据构建先验，计算估计对假设违背的期望敏感性，并使用蒙特卡洛近似进行估计。

Result: 实证研究发现，基于最坏情况的敏感性结论确实依赖于对数据生成过程的不切实际的改变；提出的BSV方法能够有效评估假设违背的敏感性，并在一项关于糖尿病治疗对减重效果的研究中展示了其应用价值。

Conclusion: 传统的最坏情况敏感性分析可能过于保守，BSV方法提供了一种更具信息量的替代方案，能够结合先验知识更合理地评估因果结论的稳健性。

Abstract: Causal inference, especially in observational studies, relies on untestable assumptions about the true data-generating process. Sensitivity analysis helps us determine how robust our conclusions are when we alter these underlying assumptions. Existing frameworks for sensitivity analysis are concerned with worst-case changes in assumptions. In this work, we argue that using such pessimistic criteria can often become uninformative or lead to conclusions contradicting our prior knowledge about the world. To demonstrate this claim, we generalize the recent s-value framework (Gupta & Rothenhäusler, 2023) to estimate the sensitivity of three different common assumptions in causal inference. Empirically, we find that, indeed, worst-case conclusions about sensitivity can rely on unrealistic changes in the data-generating process. To overcome this, we extend the s-value framework with a new sensitivity analysis criterion: Bayesian Sensitivity Value (BSV), which computes the expected sensitivity of an estimate to assumption violations under priors constructed from real-world evidence. We use Monte Carlo approximations to estimate this quantity and illustrate its applicability in an observational study on the effect of diabetes treatments on weight loss.

</details>


### [331] [STEPS: A Temporal Smooth Error Propagation Solver on the Manifolds for Test-Time Adaptation in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08005)
*Jiaqi Liu,Yifan Ouyang,Zhifei Song,Sim Kuan Goh,Ashwaq Qasem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STEPS方法，通过将时间序列预测的测试时适应问题重构为时间流形上的Dirichlet边界值问题，实现了在稀疏和噪声条件下稳定且鲁棒的误差传播与修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时适应（TTA）旨在解决分布偏移下的时间序列预测问题，但在源无关的在线设置中，适应信号短、时间相关性强且可能存在噪声。现有方法在揭示前缀稀疏或受污染时，面临可识别性弱、误差累积和长程修正不稳定的问题。

Method: STEPS将预测TTA建模为时间流形上的Dirichlet边界值问题，以揭示前缀的误差作为未知未来误差场的边界条件。方法包含三个核心组件：局部求解器在时间平滑性约束下传播前缀误差；全局求解器检索稳定的跨窗口误差记忆；时空流形融合（SMF）将两者整合生成最终修正场。

Result: 在六个标准基准和四种冻结骨干网络上，STEPS相比零样本骨干网络实现了平均26.82%的相对MSE降低，相比最强的TTA基线方法提升了12.77%。稀疏前缀和污染测试进一步证实了STEPS在有限和噪声前缀条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: STEPS成功地将物理启发式的边界值问题求解框架引入时间序列预测的测试时适应领域，通过局部-全局协同求解机制有效解决了稀疏和噪声场景下的误差传播问题，为实际部署环境中的鲁棒预测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) aims to improve time series forecasting under distribution shifts by using limited observations revealed during inference. However, forecasting TTA must operate in a source-free online setting, where the adaptation signal is short, temporally correlated, and potentially noisy. Existing methods can therefore suffer from weak identifiability, error accumulation, and unstable long-horizon corrections when the revealed prefix is sparse or contaminated. To address these issues, we propose STEPS, a Smooth Temporal Error Propagation Solver for TTA in time-series forecasting. STEPS reformulates forecasting TTA as a Dirichlet Boundary Value Problem on a temporal manifold, where the revealed prefix error serves as the boundary condition for the unknown future error field. Then, STEPS solves a smooth and bounded correction field in prediction space: a Local Solver propagates prefix errors under temporal smoothness, a Global Solver retrieves stable cross-window error memory and Spatiotemporal Manifold Fusion (SMF) integrates both solutions into the final correction. Across six standard benchmarks and four frozen backbones, STEPS achieves an average relative MSE reduction of 26.82% over the zero-shot backbone, exceeding the strongest compared TTA baseline by 12.77%. Additional sparse prefix and contamination tests confirm the robustness of STEPS under limited and noisy prefixes.

</details>


### [332] [Interpreting Reinforcement Learning Agents with Susceptibilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08007)
*Chris Elliott,Einar Urdshals,David Quarel,Daniel Murfet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将“敏感度”技术从神经网络可解释性推广至深度强化学习中的后悔值设置，并在网格世界模型中验证其能揭示仅通过策略学习无法检测到的模型内部发展特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的神经网络可解释性技术（如敏感度分析）主要应用于监督学习场景，针对损失函数的扰动进行研究。然而，深度强化学习（DRL）具有独特的训练动态和目标函数（后悔值），目前缺乏对其内部表征发展过程的有效分析工具。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索敏感度分析在DRL可解释性中的适用性和价值。

Method: 作者将原本应用于神经网络后验期望值的敏感度技术泛化到深度强化学习的后悔值设置中。研究采用一个虽简单但展现出非平凡阶段式发展的网格世界模型作为实验环境。通过计算和分析敏感度，研究模型在参数空间中的内部发展特征，并使用激活引导技术对结果进行验证，同时探讨了该方法向RLHF后训练扩展的可能性。

Result: 实验结果表明，敏感度分析能够揭示深度强化学习模型在参数空间中的内部发展特征，这些特征仅通过观察学习策略的发展是无法检测到的。激活引导实验验证了这些发现的有效性，证明了敏感度作为DRL可解释性工具的实用性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将敏感度技术推广至深度强化学习领域，证明了其作为分析工具在揭示模型内部发展机制方面的独特价值。该框架不仅适用于基础的强化学习模型，还可扩展至RLHF后训练等复杂场景，为深度强化学习的可解释性研究提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: Susceptibilities are a technique for neural network interpretability that studies the response of posterior expectation values of observables to perturbations of the loss. We generalize this construction to the setting of the regret in deep reinforcement learning and investigate the utility of susceptibilities in a simple gridworld model that nevertheless exhibits non-trivial stagewise development. We argue that susceptibilities reveal internal features of the development of the model in parameter space that one cannot detect purely by studying the development of the learned policy. We validate these results with activation-steering, and discuss the framework's extension to RLHF post-training.

</details>


### [333] [Adaptive Domain Decomposition Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Traffic State Estimation with Sparse Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08028)
*Eunhan Ka,Ludovic Leclercq,Satish V. Ukkusuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应区域分解物理信息神经网络（ADD-PINN），通过残差引导的空间分解解决稀疏固定传感器交通状态估计中PINN过度平滑激波的问题，在I-24 MOTION数据集上优于基线方法且训练速度提升2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通状态估计中，基于LWR模型的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在稀疏固定传感器条件下容易过度平滑激波，导致对交通流突变现象的重建精度不足，亟需一种能有效捕捉局部过渡区域的方法。

Method: 提出ADD-PINN两阶段框架：首先训练粗粒度全局PINN；然后利用空间残差分布指导子区域边界划分和子网络初始化，实现区域分解模式；同时引入数据驱动的激波指示器，在过渡区域证据较弱时回退到单域模式。

Result: 在I-24 MOTION五天数据、五种传感器配置、每种配置10个随机种子的1500次实验中，ADD-PINN在25个配置中的18个、15个稀疏感知案例中的14个取得最低相对L2误差，训练速度比XPINN基线快2.4倍。NGSIM对照实验中激波指示器在全部50次运行中抑制了分解，单域回退模式在所有配置中排名第一。

Conclusion: 残差引导的空间分解是稀疏固定传感器条件下离线交通重建的有效PINN设计策略，当稀疏传感与局部过渡区域重合时表现优异，同时空间专用分解可作为固定传感器交通重建的有效默认设置。

Abstract: Traffic state estimation from sparse fixed sensors is challenging because physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) tend to over-smooth the shockwaves admitted by the Lighthill-Whitham-Richards (LWR) model. This study proposes Adaptive Domain Decomposition Physics-Informed Neural Networks (ADD-PINN), a two-stage residual-guided framework for LWR-based offline speed-field reconstruction. A coarse global PINN is first trained; its spatial residual profile is then used to place subdomain boundaries and initialize child subnetworks in a decomposition-enabled mode, while a data-driven shock indicator can retain a single-domain fallback when localized evidence of transition is weak. The primary offline I-24 MOTION evaluation spans five days, five sensor configurations, and ten seeds per configuration, yielding 1,500 runs in total. Against neural and physics-informed baselines, ADD-PINN attains the lowest relative L2 error in 18 of 25 configurations and in 14 of 15 sparse-sensing cases, while training 2.4 times faster than the extended PINN (XPINN) baseline. An ablation study supports spatial-only decomposition as an effective default for fixed-sensor traffic reconstruction in the evaluated settings. Supplementary Next Generation Simulation (NGSIM) experiments serve as a negative control: the shock indicator suppresses decomposition in all 50 runs, and the default single-domain fallback ranks first across all sensor configurations. These results support residual-guided spatial decomposition as an effective PINN-family design for offline reconstruction when sparse fixed sensing coincides with localized transition regions.

</details>


### [334] [Don't Get Your Kroneckers in a Twist: Gaussian Processes on High-Dimensional Incomplete Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08036)
*Mads Greisen Højlund,August Smart Lykke-Møller,Henry Moss,Ove Christiansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CUTS-GPR方法，通过快速核矩阵-向量乘积实现高维高斯过程回归的数值精确计算，在数十亿数据点和数千维度上展现近线性扩展性，成功应用于计算化学中高维势能面的贝叶斯建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维设置下的高斯过程回归（GPR）面临计算复杂度的巨大挑战，传统方法难以扩展到大规模数据和高维度，这限制了其在计算化学等领域（如高维势能面建模）的应用。

Method: 结合加性核（additive kernel）与不完整网格（incomplete grid），利用所得核矩阵的特殊结构，实现极快的核矩阵-向量乘积运算，该运算随训练数据量N呈近线性或线性扩展，随维度D呈低阶多项式扩展。

Result: 矩阵-向量乘积在数十亿数据点和数千维度的基准测试中展现优异扩展性；完整GPR计算（含超参数优化）在N=447,265、D=24时仅需数小时完成。

Conclusion: CUTS-GPR成功克服了高维GPR的计算瓶颈，使高维势能面的贝叶斯建模成为可能，解决了计算化学领域长期存在的挑战。

Abstract: We introduce CUTS-GPR, a new method for performing numerically exact Gaussian process regression (GPR) in high-dimensional settings. The key component of CUTS-GPR is an extremely fast kernel matrix-vector product, which exhibits near-linear or even linear scaling with the amount of training data, $N$, and low-order polynomial scaling with dimensionality, $D$. This is obtained by combining an additive kernel with an incomplete grid and exploiting the resulting structure of the kernel matrix. We demonstrate the scalability of the matrix-vector product by running benchmarks with billions of data points and thousands of dimensions. Full GPR calculations, including hyperparameter optimization, are completed in a matter of hours for $N = 447 265$ and $D = 24$. We demonstrate that our CUTS-GPR enables Bayesian modeling of high-dimensional potential energy surfaces - a longstanding challenge in computational chemistry.

</details>


### [335] [Reinforcement Learning for Exponential Utility: Algorithms and Convergence in Discounted MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08053)
*Gugan Thoppe,L. A. Prashanth,Ankur Naskar,Sanjay Bhat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对折扣MDP中的指数效用优化问题，提出了两种基于值函数的无模型强化学习算法，并证明了其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在折扣马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中，针对指数效用优化的强化学习（RL）缺乏基于值函数的原则性算法。本文旨在填补这一空白，解决固定风险厌恶设定下的算法缺失问题。

Method: 基于指数效用的Bellman型方程，推导了两种Q值形式的扩展，证明了相关算子在$L_\infty$和sup-log/Thompson度量下分别为收缩映射。据此提出了两种无模型算法：一种双时间尺度Q学习风格算法，以及一种由次线性幂律算子控制的单时间尺度算法。

Result: 证明了所诱导的贪婪平稳策略在平稳策略中对指数效用目标是最优的。对于双时间尺度算法，建立了几乎必然收敛性并通过时间尺度分离给出了有限时间收敛速率。对于单时间尺度算法，利用局部Lipschitz性、单调性、齐次性和Dini导数等性质证明了其收敛性，并给出了标量情形的有限时间分析。

Conclusion: 本文为指数效用目标下的基于值函数的强化学习奠定了理论基础，提供了结构化分析以及收敛性保证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for exponential-utility optimization in discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) lacks principled value-based algorithms. We address this gap in the fixed risk-aversion setting. Building on the Bellman-type equation for exponential utility studied in \cite{porteus1975optimality}, we derive two Q-value-style extensions and show that the associated operators are contractions in the $L_\infty$ and sup-log/Thompson metrics, respectively. We characterize their fixed points and prove that the induced greedy stationary policy is optimal for the exponential-utility objective among stationary policies. These structural results lead to two model-free algorithms: a two-timescale Q-learning--style algorithm, for which we establish almost-sure convergence and provide finite-time convergence rates via timescale separation, and a one-timescale algorithm governed by a sublinear power-law operator. Since the latter does not admit a global contraction in standard metrics, we prove its convergence using delicate arguments based on local Lipschitzness, monotonicity, homogeneity, and Dini derivatives, and provide a scalar finite-time analysis that highlights the challenges in obtaining convergence rates in the vector case. Our work provides a foundation for value-based RL under exponential-utility objectives.

</details>


### [336] [GRAPHLCP: Structure-Aware Localized Conformal Prediction on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08074)
*Peyman Baghershahi,Fangxin Wang,Debmalya Mandal,Sourav Medya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRAPHLCP框架，将图拓扑结构和节点间依赖关系融入局部化共形预测中，解决了图神经网络不确定性量化中的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测虽然能提供有限样本保证的不确定性量化，但在图神经网络中应用困难，因为图的组合特性导致预测不确定性不足且嵌入缺乏区分性；现有方法主要依赖嵌入空间邻近性进行局部化，在图上不可靠且预测集效率低。

Method: 提出GRAPHLCP框架：(1)引入特征感知的稠密化步骤缓解稀疏图中的局部性偏差；(2)基于个性化PageRank的核计算建模结构邻近性；(3)实现拓扑相关的锚点采样和校准加权，捕获局部和长程依赖关系。

Result: 在多个回归和分类数据集上的实验表明，GRAPHLCP在保证有限样本边缘覆盖率的同时，在各种条件场景下有效地获得了良好的测试条件覆盖率。

Conclusion: GRAPHLCP成功将图拓扑显式融入共形预测的局部化和加权过程，为图神经网络提供了有效的不确定性量化方法，在保证理论覆盖的同时提升了预测集效率。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides a distribution-free approach to uncertainty quantification with finite-sample guarantees. However, applying CP to graph neural networks (GNNs) remains challenging as the combinatorial nature of graphs often leads to insufficiently certain predictions and indiscriminative embeddings. Existing methods primarily rely on embedding-space proximity for localization, which can be unreliable for graphs and yield inefficient prediction sets. We propose GRAPHLCP, a proximity-based localized CP framework that explicitly incorporates graph topology and inter-node dependencies into localization and weighting. Our approach introduces a feature-aware densification step to mitigate locality bias in sparse graphs, followed by a Personalized PageRank-based kernel computation to model structural proximity. This enables topology-dependent anchor sampling and calibration weighting that captures both local and long-range dependencies. Extensive experiments on several regression and classification datasets demonstrate that GRAPHLCP guarantees marginal coverage with finite samples while efficiently attaining favorable test conditional coverage across various conditioning scenarios.

</details>


### [337] [Zero-Shot Imagined Speech Decoding via Imagined-to-Listened MEG Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08075)
*Maryam Maghsoudi,Shihab Shamma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用听觉MEG信号辅助解码想象语音的新方法，通过跨条件映射实现了在留被试上的想象语音解码，性能显著高于随机水平且随训练数据量增加而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式脑信号解码想象语音面临两大挑战：想象语音数据集稀缺，且跨被试和跨会话的时间对齐困难。由于听觉语音数据更丰富且标注更可靠，研究者希望利用听觉数据来辅助想象语音的解码。

Method: 1. 数据采集：采集训练有素的音乐家在节奏旋律和语音刺激下的成对听觉与想象MEG信号，利用音乐家的节奏感改善时间对齐。
2. 三阶段解码流水线：
   - 第一阶段：训练6种线性与神经网络模型，将想象MEG响应映射到听觉MEG响应，并在未见被试上验证预测的听觉响应保留刺激特异性信息；
   - 第二阶段：仅在听觉MEG响应上训练对比词解码器，评估四种嵌入策略（语义、声学、音素等）；
   - 第三阶段：将留被试的想象MEG响应通过映射流水线转换为预测听觉响应，再用听觉解码器进行解码，采用基于排序的分析方法。

Result: 1. 预测的听觉响应成功保留了刺激特异性信息，显著优于基于未见被试的零基线；
2. 想象词语的解码准确率显著高于随机水平；
3. 性能随训练数据量增加而提升，证明方法具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功展示了利用听觉MEG信号辅助解码想象语音的概念验证，所有评估均在留被试上进行。该方法为现实脑机接口场景提供了一种可扩展的、非侵入式的想象语音解码方案。

Abstract: Decoding imagined speech from non-invasive brain recordings is challenging because imagined datasets are scarce and difficult to align temporally across subjects and sessions In this work, we propose a new approach to the decoding of imagined speech that leverages the richer and more reliably labeled recordings during listening to speech. We collected paired listened and imagined MEG recordings to rhythmic melodic and spoken stimuli from trained musicians. Using trained musicians helped improve temporal alignment across conditions. We then developed a three-stage decoding pipeline that revealed consistent and meaningful relationships between neural activity evoked by imagining and listening to the same stimuli. First, we trained six linear and neural models to map imagined MEG responses to listened responses. We evaluated these models against a null baseline from unseen subjects to validate that the predicted-listening responses preserve stimulus-specific information. In the second stage, we trained a contrastive word decoder exclusively on the listened MEG responses, and evaluated it using four embedding strategies including semantic, acoustic, and phonetic representations. In the third stage, we process the imagined MEG responses from held-out subjects through the mapping pipeline to compute the corresponding listening responses that are then decoded by the listened decoder. Using rank-based analysis, we show that the imagined words are decodable significantly above chance. We shall report here the results of a proof-of-concept implementation to decode imagined speech, where all evaluations are performed on held-out subjects. We also demonstrate that performance improves with training data size, suggesting that this approach is scalable and can directly be made applicable to realistic brain-computer interface scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [338] [EULER-ADAS: Energy-Efficient & SIMD-Unified Logarithmic-Posit Engine for Precision-Reconfigurable Approximate ADAS Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06875)
*Mukul Lokhande,Ratko Pilipovic,Omkar Kokane,Adam Teman,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出EULER-ADAS，一种面向高级驾驶辅助系统（ADAS）的对数有界Posit神经网络计算引擎，通过有界域Posit表示、阶段自适应对数尾数乘法及SIMD共享累加路径，在FPGA上相比精确Posit引擎最多减少41.4% LUT、76.1%延迟和71.9%功耗，能效延迟积提升最高10倍，应用精度损失约1.5个百分点，适合低功耗实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级驾驶辅助系统（ADAS）的神经网络计算引擎需要在严格的功耗和面积约束下实现低延迟推理。Posit算术因其在低精度下提供高数值保真度而具有吸引力，但其可变长度域编码增加了编解码成本，并使数据通路易受大域字段故障影响，这限制了其在ADAS加速器中的应用。

Method: 提出EULER-ADAS神经网络计算引擎，结合三项关键技术：1）有界域Posit表示以降低编解码复杂度和故障影响；2）阶段自适应对数尾数乘法配合比特截断，简化乘法运算；3）SIMD共享quire累加路径，支持Posit-(8,0)、Posit-(16,1)和Posit-(32,2)的统一执行。该架构无需复制精度特定硬件即可支持4xPosit-8、2xPosit-16或1xPosit-32操作。

Result: FPGA实现表明，相比精确Posit神经网络计算引擎，所提配置最多减少41.4% LUT数量、76.1%延迟和71.9%功耗，能效延迟积相比基4 Booth Posit乘法器提升最高10倍。在28nm CMOS工艺下，有界变体占用0.013-0.016 mm²面积，功耗19.8-22.1 mW，工作频率最高1.84 GHz。应用级评估显示Posit-16和Posit-32配置相比FP32精度损失约1.5个百分点。TinyYOLOv3原型在Pynq-Z2上实现78 ms延迟、0.29 W功耗和22.6 mJ/帧能耗。

Conclusion: EULER-ADAS通过有界域Posit表示和对数乘法优化，成功平衡了数值精度、硬件效率和可靠性，为ADAS应用提供了一种可行的低功耗实时推理解决方案，在保持可接受精度损失的同时显著提升了能效和降低了硬件开销。

Abstract: Advanced driver-assistance systems (ADAS) require neural compute engines that deliver low-latency inference under strict power and area constraints. Posit arithmetic is attractive for such accelerators because it provides high numerical fidelity at low precision, but its variable-length regime encoding increases encode/decode cost and exposes the datapath to large regime-field fault effects. This paper presents EULER-ADAS, a SIMD-enabled logarithmic bounded-Posit neural compute engine for energyefficient and reliability-aware ADAS acceleration. The proposed datapath combines bounded-regime Posit representation, stageadaptive logarithmic mantissa multiplication with bit truncation, and a SIMD-shared quire accumulation path supporting Posit- (8,0), Posit-(16,1), and Posit-(32,2) execution. The unified architecture enables 4xPosit-8, 2xPosit-16, or 1xPosit-32 operation without duplicating precision-specific hardware. FPGA implementation shows that the proposed configurations reduce LUT count by up to 41.4%, delay by up to 76.1%, and power by up to 71.9% relative to exact Posit neural compute engines, while achieving up to 10x lower energy-delay product than radix-4 Booth-based Posit multipliers. In 28-nm CMOS, the bounded variants occupy 0.013-0.016 mm2 , consume 19.8-22.1 mW, and operate at up to 1.84 GHz. Application-level evaluation across image-classification, ADAS, and edge-inference workloads shows that the evaluated Posit-16 and Posit-32 configurations remain within about 1.5 percentage points of FP32 accuracy. A TinyYOLOv3 prototype on Pynq-Z2 achieves 78 ms latency at 0.29 W and 22.6 mJ/frame, demonstrating the suitability of EULERADAS for low-power real-time ADAS inference.

</details>


### [339] [CARMEN: CORDIC-Accelerated Resource-Efficient Multi-Precision Inference Engine for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06878)
*Sonu Kumar,Mukul Lokhande,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma,Adam Teman*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出CARMEN运行时自适应、CORDIC加速的多精度向量引擎，通过动态调整CORDIC迭代深度实现精度与效率的灵活权衡，在28nm ASIC上实现4.83 TOPS/mm²计算密度和11.67 TOPS/W能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习推理对计算精度和资源效率有不同需求，传统硬件难以在不修改架构的情况下动态调整计算精度。论文利用CORDIC迭代深度直接控制计算精度的特性，旨在实现运行时自适应的精度-效率权衡。

Method: 设计基于CORDIC的低资源迭代型MAC单元，结合时间复用多激活函数模块，支持8/16位灵活精度；通过动态控制CORDIC迭代深度在近似模式与精确模式间切换，无需硬件修改即可适配不同精度需求。

Result: 28nm CMOS ASIC实现中，单MAC阶段计算周期减少33%、功耗降低21%；256-PE配置达到4.83 TOPS/mm²计算密度和11.67 TOPS/W能效；FPGA平台（PynqZ2）实时目标检测延迟154.6ms，功耗0.43W。

Conclusion: CORDIC迭代深度的运行时调控为深度学习推理提供了一种有效的精度-效率权衡机制，CARMEN架构在资源效率和能效方面展现优越性，适合资源受限的边缘AI部署场景。

Abstract: This paper presents CARMEN, a runtime-adaptive, CORDIC-accelerated multi-precision vector engine for resource-efficient deep learning inference. The key insight is that CORDIC iteration depth directly governs computational accuracy, enabling dynamic switching between approximate and accurate execution modes without hardware modification. The architecture integrates a low-resource iterative CORDIC-based MAC unit with a time-multiplexed multi-activation function block, supporting flexible 8/16-bit precision and high hardware utilization. ASIC implementation in 28 nm CMOS achieves up to 33% reduction in computation cycles and 21% power savings per MAC stage; a 256-PE configuration delivers 4.83 TOPS/mm2 compute density and 11.67 TOPS/W energy efficiency. FPGA deployment on PynqZ2 validates 154.6 ms latency at 0.43 W for real-time object detection.

</details>


### [340] [Bridging the Last Mile of Circuit Design: PostEDA-Bench, a Hierarchical Benchmark for PPA Convergence and DRC Fixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06936)
*Pengju Liu,Nuo Xu,Jinwei Tang,Yu Cao,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了PostEDA-Bench，首个针对EDA流程“最后一公里”（DRC修复与PPA收敛）的分层基准测试，发现现有LLM智能体在复杂推理任务上表现不佳，视觉增强有效，且权衡推理是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EDA-LLM基准测试完全忽略了设计规则检查（DRC）修复任务，且依赖单一工具链的扁平层级结构，无法评估LLM智能体在实际芯片设计“最后一公里”中的表现，亟需一个更全面的评测基准。

Method: 构建PostEDA-Bench分层基准测试，包含145个任务，涵盖DRC-Essential、DRC-Reasoning、PPA-Mono和PPA-Multi四个类别，支持多种EDA工具链并提供机器可检查的评估机制；在多种智能体架构下测试了8个商业及开源LLM。

Result: 智能体在合成类的DRC-Essential和单目标PPA-Mono任务上表现尚可，但在实际的DRC-Reasoning（最佳成功率36.66%）和多目标PPA-Multi（最佳成功率20.00%）任务上性能急剧下降；视觉增强持续提升DRC任务表现；多目标优化的主要瓶颈是权衡推理能力而非参数知识。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在处理实际EDA后端任务时仍面临巨大挑战，特别是在复杂推理和多目标权衡方面，视觉增强是有效的改进方向，未来需重点提升模型的权衡推理能力。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly applied to the "last mile" of Electronic Design Automation (EDA): repairing residual sign-off Design Rule Check (DRC) violations and converging Power-Performance-Area (PPA) targets after tool runs. Existing EDA-LLM benchmarks, however, omit DRC fixing entirely and rely on flat hierarchies tied to a single toolchain. We introduce PostEDA-Bench, a hierarchical benchmark with 145 tasks across DRC-Essential, DRC-Reasoning, PPA-Mono, and PPA-Multi, supported by EDA toolchains with machine-checkable evaluation. Across eight commercial and open-source LLMs under multiple agent scaffolds, we find that agents handle synthetic DRC-Essential and single-objective PPA-Mono reasonably well but degrade sharply on the more practical DRC-Reasoning, where the best success rate is 36.66%, and PPA-Multi, where the best success rate is 20.00%; vision augmentation consistently enhances DRC-Bench; and trade-off reasoning, rather than knob knowledge, is the dominant PPA-Multi bottleneck.

</details>


### [341] [EDA-Schema-V2: A Multimodal Schema, Open Datasets, and Benchmarks for Machine Learning in Digital Physical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06952)
*Pratik Shrestha,Alec Aversa,Ioannis Savidis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了EDA-Schema-V2，一个用于数字物理设计的开源多模态框架，并基于该框架生成了一个包含7776个设计实例的大规模公开数据集，为ML在EDA领域的研究提供了标准化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着CMOS工艺持续微缩，集成电路复杂度显著增加，机器学习（ML）在电子设计自动化（EDA）中的应用备受关注。然而，开源和标准化数据集的匮乏严重限制了ML研究的互操作性、可比性和可复现性。

Method: 提出了开源多模态框架EDA-Schema-V2，用于表征和分析数字物理设计流程（包括逻辑综合、布局规划、布局、时钟网络综合和布线）中的物理属性与结果质量指标。利用OpenROAD工具流结合多种开源工艺设计包（SkyWater 130nm、Nangate 45nm等），对IWLS'05基准测试套件进行参数扫描，生成大规模数据集。

Result: 构建了包含7776个设计实例的数据集，涵盖18个基准电路，包含超过2.75亿个门、7500万条网络和3600万条提取的时序路径。定义了涵盖时序、功耗、面积和布线指标的12个代表性预测任务，并提供了表征设计流程各阶段间可预测性的基线分析。

Conclusion: 该工作通过公开发布标准化数据集和基线结果，为数字物理设计中的ML研究提供了可复现的基础，建立了评估ML方法的标准化基准，促进了该领域的互操作性与可比性发展。

Abstract: The continuous scaling of CMOS technology has significantly increased the complexity of very large-scale integrated circuits, driving interest in applying machine learning (ML) to electronic design automation (EDA). However, the limited availability of open and standardized datasets limits interoperability, comparability, and reproducibility in ML-based research. This paper introduces EDA-Schema-V2, an open multimodal schema that provides a structured framework for representing and analyzing datasets in digital physical design. The schema includes representations of physical attributes and quality-of-results metrics across multiple stages of the design flow, including logic synthesis, floorplanning, placement, clock network synthesis, and routing.
  Utilizing the SkyWater 130nm, Nangate 45nm, IHP SG13G2 130nm, and ASAP 7nm open-source process design kits with the OpenROAD tool flow, datasets of physical circuit designs from the IWLS'05 benchmark suite are generated and analyzed. The dataset comprises 7,776 design instances spanning 18 benchmark circuits and includes stage-resolved representations from synthesis through detailed routing, generated through parameter sweeps over clock period, core utilization, and aspect ratio. The dataset contains over 275 million gates, 75 million nets, and more than 36 million extracted timing paths. In addition, twelve representative prediction tasks spanning timing, power, area, and routing metrics are identified, along with baseline analyses that characterize stage-to-stage predictability across the design flow. The resulting datasets and baselines are publicly released to support reproducible ML research and establish standardized benchmarks for evaluating ML-based approaches in digital physical design.

</details>


### [342] [TransDot: An Area-efficient Reconfigurable Floating-Point Unit for Trans-Precision Dot-Product Accumulation for FPGA AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07245)
*Jiayi Wang,Maohua Nie,Sin-Chen Lin,C. -J. Richard Shi,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TransDot，一种可重构浮点单元，通过统一多精度SIMD FMA和跨精度点积累加，在共享数据通路中实现了FP16/FP8/FP4到FP32的高效计算，相比FPnew基线在FP16和FP8 DPA下分别提升1.46倍和2.92倍面积效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用FPGA（如AMD Versal）中的AI引擎利用低精度打包SIMD融合乘累加（FMA）获得吞吐量增益，但保持数值稳定性的跨精度FMA（如FP16乘法结果累加到FP32累加器）受限于最高精度、最低吞吐量的操作。点积累加（DPA）可充分利用输入输出带宽和计算资源，但现有开源FPU（如FPnew）不支持DPA，且通过复制独立FMA通道实现SIMD FMA，导致面积增加、共享算术资源利用不足及DPA集成复杂。

Method: 提出TransDot可重构FPU，在共享可重构数据通路中统一多精度SIMD FMA和跨精度DPA。设计扩展支持2项FP16、4项FP8和8项FP4点积累加到FP32，通过可重构子组件实现资源共享和灵活精度转换。

Result: TransDot通过FP32累加的DPA实现FP16 2倍、FP8 4倍、FP4 8倍吞吐量；FP16 DPA和FP8 DPA分别实现1.46倍和2.92倍面积效率提升；相比FPnew基线平均面积增大37.3%，点积模式增加一个流水线阶段。

Conclusion: TransDot的面积高效设计证明了其在下一代AMD Versal AI引擎中可扩展部署的可行性，为跨精度计算提供了统一、高效的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Commercial FPGAs, such as AMD Versal devices, increasingly incorporate AI engines that exploit low-precision packed-SIMD fused multiply-accumulate (FMA) to achieve proportional throughput gains. However, trans-precision FMA (e.g., multiplying two FP16 numbers and adding their result to an FP32 accumulator), which preserves numerical stability by accumulating in higher precision, remains bottlenecked by the highest-precision, lowest-throughput operation. Dot-product accumulation (DPA) (e.g., performing a dot-product on two 4-element FP8 vectors and adding its result to an FP32 accumulator) can fully utilize the input/output bandwidth and computational resources. Existing flexible open-source FPUs, such as FPnew, do not support DPA and implement SIMD FMA on low-precision formats by replicating independent FMA lanes, which increases area, underutilizes shared arithmetic resources, and complicates the integration of DPA operations. This paper presents TransDot, a reconfigurable FPU that unifies multi-precision SIMD FMA and trans-precision DPA within a shared, reconfigurable datapath. TransDot extends the baseline design with 2-term FP16, 4-term FP8, and 8-term FP4 dot-product accumulation into FP32 using reconfigurable subcomponents. Evaluation shows that TransDot delivers 2$\times$ FP16, 4$\times$ FP8, and 8$\times$ FP4 throughput via DPA with FP32 accumulation, and 1.46$\times$ area efficiency in FP16 DPA and 2.92$\times$ area efficiency in FP8 DPA, at the cost of 37.3% larger area on average and an additional pipeline stage in dot-product mode compared to the FPnew baseline. These results demonstrate that TransDot's area-efficient design enables scalable deployment in next-generation AMD Versal AI engines.

</details>


### [343] [TREA: Low-precision Time-Multiplexed, Resource-Efficient Edge Accelerator for Object Detection and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07321)
*Vijay Pratap Sharma,Mukul Lokhande,Ratko Pilipovic,Omkar Kokane,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TREA，一种面向边缘视觉平台的高效AI加速器，通过双精度MAC单元、结构化剪枝策略和可配置激活函数核心，在严格的面积-功耗-延迟约束下实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘视觉平台在目标检测和分类任务中面临严格的面积、功耗和延迟约束。传统加速器存在乘法器开销大、累加器位宽高、硬件利用率低以及缺乏灵活性等问题，难以满足边缘场景的实时处理需求。

Method: 提出TREA加速器架构，包含三大核心创新：1）基于最高有效位优先（MSDF）移位加法计算的双精度（4/8位）SIMD乘累加（DQ-MAC）单元，支持运行时位截断，消除传统乘法器开销；2）与SIMD数据通路协同设计的结构化硬件感知剪枝（SHARP）策略，实现约50%的结构化稀疏性同时保持完整MAC利用率；3）可配置CORDIC非线性激活函数（RQ-NAF）核心，采用9级流水线支持Sigmoid、Tanh和ReLU，通过时间复用实现硬件重用。

Result: DQ-MAC单元支持每周期4个FxP4或1个FxP8操作，实现高达4倍吞吐量提升且无硬件重复；3x3卷积核在FxP4模式下仅需1周期（对比FxP8的9周期），5x5核仅需3周期（对比25周期），核级延迟降低高达9倍；100个SIMD DQ-MAC单元的1D阵列设计实现了显著的硬件复用和复杂度降低。

Conclusion: TREA加速器在延迟、硬件利用率和能效方面相比传统固定精度和不可配置加速器取得显著改进，验证了其作为实时边缘视觉工作负载有效解决方案的价值，为边缘AI应用提供了高效、灵活的硬件平台。

Abstract: This work presents TREA, a low-precision time-multiplexed and resource-efficient edge-AI accelerator for object detection and classification, targeting stringent area-power-latency constraints of edge vision platforms. The proposed architecture integrates a dual-precision (4/8-bit) SIMD multiply-accumulate (DQ-MAC) unit based on most-significant-digit-first (MSDF) shift-and-add computation with run-time bit truncation, eliminating conventional multiplier overhead and reducing accumulator bit-width. The DQ-MAC supports 4x FxP4 or 1x FxP8 operations per cycle, achieving up to 4x throughput improvement without hardware duplication. A structured hardware-aware reductive pruning (SHARP) strategy is co-designed with the SIMD datapath, enabling near 50% structured sparsity while maintaining full MAC utilization. This allows a 3x3 convolution kernel to be computed in 1 cycle in FxP4 mode compared to 9 cycles in FxP8, and a 5x5 kernel in 3 cycles versus 25 cycles, yielding up to 9x latency reduction at the kernel level. The accelerator further incorporates a reconfigurable CORDIC-based nonlinear activation function (RQ-NAF) core with a 9-stage pipeline, supporting Sigmoid, Tanh, and ReLU at one output per cycle after pipeline fill, while enabling (N-1) hardware reuse through time-multiplexing. The complete TREA architecture employs a 1D array of 100 SIMD DQ-MAC units with layer-wise hardware reuse, significantly reducing area and control complexity. Experimental results demonstrate substantial improvements in latency, hardware utilization, and energy efficiency compared to conventional fixed-precision and non-reconfigurable accelerators, validating TREA as an effective solution for real-time edge vision workloads.

</details>


### [344] [Effective and Memory-Efficient Alternatives to ECC for Reliable Large-Scale DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07417)
*Mohammad Hasan Ahmadilivani,Marten Roots,Marco Restifo,Sven-Markus Loorits,Luca Di Mauro,Jaan Raik*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对安全关键领域的深度学习工作负载，本文提出了两种优于传统ECC的无存储开销轻量级容错方案（MSET和CEP），显著提升了CNN和ViT的可靠性，同时大幅降低了面积和延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习工作负载广泛应用于汽车系统和超大规模数据中心等安全关键领域，瞬态硬件故障严重威胁系统可靠性。这些工作负载属于内存密集型，其正确性高度依赖于存储在内存中的模型参数，而传统的错误纠正码（ECC）存在开销大且保护策略不够灵活的问题。

Method: 提出了两种轻量级的ECC替代方案：1) MSET（选择性加固），针对性地保护CNN和ViT参数中最脆弱的位；2) CEP（细粒度保护），为所有参数位提供精细保护。两种方法均无需额外的内存开销。

Result: 实验结果表明，两种方法显著增强了大型CNN和ViT的可靠性，在多数情况下优于传统的SECDED ECC方案。ViT仅需保护FP16和FP32表示中的最高指数位即可获得有效保护。CEP技术可将DNN的弹性提高一个数量级的BER，同时面积开销降低3.5倍，解码速度提升7倍。

Conclusion: 本文证明了针对深度学习模型参数的特定保护策略优于通用ECC方案，为安全关键系统的可靠性设计提供了高效、低开销的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern Deep Learning (DL) workloads are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains, such as automotive systems and hyperscale data centers, where transient hardware faults pose a serious threat to system reliability. These workloads are highly memory-intensive, and their correct functionality strongly depends on model parameters stored in memory, which are typically protected using Error Correction Codes (ECCs). In this work, we study ECC's impact on such models and propose two lightweight alternatives to ECCs that achieve superior reliability. The first approach, MSET, selectively hardens the most vulnerable bits in CNN and ViT parameters, while the second approach, CEP, provides fine-grained protection for all parameter bits. Experimental results demonstrate that both methods significantly enhance the reliability of large CNNs and ViTs, mostly outperforming conventional Single Error Detection Double Error Correction (SECDED) ECC schemes, with no memory overhead and, in fact, with considerably lower area and delay characteristics when compared to SECDEC. Experimental results indicate that ViTs can be effectively protected by merely protecting their highest exponent bits in FP16 and FP32 representations. Furthermore, applying the CEP technique can guarantee the resilience of DNNs by up to one order of magnitude higher BERs, with a 3.5x lower area overhead and 7x faster decoder compared to SECDED ECC.

</details>


### [345] [Accelerating Precise End-to-End Simulation: Latency-Sensitive Many-core System Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07750)
*Yinrong Li,Zexin Fu,Yichao Zhang,Germain Haugou,Chi Zhang,Marco Bertuletti,Bowen Wang,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大规模延迟敏感众核架构（如1024核TeraNoC）的快速端到端建模方法，在保持误差低于7%的同时实现了高达115倍的仿真加速，有效解决了RTL仿真过慢的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型工作负载对并行计算能力和片上存储容量提出了更高要求，同时对细粒度数据移动和同步也造成了压力。这促使研究者探索采用紧密耦合暂存器存储器（SPM）的众核加速器设计。然而，这种架构转变带来了两个挑战：随着系统复杂度增加，周期精确的RTL仿真变得极其缓慢；性能评估需要精确建模延迟敏感的互连行为。

Method: 提出了一种快速且精确的端到端建模方法，针对具有1024个核心和4MiB全局共享L1 SPM的大规模TeraNoC架构。该方法捕获跨多个互连规模的延迟敏感SPM访问的时序行为，同时抽象非必要的硬件细节，从而在保证精度的前提下大幅提升仿真速度。

Result: 在多种基准测试中，该模型与周期精确的RTL金模型的误差低于7%，同时仿真速度提升高达115倍。该框架还提供了跨处理单元和互连的详细性能分析能力。通过两个案例研究验证了其实用性：基于性能分析的FlashAttention-2优化，减少了互连停顿和同步开销；以及片上网络路由器重映射的设计空间探索，缓解了流量不平衡并提升了吞吐量。

Conclusion: 该建模框架成功平衡了仿真精度与效率，为大语言模型众核加速器的软件优化和硬件设计探索提供了高效实用的工具，能够支持端到端的软件开发和架构设计迭代。

Abstract: Modern large language model workloads put increasing demands on parallel compute capability and on-chip memory capacity, while also stressing fine-grained data movement and synchronization. These trends motivate exploring and designing many-core accelerators with tightly coupled scratchpad memory (SPM) for scalable compute and predictable, explicitly managed data access. However, this architectural shift raises two challenges: cycle-accurate register-transfer level (RTL) simulation becomes prohibitively slow as system complexity grows, and performance estimation requires precise modeling of latency-sensitive interconnect behavior. This paper presents a fast yet accurate end-to-end modeling approach for latency-sensitive many-core architectures, targeting large-scale instances such as TeraNoC with 1024 cores and a 4MiB globally shared L1 SPM. The approach captures timing behavior of latency-sensitive SPM accesses across multiple interconnect scales, while abstracting non-essential hardware details. Across diverse benchmarks, the model tracks a cycle-accurate RTL golden model with errors below 7%, while delivering up to 115x faster simulation. The framework also provides detailed profiling across processing elements and interconnect, enabling efficient end-to-end software development and hardware design exploration. Two case studies demonstrate its practicality: profiling-guided optimization of FlashAttention-2 to reduce interconnect stalls and synchronization overhead, and design space exploration of network-on-chip (NoC) router remapping to alleviate traffic imbalance and improve throughput.

</details>


### [346] [AccelSync: Verifying Synchronization Coverage in Accelerator Pipeline Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07881)
*Hangcheng An,Rui Wang,Depei Qian*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AccelSync，一个针对AI加速器流水线程序的静态验证工具，用于检测跨单元数据竞争。该方法将同步正确性问题归结为“屏障充分性”判定，证明了其可在O(|E|²)时间内判定且检测器完备可靠。在华为昇腾910B2和寒武纪MLU370上的实验显示，AccelSync在6292个生产内核中发现3个未知同步隐患，在LLM生成的内核中检出19.2%的缺陷率，且检测成本比华为运行时工具低400倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI加速器算子被编译为多级流水线程序，其中DMA、向量、矩阵和标量单元在共享片上缓冲区上并发执行。缺失或错位的同步原语会引入硬件可见的数据竞争，这类竞争既逃避了模拟测试也逃避了黄金测试，因为两者均未对加速器的跨单元可见性语义进行建模。现有的测试方法无法有效检测此类同步缺陷，因此亟需一种形式化的静态验证方法来确保加速器流水线程序的正确性。

Method: 本文将加速器流水线程序形式化为一种受限并发语言，定义了参数化的硬件事件语义，包含三种排序关系：程序序、同步序和屏障序。方法将正确性问题归约为屏障充分性判定，即检查同一缓冲区上的每个跨单元写-读对是否按happens-before关系排序。作者证明了屏障充分性在O(|E|²)时间内可判定，且检测器在建模语义下既可靠又完备。基于此理论框架，实现了AccelSync工具，通过仅修改硬件模型即可适配不同加速器架构。

Result: AccelSync在华为昇腾910B2和寒武纪MLU370两个平台上进行了验证。在来自CANN算子库的6292个生产内核中，发现了3个此前未知的同步隐患，其中1个与在昇腾910B2上观察到的非确定性输出隐患类别相匹配。在120个LLM生成的内核中，检出缺陷率为19.2%（95%置信区间[13.0%, 27.4%]）。对688个非等价变体的变异研究达到100%检测率。与华为运行时消毒器msSanitizer的对比显示，AccelSync能检测到后者遗漏的隐患，且每内核检测成本低400倍。

Conclusion: 本文为AI加速器流水线程序的同步正确性验证提供了首个形式化方法和可判定性保证，填补了跨单元可见性语义建模的空白。AccelSync工具展示了静态验证在生产环境中的实用价值，能够高效检测现有动态测试工具无法发现的同步缺陷。该方法具有良好可移植性，仅需更换硬件模型即可适配不同加速器架构，为AI基础设施的安全可靠性提供了新的技术保障。

Abstract: AI accelerator operators are compiled into multi-stage pipeline programs where DMA, vector, matrix, and scalar units execute concurrently on shared on-chip buffers. A missing or misplaced synchronization primitive introduces hardware-visible data races that escape both simulation and golden testing, because neither models the accelerator's cross-unit visibility semantics. We formalize accelerator pipeline programs as a restricted concurrent language, define a parameterized hardware event semantics with three ordering relations -- program order, synchronization order, and barrier order -- and reduce the correctness question to barrier sufficiency: whether every cross-unit write-read pair on the same buffer is ordered by happens-before. Here "barrier" denotes an abstract ordering primitive in the model, covering vendor pipe barriers, hard-event synchronization, and equivalent frontend-normalized synchronization points. We prove that barrier sufficiency is decidable in $O(|E|^2)$ time and that our checker is both sound and complete under the modeled semantics. We implement AccelSync, a static verification tool instantiated for Ascend 910B2 and Cambricon MLU370 by changing only the hardware model. On 6,292 production kernels from the CANN operator library, AccelSync identifies 3 previously unknown synchronization hazards -- one matching a hazard class for which we observed nondeterministic outputs on Ascend 910B2 under a specific toolkit/driver configuration (CANN 8.0.RC3), though this observation was not reproducible after a subsequent driver upgrade -- and on 120 LLM-generated kernels it flags a 19.2% defect rate (95% CI: [13.0%, 27.4%]). A mutation study on 688 non-equivalent mutants yields 100% detection, and a head-to-head comparison shows AccelSync detects hazards that Huawei's runtime sanitizer msSanitizer misses, at 400x lower cost per kernel.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [347] [Regulating Branch Parallelism in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06914)
*Swapnil Gandhi,Siva Hari,William J. Dally,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了TAPER，一种针对LLM请求内并行分支的每步准入控制器，通过动态评估分支外部性来解决现有系统过度或保守执行分支的问题，在保持高SLO达成率的同时显著提升了系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统在处理请求内并行分支时存在两难困境：激进地接纳分支会因共享解码步骤延迟增加而损害同批次其他请求，导致串行阶段性能下降；而保守的固定上限策略则浪费了引入并行分支所带来的吞吐量潜力。这种由已接纳分支引起的额外步骤延迟被称为“分支外部性”，其安全宽度取决于批次组成、上下文长度和累积松弛量，这些因素在工作负载追踪中持续变化。

Method: 提出TAPER系统，一种细粒度的每步准入控制器，将额外分支视为机会性工作，仅在预测的分支外部性符合批次当前松弛预算时才允许接纳。该方法利用分支级调度将计算与内存解耦的优势：分支共享请求的前缀KV缓存，因此扩展或收缩宽度无需内存回收操作，使得每步动态调节在工程上切实可行。

Result: 在Qwen3-32B模型上的实验表明，TAPER相比IRP-Off（关闭分支）将有效吞吐量提升1.77倍，相比IRP-Eager（激进策略）提升1.48倍，同时维持超过95%的SLO（服务等级目标）达成率。

Conclusion: 通过引入分支外部性概念并基于实时松弛预算进行动态准入控制，TAPER成功平衡了并行分支带来的吞吐增益与对同批次请求的延迟影响，证明了细粒度自适应策略在LLM分支并行场景下的有效性和必要性。

Abstract: Recent methods expose intra-request parallelism in LLM outputs, allowing independent branches to decode concurrently. Existing serving systems execute these branches eagerly or under fixed caps. We show that both are brittle: eager admission inflates the shared decode step, degrading co-batched requests in serial stages, while conservative fixed caps forgo the throughput that motivated exposing branches in the first place. We call the excess step latency caused by admitted branches the branch externality and show that the safe width depends on batch composition, context lengths, and accumulated slack, all of which change continuously over a workload trace. We introduce TAPER, a per-step admission controller that treats extra branches as opportunistic work, admitted only when the predicted branch externality fits within the batch's current slack budget. Per-step regulation is practical because branch-level scheduling decouples compute from memory: branches share the request's prefix KV, so expanding or contracting width requires no memory reclamation. On Qwen3-32B, TAPER improves goodput by $1.77\times$ over IRP-Off and by $1.48\times$ over IRP-Eager, while maintaining over $95\%$ SLO attainment.

</details>


### [348] [On Similarity of Computational Kernels in our Codes and Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06968)
*Michael McKinsey,Stephanie Brink,Olga Pearce*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于硬件使用情况的性能相似性度量方法，用于自动评估基准测试与HPC应用程序代码之间的代表性，替代传统的人工评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高性能计算系统快速发展，节点并行度增加且加速器广泛使用，理解代码到硬件的映射对性能优化至关重要。现有评估基准测试代表HPC代码性能特征的方法是人工的、劳动密集型的，且难以扩展到多个基准测试。

Method: 提出基于代码使用计算硬件方式的性能相似性度量指标，定义并表征两类具有相似性能特征的广泛内核类别。在Kripke代理应用和RAJA性能套件的内核上，使用纯CPU系统和GPU加速系统评估成对相似性度量。

Result: 验证了相似性度量指标能够正确地将Kripke代理应用中的内核与RAJA性能套件中的内核进行匹配。

Conclusion: 提出的相似性度量指标能够有效评估计算内核与用于代表这些代码的代理应用之间的相似性，为基准测试代表性评估提供了自动化解决方案。

Abstract: As high-performance computing (HPC) systems rapidly evolve, with increasing on-node parallelism and widespread use of accelerators, understanding how the code maps to hardware is essential for reaching optimal performance. Benchmarks are commonly used for early assessment of emerging architectures (as well as for informing the design of future hardware), but it is often unknown how well the benchmarks represent the performance characteristics of simulation codes. Existing methods for evaluating how well our benchmarks represent our HPC codes are manual, labor intensive, and challenging to scale to many benchmarks. In this paper, we propose performance similarity metrics based on how the code uses the compute hardware. We define and characterize two broad categories of kernels that exhibit similar performance characteristics. We evaluate the pairwise similarity metrics on kernels in the Kripke proxy application and the RAJA Performance Suite, using both a CPU-only system and a GPU-accelerated system. We validate that our similarity metrics correctly match a kernel in the Kripke proxy app to a kernel in the RAJA Performance Suite. Our proposed similarity metrics enable assessment of the similarity of computational kernels in our codes and the proxy applications we use to represent the codes.

</details>


### [349] [FATE: Future-State-Aware Scheduling for Heterogeneous LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07238)
*Zirui Huang,Yi-Xiang Hu,Feng Wu,Xiangyang Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出FATE调度器，通过感知未来状态优化异构LLM工作流调度，在真实基准上相比最强基线将归一化完工时间和P95延迟降低约8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工作流调度策略仅孤立优化即时队列状态、放置成本或重用信号，导致有用状态碎片化并增加端到端延迟。工作流DAG中的调度决策会影响下游阶段的执行状态（如模型驻留、父输出局部性、前缀重用、设备可达性），需要整体考量。

Method: FATE采用CP-SAT支持的前沿规划器、感知地平线的候选评分、有界多设备分片执行和状态条件成本估计。通过反复规划当前就绪前沿，根据即时成本和诱导的下游状态对分配进行评分，而非求解单体全DAG问题。

Result: 在真实DAG基准上，FATE的归一化完工时间和归一化P95延迟分别为0.675和0.677，相比RoundRobin降低32.5%和32.3%，相比最强非FATE基线降低8.9%和8.8%。机制分析表明收益源于联合保留多维未来执行状态。

Conclusion: 未来状态保留应被视为异构LLM工作流服务的一等调度目标，FATE通过联合保护多维度执行状态有效降低了端到端延迟。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications are increasingly executed as heterogeneous multi-stage workflows rather than isolated inference calls. In these workflow directed acyclic graphs (DAGs), scheduling decisions affect not only the currently ready stage, but also the execution state inherited by downstream stages, including model residency, parent-output locality, prefix reuse, and future device reachability. Existing serving and DAG-scheduling policies mainly optimize immediate queue state, placement cost, or reuse signals in isolation, which can fragment useful state and increase end-to-end latency. We present FATE, a future-state-aware scheduler for heterogeneous LLM workflows. FATE combines a CP-SAT-backed frontier planner, horizon-aware candidate scoring, bounded multi-device shard execution, and state-conditional cost estimation. Rather than solving a monolithic full-DAG problem, FATE repeatedly plans over the current ready frontier and scores assignments by both immediate cost and the downstream state they induce. Across real-DAG and controlled prefix-reuse benchmarks, FATE outperforms practical heuristics, classical DAG scheduling, and proxy adaptations of recent workflow-serving policies. On the real-DAG benchmark, it achieves normalized makespan and normalized P95 latency of 0.675 and 0.677, reducing them by 32.5% and 32.3% over RoundRobin and by 8.9% and 8.8% over the strongest non-FATE baseline. Mechanism analysis and ablations show that these gains arise from jointly preserving multiple dimensions of future execution state rather than prefix reuse alone. These results indicate that future-state preservation should be treated as a first-class scheduling objective for heterogeneous LLM workflow serving.

</details>


### [350] [MERBIT: A GPU-Based SpMV Method for Iterative Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07391)
*Qi Zhang,Zhengan Yao,Zhenglu Jiang,Zan-Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MERBIT是一种针对图结构不规则稀疏矩阵重复SpMV计算的GPU优化方法，结合merge-path划分和紧凑位域描述符，在50个大型不规则数据集上平均比cuSPARSE COO加速1.27倍（单精度）和1.25倍（双精度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏矩阵-向量乘法（SpMV）是大规模图分析和稀疏迭代求解器等迭代工作负载的核心算子。在真实世界图数据上加速SpMV具有挑战性，因为存在高度不规则的稀疏模式。现有方法在处理图状不规则稀疏矩阵时面临工作负载不平衡和内存访问不连续等问题。

Method: MERBIT结合了两个关键技术：全局层面采用merge-path划分来平衡非零元素和行边界上的工作负载；局部层面使用紧凑位域描述符编码每个merge-path段。该方法改善了工作负载平衡，促进了矩阵加载和输出写入的合并内存访问。此外，还集成了三种优化策略进一步提升性能。

Result: 在50个大型不规则数据集上的实验表明，MERBIT优于cuSPARSE、Ginkgo和学术方法等竞争基线。相比cuSPARSE COO，单精度平均加速1.27倍，双精度平均加速1.25倍。

Conclusion: MERBIT通过结合merge-path划分和位域描述符编码，有效解决了图结构稀疏矩阵SpMV计算中的负载不平衡和内存访问问题，为PageRank等迭代工作负载提供了显著的性能提升，证明了该方法在处理不规则稀疏模式方面的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) is the cornerstone in many iterative workloads, including large-scale graph analytics and sparse iterative solvers. Accelerating SpMV on real-world graphs remains challenging due to highly irregular sparsity patterns. In this paper, we propose MERBIT, a GPU SpMV method designed for repeated SpMV on irregular, graph-like sparse matrices, with PageRank as a representative motivating workload. MERBIT combines two key ideas from existing GPU SpMV methods. At the global level, it uses merge-path partitioning to balance work over nonzeros and row boundaries. At the local level, it encodes each merge-path segment using a compact bit-field descriptor. MERBIT improves workload balance and promotes coalesced memory access for both matrix loading and output writes; moreover, three optimization strategies are incorporated to further enhance performance. Experiments on 50 large irregular datasets demonstrate that MERBIT outperforms competitive baselines, including cuSPARSE, Ginkgo, and academic approaches, achieving average speedups of 1.27 and 1.25 over cuSPARSE COO in single and double precision, respectively.

</details>


### [351] [RcLLM: Accelerating Generative Recommendation via Beyond-Prefix KV Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07443)
*Zhan Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了RcLLM系统，通过超越前缀的KV缓存技术，解决了大语言模型在生成式推荐任务中因长个性化提示导致的推理延迟问题，将TTFT降低了1.31倍至9.51倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正在将推荐系统从排序任务转变为生成式任务，但工业部署受限于处理长个性化提示的高延迟。标准前缀缓存收益有限，因为推荐工作负载中的重用往往在用户历史和物品上下文之间是非连续的。

Method: 提出了RcLLM分布式推理系统，包含以下核心技术：(1) 将提示分解为可重用块；(2) 分层分布式存储设计：紧凑的用户历史缓存被复制以实现零延迟检索，海量物品缓存使用相似性感知放置进行分片；(3) 结合基于亲和力的全局调度器提高数据局部性；(4) 采用选择性注意力机制纠正近似误差以减少冗余的二次注意力计算。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，与最先进的前缀缓存系统相比，RcLLM将首字生成时间（TTFT）减少了1.31倍至9.51倍，实现了实时服务且对推荐精度的影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: RcLLM成功解决了生成式推荐中的高延迟问题，通过超越前缀的KV缓存技术和系统优化，在保持推荐精度的同时显著提升了推理速度，为工业部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are transforming recommendation from ranking into a generative task, but industrial deployment remains limited by the high latency of processing long, personalized prompts. Standard prefix caching provides limited benefit because reuse in recommendation workloads is often non-contiguous across user histories and item contexts. We present RcLLM, a distributed inference system for generative recommendation with Beyond-Prefix KV Caching. RcLLM decomposes prompts into reusable blocks and supports large item catalogs with a stratified distributed storage design: compact user-history caches are replicated for zero-latency retrieval, while massive item caches are sharded using similarity-aware placement. To reduce redundant quadratic attention computation, RcLLM combines an affinity-based global scheduler that improves data locality with a selective attention mechanism that corrects approximation errors. Experiments on real-world datasets show that RcLLM reduces Time-To-First-Token (TTFT) by 1.31x-9.51x compared with state-of-the-art prefix caching systems, enabling real-time serving with negligible impact on recommendation accuracy.

</details>


### [352] [Deadline-Driven Hierarchical Agentic Resource Sharing for AI Services and RAN Functions in AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07547)
*Haiyuan Li,Yulei Wu,Dimitra Simeonidou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层智能体框架（HAF），通过结合大语言模型（LLM）智能体进行慢时间尺度的服务放置，以及基于凸优化的快速算法进行实时资源分配，显著提升了AI-RAN系统中的计算资源共享效率和SLO达成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI-RAN将AI服务与无线接入网（RAN）功能统一部署在GPU加速的边缘基础设施上，但两者在计算资源共享方面存在核心挑战：实时RAN功能与异构AI服务的调度决策需要在不同的时间尺度上进行协调，且服务迁移会带来不可忽视的中断，影响服务级别目标（SLO）。

Method: 提出分层智能体框架（HAF）：上层采用基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体负责慢时间尺度的AI服务与RAN功能放置决策；下层采用闭式、感知截止时间的凸优化算法负责快时间尺度的GPU/CPU资源分配。LLM智能体还配备了预测评判器，用于在迁移导致的服务中断超过预期SLO收益时过滤掉迁移决策。

Result: 实验结果表明，HAF实现了90.0%的整体SLO达成率，相比最强基线提升20.5%；将AI服务请求满足率从51%提升至85.3%。在不同负载条件下HAF均保持优势，且评判器在多种开源LLM智能体上持续提升SLO达成率。

Conclusion: HAF框架有效解决了AI-RAN中多时间尺度计算资源共享的协调难题，通过LLM智能体与凸优化算法的协同，在保证实时性要求的同时显著提升了资源利用效率和服务质量，为边缘AI-RAN系统的资源管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: AI-RAN consolidates AI services and Radio Access Network (RAN) functions onto a unified, GPU-accelerated infrastructure at the network edge. However, compute sharing between real-time RAN functions and highly heterogeneous AI services requires coordination of scheduling decisions at mismatched timescales, and placement adaptation may require service migration across nodes with non-negligible interruptions.
